\frac{1}{\tfrac{1}{2}\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\cdot\frac{2}{DL\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}
.

Next, using a similar argument, we will obtain a tail bound on the uniform deviation between the empirical variance, and its population level counterpart. Since this type of bound is not very common for variances, we will first go through the details of deriving the bounded differences coefficients. Towards that end, for any real valued function
f
, let
\widetilde{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[f]
denote the variance estimate that is based on the same data used to compute
\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[f]
, except for one data point, which has been arbitrarily altered. Further suppose that this data point belonged to class
k
, for some
k\in\mathcal{Y}
. Then:

\displaystyle\Delta

\displaystyle:=\sup_{\Omega(h_{y})\leq\frac{1}{L\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}}\Big|\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[h_{y}]-\textup{\text{Var}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[h_{y}]\Big|-\sup_{\Omega(h_{y})\leq\frac{1}{L\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}}\Big|\widetilde{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[h_{y}]-\textup{\text{Var}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[h_{y}]\Big|

\displaystyle=\sup_{\Omega(h_{y})\leq\frac{1}{L\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}}\Bigg\{\Big|\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[h_{y}]-\textup{\text{Var}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[h_{y}]\Big|-\sup_{\Omega(h_{y}^{\prime})\leq\frac{1}{L\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}}\Big|\widetilde{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[h_{y}^{\prime}]-\textup{\text{Var}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[h_{y}^{\prime}]\Big|\Bigg\}

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{\Omega(h_{y})\leq\frac{1}{L\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}}\Bigg\{\Big|\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[h_{y}]-\textup{\text{Var}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[h_{y}]\Big|-\Big|\widetilde{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[h_{y}]-\textup{\text{Var}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[h_{y}]\Big|\Bigg\}

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{\Omega(h_{y})\leq\frac{1}{L\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}}\Big|\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[h_{y}]-\widetilde{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[h_{y}]\Big|

\displaystyle=\sup_{g_{y}=h_{y}-\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[h_{y}],\Omega(h_{y})\leq\frac{1}{L\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}}\Big|\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[g_{y}]-\widetilde{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[g_{y}]\Big|

\displaystyle=\sup_{g_{y}=h_{y}-\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[h_{y}],\Omega(h_{y})\leq\frac{1}{L\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}}\Big|\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[g_{y}^{2}]-\big(\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[g_{y}]\big)^{2}-\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[g_{y}^{2}]\Big|

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{g_{y}=h_{y}-\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[h_{y}],\Omega(h_{y})\leq\frac{1}{L\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}}\Big|\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[g_{y}^{2}]-\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[g_{y}^{2}]\Big|+\Bigg(\sup_{g_{y}=h_{y}-\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[h_{y}],\Omega(h_{y})\leq\frac{1}{L\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}}\Big|\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[g_{y}]\Big|\Bigg)^{2}

\displaystyle=\sup_{g_{y}=h_{y}-\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[h_{y}],\Omega(h_{y})\leq\frac{1}{L\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}}\Big|\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[g_{y}^{2}]-\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[g_{y}^{2}]\Big|+\Bigg(\sup_{\Omega(h_{y})\leq\frac{1}{L\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}}\Big|\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[h_{y}]-\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[h_{y}]\Big|\Bigg)^{2}

\displaystyle=\pi_{k}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}\sup_{g_{y}=h_{y}-\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[h_{y}],\Omega(h_{y})\leq\frac{1}{L\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}}\Big|\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{k}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[g_{y}^{2}]-\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}_{k}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[g_{y}^{2}]\Big|+\Bigg(\pi_{k}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}\sup_{\Omega(h_{y})\leq\frac{1}{L\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}}\Big|\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{k}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[h_{y}]-\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}_{k}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[h_{y}]\Big|\Bigg)^{2}.

Now, if
g_{y}=h_{y}-\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[h_{y}]
where
h_{y}
satisfies
\Omega(h_{y})\leq\frac{1}{L\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}
, then by Assumption (
•
‣
A2
), it follows that

\implies||h_{y}||_{\infty}\leq\frac{1}{DL\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}\implies||g_{y}||_{\infty}\leq\frac{2}{DL\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}\implies||g_{y}^{2}||_{\infty}\leq\frac{4}{D^{2}L^{2}\lambda_{y}}.

Next, notice that the operators
\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{k}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}
and
\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}_{k}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}
are computed on datasets that only differ by one data point. So, it follows that:

\displaystyle\implies\Delta

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\pi_{k}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}{\frac{1}{2}\pi_{k}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\frac{8}{D^{2}L^{2}\lambda_{y}}+\Bigg(\frac{\pi_{k}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}{\frac{1}{2}\pi_{k}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\frac{2}{DL\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}\Bigg)^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\frac{1}{2}n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\frac{8}{D^{2}L^{2}\lambda_{y}}+\frac{1}{\big(\frac{1}{2}n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}\big)^{2}}\frac{4}{D^{2}L^{2}\lambda_{y}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\frac{1}{2}n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\frac{8}{D^{2}L^{2}\lambda_{y}}+\frac{1}{\frac{1}{2}n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\frac{8}{D^{2}L^{2}\lambda_{y}}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\frac{1}{2}n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\frac{16}{D^{2}L^{2}\lambda_{y}}.

Thus, by McDiarmid’s inequality, it follows that:

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Bigg[\sup_{\Omega(h_{y})\leq\frac{1}{L\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}}\Big|\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[h_{y}]-\textup{\text{Var}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[h_{y}]\Big|-\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Bigg(\sup_{\Omega(h_{y})\leq\frac{1}{L\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}}\Big|\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[h_{y}]-\textup{\text{Var}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[h_{y}]\Big|\Bigg)\geq\frac{1}{2}a\Bigg]

\displaystyle\leq\exp\Bigg\{-2\frac{a^{2}/4}{\frac{1}{2}n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}\Delta^{2}}\Bigg\}

\displaystyle=\exp\Bigg\{-2\frac{a^{2}/4}{\frac{1}{2}n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}\Big(\frac{1}{\frac{1}{2}n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\frac{16}{D^{2}L^{2}\lambda_{y}}\Big)^{2}}\Bigg\}

\displaystyle=\exp\Bigg\{-\frac{D^{4}L^{4}}{1024}\lambda_{y}^{2}n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}a^{2}\Bigg\}.

Ergo:

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Bigg[\sup_{\Omega(h_{y})\leq\frac{1}{L\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}}\Big|\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[h_{y}]-\textup{\text{Var}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[h_{y}]\Big|\geq a\Bigg]

\displaystyle\leq\exp\Bigg\{-\frac{D^{4}L^{4}}{1024}\lambda_{y}^{2}n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}a^{2}\Bigg\}

\displaystyle\hskip 18.49988pt+\mathbbold{1}{\Bigg\{\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Bigg(\sup_{\Omega(h_{y})\leq\frac{1}{L\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}}\Big|\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[h_{y}]-\textup{\text{Var}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[h_{y}]\Big|\Bigg)\geq\frac{1}{2}a\Bigg\}}.

∎

Proof of Lemma (
3.1
)
.

We’ll begin by making use of the following decomposition:

\big(\mathbb{E}_{y}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\mathbb{E}_{0}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\big)-\big(\mathbb{E}_{y}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\mathbb{E}_{0}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\big)=A+B,

where

A=\big(\mathbb{E}_{y}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\mathbb{E}_{0}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\big)-\Big[\big(\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\big)-\lambda_{y}\Omega^{2}(\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y})\Big]

B=\Big[\big(\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\big)-\lambda_{y}\Omega^{2}(\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y})\Big]-\big(\mathbb{E}_{y}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\mathbb{E}_{0}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\big).

Next, we may rewrite and bound each of (A) and (B), individually. For term (A), note that:

\displaystyle A

\displaystyle=\big(\mathbb{E}_{y}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\mathbb{E}_{0}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\big)-\Big[\big(\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\big)-\lambda_{y}\Omega^{2}(\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y})\Big]

\displaystyle\leq\big(\mathbb{E}_{y}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\mathbb{E}_{0}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\big)

\displaystyle\hskip 18.49988pt-\Bigg[\frac{\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]}{\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]}\big(\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\big)-\lambda_{y}\Omega^{2}\bigg(\frac{\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]}{\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]}s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}\bigg)\Bigg]

\displaystyle=\big(\mathbb{E}_{y}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\mathbb{E}_{0}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\big)

\displaystyle\hskip 18.49988pt-\frac{\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]}{\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]}\big(\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\big)+\lambda_{y}\Bigg(\frac{\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]}{\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]}\Bigg)^{2}\Omega^{2}(s_{\gamma^{\ast},y})

\displaystyle=\big(\mathbb{E}_{y}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\mathbb{E}_{0}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\big)-\big(\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\big)

\displaystyle\hskip 18.49988pt+\Bigg(1-\frac{\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]}{\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]}\Bigg)\big(\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\big)

\displaystyle\hskip 18.49988pt+\lambda_{y}\Bigg(\frac{\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]}{\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]}\Bigg)^{2}\Omega^{2}(s_{\gamma^{\ast},y})

\displaystyle\leq\big(\mathbb{E}_{y}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\mathbb{E}_{0}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\big)-\big(\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\big)

\displaystyle+\Bigg|\Bigg(1-\frac{\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]}{\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]}\Bigg)\big(\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\big)\Bigg|

\displaystyle\hskip 18.49988pt+2\lambda_{y}\Omega^{2}(s_{\gamma^{\ast},y})+2\Bigg(\frac{\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]}{\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]}-1\Bigg)^{2}\lambda_{y}\Omega^{2}(s_{\gamma^{\ast},y})

\displaystyle\leq\Big|\big(\mathbb{E}_{y}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\mathbb{E}_{0}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\big)-\big(\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\big)\Big|+\frac{4}{L}\Bigg|\frac{\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]}{\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]}-1\Bigg|

\displaystyle\hskip 18.49988pt+2\lambda_{y}\Omega^{2}(s_{\gamma^{\ast},y})+2\Bigg(\frac{\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]}{\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]}-1\Bigg)^{2}\lambda_{y}\Omega^{2}(s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}),

where the second line is due to Assumption (
A1
) and the fact that
\frac{\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]}{\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]}s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}
is inside the feasible set of the optimization problem, the third line is due to
\Omega(\cdot)
’s assumed properties, and the sixth line uses Lemma (
6.1
) and the fact that
|s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}|\leq\frac{1}{\gamma_{0}^{\ast}}+\frac{1}{\gamma_{y}^{\ast}}\leq\frac{2}{L}
. As for term (B), note that:

\displaystyle B

\displaystyle=\Big[\big(\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\big)-\lambda_{y}\Omega^{2}(\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y})\Big]-\big(\mathbb{E}_{y}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\mathbb{E}_{0}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\big)

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{\Omega(h_{y})\leq\frac{1}{L\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}}\Big|\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[h_{y}]-\mathbb{E}_{y}[h_{y}]\Big|+\sup_{\Omega(h_{y})\leq\frac{1}{L\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}}\Big|\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[h_{y}]-\mathbb{E}_{0}[h_{y}]\Big|-\lambda_{y}\Omega^{2}(\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}),

where the second line is because
\Omega(\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y})\leq\frac{1}{L\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}
due to Lemma (
6.2
). Therefore:

\displaystyle\big(\mathbb{E}_{y}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\mathbb{E}_{0}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\big)-\big(\mathbb{E}_{y}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\mathbb{E}_{0}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\big)

\displaystyle=A+B

\displaystyle\leq R_{y}-\lambda_{y}\Omega^{2}(\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}),

where
R_{y}
is given by

\displaystyle R_{y}

\displaystyle:=\Big|\big(\mathbb{E}_{y}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\mathbb{E}_{0}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\big)-\big(\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\big)\Big|+\frac{4}{L}\Bigg|\frac{\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]}{\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]}-1\Bigg|

\displaystyle\qquad+2\lambda_{y}\Omega^{2}(s_{\gamma^{\ast},y})+2\Bigg(\frac{\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]}{\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]}-1\Bigg)^{2}\lambda_{y}\Omega^{2}(s_{\gamma^{\ast},y})

\displaystyle\qquad+\sup_{\Omega(h_{y})\leq\frac{1}{L\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}}\Big|\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[h_{y}]-\mathbb{E}_{y}[h_{y}]\Big|+\sup_{\Omega(h_{y})\leq\frac{1}{L\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}}\Big|\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[h_{y}]-\mathbb{E}_{0}[h_{y}]\Big|.

(25)

Now, it is important to note that there is another upper bound on
\big(\mathbb{E}_{y}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\mathbb{E}_{0}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\big)-\big(\mathbb{E}_{y}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\mathbb{E}_{0}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\big)
, albeit a trivial one. By Assumption (
•
‣
A2
), we have that
||\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}||_{\infty}\leq\frac{1}{D}\Omega(\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y})
, and by Lemma (
6.2
), we have that
\Omega(\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y})\leq\frac{1}{L\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}
. This implies that
||\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}||_{\infty}\leq\frac{1}{DL\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}
. We also have that
||s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}||_{\infty}\leq\frac{2}{L}
. Thus:

\displaystyle\big(\mathbb{E}_{y}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\mathbb{E}_{0}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\big)-\big(\mathbb{E}_{y}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\mathbb{E}_{0}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\big)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{4}{L}+\frac{2}{DL\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}.

So, combining our two upper bounds together, it follows that:

\displaystyle\big(\mathbb{E}_{y}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\mathbb{E}_{0}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\big)-\big(\mathbb{E}_{y}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\mathbb{E}_{0}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\big)

(26)

\displaystyle\leq\min\Bigg\{R_{y}-\lambda_{y}\Omega^{2}(\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y})\text{ },\text{ }\frac{4}{L}+\frac{2}{DL\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}\Bigg\}

\displaystyle=\min\Bigg\{R_{y}\text{ },\text{ }\frac{4}{L}+\frac{2}{DL\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}+\lambda_{y}\Omega^{2}(\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y})\Bigg\}-\lambda_{y}\Omega^{2}(\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y})

\displaystyle\leq\min\Bigg\{R_{y}\text{ },\text{ }\frac{4}{L}+\frac{2}{DL\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}+\frac{1}{L^{2}}\Bigg\}-\lambda_{y}\Omega^{2}(\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y})

\displaystyle\leq\min\Bigg\{R_{y}\text{ },\text{ }\frac{5}{L^{2}}+\frac{2}{DL\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}\Bigg\}-\lambda_{y}\Omega^{2}(\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y})

\displaystyle=T_{y}-\lambda_{y}\Omega^{2}(\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y})

(27)

\displaystyle\leq T_{y},

where the third line is by Lemma (
6.2
), the fourth line is because
0<L<1
, and

T_{y}:=\min\Bigg\{R_{y}\text{ },\text{ }\frac{5}{L^{2}}+\frac{2}{DL\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}\Bigg\}.

(28)

Next, let’s analyze the asymptotic behavior of
\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}[T_{y}]
. Observe that:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}[T_{y}]

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{\infty}\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}[T_{y}\geq\epsilon]d\epsilon

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{\frac{5}{L^{2}}+\frac{2}{DL\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}}\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}[T_{y}\geq\epsilon]d\epsilon

\displaystyle\leq\int_{0}^{\frac{5}{L^{2}}+\frac{2}{DL\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}}\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}[R_{y}\geq\epsilon]d\epsilon

\displaystyle\leq\int_{0}^{\frac{5}{L^{2}}+\frac{2}{DL\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}}\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\bigg[\Big|\big(\mathbb{E}_{y}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\mathbb{E}_{0}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\big)-\big(\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\big)\Big|\geq\frac{1}{6}\epsilon\bigg]d\epsilon

\displaystyle\qquad+\int_{0}^{\frac{5}{L^{2}}+\frac{2}{DL\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}}\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Bigg[\Bigg|\frac{\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]}{\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]}-1\Bigg|\geq\frac{L}{24}\epsilon\Bigg]d\epsilon

\displaystyle\qquad+\int_{0}^{\frac{5}{L^{2}}+\frac{2}{DL\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}}\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Big[12\lambda_{y}\Omega^{2}(s_{\gamma^{\ast},y})\geq\epsilon\Big]d\epsilon

\displaystyle\qquad+\int_{0}^{\frac{5}{L^{2}}+\frac{2}{DL\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}}\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Bigg[\Bigg(\frac{\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]}{\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]}-1\Bigg)^{2}\geq\frac{1}{12\lambda_{y}\Omega^{2}(s_{\gamma^{\ast},y})}\epsilon\Bigg]d\epsilon

\displaystyle\qquad+\int_{0}^{\frac{5}{L^{2}}+\frac{2}{DL\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}}\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Bigg[6\sup_{\Omega(h_{y})\leq\frac{1}{L\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}}\Big|\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[h_{y}]-\mathbb{E}_{y}[h_{y}]\Big|\geq\epsilon\Bigg]d\epsilon

\displaystyle\qquad+\int_{0}^{\frac{5}{L^{2}}+\frac{2}{DL\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}}\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Bigg[6\sup_{\Omega(h_{y})\leq\frac{1}{L\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}}\Big|\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[h_{y}]-\mathbb{E}_{0}[h_{y}]\Big|\geq\epsilon\Bigg]d\epsilon

\displaystyle\leq\int_{0}^{\infty}\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\bigg[6\Big|\big(\mathbb{E}_{y}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\mathbb{E}_{0}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\big)-\big(\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\big)\Big|\geq\epsilon\bigg]d\epsilon

\displaystyle\qquad+\int_{0}^{\frac{5}{L^{2}}+\frac{2}{DL\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}}\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Bigg[\Bigg|\frac{\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]}{\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]}-1\Bigg|\geq\frac{L}{24}\epsilon\Bigg]d\epsilon

\displaystyle\qquad+12\lambda_{y}\Omega^{2}(s_{\gamma^{\ast},y})

\displaystyle\qquad+\int_{0}^{\frac{5}{L^{2}}+\frac{2}{DL\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}}\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Bigg[\Bigg|\frac{\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]}{\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]}-1\Bigg|\geq\frac{1}{\sqrt{12\lambda_{y}}\Omega(s_{\gamma^{\ast},y})}\sqrt{\epsilon}\Bigg]d\epsilon

\displaystyle\qquad+\int_{0}^{\infty}\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Bigg[6\sup_{\Omega(h_{y})\leq\frac{1}{L\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}}\Big|\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[h_{y}]-\mathbb{E}_{y}[h_{y}]\Big|\geq\epsilon\Bigg]d\epsilon

\displaystyle\qquad+\int_{0}^{\infty}\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Bigg[6\sup_{\Omega(h_{y})\leq\frac{1}{L\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}}\Big|\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[h_{y}]-\mathbb{E}_{0}[h_{y}]\Big|\geq\epsilon\Bigg]d\epsilon

\displaystyle\leq 6\sqrt{\textup{\text{Var}}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Big[\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\Big]}

\displaystyle\qquad+\int_{0}^{\frac{5}{L^{2}}+\frac{2}{DL\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}}\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Bigg[\Bigg|\frac{\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]}{\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]}-1\Bigg|\geq\frac{L}{24}\epsilon\Bigg]d\epsilon

\displaystyle\qquad+12\lambda_{y}\Omega^{2}(s_{\gamma^{\ast},y})

\displaystyle\qquad+\int_{0}^{\frac{5}{L^{2}}+\frac{2}{DL\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}}\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Bigg[\Bigg|\frac{\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]}{\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]}-1\Bigg|\geq\frac{1}{\sqrt{12\lambda_{y}}\Omega(s_{\gamma^{\ast},y})}\sqrt{\epsilon}\Bigg]d\epsilon

\displaystyle\qquad+6\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Bigg[\sup_{\Omega(h_{y})\leq\frac{1}{L\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}}\Big|\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[h_{y}]-\mathbb{E}_{y}[h_{y}]\Big|\Bigg]+6\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Bigg[\sup_{\Omega(h_{y})\leq\frac{1}{L\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}}\Big|\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[h_{y}]-\mathbb{E}_{0}[h_{y}]\Big|\Bigg].

Now, note that:

\displaystyle\Bigg|\frac{\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]}{\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]}-1\Bigg|

\displaystyle=\frac{\Big|\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\Big|}{\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]}

\displaystyle=\frac{\Big|\Big\{\big(\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\big)-\big(\mathbb{E}_{y}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\mathbb{E}_{0}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\big)\Big\}+\Big\{\mathbb{E}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}^{2}]-\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\Big\}\Big|}{\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\Big|\big(\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\big)-\big(\mathbb{E}_{y}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\mathbb{E}_{0}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\big)\Big|+\Big|\mathbb{E}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}^{2}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}^{2}]\Big|+\big(\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\big)^{2}}{\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]}.

So, for any
\delta>0
:

\Bigg|\frac{\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]}{\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]}-1\Bigg|\geq\delta

\implies\Big|\big(\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\big)-\big(\mathbb{E}_{y}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\mathbb{E}_{0}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\big)\Big|+\Big|\mathbb{E}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}^{2}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}^{2}]\Big|+\big(\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\big)^{2}\geq\delta\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]

\iff\Big|\big(\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\big)-\big(\mathbb{E}_{y}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\mathbb{E}_{0}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\big)\Big|+\Big|\mathbb{E}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}^{2}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}^{2}]\Big|+\big(\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\big)^{2}+\delta\big(\mathbb{E}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}^{2}]-\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\big)\geq\delta\mathbb{E}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}^{2}]

\implies\Big|\big(\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\big)-\big(\mathbb{E}_{y}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\mathbb{E}_{0}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\big)\Big|+\Big|\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}^{2}]-\mathbb{E}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}^{2}]\Big|+\big(\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\big)^{2}+\delta\Big|\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\mathbb{E}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}^{2}]\Big|\geq\delta\sqrt{\Lambda}

Ergo:

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Bigg[\Bigg|\frac{\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]}{\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]}-1\Bigg|\geq\delta\Bigg]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Bigg[\frac{4}{\sqrt{\Lambda}}\Big|\big(\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\big)-\big(\mathbb{E}_{y}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\mathbb{E}_{0}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\big)\Big|\geq\delta\Bigg]

\displaystyle\hskip 17.70859pt+\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Bigg[\frac{4}{\sqrt{\Lambda}}\Big|\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}^{2}]-\mathbb{E}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}^{2}]\Big|\geq\delta\Bigg]

\displaystyle\hskip 17.70859pt+\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Bigg[\frac{4}{\sqrt{\Lambda}}\big(\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\big)^{2}\geq\delta\Bigg]

\displaystyle\hskip 17.70859pt+\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Bigg[\Big|\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\mathbb{E}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}^{2}]\Big|\geq\frac{\sqrt{\Lambda}}{4}\Bigg].

Therefore:

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\frac{5}{L^{2}}+\frac{2}{DL\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}}\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Bigg[\Bigg|\frac{\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]}{\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]}-1\Bigg|\geq\frac{L}{24}\epsilon\Bigg]d\epsilon

\displaystyle\leq\int_{0}^{\infty}\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Bigg[\frac{4}{\sqrt{\Lambda}}\Big|\big(\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\big)-\big(\mathbb{E}_{y}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\mathbb{E}_{0}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\big)\Big|\geq\frac{L}{24}\epsilon\Bigg]d\epsilon

\displaystyle\hskip 17.70859pt+\int_{0}^{\infty}\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Bigg[\frac{4}{\sqrt{\Lambda}}\Big|\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}^{2}]-\mathbb{E}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}^{2}]\Big|\geq\frac{L}{24}\epsilon\Bigg]d\epsilon

\displaystyle\hskip 17.70859pt+\int_{0}^{\infty}\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Bigg[\frac{4}{\sqrt{\Lambda}}\big(\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\big)^{2}\geq\frac{L}{24}\epsilon\Bigg]d\epsilon

\displaystyle\hskip 17.70859pt+\bigg(\frac{5}{L^{2}}+\frac{2}{DL\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}\bigg)\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Bigg[\Big|\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\mathbb{E}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}^{2}]\Big|\geq\frac{\sqrt{\Lambda}}{4}\Bigg]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{96}{L\sqrt{\Lambda}}\sqrt{\textup{\text{Var}}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Big[\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\Big]}+\frac{96}{L\sqrt{\Lambda}}\sqrt{\textup{\text{Var}}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Big[\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}^{2}]\Big]}

\displaystyle\hskip 17.70859pt+\frac{96}{L\sqrt{\Lambda}}\textup{\text{Var}}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Big[\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\Big]

\displaystyle\hskip 17.70859pt+\bigg(\frac{5}{L^{2}}+\frac{2}{DL\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}\bigg)\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Bigg[\Big|\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\mathbb{E}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}^{2}]\Big|\geq\frac{\sqrt{\Lambda}}{4}\Bigg]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{96}{L\sqrt{\Lambda}}\sqrt{\textup{\text{Var}}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Big[\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\Big]}+\frac{96}{L\sqrt{\Lambda}}\sqrt{\textup{\text{Var}}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Big[\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}^{2}]\Big]}

\displaystyle\hskip 17.70859pt+\frac{96}{L\sqrt{\Lambda}}\textup{\text{Var}}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Big[\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\Big]

\displaystyle\hskip 17.70859pt+\bigg(\frac{5}{L^{2}}+\frac{2}{DL\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}\bigg)\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Bigg[\Big|\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}^{2}]-\mathbb{E}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}^{2}]\Big|\geq\frac{\sqrt{\Lambda}}{8}\Bigg]

\displaystyle\hskip 17.70859pt+\bigg(\frac{5}{L^{2}}+\frac{2}{DL\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}\bigg)\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Bigg[\Big|\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\Big|\geq\sqrt{\frac{\sqrt{\Lambda}}{8}}\Bigg]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{96}{L\sqrt{\Lambda}}\sqrt{\textup{\text{Var}}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Big[\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\Big]}+\frac{96}{L\sqrt{\Lambda}}\sqrt{\textup{\text{Var}}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Big[\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}^{2}]\Big]}

\displaystyle\hskip 17.70859pt+\frac{96}{L\sqrt{\Lambda}}\textup{\text{Var}}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Big[\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\Big]

\displaystyle\hskip 17.70859pt+2\bigg(\frac{5}{L^{2}}+\frac{2}{DL\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}\bigg)\Bigg(\exp\bigg\{-\frac{L^{4}\sqrt{\Lambda}^{2}}{1024}\xi n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}\bigg\}+\exp\bigg\{-\frac{L^{2}\sqrt{\Lambda}}{32}\xi n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}\bigg\}\Bigg),

where the last line is by Hoeffding’s inequality. Analogously:

\int_{0}^{\frac{5}{L^{2}}+\frac{2}{DL\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}}\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Bigg[\Bigg|\frac{\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]}{\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]}-1\Bigg|\geq\frac{1}{\sqrt{12\lambda_{y}}\Omega(s_{\gamma^{\ast},y})}\sqrt{\epsilon}\Bigg]d\epsilon

\displaystyle\leq\int_{0}^{\infty}\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Bigg[\frac{4}{\sqrt{\Lambda}}\Big|\big(\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\big)-\big(\mathbb{E}_{y}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\mathbb{E}_{0}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\big)\Big|\geq\frac{1}{\sqrt{12\lambda_{y}}\Omega(s_{\gamma^{\ast},y})}\sqrt{\epsilon}\Bigg]d\epsilon

\displaystyle\hskip 17.70859pt+\int_{0}^{\infty}\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Bigg[\frac{4}{\sqrt{\Lambda}}\Big|\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}^{2}]-\mathbb{E}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}^{2}]\Big|\geq\frac{1}{\sqrt{12\lambda_{y}}\Omega(s_{\gamma^{\ast},y})}\sqrt{\epsilon}\Bigg]d\epsilon

\displaystyle\hskip 17.70859pt+\int_{0}^{\infty}\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Bigg[\frac{4}{\sqrt{\Lambda}}\big(\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\big)^{2}\geq\frac{1}{\sqrt{12\lambda_{y}}\Omega(s_{\gamma^{\ast},y})}\sqrt{\epsilon}\Bigg]d\epsilon

\displaystyle\hskip 17.70859pt+\bigg(\frac{5}{L^{2}}+\frac{2}{DL\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}\bigg)\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Bigg[\Big|\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\mathbb{E}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}^{2}]\Big|\geq\frac{\sqrt{\Lambda}}{4}\Bigg]

\displaystyle\leq\int_{0}^{\infty}\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Bigg[\frac{16}{\sqrt{\Lambda}^{2}}\Big|\big(\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\big)-\big(\mathbb{E}_{y}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\mathbb{E}_{0}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\big)\Big|^{2}\geq\frac{1}{12\lambda_{y}\Omega(s_{\gamma^{\ast},y})^{2}}\epsilon\Bigg]d\epsilon

\displaystyle\hskip 17.70859pt+\int_{0}^{\infty}\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Bigg[\frac{16}{\sqrt{\Lambda}^{2}}\Big|\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}^{2}]-\mathbb{E}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}^{2}]\Big|^{2}\geq\frac{1}{12\lambda_{y}\Omega(s_{\gamma^{\ast},y})^{2}}\epsilon\Bigg]d\epsilon

\displaystyle\hskip 17.70859pt+\int_{0}^{\infty}\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Bigg[\frac{16}{\sqrt{\Lambda}^{2}}\big(\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\big)^{4}\geq\frac{1}{12\lambda_{y}\Omega(s_{\gamma^{\ast},y})^{2}}\epsilon\Bigg]d\epsilon

\displaystyle\hskip 17.70859pt+\bigg(\frac{5}{L^{2}}+\frac{2}{DL\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}\bigg)\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Bigg[\Big|\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\mathbb{E}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}^{2}]\Big|\geq\frac{\sqrt{\Lambda}}{4}\Bigg]

\displaystyle=192\frac{\lambda_{y}\Omega(s_{\gamma^{\ast},y})^{2}}{\sqrt{\Lambda}^{2}}\bigg(\textup{\text{Var}}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Big[\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\Big]+\textup{\text{Var}}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Big[\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}^{2}]\Big]+\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Big[\big(\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\big)^{4}\Big]\bigg)

\displaystyle\hskip 17.70859pt+\bigg(\frac{5}{L^{2}}+\frac{2}{DL\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}\bigg)\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Bigg[\Big|\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\mathbb{E}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}^{2}]\Big|\geq\frac{\sqrt{\Lambda}}{4}\Bigg]

\displaystyle\leq 192\frac{\lambda_{y}\Omega(s_{\gamma^{\ast},y})^{2}}{\sqrt{\Lambda}^{2}}\bigg(\textup{\text{Var}}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Big[\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\Big]+\textup{\text{Var}}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Big[\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}^{2}]\Big]+\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Big[\big(\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\big)^{4}\Big]\bigg)

\displaystyle\hskip 17.70859pt+2\bigg(\frac{5}{L^{2}}+\frac{2}{DL\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}\bigg)\Bigg(\exp\bigg\{-\frac{L^{4}\sqrt{\Lambda}^{2}}{1024}\xi n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}\bigg\}+\exp\bigg\{-\frac{L^{2}\sqrt{\Lambda}}{32}\xi n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}\bigg\}\Bigg)

\displaystyle\leq 192\frac{\lambda_{y}\Omega(s_{\gamma^{\ast},y})^{2}}{\sqrt{\Lambda}^{2}}\bigg(\textup{\text{Var}}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Big[\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\Big]+\textup{\text{Var}}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Big[\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}^{2}]\Big]+\frac{4}{L^{2}}\textup{\text{Var}}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Big[\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\Big]\bigg)

\displaystyle\hskip 17.70859pt+2\bigg(\frac{5}{L^{2}}+\frac{2}{DL\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}\bigg)\Bigg(\exp\bigg\{-\frac{L^{4}\sqrt{\Lambda}^{2}}{1024}\xi n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}\bigg\}+\exp\bigg\{-\frac{L^{2}\sqrt{\Lambda}}{32}\xi n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}\bigg\}\Bigg),

where the last line is because
|s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}|\leq\frac{2}{L}\implies\big(\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\big)^{2}\leq\frac{4}{L^{2}}
. Therefore, overall, we have that:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}[T_{y}]

\displaystyle\leq 6\sqrt{\textup{\text{Var}}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Big[\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\Big]}+12\lambda_{y}\Omega^{2}(s_{\gamma^{\ast},y})

\displaystyle\hskip 17.70859pt+6\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Bigg[\sup_{\Omega(h_{y})\leq\frac{1}{L\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}}\Big|\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[h_{y}]-\mathbb{E}_{y}[h_{y}]\Big|\Bigg]+6\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Bigg[\sup_{\Omega(h_{y})\leq\frac{1}{L\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}}\Big|\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[h_{y}]-\mathbb{E}_{0}[h_{y}]\Big|\Bigg]

\displaystyle\hskip 17.70859pt+\int_{0}^{\frac{5}{L^{2}}+\frac{2}{DL\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}}\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Bigg[\Bigg|\frac{\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]}{\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]}-1\Bigg|\geq\frac{L}{24}\epsilon\Bigg]d\epsilon

\displaystyle\hskip 17.70859pt+\int_{0}^{\frac{5}{L^{2}}+\frac{2}{DL\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}}\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Bigg[\Bigg|\frac{\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]}{\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]}-1\Bigg|\geq\frac{1}{\sqrt{12\lambda_{y}}\Omega(s_{\gamma^{\ast},y})}\sqrt{\epsilon}\Bigg]d\epsilon

\displaystyle\leq 6\sqrt{\textup{\text{Var}}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Big[\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\Big]}+12\lambda_{y}\Omega^{2}(s_{\gamma^{\ast},y})+\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}[\mathcal{U}_{y}(\lambda_{y})]

\displaystyle\hskip 17.70859pt+\frac{96}{L\sqrt{\Lambda}}\bigg(\sqrt{\textup{\text{Var}}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Big[\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\Big]}+\sqrt{\textup{\text{Var}}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Big[\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}^{2}]\Big]}+\textup{\text{Var}}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Big[\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\Big]\bigg)

\displaystyle\hskip 17.70859pt+192\frac{\lambda_{y}\Omega(s_{\gamma^{\ast},y})^{2}}{\sqrt{\Lambda}^{2}}\bigg(\textup{\text{Var}}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Big[\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\Big]+\textup{\text{Var}}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Big[\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}^{2}]\Big]+\frac{4}{L^{2}}\textup{\text{Var}}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Big[\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\Big]\bigg)

\displaystyle\hskip 17.70859pt+4\bigg(\frac{5}{L^{2}}+\frac{2}{DL\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}\bigg)\Bigg(\exp\bigg\{-\frac{L^{4}\sqrt{\Lambda}^{2}}{1024}\xi n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}\bigg\}+\exp\bigg\{-\frac{L^{2}\sqrt{\Lambda}}{32}\xi n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}\bigg\}\Bigg)

Now, we know that we have a minimum of
\frac{1}{2}n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}\xi
samples in
\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}
for each class. Further, we know that
|s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}|\leq\frac{2}{L}
, meaning that the variance of
s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}
(and its powers) can all be bounded above in terms of solely
L
(via Popoviciu’s variance inequality), regardless of the distribution the variance is with respect to. Since
\xi
and
L
are constants that are independent of
w
, it therefore follows that all the variance terms displayed above go down like
O(1/n^{\textup{\text{tr}}})
. Ergo, the above is equal to:

\displaystyle=O\Bigg(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}}+\lambda_{y}\Omega^{2}(s_{\gamma^{\ast},y})+\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}[\mathcal{U}_{y}(\lambda_{y})]+\bigg(1+\frac{1}{\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}\bigg)e^{-Cn^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\Bigg),

for some
C>0
that is independent of
w
.

Next, we prove the tail bound on
T_{y}
. For any
a>0
, it follows from our work in bounding
\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}[T_{y}]
that:

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}[T_{y}\geq a]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}[R_{y}\geq a]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\bigg[\Big|\big(\mathbb{E}_{y}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\mathbb{E}_{0}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\big)-\big(\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\big)\Big|\geq\frac{1}{6}a\bigg]

\displaystyle\qquad+\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Bigg[\Bigg|\frac{\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]}{\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]}-1\Bigg|\geq\frac{L}{24}a\Bigg]

\displaystyle\qquad+\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Big[\lambda_{y}\Omega^{2}(s_{\gamma^{\ast},y})\geq\frac{1}{12}a\Big]

\displaystyle\qquad+\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Bigg[\Bigg(\frac{\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]}{\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]}-1\Bigg)^{2}\geq\frac{1}{12\lambda_{y}\Omega^{2}(s_{\gamma^{\ast},y})}a\Bigg]

\displaystyle\qquad+\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Bigg[\sup_{\Omega(h_{y})\leq\frac{1}{L\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}}\Big|\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[h_{y}]-\mathbb{E}_{y}[h_{y}]\Big|\geq\frac{1}{6}a\Bigg]+\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Bigg[\sup_{\Omega(h_{y})\leq\frac{1}{L\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}}\Big|\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[h_{y}]-\mathbb{E}_{0}[h_{y}]\Big|\geq\frac{1}{6}a\Bigg]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\bigg[\Big|\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\mathbb{E}_{y}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\Big|\geq\frac{1}{12}a\bigg]+\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\bigg[\Big|\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\mathbb{E}_{0}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\Big|\geq\frac{1}{12}a\bigg]

\displaystyle\qquad+2\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Bigg[\Bigg|\frac{\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]}{\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]}-1\Bigg|\geq\min\bigg\{\frac{L}{24}a,\frac{1}{\sqrt{12\lambda_{y}\Omega^{2}(s_{\gamma^{\ast},y})}}\sqrt{a}\bigg\}\Bigg]

\displaystyle\qquad+\mathbbold{1}{\Big\{\lambda_{y}\Omega^{2}(s_{\gamma^{\ast},y})\geq\frac{1}{12}a\Big\}}

\displaystyle\qquad+\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Bigg[\sup_{\Omega(h_{y})\leq\frac{1}{L\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}}\Big|\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[h_{y}]-\mathbb{E}_{y}[h_{y}]\Big|\geq\frac{1}{6}a\Bigg]+\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Bigg[\sup_{\Omega(h_{y})\leq\frac{1}{L\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}}\Big|\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[h_{y}]-\mathbb{E}_{0}[h_{y}]\Big|\geq\frac{1}{6}a\Bigg].

Let’s bound each of the probabilities above. Note that, for any class
j
, we have by Hoeffding’s inequality that:

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\bigg[\Big|\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\mathbb{E}_{j}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\Big|\geq\frac{1}{12}a\bigg]

\displaystyle\leq 2\exp\bigg\{-2\frac{\tfrac{1}{2}\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}\big(\tfrac{a}{12}\big)^{2}}{4/L^{2}}\bigg\}.

Also, by our earlier work and Hoeffding’s inequality, we have for any
\delta>0
that:

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Bigg[\Bigg|\frac{\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]}{\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]}-1\Bigg|\geq\delta\Bigg]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Bigg[\Big|\big(\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\big)-\big(\mathbb{E}_{y}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\mathbb{E}_{0}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\big)\Big|\geq\frac{\sqrt{\Lambda}}{4}\delta\Bigg]

\displaystyle\qquad+\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Bigg[\Big|\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}^{2}]-\mathbb{E}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}^{2}]\Big|\geq\frac{\sqrt{\Lambda}}{4}\delta\Bigg]

\displaystyle\qquad+\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Bigg[\Big|\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\Big|\geq\frac{\Lambda^{1/4}}{2}\sqrt{\delta}\Bigg]

\displaystyle\qquad+\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Bigg[\Big|\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\mathbb{E}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}^{2}]\Big|\geq\frac{\sqrt{\Lambda}}{4}\Bigg].

As for the first term, we have by Hoeffding’s inequality that:

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Bigg[\Big|\big(\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\big)-\big(\mathbb{E}_{y}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\mathbb{E}_{0}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\big)\Big|\geq\frac{\sqrt{\Lambda}}{4}\delta\Bigg]

\displaystyle\leq 2\exp\bigg\{-2\frac{\tfrac{1}{2}\pi_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}\big(\frac{\sqrt{\Lambda}}{8}\delta\big)^{2}}{4/L^{2}}\bigg\}+2\exp\bigg\{-2\frac{\tfrac{1}{2}\pi_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}\big(\frac{\sqrt{\Lambda}}{8}\delta\big)^{2}}{4/L^{2}}\bigg\}.

For the second term:

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Bigg[\Big|\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}^{2}]-\mathbb{E}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}^{2}]\Big|\geq\frac{\sqrt{\Lambda}}{4}\delta\Bigg]

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{j=0}^{m}\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Bigg[\Big|\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}^{2}]-\mathbb{E}_{j}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}^{2}]\Big|\geq\frac{\sqrt{\Lambda}}{4}\delta\Bigg]

\displaystyle\leq 2\sum_{j=0}^{m}\exp\bigg\{-2\frac{\tfrac{1}{2}\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}\big(\tfrac{\sqrt{\Lambda}}{4}\delta\big)^{2}}{16/L^{4}}\bigg\}.

For the third term:

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Bigg[\Big|\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\Big|\geq\frac{\Lambda^{1/4}}{2}\sqrt{\delta}\Bigg]

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{j=0}^{m}\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Bigg[\Big|\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\mathbb{E}_{j}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\Big|\geq\frac{\Lambda^{1/4}}{2}\sqrt{\delta}\Bigg]

\displaystyle\leq 2\sum_{j=0}^{m}\exp\bigg\{-2\frac{\tfrac{1}{2}\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}\big(\frac{\Lambda^{1/4}}{2}\sqrt{\delta}\big)^{2}}{4/L^{2}}\bigg\}.

For the fourth term:

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Bigg[\Big|\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\mathbb{E}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}^{2}]\Big|\geq\frac{\sqrt{\Lambda}}{4}\Bigg]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Bigg[\Big|\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}^{2}]-\mathbb{E}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}^{2}]\Big|\geq\frac{\sqrt{\Lambda}}{8}\Bigg]+\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Bigg[\Big(\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\Big)^{2}\geq\frac{\sqrt{\Lambda}}{8}\Bigg]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Bigg[\Big|\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}^{2}]-\mathbb{E}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}^{2}]\Big|\geq\frac{\sqrt{\Lambda}}{8}\Bigg]+\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Bigg[\Big|\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\Big|\geq\frac{\Lambda^{1/4}}{\sqrt{8}}\Bigg]

\displaystyle\leq 2\sum_{j=0}^{m}\exp\bigg\{-2\frac{\tfrac{1}{2}\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}\big(\tfrac{\sqrt{\Lambda}}{8}\big)^{2}}{16/L^{4}}\bigg\}+2\sum_{j=0}^{m}\exp\bigg\{-2\frac{\tfrac{1}{2}\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}\big(\frac{\Lambda^{1/4}}{\sqrt{8}}\big)^{2}}{4/L^{2}}\bigg\}.

Thus, it follows that:

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Bigg[\Bigg|\frac{\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]}{\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]}-1\Bigg|\geq\delta\Bigg]

\displaystyle\leq 2\exp\bigg\{-2\frac{\tfrac{1}{2}\pi_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}\big(\frac{\sqrt{\Lambda}}{8}\delta\big)^{2}}{4/L^{2}}\bigg\}+2\exp\bigg\{-2\frac{\tfrac{1}{2}\pi_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}\big(\frac{\sqrt{\Lambda}}{8}\delta\big)^{2}}{4/L^{2}}\bigg\}

\displaystyle\qquad+2\sum_{j=0}^{m}\exp\bigg\{-2\frac{\tfrac{1}{2}\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}\big(\tfrac{\sqrt{\Lambda}}{4}\delta\big)^{2}}{16/L^{4}}\bigg\}+2\sum_{j=0}^{m}\exp\bigg\{-2\frac{\tfrac{1}{2}\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}\big(\frac{\Lambda^{1/4}}{2}\sqrt{\delta}\big)^{2}}{4/L^{2}}\bigg\}

\displaystyle\qquad+2\sum_{j=0}^{m}\exp\bigg\{-2\frac{\tfrac{1}{2}\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}\big(\tfrac{\sqrt{\Lambda}}{8}\big)^{2}}{16/L^{4}}\bigg\}+2\sum_{j=0}^{m}\exp\bigg\{-2\frac{\tfrac{1}{2}\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}\big(\frac{\Lambda^{1/4}}{\sqrt{8}}\big)^{2}}{4/L^{2}}\bigg\}

\displaystyle\leq 2\exp\bigg\{-2\frac{\tfrac{1}{2}\pi_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}\big(\frac{\sqrt{\Lambda}}{8}\delta\big)^{2}}{16/L^{4}}\bigg\}+2\exp\bigg\{-2\frac{\tfrac{1}{2}\pi_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}\big(\frac{\sqrt{\Lambda}}{8}\delta\big)^{2}}{16/L^{4}}\bigg\}

\displaystyle\qquad+4\sum_{j=0}^{m}\exp\bigg\{-2\frac{\tfrac{1}{2}\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}\big(\tfrac{\sqrt{\Lambda}}{4}\delta\wedge\frac{\Lambda^{1/4}}{2}\sqrt{\delta}\big)^{2}}{16/L^{4}}\bigg\}

\displaystyle\qquad+4\sum_{j=0}^{m}\exp\bigg\{-2\frac{\tfrac{1}{2}\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}\big(\tfrac{\sqrt{\Lambda}}{8}\wedge\frac{\Lambda^{1/4}}{\sqrt{8}}\big)^{2}}{16/L^{4}}\bigg\}

\displaystyle\leq 4\exp\bigg\{-2\frac{\tfrac{1}{2}\xi n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}\big(\frac{\sqrt{\Lambda}}{8}\delta\big)^{2}}{16/L^{4}}\bigg\}

\displaystyle\qquad+4m\exp\bigg\{-2\frac{\tfrac{1}{2}\xi n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}\big(\tfrac{\sqrt{\Lambda}}{4}\delta\wedge\frac{\Lambda^{1/4}}{2}\sqrt{\delta}\big)^{2}}{16/L^{4}}\bigg\}

\displaystyle\qquad+4m\exp\bigg\{-2\frac{\tfrac{1}{2}\xi n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}\big(\tfrac{\sqrt{\Lambda}}{8}\wedge\frac{\Lambda^{1/4}}{\sqrt{8}}\big)^{2}}{16/L^{4}}\bigg\}

\displaystyle\leq 4m\exp\bigg\{-2\frac{\tfrac{1}{2}\xi n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}\big(\tfrac{\sqrt{\Lambda}}{4}\delta\wedge\frac{\Lambda^{1/4}}{2}\sqrt{\delta}\big)^{2}}{16/L^{4}}\bigg\}

\displaystyle\qquad+(4m+4)\exp\bigg\{-2\frac{\tfrac{1}{2}\xi n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}\big(\tfrac{\sqrt{\Lambda}}{8}\wedge\frac{\Lambda^{1/4}}{\sqrt{8}}\big)^{2}}{16/L^{4}}\bigg\}

\displaystyle\leq(8m+4)\exp\bigg\{-2\frac{\tfrac{1}{2}\xi n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}\big(\tfrac{\sqrt{\Lambda}}{8}\delta\wedge\frac{\Lambda^{1/4}}{8}\sqrt{\delta}\wedge\tfrac{\sqrt{\Lambda}}{8}\wedge\frac{\Lambda^{1/4}}{8}\big)^{2}}{16/L^{4}}\bigg\}

\displaystyle=4(2m+1)\exp\bigg\{-\frac{L^{4}\xi n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}\big(\sqrt{\Lambda}\delta\wedge\Lambda^{1/4}\sqrt{\delta}\wedge\sqrt{\Lambda}\wedge\Lambda^{1/4}\big)^{2}}{2^{10}}\bigg\}.

Therefore, we have that:

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Bigg[\Bigg|\frac{\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]}{\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]}-1\Bigg|\geq\min\bigg\{\frac{L}{24}a,\frac{1}{\sqrt{12\lambda_{y}\Omega^{2}(s_{\gamma^{\ast},y})}}\sqrt{a}\bigg\}\Bigg]

\displaystyle\leq 4(2m+1)\exp\bigg\{-\frac{L^{4}\xi n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}\tau(a)}{2^{10}}\bigg\},

where

\displaystyle\tau(a)

\displaystyle=\Bigg(\sqrt{\Lambda}\min\bigg\{\frac{L}{24}a,\frac{1}{\sqrt{12\lambda_{y}\Omega^{2}(s_{\gamma^{\ast},y})}}\sqrt{a}\bigg\}\wedge\Lambda^{1/4}\sqrt{\min\bigg\{\frac{L}{24}a,\frac{1}{\sqrt{12\lambda_{y}\Omega^{2}(s_{\gamma^{\ast},y})}}\sqrt{a}\bigg\}}\wedge\sqrt{\Lambda}\wedge\Lambda^{1/4}\Bigg)^{2}

\displaystyle=\Lambda\min\bigg\{\frac{L^{2}}{24^{2}}a^{2},\frac{1}{12\lambda_{y}\Omega^{2}(s_{\gamma^{\ast},y})}a\bigg\}\wedge\Lambda^{1/2}\min\bigg\{\frac{L}{24}a,\frac{1}{\sqrt{12\lambda_{y}\Omega^{2}(s_{\gamma^{\ast},y})}}\sqrt{a}\bigg\}\wedge\Lambda\wedge\Lambda^{1/2}.

Thus, combining all of our work on the tail bound, we have that:

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}[T_{y}\geq a]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\bigg[\Big|\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\mathbb{E}_{y}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\Big|\geq\frac{1}{12}a\bigg]+\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\bigg[\Big|\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\mathbb{E}_{0}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\Big|\geq\frac{1}{12}a\bigg]

\displaystyle\qquad+2\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Bigg[\Bigg|\frac{\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]}{\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]}-1\Bigg|\geq\min\bigg\{\frac{L}{24}a,\frac{1}{\sqrt{12\lambda_{y}\Omega^{2}(s_{\gamma^{\ast},y})}}\sqrt{a}\bigg\}\Bigg]

\displaystyle\qquad+\mathbbold{1}{\Big\{\lambda_{y}\Omega^{2}(s_{\gamma^{\ast},y})\geq\frac{1}{12}a\Big\}}

\displaystyle\qquad+\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Bigg[\sup_{\Omega(h_{y})\leq\frac{1}{L\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}}\Big|\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[h_{y}]-\mathbb{E}_{y}[h_{y}]\Big|\geq\frac{1}{6}a\Bigg]+\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Bigg[\sup_{\Omega(h_{y})\leq\frac{1}{L\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}}\Big|\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[h_{y}]-\mathbb{E}_{0}[h_{y}]\Big|\geq\frac{1}{6}a\Bigg]

\displaystyle\leq 4\exp\bigg\{-2\frac{\tfrac{1}{2}\xi n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}\big(\tfrac{a}{12}\big)^{2}}{4/L^{2}}\bigg\}+8(2m+1)\exp\bigg\{-\frac{L^{4}\xi n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}\tau(a)}{2^{10}}\bigg\}+\mathbbold{1}{\Big\{\lambda_{y}\Omega^{2}(s_{\gamma^{\ast},y})\geq\frac{1}{12}a\Big\}}

\displaystyle\qquad+\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Bigg[\sup_{\Omega(h_{y})\leq\frac{1}{L\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}}\Big|\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[h_{y}]-\mathbb{E}_{y}[h_{y}]\Big|\geq\frac{1}{6}a\Bigg]+\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Bigg[\sup_{\Omega(h_{y})\leq\frac{1}{L\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}}\Big|\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[h_{y}]-\mathbb{E}_{0}[h_{y}]\Big|\geq\frac{1}{6}a\Bigg]

\displaystyle=4\exp\bigg\{-\frac{L^{2}\xi n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}{576}a^{2}\bigg\}+8(2m+1)\exp\bigg\{-\frac{L^{4}\xi n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}{2^{10}}\tau(a)\bigg\}+\mathbbold{1}{\Big\{\lambda_{y}\Omega^{2}(s_{\gamma^{\ast},y})\geq\frac{1}{12}a\Big\}}

\displaystyle\qquad+\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Bigg[\sup_{\Omega(h_{y})\leq\frac{1}{L\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}}\Big|\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[h_{y}]-\mathbb{E}_{y}[h_{y}]\Big|\geq\frac{1}{6}a\Bigg]+\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Bigg[\sup_{\Omega(h_{y})\leq\frac{1}{L\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}}\Big|\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[h_{y}]-\mathbb{E}_{0}[h_{y}]\Big|\geq\frac{1}{6}a\Bigg],

as claimed!

∎

Proof of Lemma (
3.2
)
.

Consider any
y\in[m]
. Observe that:

\displaystyle\textup{\text{Var}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}-s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]

(29)

\displaystyle=\textup{\text{Var}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]+\textup{\text{Var}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-2\textup{\text{Cov}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y},\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]

\displaystyle=\textup{\text{Var}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]+\big(\mathbb{E}_{y}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\mathbb{E}_{0}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\big)-2\big(\mathbb{E}_{y}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\mathbb{E}_{0}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\big)

\displaystyle=\Big[\textup{\text{Var}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\big(\mathbb{E}_{y}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\mathbb{E}_{0}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\big)\Big]

\displaystyle\hskip 18.49988pt+\Big[\big(\mathbb{E}_{y}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\mathbb{E}_{0}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\big)-\big(\mathbb{E}_{y}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\mathbb{E}_{0}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\big)\Big]

\displaystyle=\Big[\textup{\text{Var}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\Big]+\Big[\big(\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\big)-\big(\mathbb{E}_{y}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\mathbb{E}_{0}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\big)\Big]

\displaystyle\hskip 18.49988pt+\Big[\big(\mathbb{E}_{y}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\mathbb{E}_{0}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\big)-\big(\mathbb{E}_{y}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\mathbb{E}_{0}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\big)\Big]

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{\Omega(h_{y})\leq\frac{1}{L\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}}\Big|\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[h_{y}]-\textup{\text{Var}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[h_{y}]\Big|+\sup_{\Omega(h_{y})\leq\frac{1}{L\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}}\Big|\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[h_{y}]-\mathbb{E}_{y}[h_{y}]\Big|+\sup_{\Omega(h_{y})\leq\frac{1}{L\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}}\Big|\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[h_{y}]-\mathbb{E}_{0}[h_{y}]\Big|

\displaystyle\hskip 18.49988pt+\Big[\big(\mathbb{E}_{y}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\mathbb{E}_{0}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\big)-\big(\mathbb{E}_{y}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\mathbb{E}_{0}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\big)\Big]

(30)

\displaystyle=\mathcal{U}_{y}(\lambda_{y})+\Big[\big(\mathbb{E}_{y}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\mathbb{E}_{0}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\big)-\big(\mathbb{E}_{y}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\mathbb{E}_{0}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\big)\Big]

\displaystyle\leq\mathcal{U}_{y}(\lambda_{y})+T_{y},

where the fourth line is due to the constraints of the procedure used to create
\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}
, the fifth line uses Lemma (
6.2
), and the seventh line is by Lemma (
3.1
). Ergo:

\displaystyle\implies\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Big[\textup{\text{Var}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}-s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\Big]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}[\mathcal{U}_{y}(\lambda_{y})]+\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}[T_{y}]=O\Big(\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}[T_{y}]\Big),

as claimed. Finally, as for the tail bound on
\textup{\text{Var}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}-s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]
, it follows from our work above that, for any
a>0
:

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Big[\textup{\text{Var}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}-s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\geq a\Big]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\bigg[T_{y}\geq\frac{1}{4}a\bigg]+\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Bigg[\sup_{\Omega(h_{y})\leq\frac{1}{L\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}}\Big|\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[h_{y}]-\textup{\text{Var}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[h_{y}]\Big|\geq\frac{1}{4}a\Bigg]

\displaystyle\qquad+\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Bigg[\sup_{\Omega(h_{y})\leq\frac{1}{L\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}}\Big|\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[h_{y}]-\mathbb{E}_{y}[h_{y}]\Big|\geq\frac{1}{4}a\Bigg]

\displaystyle\qquad+\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Bigg[\sup_{\Omega(h_{y})\leq\frac{1}{L\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}}\Big|\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[h_{y}]-\mathbb{E}_{0}[h_{y}]\Big|\geq\frac{1}{4}a\Bigg],

as claimed!

∎

Proof of Lemma (
6.10
)
.

It follows from line (
27
) in the proof of Lemma (
3.1
) that:

\displaystyle\big(\mathbb{E}_{y}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\mathbb{E}_{0}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\big)-\big(\mathbb{E}_{y}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},y}]-\mathbb{E}_{0}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},y}]\big)

\displaystyle=\Big[\big(\mathbb{E}_{y}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\mathbb{E}_{0}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\big)-\big(\mathbb{E}_{y}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\mathbb{E}_{0}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\big)\Big]

\displaystyle\qquad+\Big[\big(\mathbb{E}_{y}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\mathbb{E}_{0}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\big)-\big(\mathbb{E}_{y}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},y}]-\mathbb{E}_{0}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},y}]\big)\Big]

\displaystyle\leq T_{y}-\lambda_{y}\Omega^{2}(\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y})

\displaystyle\qquad+\Big[\big(\mathbb{E}_{y}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\mathbb{E}_{0}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\big)-\big(\mathbb{E}_{y}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},y}]-\mathbb{E}_{0}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},y}]\big)\Big].

Next, observe that:

\displaystyle\big(\mathbb{E}_{y}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\mathbb{E}_{0}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\big)-\big(\mathbb{E}_{y}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},y}]-\mathbb{E}_{0}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},y}]\big)

\displaystyle=\Big[\big(\mathbb{E}_{y}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\mathbb{E}_{0}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\big)-\big(\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\big)\Big]

\displaystyle\qquad+\Big[\big(\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\big)-\big(\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},y}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},y}]\big)\Big]

\displaystyle\qquad+\Big[\big(\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},y}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},y}]\big)-\big(\mathbb{E}_{y}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},y}]-\mathbb{E}_{0}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},y}]\big)\Big]

\displaystyle\leq 2\sup_{\Omega(h_{y})\leq\frac{1}{L\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}}\big|\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[h_{y}]-\mathbb{E}_{y}[h_{y}]\big|+2\sup_{\Omega(h_{y})\leq\frac{1}{L\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}}\big|\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[h_{y}]-\mathbb{E}_{y}[h_{y}]\big|

\displaystyle\qquad+\Big[\big(\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\big)-\big(\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},y}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},y}]\big)\Big],

where the inequality follows from Lemma (
6.2
) and the fact that Lemma (
6.2
) also holds if we replace
\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}
with
\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},y}
, due to Assumption (
A7
) (this is evident from a quick inspect of the proof of Lemma (
6.2
), wherein the only property of
\gamma^{\ast}
that is used is the fact that the components are all lower bounded by
L
). Also observe that:

\displaystyle\big(\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\big)-\big(\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},y}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},y}]\big)

\displaystyle\leq\big(\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\big)-\big(\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},y}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},y}]-\lambda_{y}\Omega(\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},y})^{2}\big)

\displaystyle\leq\big(\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\big)

\displaystyle\hskip 18.49988pt-\frac{\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]}{\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}_{\widehat{\gamma}}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]}\big(\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\big)

\displaystyle\hskip 18.49988pt+\Bigg(\frac{\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]}{\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}_{\widehat{\gamma}}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]}\Bigg)^{2}\lambda_{y}\Omega(\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y})^{2}

\displaystyle=\big(\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\big)\Bigg[1-\frac{\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]}{\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}_{\widehat{\gamma}}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]}\Bigg]

\displaystyle\hskip 18.49988pt+\Bigg(\frac{\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]}{\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}_{\widehat{\gamma}}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]}\Bigg)^{2}\lambda_{y}\Omega(\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y})^{2}

\displaystyle=\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\Bigg[1-\frac{\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]}{\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}_{\widehat{\gamma}}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]}\Bigg]+\Bigg(\frac{\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]}{\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}_{\widehat{\gamma}}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]}\Bigg)^{2}\lambda_{y}\Omega(\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y})^{2},

where the second inequality is because of
\Omega(\cdot)
’s assumed properties and the fact that
\frac{\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]}{\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}_{\widehat{\gamma}}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]}\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}
is inside the feasible set of the optimization problem which produced
\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},y}
, and the last line uses the moment constraints of the optimization problem which produced
\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}
. Combining all of our work in this proof so far, it follows that:

\displaystyle\big(\mathbb{E}_{y}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\mathbb{E}_{0}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\big)-\big(\mathbb{E}_{y}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},y}]-\mathbb{E}_{0}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},y}]\big)

(31)

\displaystyle\leq T_{y}+2\sup_{\Omega(h_{y})\leq\frac{1}{L\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}}\big|\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[h_{y}]-\mathbb{E}_{y}[h_{y}]\big|+2\sup_{\Omega(h_{y})\leq\frac{1}{L\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}}\big|\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[h_{y}]-\mathbb{E}_{y}[h_{y}]\big|

\displaystyle\qquad+\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\Bigg|1-\frac{\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]}{\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}_{\widehat{\gamma}}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]}\Bigg|

\displaystyle\qquad+\lambda_{y}\Omega^{2}(\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y})\Bigg|\Bigg(\frac{\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]}{\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}_{\widehat{\gamma}}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]}\Bigg)^{2}-1\Bigg|

\displaystyle\leq T_{y}+2\sup_{\Omega(h_{y})\leq\frac{1}{L\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}}\big|\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[h_{y}]-\mathbb{E}_{y}[h_{y}]\big|+2\sup_{\Omega(h_{y})\leq\frac{1}{L\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}}\big|\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[h_{y}]-\mathbb{E}_{y}[h_{y}]\big|

\displaystyle\qquad+\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\Bigg|1-\frac{\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]}{\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}_{\widehat{\gamma}}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]}\Bigg|+\frac{1}{L^{2}}\Bigg|1-\Bigg(\frac{\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]}{\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}_{\widehat{\gamma}}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]}\Bigg)^{2}\Bigg|

\displaystyle=T_{y}+2\sup_{\Omega(h_{y})\leq\frac{1}{L\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}}\big|\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[h_{y}]-\mathbb{E}_{y}[h_{y}]\big|+2\sup_{\Omega(h_{y})\leq\frac{1}{L\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}}\big|\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[h_{y}]-\mathbb{E}_{y}[h_{y}]\big|

\displaystyle\qquad+\Bigg(\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]+\frac{1}{L^{2}}\Bigg|1+\frac{\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]}{\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}_{\widehat{\gamma}}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]}\Bigg|\Bigg)\Bigg|1-\frac{\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]}{\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}_{\widehat{\gamma}}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]}\Bigg|,

(32)

where the second inequality follows from Lemma (
6.2
). Next, we bound
\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]
:

\displaystyle\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]

(33)

\displaystyle=\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\textup{\text{Var}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]+\textup{\text{Var}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{\Omega(h_{y})\leq\frac{1}{L\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}}\big|\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[h_{y}]-\textup{\text{Var}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[h_{y}]\big|+\textup{\text{Var}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]

\displaystyle=\sup_{\Omega(h_{y})\leq\frac{1}{L\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}}\big|\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[h_{y}]-\textup{\text{Var}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[h_{y}]\big|+\textup{\text{Var}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}+(\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}-s_{\gamma^{\ast},y})]

\displaystyle=\sup_{\Omega(h_{y})\leq\frac{1}{L\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}}\big|\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[h_{y}]-\textup{\text{Var}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[h_{y}]\big|+\textup{\text{Var}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]+\textup{\text{Var}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}-s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]+2\textup{\text{Cov}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y},\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}-s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{\Omega(h_{y})\leq\frac{1}{L\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}}\big|\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[h_{y}]-\textup{\text{Var}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[h_{y}]\big|+\frac{1}{L^{2}}+\textup{\text{Var}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}-s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]+\frac{2}{L}\sqrt{\textup{\text{Var}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}-s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]},

(34)

where the last line is due to Popoviciu’s variance inequality and the fact that
|s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}|\leq\frac{2}{L}
. Next, we will show that
\frac{\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]}{\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\widehat{\gamma}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]}
