cannot be too far from
1
. Towards that end, note that the proof of Lemma (
2.5
) can easily be extended to the case where the class mixture densities are replaced by their empirical counterparts. That is, let
\beta
be any vector in the
(m+1)
-dimensional probability simplex, let
f
be any real-valued function, and define the row vector
\widehat{a}_{f}=:\big(\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{1}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[f]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[f],\dots,\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{m}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[f]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[f]\big)
. Then:

\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\beta}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[f]=\sum_{j=0}^{m}\beta_{j}\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[f]+\widehat{a}_{f}\mathcal{I}(\beta;\text{Cat})^{-1}\widehat{a}_{f}^{T}.

Thus, if
\alpha
is another vector in the
(m+1)
-dimensional probability simplex, we can write:

\frac{\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\beta}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[f]}{\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\alpha}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[f]}=\frac{\sum_{j=0}^{m}\beta_{j}\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[f]+\widehat{a}_{f}\mathcal{I}(\beta;\text{Cat})^{-1}\widehat{a}_{f}^{T}}{\sum_{j=0}^{m}\alpha_{j}\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[f]+\widehat{a}_{f}\mathcal{I}(\alpha;\text{Cat})^{-1}\widehat{a}_{f}^{T}}

Now, a consequence of Lemma (
6.3
) is that both
\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast};\text{Cat})^{-1}
and
\mathcal{I}(\widehat{\gamma};\text{Cat})^{-1}
are positive definite. Thus, in the RHS above, both the numerator and denominator are sums of two positive real numbers. Ergo, by the mediant inequality, it follows that:

\displaystyle\min\Bigg\{\frac{\sum_{j=0}^{m}\beta_{j}\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[f]}{\sum_{j=0}^{m}\alpha_{j}\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[f]}\text{ },\text{ }\frac{\widehat{a}_{f}\mathcal{I}(\beta;\text{Cat})^{-1}\widehat{a}_{f}^{T}}{\widehat{a}_{f}\mathcal{I}(\alpha;\text{Cat})^{-1}\widehat{a}_{f}^{T}}\Bigg\}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\beta}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[f]}{\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\alpha}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[f]}\leq\max\Bigg\{\frac{\sum_{j=0}^{m}\beta_{j}\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[f]}{\sum_{j=0}^{m}\alpha_{j}\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[f]}\text{ },\text{ }\frac{\widehat{a}_{f}\mathcal{I}(\beta;\text{Cat})^{-1}\widehat{a}_{f}^{T}}{\widehat{a}_{f}\mathcal{I}(\alpha;\text{Cat})^{-1}\widehat{a}_{f}^{T}}\Bigg\}

Further, since
\mathcal{I}(\beta;\text{Cat})^{-1}
is clearly symmetric and
\mathcal{I}(\alpha;\text{Cat})^{-1}
is positive definite, we know that:

\lambda_{\textup{min}}\big(\mathcal{I}(\alpha;\text{Cat})\mathcal{I}(\beta;\text{Cat})^{-1}\big)\leq\frac{\widehat{a}_{f}\mathcal{I}(\beta;\text{Cat})^{-1}\widehat{a}_{f}^{T}}{\widehat{a}_{f}\mathcal{I}(\alpha;\text{Cat})^{-1}\widehat{a}_{f}^{T}}\leq\lambda_{\textup{max}}\big(\mathcal{I}(\alpha;\text{Cat})\mathcal{I}(\beta;\text{Cat})^{-1}\big).

Applying Lemma (
6.4
), it follows that

\implies 1-(m+1)\frac{||\alpha-\beta||_{1}}{\min_{j}\alpha_{j}}\leq\frac{\widehat{a}_{f}\mathcal{I}(\beta;\text{Cat})^{-1}\widehat{a}_{f}^{T}}{\widehat{a}_{f}\mathcal{I}(\alpha;\text{Cat})^{-1}\widehat{a}_{f}^{T}}\leq 1+(m+1)\frac{||\alpha-\beta||_{1}}{\min_{j}\alpha_{j}}.

By virtue of the generalized weighted mediant inequality
[
Bensimhoun, 2013
]
, we can also get the following lower and upper bounds on
\frac{\sum_{j=0}^{m}\beta_{j}\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[f]}{\sum_{j=0}^{m}\alpha_{j}\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[f]}
:

\min_{j}\frac{\beta_{j}}{\alpha_{j}}\leq\frac{\sum_{j=0}^{m}\beta_{j}\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[f]}{\sum_{j=0}^{m}\alpha_{j}\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[f]}\leq\max_{j}\frac{\beta_{j}}{\alpha_{j}}

\implies 1+\min_{j}\frac{\beta_{j}-\alpha_{j}}{\alpha_{j}}\leq\frac{\sum_{j=0}^{m}\beta_{j}\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[f]}{\sum_{j=0}^{m}\alpha_{j}\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[f]}\leq 1+\max_{j}\frac{\beta_{j}-\alpha_{j}}{\alpha_{j}}

\implies 1-\frac{||\alpha-\beta||_{1}}{\min_{j}\alpha_{j}}\leq\frac{\sum_{j=0}^{m}\beta_{j}\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[f]}{\sum_{j=0}^{m}\alpha_{j}\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[f]}\leq 1+\frac{||\alpha-\beta||_{1}}{\min_{j}\alpha_{j}}.

Ergo:

1-(m+1)\frac{||\alpha-\beta||_{1}}{\min_{j}\alpha_{j}}\leq\frac{\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\beta}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[f]}{\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\alpha}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[f]}\leq 1+(m+1)\frac{||\alpha-\beta||_{1}}{\min_{j}\alpha_{j}}.

Thus, we have that

\Bigg|1-\frac{\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\beta}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[f]}{\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\alpha}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[f]}\Bigg|\leq(m+1)\frac{||\alpha-\beta||_{1}}{\min_{j}\alpha_{j}},

and that

\Bigg|1+\frac{\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\beta}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[f]}{\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\alpha}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[f]}\Bigg|\leq 2+(m+1)\frac{||\alpha-\beta||_{1}}{\min_{j}\alpha_{j}}.

Since these last two displays are true for all real-valued functions
f
and probability vectors
\alpha,\beta
, we may apply them to
f=\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast}}
,
\alpha=\widehat{\gamma}
, and
\beta=\gamma^{\ast}
. Doing so yields

\Bigg|1-\frac{\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast}}]}{\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\widehat{\gamma}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast}}]}\Bigg|\leq(m+1)\frac{||\widehat{\gamma}-\gamma^{\ast}||_{1}}{\min_{j}\widehat{\gamma}_{j}}\leq\frac{m+1}{L}||\widehat{\gamma}-\gamma^{\ast}||_{1},

and

\Bigg|1+\frac{\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast}}]}{\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\widehat{\gamma}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast}}]}\Bigg|\leq 2+\frac{m+1}{L}||\widehat{\gamma}-\gamma^{\ast}||_{1}.

Combined these results with the upper bounds in line (
32
) and (
34
), it follows that:

\big(\mathbb{E}_{y}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\mathbb{E}_{0}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\big)-\big(\mathbb{E}_{y}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},y}]-\mathbb{E}_{0}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},y}]\big)

\leq T_{y}+2\sup_{\Omega(h_{y})\leq\frac{1}{L\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}}\big|\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[h_{y}]-\mathbb{E}_{y}[h_{y}]\big|+2\sup_{\Omega(h_{y})\leq\frac{1}{L\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}}\big|\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[h_{y}]-\mathbb{E}_{y}[h_{y}]\big|

+\frac{m+1}{L}||\widehat{\gamma}-\gamma^{\ast}||_{1}\Bigg(\frac{3}{L^{2}}+\sup_{\Omega(h_{y})\leq\frac{1}{L\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}}\big|\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[h_{y}]-\textup{\text{Var}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[h_{y}]\big|+\textup{\text{Var}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}-s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]+\frac{2}{L}\sqrt{\textup{\text{Var}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}-s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]}+\frac{m+1}{L^{3}}||\widehat{\gamma}-\gamma^{\ast}||_{1}\Bigg)

\leq T_{y}+2\sup_{\Omega(h_{y})\leq\frac{1}{L\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}}\big|\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[h_{y}]-\mathbb{E}_{y}[h_{y}]\big|+2\sup_{\Omega(h_{y})\leq\frac{1}{L\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}}\big|\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[h_{y}]-\mathbb{E}_{y}[h_{y}]\big|

+\frac{m+1}{L}||\widehat{\gamma}-\gamma^{\ast}||_{1}\Bigg(\frac{3+2(m+1)}{L^{3}}+\sup_{\Omega(h_{y})\leq\frac{1}{L\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}}\big|\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[h_{y}]-\textup{\text{Var}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[h_{y}]\big|+\textup{\text{Var}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}-s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]+\frac{2}{L}\sqrt{\textup{\text{Var}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}-s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]}\Bigg)

\leq T_{y}+2\sup_{\Omega(h_{y})\leq\frac{1}{L\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}}\big|\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[h_{y}]-\mathbb{E}_{y}[h_{y}]\big|+2\sup_{\Omega(h_{y})\leq\frac{1}{L\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}}\big|\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[h_{y}]-\mathbb{E}_{y}[h_{y}]\big|+2\frac{m+1}{L}\sup_{\Omega(h_{y})\leq\frac{1}{L\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}}\big|\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[h_{y}]-\textup{\text{Var}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[h_{y}]\big|

+\frac{3(m+1)+2(m+1)^{2}}{L^{4}}||\widehat{\gamma}-\gamma^{\ast}||_{1}+\frac{m+1}{L}||\widehat{\gamma}-\gamma^{\ast}||_{1}\Bigg(\textup{\text{Var}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}-s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]+\frac{2}{L}\sqrt{\textup{\text{Var}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}-s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]}\Bigg).

\leq T_{y}+2\sup_{\Omega(h_{y})\leq\frac{1}{L\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}}\big|\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[h_{y}]-\mathbb{E}_{y}[h_{y}]\big|+2\sup_{\Omega(h_{y})\leq\frac{1}{L\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}}\big|\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[h_{y}]-\mathbb{E}_{y}[h_{y}]\big|+2\frac{m+1}{L}\sup_{\Omega(h_{y})\leq\frac{1}{L\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}}\big|\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[h_{y}]-\textup{\text{Var}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[h_{y}]\big|

+\frac{3(m+1)+2(m+1)^{2}}{L^{4}}||\widehat{\gamma}-\gamma^{\ast}||_{1}+2\frac{m+1}{L}\textup{\text{Var}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}-s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]+2\frac{m+1}{L^{2}}||\widehat{\gamma}-\gamma^{\ast}||_{1}\sqrt{\textup{\text{Var}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}-s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]}.

=:\widetilde{T}_{y}.

Note that:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{II}}}}[\widetilde{T}_{y}]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}[T_{y}]+2\frac{m+1}{L}\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}[\mathcal{U}_{y}(\lambda_{y})]+\frac{3(m+1)+2(m+1)^{2}}{L^{4}}\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{II}}}}\Big[||\widehat{\gamma}-\gamma^{\ast}||_{1}\Big]

\displaystyle\hskip 18.49988pt+2\frac{m+1}{L}\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Big[\textup{\text{Var}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}-s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\Big]+2\frac{m+1}{L^{2}}\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{II}}}}\bigg[||\widehat{\gamma}-\gamma^{\ast}||_{1}\sqrt{\textup{\text{Var}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}-s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]}\bigg]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}[T_{y}]+2\frac{m+1}{L}\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}[\mathcal{U}_{y}(\lambda_{y})]+\frac{3(m+1)+2(m+1)^{2}}{L^{4}}\sqrt{\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{II}}}}\Big[||\widehat{\gamma}-\gamma^{\ast}||_{1}^{2}\Big]}

\displaystyle\hskip 18.49988pt+2\frac{m+1}{L}\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Big[\textup{\text{Var}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}-s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\Big]+2\frac{m+1}{L^{2}}\sqrt{\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{II}}}}\Big[||\widehat{\gamma}-\gamma^{\ast}||_{1}^{2}\Big]\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Big[\textup{\text{Var}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}-s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\Big]}

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}[T_{y}]+2\frac{m+1}{L}\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}[\mathcal{U}_{y}(\lambda_{y})]+\frac{3(m+1)+2(m+1)^{2}}{L^{4}}\sqrt{m\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{II}}}}\Big[||\widehat{\gamma}-\gamma^{\ast}||_{2}^{2}\Big]}

\displaystyle\hskip 18.49988pt+2\frac{m+1}{L}\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Big[\textup{\text{Var}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}-s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\Big]+2\frac{m+1}{L^{2}}\sqrt{m\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{II}}}}\Big[||\widehat{\gamma}-\gamma^{\ast}||_{2}^{2}\Big]\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Big[\textup{\text{Var}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}-s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\Big]}

\displaystyle=O\Bigg(\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}[T_{y}]+\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}[\mathcal{U}_{y}(\lambda_{y})]+\sqrt{\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{II}}}}\Big[||\widehat{\gamma}-\gamma^{\ast}||_{2}^{2}\Big]}+\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Big[\textup{\text{Var}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}-s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\Big]\Bigg),

as desired. Next, we obtain a tail bound on
\widetilde{T}_{y}
. Using the definition of
\widetilde{T}_{y}
, it follows for any
a>0
that:

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{II}}}}[\widetilde{T}_{y}\geq a]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}[T_{y}\geq a/7]+\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Bigg[\sup_{\Omega(h_{y})\leq\frac{1}{L\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}}\big|\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[h_{y}]-\mathbb{E}_{y}[h_{y}]\big|\geq\frac{1}{14}a\Bigg]+\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Bigg[\sup_{\Omega(h_{y})\leq\frac{1}{L\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}}\big|\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[h_{y}]-\mathbb{E}_{0}[h_{y}]\big|\geq\frac{1}{14}a\Bigg]

\displaystyle\hskip 18.49988pt+\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Bigg[\sup_{\Omega(h_{y})\leq\frac{1}{L\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}}\big|\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[h_{y}]-\textup{\text{Var}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[h_{y}]\big|\geq\frac{1}{14}\frac{L}{m+1}a\Bigg]+\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{II}}}}\Bigg[||\widehat{\gamma}-\gamma^{\ast}||_{1}\geq\frac{L^{4}}{21(m+1)+14(m+1)^{2}}a\Bigg]

\displaystyle\hskip 18.49988pt+\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Bigg[\textup{\text{Var}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}-s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\geq\frac{1}{14}\frac{L}{m+1}a\Bigg]+\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Bigg[||\widehat{\gamma}-\gamma^{\ast}||_{1}\sqrt{\textup{\text{Var}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}-s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]}\geq\frac{1}{14}\frac{L^{2}}{m+1}a\Bigg]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}[T_{y}\geq a/7]+\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Bigg[\sup_{\Omega(h_{y})\leq\frac{1}{L\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}}\big|\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[h_{y}]-\mathbb{E}_{y}[h_{y}]\big|\geq\frac{1}{14}a\Bigg]+\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Bigg[\sup_{\Omega(h_{y})\leq\frac{1}{L\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}}\big|\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[h_{y}]-\mathbb{E}_{0}[h_{y}]\big|\geq\frac{1}{14}a\Bigg]

\displaystyle\hskip 18.49988pt+\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Bigg[\sup_{\Omega(h_{y})\leq\frac{1}{L\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}}\big|\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[h_{y}]-\textup{\text{Var}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[h_{y}]\big|\geq\frac{1}{14}\frac{L}{m+1}a\Bigg]+\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{II}}}}\Bigg[||\widehat{\gamma}-\gamma^{\ast}||_{1}\geq\frac{L^{4}}{21(m+1)+14(m+1)^{2}}a\Bigg]

\displaystyle\hskip 18.49988pt+\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Bigg[\textup{\text{Var}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}-s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\geq\frac{1}{14}\frac{L}{m+1}a\Bigg]+\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Bigg[||\widehat{\gamma}-\gamma^{\ast}||_{1}\geq\sqrt{\frac{1}{14}\frac{L^{2}}{m+1}a}\Bigg]

\displaystyle\hskip 18.49988pt+\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Bigg[\textup{\text{Var}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}-s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\geq\frac{1}{14}\frac{L^{2}}{m+1}a\Bigg]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}[T_{y}\geq a/7]+\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Bigg[\sup_{\Omega(h_{y})\leq\frac{1}{L\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}}\big|\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[h_{y}]-\mathbb{E}_{y}[h_{y}]\big|\geq\frac{1}{14}a\Bigg]+\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Bigg[\sup_{\Omega(h_{y})\leq\frac{1}{L\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}}\big|\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[h_{y}]-\mathbb{E}_{0}[h_{y}]\big|\geq\frac{1}{14}a\Bigg]

\displaystyle\hskip 18.49988pt+\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Bigg[\sup_{\Omega(h_{y})\leq\frac{1}{L\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}}\big|\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[h_{y}]-\textup{\text{Var}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[h_{y}]\big|\geq\frac{1}{14}\frac{L}{m+1}a\Bigg]+2\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{II}}}}\Bigg[||\widehat{\gamma}-\gamma^{\ast}||_{2}\geq\widetilde{\tau}_{1}(a)\Bigg]

\displaystyle\hskip 18.49988pt+2\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Bigg[\textup{\text{Var}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}-s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\geq\frac{1}{14}\frac{L^{2}}{m+1}a\Bigg],

as desired!
∎

Proof of Lemma (
6.11
)
.

Observe that:

\displaystyle\textup{\text{Var}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},y}-s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]

\displaystyle=\textup{\text{Var}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},y}]+\textup{\text{Var}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-2\textup{\text{Cov}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},y},s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]

\displaystyle=\textup{\text{Var}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},y}]+\big(\mathbb{E}_{y}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\mathbb{E}_{0}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\big)-2\big(\mathbb{E}_{y}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},y}]-\mathbb{E}_{0}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},y}]\big)

\displaystyle=\textup{\text{Var}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},y}]-\big(\mathbb{E}_{y}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},y}]-\mathbb{E}_{0}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},y}]\big)

\displaystyle\qquad+\big(\mathbb{E}_{y}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\mathbb{E}_{0}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\big)-\big(\mathbb{E}_{y}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},y}]-\mathbb{E}_{0}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},y}]\big)

\displaystyle=\Big[\textup{\text{Var}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},y}]-\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\widehat{\gamma}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},y}]\Big]+\Big[\big(\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},y}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},y}]\big)-\big(\mathbb{E}_{y}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},y}]-\mathbb{E}_{0}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},y}]\big)\Big]

\displaystyle\qquad+\big(\mathbb{E}_{y}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\mathbb{E}_{0}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\big)-\big(\mathbb{E}_{y}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},y}]-\mathbb{E}_{0}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},y}]\big)

\displaystyle\leq\Big[\textup{\text{Var}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},y}]-\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\widehat{\gamma}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},y}]\Big]+\sup_{\Omega(h_{y})\leq\frac{1}{L\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}}\Big|\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[h_{y}]-\mathbb{E}_{y}[h_{y}]\Big|+\sup_{\Omega(h_{y})\leq\frac{1}{L\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}}\Big|\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[h_{y}]-\mathbb{E}_{0}[h_{y}]\Big|

\displaystyle\qquad+\widetilde{T}_{y},

where the last inequality uses Lemma (
6.10
). Next, let’s obtain an upper bound on
\textup{\text{Var}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},y}]-\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\widehat{\gamma}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},y}]
. Using an argument very similar to the one employed in Lemma (
6.10
), one can show that

\frac{\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\widehat{\gamma}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},y}]}{\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},y}]}\geq 1-\frac{m+1}{L}||\widehat{\gamma}-\gamma^{\ast}||_{1},

which implies that

\displaystyle\textup{\text{Var}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},y}]-\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\widehat{\gamma}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},y}]

\displaystyle=\textup{\text{Var}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},y}]-\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},y}]\frac{\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\widehat{\gamma}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},y}]}{\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},y}]}

\displaystyle\leq\textup{\text{Var}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},y}]-\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},y}]+\frac{m+1}{L}||\widehat{\gamma}-\gamma^{\ast}||_{1}\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},y}]

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{\Omega(h_{y})\leq\frac{1}{L\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}}\Big|\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[h_{y}]-\textup{\text{Var}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[h_{y}]\Big|+\frac{m+1}{L}||\widehat{\gamma}-\gamma^{\ast}||_{1}\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},y}].

Next, we will get a bound on
\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},y}]
. Observe that:

\displaystyle\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},y}]

\displaystyle=\Bigg(\frac{\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},y}]}{\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},y}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},y}]}\Bigg)^{2}\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}\Bigg[\frac{\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},y}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},y}]}{\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},y}]}\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},y}\Bigg]

\displaystyle=\Bigg(\frac{\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},y}]}{\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},y}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},y}]}\Bigg)^{2}\Bigg\{\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}\Bigg[\frac{\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},y}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},y}]}{\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},y}]}\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},y}\Bigg]-\lambda_{y}\Omega\Bigg(\frac{\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},y}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},y}]}{\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},y}]}\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},y}\Bigg)^{2}\Bigg\}

\displaystyle\hskip 18.49988pt+\lambda_{y}\Bigg(\frac{\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},y}]}{\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},y}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},y}]}\Bigg)^{2}\Omega\Bigg(\frac{\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},y}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},y}]}{\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},y}]}\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},y}\Bigg)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\Bigg(\frac{\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},y}]}{\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},y}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},y}]}\Bigg)^{2}\Bigg\{\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]-\lambda_{y}\Omega(\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y})^{2}\Bigg\}+\lambda_{y}\Omega(\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},y})^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\Bigg(\frac{\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},y}]}{\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},y}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},y}]}\Bigg)^{2}\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]+\frac{1}{L^{2}}

\displaystyle=\Bigg(\frac{\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},y}]}{\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\widehat{\gamma}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},y}]}\Bigg)^{2}\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]+\frac{1}{L^{2}}

\displaystyle=\Bigg[\Bigg(\frac{\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},y}]}{\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\widehat{\gamma}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},y}]}\Bigg)^{2}-1\Bigg]\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]+\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]+\frac{1}{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\Bigg|\Bigg(\frac{\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},y}]}{\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\widehat{\gamma}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},y}]}\Bigg)-1\Bigg|\Bigg|\Bigg(\frac{\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},y}]}{\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\widehat{\gamma}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},y}]}\Bigg)+1\Bigg|\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]+\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]+\frac{1}{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{m+1}{L}||\widehat{\gamma}-\gamma^{\ast}||_{1}\Big(2+\frac{m+1}{L}||\widehat{\gamma}-\gamma^{\ast}||_{1}\Big)\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]+\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]+\frac{1}{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq 2\frac{m+1}{L}\Big(2+2\frac{m+1}{L}\Big)\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]+\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]+\frac{1}{L^{2}}

\displaystyle=4\frac{m+1}{L}\Big(1+\frac{m+1}{L}\Big)\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]+\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]+\frac{1}{L^{2}}

\displaystyle=\bigg[4\frac{m+1}{L}\Big(1+\frac{m+1}{L}\Big)+1\bigg]\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]+\frac{1}{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{5(m+2)^{2}}{L^{2}}\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]+\frac{1}{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{5(m+2)^{2}}{L^{2}}\bigg(\sup_{\Omega(h_{y})\leq\frac{1}{L\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}}\big|\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[h_{y}]-\textup{\text{Var}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[h_{y}]\big|+\frac{1}{L^{2}}+\textup{\text{Var}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}-s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]

\displaystyle\hskip 18.49988pt\hskip 18.49988pt+\frac{2}{L}\sqrt{\textup{\text{Var}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}-s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]}\bigg)+\frac{1}{L^{2}},

where the third line utilizes the fact that the function
\frac{\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},y}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},y}]}{\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},y}]}\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},y}
is inside of the feasible set of the optimization problem solved by
\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}
, the fifth line utilizes the fact
\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},y}
satisfies the constraints of the optimization problem it solves, the eight line uses the same argument from Lemma (
6.10
) to control the ratio of empirical variances, and the last line is by line (
34
). Thus, it follows that:

\displaystyle\textup{\text{Var}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},y}]-\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\widehat{\gamma}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},y}]

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{\Omega(h_{y})\leq\frac{1}{L\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}}\Big|\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[h_{y}]-\textup{\text{Var}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[h_{y}]\Big|+\frac{m+1}{L}||\widehat{\gamma}-\gamma^{\ast}||_{1}\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},y}]

\displaystyle\leq 11\frac{(m+2)^{3}}{L^{3}}\sup_{\Omega(h_{y})\leq\frac{1}{L\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}}\Big|\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[h_{y}]-\textup{\text{Var}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[h_{y}]\Big|+\frac{6(m+2)^{3}}{L^{5}}||\widehat{\gamma}-\gamma^{\ast}||_{1}

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{10(m+2)^{3}}{L^{3}}\textup{\text{Var}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}-s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]+\frac{10(m+2)^{3}}{L^{4}}||\widehat{\gamma}-\gamma^{\ast}||_{1}\sqrt{\textup{\text{Var}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}-s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]}.

So, bringing all of our work in this proof together, it follows that:

\displaystyle\textup{\text{Var}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},y}-s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]

\displaystyle\leq\widetilde{T}_{y}+\sup_{\Omega(h_{y})\leq\frac{1}{L\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}}\Big|\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[h_{y}]-\mathbb{E}_{y}[h_{y}]\Big|+\sup_{\Omega(h_{y})\leq\frac{1}{L\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}}\Big|\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[h_{y}]-\mathbb{E}_{0}[h_{y}]\Big|+\Big[\textup{\text{Var}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},y}]-\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\widehat{\gamma}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},y}]\Big]

\displaystyle\leq\widetilde{T}_{y}+\sup_{\Omega(h_{y})\leq\frac{1}{L\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}}\Big|\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[h_{y}]-\mathbb{E}_{y}[h_{y}]\Big|+\sup_{\Omega(h_{y})\leq\frac{1}{L\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}}\Big|\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[h_{y}]-\mathbb{E}_{0}[h_{y}]\Big|

\displaystyle\qquad+11\frac{(m+2)^{3}}{L^{3}}\sup_{\Omega(h)\leq\frac{1}{L\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}}\Big|\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[h]-\textup{\text{Var}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[h]\Big|+\frac{6(m+2)^{3}}{L^{5}}||\widehat{\gamma}-\gamma^{\ast}||_{1}

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{10(m+2)^{3}}{L^{3}}\textup{\text{Var}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}-s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]+\frac{10(m+2)^{3}}{L^{4}}||\widehat{\gamma}-\gamma^{\ast}||_{1}\sqrt{\textup{\text{Var}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}-s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]}.

So:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{II}}}}\Big[\textup{\text{Var}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},y}-s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\Big]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{II}}}}[\widetilde{T}_{y}]+11\frac{(m+2)^{3}}{L^{3}}\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Big[\mathcal{U}_{y}(\lambda_{y})\Big]+\frac{6(m+2)^{3}}{L^{5}}\sqrt{m\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{II}}}}\Big[||\widehat{\gamma}-\gamma^{\ast}||_{2}^{2}\Big]}

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{10(m+2)^{3}}{L^{3}}\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{II}}}}\Big[\textup{\text{Var}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}-s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\Big]

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{10(m+2)^{3}}{L^{4}}\sqrt{m\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{II}}}}\Big[||\widehat{\gamma}-\gamma^{\ast}||_{2}^{2}\Big]\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Big[\textup{\text{Var}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}-s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\Big]}

\displaystyle=O\Bigg(\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{II}}}}[\widetilde{T}_{y}]+\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Big[\mathcal{U}_{y}(\lambda_{y})\Big]+\sqrt{\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{II}}}}\Big[||\widehat{\gamma}-\gamma^{\ast}||_{2}^{2}\Big]}+\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{II}}}}\Big[\textup{\text{Var}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}-s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\Big]\Bigg),

as claimed. Finally, let’s obtain a tail bound on
\textup{\text{Var}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},y}-s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]
. For any
a>0
, we have that:

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{II}}}}\Big[\textup{\text{Var}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},y}-s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\geq a\Big]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{II}}}}[\widetilde{T}_{y}\geq a/7]+\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Bigg[\sup_{\Omega(h_{y})\leq\frac{1}{L\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}}\Big|\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[h_{y}]-\mathbb{E}_{y}[h_{y}]\Big|\geq a/7\Bigg]

\displaystyle\qquad+\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Bigg[\sup_{\Omega(h_{y})\leq\frac{1}{L\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}}\Big|\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[h_{y}]-\mathbb{E}_{0}[h_{y}]\Big|\geq a/7\Bigg]

\displaystyle\qquad+\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Bigg[\sup_{\Omega(h_{y})\leq\frac{1}{L\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}}\Big|\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[h_{y}]-\textup{\text{Var}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[h_{y}]\Big|\geq\frac{L^{3}}{77(m+2)^{3}}a\Bigg]

\displaystyle\qquad+\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{II}}}}\Bigg[||\widehat{\gamma}-\gamma^{\ast}||_{2}\geq\frac{L^{5}}{66\sqrt{m}(m+2)^{3}}a\Bigg]

\displaystyle\qquad+\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Bigg[\textup{\text{Var}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}-s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\geq\frac{L^{3}}{70(m+2)^{3}}a\Bigg]

\displaystyle\qquad+\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Bigg[||\widehat{\gamma}-\gamma^{\ast}||_{2}\geq\sqrt{\frac{L^{4}}{70m(m+2)^{3}}a}\Bigg]

\displaystyle\qquad+\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Bigg[\textup{\text{Var}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}-s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\geq\frac{L^{4}}{70(m+2)^{3}}a\Bigg]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{II}}}}[\widetilde{T}_{y}\geq a/7]+\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Bigg[\sup_{\Omega(h_{y})\leq\frac{1}{L\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}}\Big|\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[h_{y}]-\mathbb{E}_{y}[h_{y}]\Big|\geq a/7\Bigg]

\displaystyle\qquad+\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Bigg[\sup_{\Omega(h_{y})\leq\frac{1}{L\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}}\Big|\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[h_{y}]-\mathbb{E}_{0}[h_{y}]\Big|\geq a/7\Bigg]

\displaystyle\qquad+\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Bigg[\sup_{\Omega(h_{y})\leq\frac{1}{L\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}}\Big|\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[h_{y}]-\textup{\text{Var}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[h_{y}]\Big|\geq\frac{L^{3}}{77(m+2)^{3}}a\Bigg]

\displaystyle\qquad+2\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{II}}}}\Bigg[||\widehat{\gamma}-\gamma^{\ast}||_{2}\geq\widetilde{\tau}_{2}(a)\Bigg]

\displaystyle\qquad+2\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Bigg[\textup{\text{Var}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}-s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\geq\frac{L^{4}}{70(m+2)^{3}}a\Bigg],

as claimed.

∎

Proof of Lemma (
6.12
)
.

Observe that:

\displaystyle||\text{col }y\text{ of }\widehat{A}-\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})||_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=\Big|\Big|(\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr,I}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma}}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,I}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma}}])-(\mathbb{E}_{y}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]-\mathbb{E}_{0}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}}])\Big|\Big|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 2\Big|\Big|(\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr,I}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma}}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,I}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma}}])-(\mathbb{E}_{y}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma}}]-\mathbb{E}_{0}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma}}])\Big|\Big|_{2}^{2}+2\Big|\Big|(\mathbb{E}_{y}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma}}]-\mathbb{E}_{0}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma}}])-(\mathbb{E}_{y}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]-\mathbb{E}_{0}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}}])\Big|\Big|_{2}^{2}.

Let’s get control of the second term. Note that:

\displaystyle\Big|\Big|(\mathbb{E}_{y}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma}}]-\mathbb{E}_{0}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma}}])-(\mathbb{E}_{y}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]-\mathbb{E}_{0}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}}])\Big|\Big|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=\sum_{j=1}^{m}\Big((\mathbb{E}_{y}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},j}]-\mathbb{E}_{0}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},j}])-(\mathbb{E}_{y}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},j}]-\mathbb{E}_{0}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},j}])\Big)^{2}

\displaystyle=\sum_{j=1}^{m}\Big(\textup{\text{Cov}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y},\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},j}-s_{\gamma^{\ast},j}]\Big)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\textup{\text{Var}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\sum_{j=1}^{m}\textup{\text{Var}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},j}-s_{\gamma^{\ast},j}]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{L^{2}}\sum_{j=1}^{m}\textup{\text{Var}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},j}-s_{\gamma^{\ast},j}].

Ergo:

\displaystyle||\text{col }y\text{ of }\widehat{A}-\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})||_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 2\sum_{j=1}^{m}\Big((\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr,I}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},j}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,I}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},j}])-(\mathbb{E}_{y}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},j}]-\mathbb{E}_{0}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},j}])\Big)^{2}+\frac{2}{L^{2}}\sum_{j=1}^{m}\textup{\text{Var}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},j}-s_{\gamma^{\ast},j}].

Next, let us analyze the behavior of
(\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr,I}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},j}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,I}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},j}])-(\mathbb{E}_{y}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},j}]-\mathbb{E}_{0}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},j}])
, for arbitrary
j\in[m]
. Note that the conditional expectation (given
\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{II}}}
) of this random variable is
0
. Ergo:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}}\bigg[\Big((\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr,I}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},j}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,I}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},j}])-(\mathbb{E}_{y}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},j}]-\mathbb{E}_{0}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},j}])\Big)^{2}\bigg]

(35)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{II}}}}\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,I}}}\mid\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{II}}}}\bigg[\Big((\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr,I}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},j}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,I}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},j}])-(\mathbb{E}_{y}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},j}]-\mathbb{E}_{0}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},j}])\Big)^{2}\bigg]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{II}}}}\textup{\text{Var}}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,I}}}\mid\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{II}}}}\Big[\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr,I}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},j}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,I}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},j}]\Big]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{II}}}}\bigg\{\textup{\text{Var}}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,I}}}\mid\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{II}}}}\Big[\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr,I}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},j}]\Big]+\textup{\text{Var}}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,I}}}\mid\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{II}}}}\Big[\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,I}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},j}]\Big]\bigg\}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2}{\xi n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{II}}}}\bigg\{\textup{\text{Var}}_{y}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},j}]+\textup{\text{Var}}_{0}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},j}]\bigg\}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2}{L\xi n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{II}}}}\bigg\{\gamma_{y}^{\ast}\textup{\text{Var}}_{y}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},j}]+\gamma_{0}^{\ast}\textup{\text{Var}}_{0}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},j}]\bigg\}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2}{L\xi n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{II}}}}\bigg\{\textup{\text{Var}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},j}]\bigg\}

\displaystyle=\frac{2}{L\xi n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{II}}}}\bigg\{\textup{\text{Var}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[(\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},j}-s_{\gamma^{\ast},j})+s_{\gamma^{\ast},j}]\bigg\}

\displaystyle=\frac{2}{L\xi n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{II}}}}\bigg\{\textup{\text{Var}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},j}-s_{\gamma^{\ast},j}]\bigg\}+\frac{2}{L\xi n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{II}}}}\bigg\{\textup{\text{Var}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},j}]\bigg\}

\displaystyle\hskip 18.49988pt+\frac{4}{L\xi n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{II}}}}\bigg\{\textup{\text{Cov}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},j}-s_{\gamma^{\ast},j},s_{\gamma^{\ast},j}]\bigg\}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2}{L\xi n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{II}}}}\bigg\{\textup{\text{Var}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},j}-s_{\gamma^{\ast},j}]\bigg\}+\frac{2}{L^{3}\xi n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}

\displaystyle\hskip 18.49988pt+\frac{4}{L\xi n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{II}}}}\bigg\{\sqrt{\textup{\text{Var}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},j}-s_{\gamma^{\ast},j}]\textup{\text{Var}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},j}]}\bigg\}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2}{L\xi n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{II}}}}\Big[\textup{\text{Var}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},j}-s_{\gamma^{\ast},j}]\Big]+\frac{2}{L^{3}\xi n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}

\displaystyle\hskip 18.49988pt+\frac{4}{L^{2}\xi n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\sqrt{\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{II}}}}\Big[\textup{\text{Var}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},j}-s_{\gamma^{\ast},j}]\Big]}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{4}{L^{3}\xi n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\bigg(1+\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{II}}}}\Big[\textup{\text{Var}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},j}-s_{\gamma^{\ast},j}]\Big]+\sqrt{\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{II}}}}\Big[\textup{\text{Var}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},j}-s_{\gamma^{\ast},j}]\Big]}\bigg)

\displaystyle=O\bigg(\frac{1}{n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\bigg),

(36)

where the sixth line follows from the Law of Total Variance. Thus, it follows that:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}}\Big[||\text{col }y\text{ of }\widehat{A}-\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})||_{2}^{2}\Big]

\displaystyle=O\Bigg(\frac{1}{n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}+\sum_{j=1}^{m}\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{II}}}}\Big[\textup{\text{Var}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},j}-s_{\gamma^{\ast},j}]\Big]\Bigg),

as claimed! Next, let’s prove our tail bound on
||\text{col }y\text{ of }\widehat{A}-\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})||_{2}^{2}
. By our work earlier in this proof, we have that, for any
a>0
:

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}}\Big[||\text{col }y\text{ of }\widehat{A}-\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})||_{2}^{2}\geq a\Big]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}}\bigg[2\sum_{j=1}^{m}\Big((\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr,I}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},j}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,I}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},j}])-(\mathbb{E}_{y}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},j}]-\mathbb{E}_{0}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},j}])\Big)^{2}\geq\frac{1}{2}a\bigg]

\displaystyle\qquad+\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{II}}}}\bigg[\frac{2}{L^{2}}\sum_{j=1}^{m}\textup{\text{Var}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},j}-s_{\gamma^{\ast},j}]\geq\frac{1}{2}a\bigg]

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{j=1}^{m}\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}}\bigg[\Big|(\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr,I}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},j}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,I}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},j}])-(\mathbb{E}_{y}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},j}]-\mathbb{E}_{0}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},j}])\Big|\geq\frac{1}{2\sqrt{m}}\sqrt{a}\bigg]

\displaystyle\qquad+\sum_{j=1}^{m}\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{II}}}}\bigg[\textup{\text{Var}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},j}-s_{\widehat{\gamma},j}]\geq\frac{L^{2}}{4m}a\bigg]

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{j=1}^{m}\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}}\bigg[\Big|\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,I}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},j}]-\mathbb{E}_{0}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},j}]\Big|\geq\frac{1}{4\sqrt{m}}\sqrt{a}\bigg]

\displaystyle\qquad+\sum_{j=1}^{m}\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}}\bigg[\Big|\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr,I}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},j}]-\mathbb{E}_{y}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},j}]\Big|\geq\frac{1}{4\sqrt{m}}\sqrt{a}\bigg]

\displaystyle\qquad+\sum_{j=1}^{m}\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{II}}}}\bigg[\textup{\text{Var}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},j}-s_{\gamma^{\ast},j}]\geq\frac{L^{2}}{4m}a\bigg]

\displaystyle=\sum_{j=1}^{m}\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{II}}}}\bigg\{\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,I}}}}\bigg[\Big|\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,I}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},j}]-\mathbb{E}_{0}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},j}]\Big|\geq\frac{1}{4\sqrt{m}}\sqrt{a}\bigg]\bigg\}

\displaystyle\qquad+\sum_{j=1}^{m}\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{II}}}}\bigg\{\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,I}}}}\bigg[\Big|\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr,I}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},j}]-\mathbb{E}_{y}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},j}]\Big|\geq\frac{1}{4\sqrt{m}}\sqrt{a}\bigg]\bigg\}

\displaystyle\qquad+\sum_{j=1}^{m}\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{II}}}}\bigg[\textup{\text{Var}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},j}-s_{\gamma^{\ast},j}]\geq\frac{L^{2}}{4m}a\bigg].

Towards bounding the display above, let’s get a bound on
\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,I}}}}\bigg[\Big|\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{k}^{\textup{\text{tr,I}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},j}]-\mathbb{E}_{k}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},j}]\Big|\geq\frac{1}{4\sqrt{m}}\sqrt{a}\bigg]
, for each class
k
. As was argued earlier,
||\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},j}||_{\infty}\leq\frac{1}{DL\sqrt{\lambda_{j}}}
, so applying Hoeffding’s inequality yields (for fixed
\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}
):

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,I}}}}\bigg[\Big|\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{k}^{\textup{\text{tr,I}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},j}]-\mathbb{E}_{k}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},j}]\Big|\geq\frac{1}{4\sqrt{m}}\sqrt{a}\bigg]

\displaystyle\leq 2\exp\bigg\{-2\frac{\big(\frac{1}{2}\pi_{k}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}\big)\big(\frac{1}{4\sqrt{m}}\sqrt{a}\big)^{2}}{\big(\frac{2}{DL\sqrt{\lambda_{j}}}\big)^{2}}\bigg\}

\displaystyle\leq 2\exp\bigg\{-\frac{\frac{1}{16m}a}{\frac{4}{D^{2}L^{2}\lambda_{j}}}\xi n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}\bigg\}

\displaystyle\leq 2\exp\bigg\{-\frac{\xi L^{2}D^{2}\lambda_{j}n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}{64m}a\bigg\}.

Therefore, it follows that:

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\Big[||\text{col }y\text{ of }\widehat{A}-\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})||_{2}^{2}\geq a\Big]

\displaystyle\leq 4\sum_{j=1}^{m}\exp\bigg\{-\frac{\xi L^{2}D^{2}\lambda_{j}n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}{64m}a\bigg\}+\sum_{j=1}^{m}\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\bigg[\textup{\text{Var}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},j}-s_{\gamma^{\ast},j}]\geq\frac{L^{2}}{4m}a\bigg],

as claimed!

∎

Proof of Lemma (
6.13
)
.

Observe that:

\displaystyle\sigma_{\textup{max}}\big(\widehat{A}^{-1}-\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})^{-1}\big)\mathbbold{1}{\big\{\sigma_{\textup{min}}\big(\widehat{A}\big)>0\big\}}

\displaystyle=\sigma_{\textup{max}}\big(\widehat{A}^{-1}[\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})-\widehat{A}]\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})^{-1}\big)\mathbbold{1}{\big\{\sigma_{\textup{min}}\big(\widehat{A}\big)>0\big\}}

\displaystyle\leq\sigma_{\textup{max}}\big(\widehat{A}^{-1}\big)\sigma_{\textup{max}}\big(\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})-\widehat{A}\big)\sigma_{\textup{max}}\big(\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})^{-1}\big)\mathbbold{1}{\big\{\sigma_{\textup{min}}\big(\widehat{A}\big)>0\big\}}

\displaystyle=\frac{\sigma_{\textup{max}}\big(\widehat{A}-\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})\big)}{\sigma_{\textup{min}}\big(\widehat{A}\big)\sigma_{\textup{min}}\big(\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})\big)}\mathbbold{1}{\big\{\sigma_{\textup{min}}\big(\widehat{A}\big)>0\big\}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\sigma_{\textup{max}}\big(\widehat{A}-\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})\big)}{\sigma_{\textup{min}}\big(\widehat{A}\big)\sqrt{\Lambda}}\mathbbold{1}{\big\{\sigma_{\textup{min}}\big(\widehat{A}\big)>0\big\}}.

First, let’s get control over
\sigma_{\textup{max}}\big(\widehat{A}-\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})\big)
. Note that:

\displaystyle\sigma_{\textup{max}}\big(\widehat{A}-\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})\big)

\displaystyle=\sqrt{\lambda_{\textup{max}}\big((\widehat{A}-\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast}))^{T}(\widehat{A}-\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast}))\big)}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{\sum_{i=1}^{m}\sum_{j=1}^{m}\Big|\big[(\widehat{A}-\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast}))^{T}(\widehat{A}-\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast}))\big]_{i,j}\Big|}

\displaystyle=\sqrt{\sum_{i=1}^{m}\sum_{j=1}^{m}\Big|\big\langle\text{col }i\text{ of }\widehat{A}-\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast}),\text{col }j\text{ of }\widehat{A}-\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})\big\rangle\Big|}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{\sum_{i=1}^{m}\sum_{j=1}^{m}\big|\big|\text{col }i\text{ of }\widehat{A}-\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})\big|\big|_{2}\big|\big|\text{col }j\text{ of }\widehat{A}-\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})\big|\big|_{2}}

\displaystyle=\sum_{j=1}^{m}\big|\big|\text{col }j\text{ of }\widehat{A}-\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})\big|\big|_{2}.

Thus, so far, we have shown that:

\displaystyle\implies\sigma_{\textup{max}}\big(\widehat{A}^{-1}-\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})^{-1}\big)\mathbbold{1}{\big\{\sigma_{\textup{min}}\big(\widehat{A}\big)>0\big\}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\sum_{j=1}^{m}\big|\big|\text{col }j\text{ of }\widehat{A}-\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})\big|\big|_{2}}{\sqrt{\Lambda}}\cdot\frac{\mathbbold{1}{\big\{\sigma_{\textup{min}}\big(\widehat{A}\big)>0\big\}}}{\sigma_{\textup{min}}\big(\widehat{A}\big)}.

Next, let’s get control over
\sigma_{\textup{min}}\big(\widehat{A}\big)
. Namely, we need to show that
\sigma_{\textup{min}}\big(\widehat{A}\big)
cannot be “too” small. Note that:

\displaystyle\sqrt{\Lambda}

\displaystyle\leq\sigma_{\textup{min}}\big(\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})\big)

\displaystyle=\sqrt{\min_{||v||_{2}=1}v^{T}\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})^{T}\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})v}

\displaystyle=\sqrt{\min_{||v||_{2}=1}\Big\{v^{T}\widehat{A}^{T}\widehat{A}v+v^{T}\Big(\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})^{T}\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})-\widehat{A}^{T}\widehat{A}\Big)v}\Big\}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{\min_{||v||_{2}=1}v^{T}\widehat{A}^{T}\widehat{A}v+\max_{||v||_{2}=1}v^{T}\Big(\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})^{T}\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})-\widehat{A}^{T}\widehat{A}\Big)v}

\displaystyle=\sqrt{\min_{||v||_{2}=1}v^{T}\widehat{A}^{T}\widehat{A}v+\lambda_{\textup{max}}\big(\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})^{T}\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})-\widehat{A}^{T}\widehat{A}\big)}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{\min_{||v||_{2}=1}v^{T}\widehat{A}^{T}\widehat{A}v+|\lambda_{\textup{max}}\big(\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})^{T}\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})-\widehat{A}^{T}\widehat{A}\big)|}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{\min_{||v||_{2}=1}v^{T}\widehat{A}^{T}\widehat{A}v}+\sqrt{|\lambda_{\textup{max}}\big(\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})^{T}\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})-\widehat{A}^{T}\widehat{A}\big)|}

\displaystyle=\sigma_{\textup{min}}\big(\widehat{A}\big)+\sqrt{|\lambda_{\textup{max}}\big(\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})^{T}\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})-\widehat{A}^{T}\widehat{A}\big)|}.

(37)

The next step is to find an upper bound on
\sqrt{|\lambda_{\textup{max}}\big(\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})^{T}\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})-\widehat{A}^{T}\widehat{A}\big)|}
. Towards that end, observe that:

\displaystyle\big|\lambda_{\textup{max}}\big(\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})^{T}\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})-\widehat{A}^{T}\widehat{A}\big)\big|

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{i=1}^{m}\sum_{j=1}^{m}\Big|\big[\widehat{A}^{T}\widehat{A}-\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})^{T}\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})\big]_{i,j}\Big|

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=1}^{m}\sum_{j=1}^{m}\Big|\big[\widehat{A}^{T}\widehat{A}-\widehat{A}^{T}\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})+\widehat{A}^{T}\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})-\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})^{T}\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})\big]_{i,j}\Big|

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{i=1}^{m}\sum_{j=1}^{m}\bigg|\Big[\widehat{A}^{T}\big(\widehat{A}-\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})\big)\Big]_{i,j}\bigg|+\sum_{i=1}^{m}\sum_{j=1}^{m}\bigg|\Big[\big(\widehat{A}^{T}-\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})^{T}\big)\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})\Big]_{i,j}\bigg|

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=1}^{m}\sum_{j=1}^{m}\bigg|\Big[\widehat{A}^{T}\big(\widehat{A}-\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})\big)\Big]_{i,j}\bigg|+\sum_{i=1}^{m}\sum_{j=1}^{m}\bigg|\Big[\big(\widehat{A}-\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})\big)^{T}\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})\Big]_{i,j}\bigg|.

(38)

Note that:

\displaystyle\bigg|\Big[\widehat{A}^{T}\big(\widehat{A}-\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})\big)\Big]_{i,j}\bigg|

\displaystyle=\bigg|\Big\langle\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{i}^{\textup{\text{tr,I}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma}}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,I}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma}}],\text{col }j\text{ of }\widehat{A}-\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})\Big\rangle\bigg|

\displaystyle\leq\Big|\Big|\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{i}^{\textup{\text{tr,I}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma}}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,I}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma}}]\Big|\Big|_{2}\Big|\Big|\text{col }j\text{ of }\widehat{A}-\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})\Big|\Big|_{2},

where we know, by Lemma (
6.12
), that

\Big|\Big|\textup{col }j\text{ of }\widehat{A}-\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})\Big|\Big|_{2}=O_{\mathbb{P}}\Bigg(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}}+\sqrt{\sum_{k=1}^{m}\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{II}}}}\Big[\textup{\text{Var}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},k}-s_{\gamma^{\ast},k}]\Big]}\Bigg).

As for
\Big|\Big|\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{i}^{\textup{\text{tr,I}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma}}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,I}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma}}]\Big|\Big|_{2}
, observe that:

\displaystyle\Big|\Big|\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{i}^{\textup{\text{tr,I}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma}}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,I}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma}}]\Big|\Big|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\Big|\Big|\mathbb{E}_{i}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]-\mathbb{E}_{0}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]\Big|\Big|_{2}+\Big|\Big|\mathbb{E}_{i}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma}}-s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]-\mathbb{E}_{0}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma}}-s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]\Big|\Big|_{2}

\displaystyle\qquad+\Big|\Big|\big(\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{i}^{\textup{\text{tr,I}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma}}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,I}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma}}]\big)-\big(\mathbb{E}_{i}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma}}]-\mathbb{E}_{0}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma}}]\big)\Big|\Big|_{2}

\displaystyle=\sqrt{\sum_{z=1}^{m}\big(\mathbb{E}_{i}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},z}]-\mathbb{E}_{0}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},z}]\big)^{2}}+\sqrt{\sum_{z=1}^{m}\big(\mathbb{E}_{i}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},z}-s_{\gamma^{\ast},z}]-\mathbb{E}_{0}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},z}-s_{\gamma^{\ast},z}]\big)^{2}}

\displaystyle\qquad+\Big|\Big|\big(\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{i}^{\textup{\text{tr,I}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma}}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,I}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma}}]\big)-\big(\mathbb{E}_{i}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma}}]-\mathbb{E}_{0}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma}}]\big)\Big|\Big|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{\sum_{z=1}^{m}\big(\mathbb{E}_{i}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},z}]-\mathbb{E}_{0}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},z}]\big)^{2}}+\sqrt{\textup{\text{Var}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},i}]\sum_{z=1}^{m}\textup{\text{Var}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},z}-s_{\gamma^{\ast},z}]}

\displaystyle\qquad+\Big|\Big|\big(\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{i}^{\textup{\text{tr,I}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma}}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,I}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma}}]\big)-\big(\mathbb{E}_{i}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma}}]-\mathbb{E}_{0}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma}}]\big)\Big|\Big|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{m\frac{16}{L^{2}}}+\sqrt{\frac{1}{L^{2}}\sum_{z=1}^{m}\textup{\text{Var}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},z}-s_{\gamma^{\ast},z}]}

\displaystyle\qquad+\Big|\Big|\big(\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{i}^{\textup{\text{tr,I}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma}}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,I}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma}}]\big)-\big(\mathbb{E}_{i}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma}}]-\mathbb{E}_{0}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma}}]\big)\Big|\Big|_{2}

\displaystyle=O_{\mathbb{P}}(1),

where the last line is a consequence of (
36
) from Lemma (
6.12
). Ergo, it follows that

\bigg|\Big[\widehat{A}^{T}\big(\widehat{A}-\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})\big)\Big]_{i,j}\bigg|=O_{\mathbb{P}}\Bigg(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}}+\sqrt{\sum_{k=1}^{m}\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{II}}}}\Big[\textup{\text{Var}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},k}-s_{\gamma^{\ast},k}]\Big]}\Bigg).

Next, let’s analyze the behavior of
\bigg|\Big[\big(\widehat{A}-\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})\big)^{T}\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})\Big]_{i,j}\bigg|
. Observe that:

\displaystyle\bigg|\Big[\big(\widehat{A}-\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})\big)^{T}\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})\Big]_{i,j}\bigg|

\displaystyle=\Big\langle\text{col }i\text{ of }\widehat{A}-\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast}),\mathbb{E}_{j}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]-\mathbb{E}_{0}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]\Big\rangle

\displaystyle\leq\Big|\Big|\text{col }i\text{ of }\widehat{A}-\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})\Big|\Big|_{2}\Big|\Big|\mathbb{E}_{j}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]-\mathbb{E}_{0}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]\Big|\Big|_{2}.

Note that:

\displaystyle\Big|\Big|\mathbb{E}_{j}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]-\mathbb{E}_{0}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]\Big|\Big|_{2}

\displaystyle=\sqrt{\sum_{k=1}^{m}\Big(\mathbb{E}_{j}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},k}]-\mathbb{E}_{0}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},k}]\Big)^{2}}

\displaystyle=\sqrt{\sum_{k=1}^{m}\Big(\textup{\text{Cov}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},j},s_{\gamma^{\ast},k}]\Big)^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{\textup{\text{Var}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},j}]\sum_{k=1}^{m}\textup{\text{Var}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},k}]}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\sqrt{m}}{L^{2}}.

Ergo:

\displaystyle\bigg|\Big[\big(\widehat{A}-\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})\big)^{T}\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})\Big]_{i,j}\bigg|

\displaystyle\leq\Big|\Big|\text{col }i\text{ of }\widehat{A}-\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})\Big|\Big|_{2}\frac{\sqrt{m}}{L^{2}}

\displaystyle=O_{\mathbb{P}}\Bigg(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}}+\sqrt{\sum_{k=1}^{m}\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{II}}}}\Big[\textup{\text{Var}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},k}-s_{\gamma^{\ast},k}]\Big]}\Bigg).

Therefore, combining our analysis of
\bigg|\Big[\widehat{A}^{T}\big(\widehat{A}-\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})\big)\Big]_{i,j}\bigg|
and
\bigg|\Big[\big(\widehat{A}-\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})\big)^{T}\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})\Big]_{i,j}\bigg|
with the inequality in display (
38
), it follows that:

\implies\big|\lambda_{\textup{max}}\big(\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})^{T}\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})-\widehat{A}^{T}\widehat{A}\big)\big|=O_{\mathbb{P}}\Bigg(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}}+\sqrt{\sum_{k=1}^{m}\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{II}}}}\Big[\textup{\text{Var}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},k}-s_{\gamma^{\ast},k}]\Big]}\Bigg).

Now, since we already showed that
\sqrt{\Lambda}\leq\sigma_{\textup{min}}\big(\widehat{A}\big)+\sqrt{|\lambda_{\textup{max}}\big(\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})^{T}\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})-\widehat{A}^{T}\widehat{A}\big)|}
in display (
37
), it follows that:

\implies\sqrt{\Lambda}+O_{\mathbb{P}}\Bigg(\frac{1}{(n^{\textup{\text{tr}}})^{\tfrac{1}{4}}}+\bigg(\sum_{k=1}^{m}\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{II}}}}\Big[\textup{\text{Var}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},k}-s_{\gamma^{\ast},k}]\Big]\bigg)^{\tfrac{1}{4}}\Bigg)\leq\sigma_{\textup{min}}\big(\widehat{A}\big)

\implies\frac{\mathbbold{1}{\big\{\sigma_{\textup{min}}\big(\widehat{A}\big)>0\big\}}}{\sigma_{\textup{min}}\big(\widehat{A}\big)}\leq\frac{1}{\sqrt{\Lambda}}+o_{\mathbb{P}}(1)

\displaystyle\implies\sigma_{\textup{max}}\big(\widehat{A}^{-1}-\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})^{-1}\big)\mathbbold{1}{\big\{\sigma_{\textup{min}}\big(\widehat{A}\big)>0\big\}}

\displaystyle\leq\bigg(\frac{1}{\sqrt{\Lambda}}+o_{\mathbb{P}}(1)\bigg)\frac{\sum_{j=1}^{m}\big|\big|\text{col }j\text{ of }\widehat{A}-\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})\big|\big|_{2}}{\sqrt{\Lambda}}

\displaystyle=O_{\mathbb{P}}\Bigg(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}}+\sqrt{\sum_{k=1}^{m}\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{II}}}}\Big[\textup{\text{Var}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},k}-s_{\gamma^{\ast},k}]\Big]}\Bigg),

where the last line follows from Lemma (
6.12
). Next, we need to prove that
\mathbbold{1}{\big\{\sigma_{\textup{min}}\big(\widehat{A}\big)=0\big\}}
goes down exponentially fast. It follows from display (
37
) that
\sqrt{\Lambda}-\sqrt{|\lambda_{\textup{max}}\big(\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})^{T}\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})-\widehat{A}^{T}\widehat{A}\big)|}\leq\sigma_{\textup{min}}\big(\widehat{A}\big)
, so

\mathbbold{1}{\big\{\sigma_{\textup{min}}\big(\widehat{A}\big)>0\big\}}\geq\mathbbold{1}{\big\{\sqrt{\Lambda}-\sqrt{|\lambda_{\textup{max}}\big(\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})^{T}\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})-\widehat{A}^{T}\widehat{A}\big)|}>0\big\}},

which implies that:

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}}\big[\sigma_{\textup{min}}\big(\widehat{A}\big)=0\big]

\displaystyle=1-\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}}\big[\sigma_{\textup{min}}\big(\widehat{A}\big)>0\big]

\displaystyle\leq 1-\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}}\Big[\sqrt{\Lambda}-\sqrt{|\lambda_{\textup{max}}\big(\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})^{T}\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})-\widehat{A}^{T}\widehat{A}\big)|}>0\Big]

\displaystyle=1-\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}}\Big[\Lambda>|\lambda_{\textup{max}}\big(\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})^{T}\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})-\widehat{A}^{T}\widehat{A}\big)|\Big]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}}\Big[\Lambda\leq|\lambda_{\textup{max}}\big(\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})^{T}\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})-\widehat{A}^{T}\widehat{A}\big)|\Big].

Now, recall from earlier in the present proof that

|\lambda_{\textup{max}}\big(\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})^{T}\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})-\widehat{A}^{T}\widehat{A}\big)|\leq\sum_{i=1}^{m}\sum_{j=1}^{m}\bigg|\Big[\widehat{A}^{T}\big(\widehat{A}-\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})\big)\Big]_{i,j}\bigg|+\sum_{i=1}^{m}\sum_{j=1}^{m}\bigg|\Big[\big(\widehat{A}-\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})\big)^{T}\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})\Big]_{i,j}\bigg|,

and that we also showed that

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{m}\sum_{j=1}^{m}\bigg|\Big[\widehat{A}^{T}\big(\widehat{A}-\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})\big)\Big]_{i,j}\bigg|

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{i=1}^{m}\Big|\Big|\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{i}^{\textup{\text{tr,I}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma}}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,I}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma}}]\Big|\Big|_{2}\sum_{j=1}^{m}\Big|\Big|\text{col }j\text{ of }\widehat{A}-\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})\Big|\Big|_{2},

as well as that

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{m}\sum_{j=1}^{m}\bigg|\Big[\big(\widehat{A}-\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})\big)^{T}\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})\Big]_{i,j}\bigg|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\sqrt{m}}{L^{2}}\sum_{i=1}^{m}\sum_{j=1}^{m}\Big|\Big|\text{col }i\text{ of }\widehat{A}-\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})\Big|\Big|_{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{m^{3/2}}{L^{2}}\sum_{i=1}^{m}\Big|\Big|\text{col }i\text{ of }\widehat{A}-\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})\Big|\Big|_{2}.

So, from this earlier work, it follows that

\displaystyle|\lambda_{\textup{max}}\big(\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})^{T}\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})-\widehat{A}^{T}\widehat{A}\big)|

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{i=1}^{m}\Big|\Big|\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{i}^{\textup{\text{tr,I}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma}}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,I}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma}}]\Big|\Big|_{2}\sum_{j=1}^{m}\Big|\Big|\text{col }j\text{ of }\widehat{A}-\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})\Big|\Big|_{2}+\frac{m^{3/2}}{L^{2}}\sum_{i=1}^{m}\Big|\Big|\text{col }i\text{ of }\widehat{A}-\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})\Big|\Big|_{2}

\displaystyle=\Bigg(\sum_{i=1}^{m}\Big|\Big|\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{i}^{\textup{\text{tr,I}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma}}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,I}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma}}]\Big|\Big|_{2}+\frac{m^{3/2}}{L^{2}}\Bigg)\sum_{i=1}^{m}\Big|\Big|\text{col }i\text{ of }\widehat{A}-\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})\Big|\Big|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\Bigg(\sum_{i=1}^{m}\Big|\Big|\text{col }i\text{ of }\widehat{A}-\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})\Big|\Big|_{2}+\sum_{i=1}^{m}\Big|\Big|\text{col }i\text{ of }\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})\Big|\Big|_{2}+\frac{m^{3/2}}{L^{2}}\Bigg)\sum_{i=1}^{m}\Big|\Big|\text{col }i\text{ of }\widehat{A}-\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})\Big|\Big|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\Bigg(\sum_{i=1}^{m}\Big|\Big|\text{col }i\text{ of }\widehat{A}-\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})\Big|\Big|_{2}+2\frac{m^{3/2}}{L^{2}}\Bigg)\sum_{i=1}^{m}\Big|\Big|\text{col }i\text{ of }\widehat{A}-\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})\Big|\Big|_{2}

\displaystyle=\bigg(\sum_{i=1}^{m}\Big|\Big|\text{col }i\text{ of }\widehat{A}-\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})\Big|\Big|_{2}\bigg)^{2}+2\frac{m^{3/2}}{L^{2}}\sum_{i=1}^{m}\Big|\Big|\text{col }i\text{ of }\widehat{A}-\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})\Big|\Big|_{2},

where the second inequality above uses the fact that
\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{i}^{\textup{\text{tr,I}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma}}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,I}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma}}]
is the
{i}^{\text{th}}
column of
\widehat{A}
. Ergo:

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\big[\sigma_{\textup{min}}\big(\widehat{A}\big)=0\big]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\Big[|\lambda_{\textup{max}}\big(\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})^{T}\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})-\widehat{A}^{T}\widehat{A}\big)|\geq\Lambda\Big]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\Bigg[\bigg(\sum_{i=1}^{m}\Big|\Big|\text{col }i\text{ of }\widehat{A}-\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})\Big|\Big|_{2}\bigg)^{2}\geq\frac{1}{2}\Lambda\Bigg]+\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\Bigg[2\frac{m^{3/2}}{L^{2}}\sum_{i=1}^{m}\Big|\Big|\text{col }i\text{ of }\widehat{A}-\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})\Big|\Big|_{2}\geq\frac{1}{2}\Lambda\Bigg]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\Bigg[\sum_{i=1}^{m}\Big|\Big|\text{col }i\text{ of }\widehat{A}-\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})\Big|\Big|_{2}\geq\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\sqrt{\Lambda}\Bigg]+\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\Bigg[\sum_{i=1}^{m}\Big|\Big|\text{col }i\text{ of }\widehat{A}-\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})\Big|\Big|_{2}\geq\frac{1}{4}\frac{L^{2}}{m^{3/2}}\Lambda\Bigg]

\displaystyle\leq 2\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\Bigg[\sum_{i=1}^{m}\Big|\Big|\text{col }i\text{ of }\widehat{A}-\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})\Big|\Big|_{2}\geq\min\bigg\{\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\sqrt{\Lambda},\text{ }\frac{1}{4}\frac{L^{2}}{m^{3/2}}\Lambda\bigg\}\Bigg]

\displaystyle\leq 2\sum_{i=1}^{m}\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\Bigg[\Big|\Big|\text{col }i\text{ of }\widehat{A}-\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})\Big|\Big|_{2}\geq\frac{1}{m}\min\bigg\{\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\sqrt{\Lambda},\text{ }\frac{1}{4}\frac{L^{2}}{m^{3/2}}\Lambda\bigg\}\Bigg]

\displaystyle=2\sum_{i=1}^{m}\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\Bigg[\Big|\Big|\text{col }i\text{ of }\widehat{A}-\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})\Big|\Big|_{2}^{2}\geq\frac{1}{m^{2}}\min\bigg\{\frac{1}{2}\Lambda,\text{ }\frac{1}{16}\frac{L^{4}}{m^{3}}\Lambda^{2}\bigg\}\Bigg].

Since
\mathbbold{1}{\big\{\sigma_{\textup{min}}\big(\widehat{A}\big)=0\big\}}=O_{\mathbb{P}}\Big(\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\big[\sigma_{\textup{min}}\big(\widehat{A}\big)=0\big]\Big)
, the claim in the Lemma holds.

∎

Proof of Lemma (
6.14
)
.

Observe that:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}}\bigg[\Big|\Big|\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\pi^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{te,I}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma}}-s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\pi^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,I}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma}}-s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]\Big|\Big|_{2}^{2}\bigg]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{II}}}}\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{I}}}\mid\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{II}}}}\bigg[\Big|\Big|\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\pi^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{te,I}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma}}-s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\pi^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,I}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma}}-s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]\Big|\Big|_{2}^{2}\bigg]

\displaystyle=\sum_{j=1}^{m}\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{II}}}}\bigg\{\textup{\text{Var}}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{I}}}\mid\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{II}}}}\Big[\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\pi^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{te,I}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},j}-s_{\gamma^{\ast},j}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\pi^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,I}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},j}-s_{\gamma^{\ast},j}]\Big]\bigg\}.

Let’s focus on controlling the conditional variance term, for arbitrary
j\in[m]
. Define the random
1\times m

row
vector

\delta_{j}:=\Big(\mathbb{E}_{1}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},j}-s_{\gamma^{\ast},j}]-\mathbb{E}_{0}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},j}-s_{\gamma^{\ast},j}],\dots,\mathbb{E}_{m}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},j}-s_{\gamma^{\ast},j}]-\mathbb{E}_{0}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},j}-s_{\gamma^{\ast},j}]\Big).

By Corollary (
2.1
), we have that:

\frac{1}{2}\textup{\text{Var}}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{I}}}\mid\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{II}}}}\Big[\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\pi^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{te,I}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},j}-s_{\gamma^{\ast},j}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\pi^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,I}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},j}-s_{\gamma^{\ast},j}]\Big]

=\Bigg[\frac{1}{n^{\textup{\text{te}}}}+\frac{1}{n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\sum_{y=0}^{m}\frac{(\pi_{y}^{\ast})^{2}}{\pi_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\Bigg]\Bigg(\textup{\text{Var}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},j}-s_{\gamma^{\ast},j}]-\delta_{j}\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast};\text{Cat})^{-1}\delta_{j}^{T}\Bigg)+\frac{1}{n^{\textup{\text{te}}}}\delta_{j}\mathcal{I}(\pi^{\ast};\textup{Cat})^{-1}\delta_{j}^{T}

\leq\Bigg[\frac{1}{n^{\textup{\text{te}}}}+\frac{1}{n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\sum_{y=0}^{m}\frac{(\pi_{y}^{\ast})^{2}}{\pi_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\Bigg]\textup{\text{Var}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},j}-s_{\gamma^{\ast},j}]+\frac{1}{n^{\textup{\text{te}}}}\delta_{j}\mathcal{I}(\pi^{\ast};\textup{Cat})^{-1}\delta_{j}^{T}

\leq\Bigg[\frac{1}{n^{\textup{\text{te}}}}+\frac{1}{n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\sum_{y=0}^{m}\frac{(\pi_{y}^{\ast})^{2}}{\pi_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\Bigg]\textup{\text{Var}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},j}-s_{\gamma^{\ast},j}]+\frac{1}{n^{\textup{\text{te}}}}\lambda_{\textup{max}}\big(\mathcal{I}(\pi^{\ast};\textup{Cat})^{-1}\big)\big|\big|\delta_{j}\big|\big|_{2}^{2}

\leq\Bigg[\frac{1}{n^{\textup{\text{te}}}}+\frac{1}{n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\sum_{y=0}^{m}\frac{(\pi_{y}^{\ast})^{2}}{\pi_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\Bigg]\textup{\text{Var}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},j}-s_{\gamma^{\ast},j}]+\frac{1}{2n^{\textup{\text{te}}}}\big|\big|\delta_{j}\big|\big|_{2}^{2},

where the first and third inequalities are consequences of Lemma (
6.3
) and the fact that the components of
\widehat{\gamma}
and
\pi^{\ast}
are globally bounded away from
0
and
1
. Next, bound the
\big|\big|\delta_{j}\big|\big|_{2}^{2}
term:

\displaystyle\big|\big|\delta_{j}\big|\big|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=\sum_{k=1}^{m}\big(\mathbb{E}_{k}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},j}-s_{\gamma^{\ast},j}]-\mathbb{E}_{0}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},j}-s_{\gamma^{\ast},j}]\big)^{2}

\displaystyle=\sum_{k=1}^{m}\big(\textup{\text{Cov}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},k},\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},j}-s_{\gamma^{\ast},j}]\big)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\textup{\text{Var}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},j}-s_{\gamma^{\ast},j}]\sum_{k=1}^{m}\textup{\text{Var}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},k}].

Ergo, it follows that:

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\textup{\text{Var}}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{I}}}\mid\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{II}}}}\Big[\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\pi^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{te,I}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},j}-s_{\gamma^{\ast},j}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\pi^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,I}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},j}-s_{\gamma^{\ast},j}]\Big]

\displaystyle\leq\Bigg(\Bigg[\frac{1}{n^{\textup{\text{te}}}}+\frac{1}{n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\sum_{y=0}^{m}\frac{(\pi_{y}^{\ast})^{2}}{\pi_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\Bigg]+\frac{1}{2n^{\textup{\text{te}}}}\sum_{k=1}^{m}\textup{\text{Var}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},k}]\Bigg)\textup{\text{Var}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},j}-s_{\gamma^{\ast},j}]

Thus:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}}\bigg[\Big|\Big|\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\pi^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{te,I}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma}}-s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\pi^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,I}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma}}-s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]\Big|\Big|_{2}^{2}\bigg]

\displaystyle=\sum_{j=1}^{m}\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{II}}}}\bigg\{\textup{\text{Var}}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{I}}}\mid\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{II}}}}\Big[\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\pi^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{te,I}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},j}-s_{\gamma^{\ast},j}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\pi^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,I}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},j}-s_{\gamma^{\ast},j}]\Big]\bigg\}

\displaystyle\leq 2\sum_{j=1}^{m}\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{II}}}}\Bigg\{\Bigg(\Bigg[\frac{1}{n^{\textup{\text{te}}}}+\frac{1}{n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\sum_{y=0}^{m}\frac{(\pi_{y}^{\ast})^{2}}{\pi_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\Bigg]+\frac{1}{2n^{\textup{\text{te}}}}\sum_{k=1}^{m}\textup{\text{Var}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},k}]\Bigg)\textup{\text{Var}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},j}-s_{\gamma^{\ast},j}]\Bigg\}

\displaystyle=2\Bigg(\Bigg[\frac{1}{n^{\textup{\text{te}}}}+\frac{1}{n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\sum_{y=0}^{m}\frac{(\pi_{y}^{\ast})^{2}}{\pi_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\Bigg]+\frac{1}{2n^{\textup{\text{te}}}}\sum_{k=1}^{m}\textup{\text{Var}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},k}]\Bigg)\sum_{j=1}^{m}\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{II}}}}\Big[\textup{\text{Var}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},j}-s_{\gamma^{\ast},j}]\Big]

\displaystyle\leq 2\Bigg(\Bigg[\frac{1}{n^{\textup{\text{te}}}}+\frac{1}{n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}m\frac{(1-\xi)^{2}}{\xi}\Bigg]+\frac{1}{2n^{\textup{\text{te}}}}\frac{m}{L^{2}}\Bigg)\sum_{j=1}^{m}\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{II}}}}\Big[\textup{\text{Var}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},j}-s_{\gamma^{\ast},j}]\Big]

\displaystyle=O\Bigg(\bigg(\frac{1}{n^{\textup{\text{te}}}}+\frac{1}{n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\bigg)\sum_{j=1}^{m}\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{II}}}}\Big[\textup{\text{Var}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},j}-s_{\gamma^{\ast},j}]\Big]\Bigg),

as desired!

∎

Proof of Lemma (
3.3
)
.

Define the following:

•

\kappa:=\textup{\text{Var}}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,I}}}}\Big[\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})^{-1}\big(\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\pi^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{te,I}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\pi^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,I}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]\big)\Big]

•

\phi(x):=\kappa^{-\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})^{-1}s_{\gamma^{\ast}}(x)
.

Our goal is to show that:

\displaystyle\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\pi^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{te,I}}}[\phi]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\pi^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,I}}}[\phi]

\displaystyle\equiv\bigg(\textup{\text{Var}}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,I}}}}\Big[\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})^{-1}\big(\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\pi^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{te,I}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\pi^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,I}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]\big)\Big]\bigg)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})^{-1}\big(\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\pi^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{te,I}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\pi^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,I}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]\big)\xrightarrow{\hskip 4.09723ptd\hskip 4.09723pt}\mathcal{N}(0,I_{m}).

Towards that end, rewrite
\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\pi^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{te,I}}}[\phi]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\pi^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,I}}}[\phi]
as follows:

\displaystyle\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\pi^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{te,I}}}[\phi]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\pi^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,I}}}[\phi]

\displaystyle=\big(\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\pi^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{te,I}}}[\phi]-\mathbb{E}_{\pi^{\ast}}[\phi]\big)+\big(\mathbb{E}_{\pi^{\ast}}[\phi]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\pi^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,I}}}[\phi]\big)

\displaystyle=\big(\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\pi^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{te,I}}}[\phi]-\mathbb{E}_{\pi^{\ast}}[\phi]\big)+\sum_{y=0}^{m}\pi_{y}^{\ast}\big(\mathbb{E}_{y}[\phi]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr,I}}}[\phi]\big)

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=1}^{\tfrac{1}{2}n^{\textup{\text{te}}}}\frac{1}{\tfrac{1}{2}n^{\textup{\text{te}}}}\big(\phi(X_{i}^{\textup{\text{te}}})-\mathbb{E}_{\pi^{\ast}}[\phi]\big)+\sum_{y=0}^{m}\sum_{i=1}^{\tfrac{1}{2}\pi_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\frac{\pi_{y}^{\ast}}{\tfrac{1}{2}\pi_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\big(\mathbb{E}_{y}[\phi]-\phi(X_{i,y}^{\textup{\text{tr}}})\big)

(39)

For convenience, let
r_{w}:=\frac{1}{2}n^{\textup{\text{te}}}+\frac{1}{2}n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}
. Construct the random variables
\{Q_{w,j}\mid 1\leq j\leq r_{w}\}
, as follows:

•

If
1\leq j\leq\tfrac{1}{2}n^{\textup{\text{te}}}
, then
Q_{w,j}:=\frac{1}{\tfrac{1}{2}n^{\textup{\text{te}}}}\big(\phi(X_{j}^{\textup{\text{te}}})-\mathbb{E}_{\pi^{\ast}}[\phi]\big)
.

•

If
\tfrac{1}{2}n^{\textup{\text{te}}}+1\leq j\leq\tfrac{1}{2}n^{\textup{\text{te}}}+\tfrac{1}{2}\pi_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}
, then
Q_{w,j}:=\frac{\pi_{0}^{\ast}}{\tfrac{1}{2}\pi_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\big(\mathbb{E}_{0}[\phi]-\phi(X_{j-n^{\textup{\text{te}}}/2,0}^{\textup{\text{tr}}})\big)
.

•

….etc.

Then, by display (
39
), it follows that
\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\pi^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{te,I}}}[\phi]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\pi^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,I}}}[\phi]=\sum\limits_{j=1}^{r_{w}}Q_{w,j}
, so our goal is to now show that:

\sum_{j=1}^{r_{w}}Q_{w,j}\xrightarrow{\hskip 4.09723ptd\hskip 4.09723pt}\mathcal{N}(0,I_{m}).

Note that
\mathbb{E}[Q_{w,j}]=0
and
\sum_{j=1}^{r_{w}}\textup{\text{Var}}[Q_{w,j}]=\textup{\text{Var}}\Big[\sum_{j=1}^{r_{w}}Q_{w,j}\Big]=I_{m}
, so by Theorem 6.9.2 of
[
Hansen, 2018
]
, to show the above weak convergence result, it suffices to prove that

\lim_{w\to\infty}\sum_{j=1}^{r_{w}}\mathbb{E}\Big[||Q_{w,j}||_{2}^{2}\mathbbold{1}{\big\{||Q_{w,j}||_{2}^{2}\geq\epsilon\big\}}\Big]=0,

(40)

for every
\epsilon\in(0,\infty)
. But how do we show this? Well, start by examining
Q_{w,j}
. First, suppose that
1\leq j\leq\tfrac{1}{2}n^{\textup{\text{te}}}
. Then:

\displaystyle Q_{w,j}
