\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\tfrac{1}{2}n^{\textup{\text{te}}}}\big(\phi(X_{j}^{\textup{\text{te}}})-\mathbb{E}_{\pi^{\ast}}[\phi]\big)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\tfrac{1}{2}n^{\textup{\text{te}}}}\kappa^{-\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})^{-1}\big(s_{\gamma^{\ast}}(X_{j}^{\textup{\text{te}}})-\mathbb{E}_{\pi^{\ast}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]\big).

\displaystyle||Q_{w,j}||_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{(\tfrac{1}{2}n^{\textup{\text{te}}})^{2}}\big(s_{\gamma^{\ast}}(X_{j}^{\textup{\text{te}}})-\mathbb{E}_{\pi^{\ast}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]\big)^{T}\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})^{-1}\kappa^{-\frac{1}{2}}\kappa^{-\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})^{-1}\big(s_{\gamma^{\ast}}(X_{j}^{\textup{\text{te}}})-\mathbb{E}_{\pi^{\ast}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]\big)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{(\tfrac{1}{2}n^{\textup{\text{te}}})^{2}}\big(s_{\gamma^{\ast}}(X_{j}^{\textup{\text{te}}})-\mathbb{E}_{\pi^{\ast}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]\big)^{T}\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})^{-1}\kappa^{-1}\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})^{-1}\big(s_{\gamma^{\ast}}(X_{j}^{\textup{\text{te}}})-\mathbb{E}_{\pi^{\ast}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]\big)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{(\tfrac{1}{2}n^{\textup{\text{te}}})^{2}}\big(s_{\gamma^{\ast}}(X_{j}^{\textup{\text{te}}})-\mathbb{E}_{\pi^{\ast}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]\big)^{T}\Big(\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})\kappa\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})\Big)^{-1}\big(s_{\gamma^{\ast}}(X_{j}^{\textup{\text{te}}})-\mathbb{E}_{\pi^{\ast}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]\big)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{(\tfrac{1}{2}n^{\textup{\text{te}}})^{2}}\big(s_{\gamma^{\ast}}(X_{j}^{\textup{\text{te}}})-\mathbb{E}_{\pi^{\ast}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]\big)^{T}\bigg(\textup{\text{Var}}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,I}}}}\Big[\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\pi^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{te,I}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\pi^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,I}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]\Big]\bigg)^{-1}\big(s_{\gamma^{\ast}}(X_{j}^{\textup{\text{te}}})-\mathbb{E}_{\pi^{\ast}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]\big).

Now, note that:

\displaystyle\textup{\text{Var}}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,I}}}}\Big[\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\pi^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{te,I}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\pi^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,I}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]\Big]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\tfrac{1}{2}n^{\textup{\text{te}}}}\textup{\text{Var}}_{\pi^{\ast}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]+\frac{1}{\tfrac{1}{2}n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\sum_{y=0}^{m}\frac{(\pi_{y}^{\ast})^{2}}{\pi_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\textup{\text{Var}}_{y}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]

\displaystyle\succeq\frac{1}{\tfrac{1}{2}n^{\textup{\text{te}}}}\textup{\text{Var}}_{\pi^{\ast}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}}].

By Lemma (
6.5
), we know that
\textup{\text{Var}}_{\pi^{\ast}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]\succ 0
, so its inverse exists. Thus, we have that:

\implies\bigg(\textup{\text{Var}}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,I}}}}\Big[\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\pi^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{te,I}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\pi^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,I}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]\Big]\bigg)^{-1}\preceq\tfrac{1}{2}n^{\textup{\text{te}}}\big(\textup{\text{Var}}_{\pi^{\ast}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]\big)^{-1}.

\displaystyle\implies||Q_{w,j}||_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\tfrac{1}{2}n^{\textup{\text{te}}}}{(\tfrac{1}{2}n^{\textup{\text{te}}})^{2}}\big(s_{\gamma^{\ast}}(X_{j}^{\textup{\text{te}}})-\mathbb{E}_{\pi^{\ast}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]\big)^{T}\big(\textup{\text{Var}}_{\pi^{\ast}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]\big)^{-1}\big(s_{\gamma^{\ast}}(X_{j}^{\textup{\text{te}}})-\mathbb{E}_{\pi^{\ast}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]\big)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\tfrac{1}{2}n^{\textup{\text{te}}}}\big(s_{\gamma^{\ast}}(X_{j}^{\textup{\text{te}}})-\mathbb{E}_{\pi^{\ast}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]\big)^{T}\big(\textup{\text{Var}}_{\pi^{\ast}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]\big)^{-1}\big(s_{\gamma^{\ast}}(X_{j}^{\textup{\text{te}}})-\mathbb{E}_{\pi^{\ast}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]\big)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\tfrac{1}{2}n^{\textup{\text{te}}}}||s_{\gamma^{\ast}}(X_{j}^{\textup{\text{te}}})-\mathbb{E}_{\pi^{\ast}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]||_{2}^{2}\lambda_{\textup{max}}\big(\textup{\text{Var}}_{\pi^{\ast}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]^{-1}\big)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\tfrac{1}{2}n^{\textup{\text{te}}}}||s_{\gamma^{\ast}}(X_{j}^{\textup{\text{te}}})-\mathbb{E}_{\pi^{\ast}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]||_{2}^{2}/\lambda_{\textup{min}}\big(\textup{\text{Var}}_{\pi^{\ast}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]\big)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\tfrac{1}{2}n^{\textup{\text{te}}}}||s_{\gamma^{\ast}}(X_{j}^{\textup{\text{te}}})-\mathbb{E}_{\pi^{\ast}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]||_{2}^{2}\cdot\frac{m+1}{\Lambda\xi}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\tfrac{1}{2}n^{\textup{\text{te}}}}\cdot\frac{16m(m+1)}{L^{2}\Lambda\xi},

where the second to last inequality uses Lemma (
6.5
). Next, let’s handle the case when
j\in[\tfrac{1}{2}n^{\textup{\text{te}}}+1,\tfrac{1}{2}n^{\textup{\text{te}}}+\tfrac{1}{2}\pi_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}]
. Observe that:

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\lambda_{\textup{min}}\Bigg\{\textup{\text{Var}}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,I}}}}\Big[\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\pi^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{te,I}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\pi^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,I}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]\Big]\Bigg\}

\displaystyle\geq\Bigg[\frac{1}{n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\sum_{y=0}^{m}\frac{(\pi_{y}^{\ast})^{2}}{\pi_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\Bigg]\lambda_{\textup{min}}\big(\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})-\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast};\textup{Cat})^{-1}\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})\big)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{m\xi^{2}}{n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\lambda_{\textup{min}}\big(\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})[\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})^{-1}-\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast};\textup{Cat})^{-1}]\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})\big),

where the first line is due to Lemmas (
6.3
) and (
2.1
), and the second line is due to Assumption (
A5
). Now, note that due to Lemma (
3.5
), the difference
\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})^{-1}-\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast};\textup{Cat})^{-1}
is positive semi-definite. Since the matrix is also symmetric, it follows that the eigenvalues and singular values coincide; thus, by Lemma (
6.7
), we know that all the eigenvalues are strictly positive. Hence,
\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})^{-1}-\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast};\textup{Cat})^{-1}
is symmetric positive definite. Since
\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})
is symmetric positive definite by Assumption (
A6
), it follows that the product
\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})[\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})^{-1}-\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast};\textup{Cat})^{-1}]\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})
is symmetric positive definite as well. Thus, the eigenvalues and singular values coincide, meaning that:

\displaystyle\lambda_{\textup{min}}\big(\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})[\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})^{-1}-\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast};\textup{Cat})^{-1}]\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})\big)

\displaystyle=\sigma_{\textup{min}}\big(\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})[\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})^{-1}-\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast};\textup{Cat})^{-1}]\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})\big)

\displaystyle\geq\sigma_{\textup{min}}\big(\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})\big)\sigma_{\textup{min}}\big(\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})^{-1}-\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast};\textup{Cat})^{-1}\big)\sigma_{\textup{min}}\big(\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})\big)

\displaystyle\geq\Lambda\nu\bigg(\frac{L}{m+1}\bigg)^{2},

where the last inequality is due to Assumption (
A6
) and Lemma (
6.7
). Ergo, it follows that:

\implies\lambda_{\textup{min}}\Bigg\{\textup{\text{Var}}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,I}}}}\Big[\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\pi^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{te,I}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\pi^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,I}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]\Big]\Bigg\}\geq 2\frac{m\xi^{2}}{n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\Lambda\nu\bigg(\frac{L}{m+1}\bigg)^{2}.

Thus,
\textup{\text{Var}}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,I}}}}\Big[\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\pi^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{te,I}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\pi^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,I}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]\Big]
is positive definite. Now, using our existing work in this proof, we have that:

\displaystyle||Q_{w,j}||_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{(\pi_{0}^{\ast})^{2}}{\big(\tfrac{1}{2}\pi_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}\big)^{2}}\big(s_{\gamma^{\ast}}(X_{j-n^{\textup{\text{te}}}/2,0}^{\textup{\text{tr}}})-\mathbb{E}_{0}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]\big)^{T}\Bigg(\textup{\text{Var}}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,I}}}}\Big[\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\pi^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{te,I}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\pi^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,I}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]\Big]\Bigg)^{-1}\big(s_{\gamma^{\ast}}(X_{j-n^{\textup{\text{te}}}/2,0}^{\textup{\text{tr}}})-\mathbb{E}_{0}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]\big)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{(\pi_{0}^{\ast})^{2}}{\big(\tfrac{1}{2}\pi_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}\big)^{2}}\big|\big|s_{\gamma^{\ast}}(X_{j-n^{\textup{\text{te}}}/2,0}^{\textup{\text{tr}}})-\mathbb{E}_{0}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]\big|\big|_{2}^{2}\lambda_{\textup{max}}\Bigg\{\textup{\text{Var}}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,I}}}}\Big[\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\pi^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{te,I}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\pi^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,I}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]\Big]^{-1}\Bigg\}

\displaystyle=\frac{(\pi_{0}^{\ast})^{2}}{\big(\tfrac{1}{2}\pi_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}\big)^{2}(n^{\textup{\text{tr}}})^{2}}\big|\big|s_{\gamma^{\ast}}(X_{j-n^{\textup{\text{te}}}/2,0}^{\textup{\text{tr}}})-\mathbb{E}_{0}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]\big|\big|_{2}^{2}/\lambda_{\textup{min}}\Bigg\{\textup{\text{Var}}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,I}}}}\Big[\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\pi^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{te,I}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\pi^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,I}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]\Big]\Bigg\}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{(\pi_{0}^{\ast})^{2}}{\big(\tfrac{1}{2}\pi_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}\big)^{2}(n^{\textup{\text{tr}}})^{2}}\big|\big|s_{\gamma^{\ast}}(X_{j-n^{\textup{\text{te}}}/2,0}^{\textup{\text{tr}}})-\mathbb{E}_{0}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]\big|\big|_{2}^{2}\frac{1}{2\frac{m\xi^{2}}{n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\Lambda\nu\big(\frac{L}{m+1}\big)^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{(\pi_{0}^{\ast})^{2}}{\big(\tfrac{1}{2}\pi_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}\big)^{2}n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\cdot m\frac{16}{L^{2}}\cdot\frac{1}{2m\xi^{2}\Lambda\nu}\bigg(\frac{m+1}{L}\bigg)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\tfrac{1}{2}\pi_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\cdot\frac{16}{L^{2}\xi^{3}\Lambda\nu}\bigg(\frac{m+1}{L}\bigg)^{2}.

A similar argument can be used to bound
||Q_{w,j}||_{2}^{2}
for
j>\tfrac{1}{2}n^{\textup{\text{te}}}+\tfrac{1}{2}\pi_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}
. It follows that, for each
\epsilon>0
,

\displaystyle\sum_{j=1}^{r_{w}}\mathbb{E}\Big[||Q_{w,j}||_{2}^{2}\mathbbold{1}{\big\{||Q_{w,j}||_{2}^{2}\geq\epsilon\big\}}\Big]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{16m(m+1)}{L^{2}\Lambda\xi}\mathbbold{1}{\bigg\{\frac{16m(m+1)}{L^{2}\Lambda\xi}\geq\tfrac{1}{2}n^{\textup{\text{te}}}\epsilon\bigg\}}

\displaystyle\qquad+\sum_{y=0}^{m}\frac{16}{L^{2}\xi^{3}\Lambda\nu}\bigg(\frac{m+1}{L}\bigg)^{2}\mathbbold{1}{\Bigg\{\frac{16}{L^{2}\xi^{3}\Lambda\nu}\bigg(\frac{m+1}{L}\bigg)^{2}\geq\tfrac{1}{2}\pi_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}\epsilon\Bigg\}},

which clearly goes to zero as
w\to\infty
, meaning we’ve proven the desired convergence result.

∎

Proof of Theorem (
6.15
)
.

Note that:

\displaystyle\widehat{\pi}^{(a)}-\pi^{\ast}

\displaystyle=(\widehat{\pi}^{(a)}-\pi^{\ast})\mathbbold{1}{\big\{\sigma_{\textup{min}}\big(\widehat{A}\big)>0\big\}}+(\widehat{\pi}^{(a)}-\pi^{\ast})\mathbbold{1}{\big\{\sigma_{\textup{min}}\big(\widehat{A}\big)=0\big\}}

\displaystyle=(\widehat{\pi}^{(a)}-\pi^{\ast})\mathbbold{1}{\big\{\sigma_{\textup{min}}\big(\widehat{A}\big)>0\big\}}+\Big(\frac{1}{m+1}\vec{1}_{m}-\pi^{\ast}\Big)\mathbbold{1}{\big\{\sigma_{\textup{min}}\big(\widehat{A}\big)=0\big\}}.

And also that:

\displaystyle(\widehat{\pi}^{(a)}-\pi^{\ast})\mathbbold{1}{\big\{\sigma_{\textup{min}}\big(\widehat{A}\big)>0\big\}}

\displaystyle=\Big[\widehat{A}^{-1}(\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\pi^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{te,I}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma}}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,I}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma}}])-\pi^{\ast}\Big]\mathbbold{1}{\big\{\sigma_{\textup{min}}\big(\widehat{A}\big)>0\big\}}

\displaystyle=\widehat{A}^{-1}\Big[(\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\pi^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{te,I}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma}}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,I}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma}}])-\widehat{A}\pi^{\ast}\Big]\mathbbold{1}{\big\{\sigma_{\textup{min}}\big(\widehat{A}\big)>0\big\}}

\displaystyle=\widehat{A}^{-1}\Bigg[(\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\pi^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{te,I}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma}}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,I}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma}}])-\sum_{y=1}^{m}\pi_{y}^{\ast}(\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr,I}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma}}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,I}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma}}])\Bigg]\mathbbold{1}{\big\{\sigma_{\textup{min}}\big(\widehat{A}\big)>0\big\}}

\displaystyle=\widehat{A}^{-1}(\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\pi^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{te,I}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma}}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\pi^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,I}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma}}])\mathbbold{1}{\big\{\sigma_{\textup{min}}\big(\widehat{A}\big)>0\big\}}

\displaystyle=\widehat{A}^{-1}\bigg[\big(\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\pi^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{te,I}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\pi^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,I}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]\big)+\big(\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\pi^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{te,I}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma}}-s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\pi^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,I}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma}}-s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]\big)\bigg]\mathbbold{1}{\big\{\sigma_{\textup{min}}\big(\widehat{A}\big)>0\big\}}

\displaystyle=\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})^{-1}\bigg[\big(\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\pi^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{te,I}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\pi^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,I}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]\big)+\big(\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\pi^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{te,I}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma}}-s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\pi^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,I}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma}}-s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]\big)\bigg]\mathbbold{1}{\big\{\sigma_{\textup{min}}\big(\widehat{A}\big)>0\big\}}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt+(\widehat{A}^{-1}-\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})^{-1})\bigg[\big(\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\pi^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{te,I}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\pi^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,I}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]\big)+\big(\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\pi^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{te,I}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma}}-s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\pi^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,I}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma}}-s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]\big)\bigg]\mathbbold{1}{\big\{\sigma_{\textup{min}}\big(\widehat{A}\big)>0\big\}}

\displaystyle=\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})^{-1}\big(\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\pi^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{te,I}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\pi^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,I}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]\big)\mathbbold{1}{\big\{\sigma_{\textup{min}}\big(\widehat{A}\big)>0\big\}}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt+\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})^{-1}\big(\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\pi^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{te,I}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma}}-s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\pi^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,I}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma}}-s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]\big)\mathbbold{1}{\big\{\sigma_{\textup{min}}\big(\widehat{A}\big)>0\big\}}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt+(\widehat{A}^{-1}-\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})^{-1})\bigg[\big(\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\pi^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{te,I}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\pi^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,I}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]\big)+\big(\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\pi^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{te,I}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma}}-s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\pi^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,I}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma}}-s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]\big)\bigg]\mathbbold{1}{\big\{\sigma_{\textup{min}}\big(\widehat{A}\big)>0\big\}}

\displaystyle=\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})^{-1}\big(\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\pi^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{te,I}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\pi^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,I}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]\big)-\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})^{-1}\big(\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\pi^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{te,I}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\pi^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,I}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]\big)\mathbbold{1}{\big\{\sigma_{\textup{min}}\big(\widehat{A}\big)=0\big\}}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt+\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})^{-1}\big(\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\pi^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{te,I}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma}}-s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\pi^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,I}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma}}-s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]\big)\mathbbold{1}{\big\{\sigma_{\textup{min}}\big(\widehat{A}\big)>0\big\}}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt+(\widehat{A}^{-1}-\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})^{-1})\bigg[\big(\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\pi^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{te,I}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\pi^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,I}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]\big)+\big(\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\pi^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{te,I}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma}}-s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\pi^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,I}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma}}-s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]\big)\bigg]\mathbbold{1}{\big\{\sigma_{\textup{min}}\big(\widehat{A}\big)>0\big\}}.

Thus, we have that:

\displaystyle\widehat{\pi}^{(a)}-\pi^{\ast}

\displaystyle=\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})^{-1}\big(\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\pi^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{te,I}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\pi^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,I}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]\big)-\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})^{-1}\big(\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\pi^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{te,I}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\pi^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,I}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]\big)\mathbbold{1}{\big\{\sigma_{\textup{min}}\big(\widehat{A}\big)=0\big\}}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt+\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})^{-1}\big(\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\pi^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{te,I}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma}}-s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\pi^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,I}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma}}-s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]\big)\mathbbold{1}{\big\{\sigma_{\textup{min}}\big(\widehat{A}\big)>0\big\}}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt+(\widehat{A}^{-1}-\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})^{-1})\bigg[\big(\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\pi^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{te,I}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\pi^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,I}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]\big)+\big(\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\pi^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{te,I}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma}}-s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\pi^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,I}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma}}-s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]\big)\bigg]\mathbbold{1}{\big\{\sigma_{\textup{min}}\big(\widehat{A}\big)>0\big\}}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt+\Big(\frac{1}{m+1}\vec{1}_{m}-\pi^{\ast}\Big)\mathbbold{1}{\big\{\sigma_{\textup{min}}\big(\widehat{A}\big)=0\big\}}.

Let’s handle each of the terms on the RHS above, one at a time. For convenience, employ the shorthand:

•

W_{1}:=\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})^{-1}\big(\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\pi^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{te,I}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\pi^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,I}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]\big)

•

W_{2}:=-\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})^{-1}\big(\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\pi^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{te,I}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\pi^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,I}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]\big)\mathbbold{1}{\big\{\sigma_{\textup{min}}\big(\widehat{A}\big)=0\big\}}

•

W_{3}:=\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})^{-1}\big(\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\pi^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{te,I}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma}}-s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\pi^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,I}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma}}-s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]\big)\mathbbold{1}{\big\{\sigma_{\textup{min}}\big(\widehat{A}\big)>0\big\}}

•

W_{4}:=(\widehat{A}^{-1}-\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})^{-1})\big(\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\pi^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{te,I}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\pi^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,I}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]\big)\mathbbold{1}{\big\{\sigma_{\textup{min}}\big(\widehat{A}\big)>0\big\}}

•

W_{5}:=(\widehat{A}^{-1}-\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})^{-1})\big(\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\pi^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{te,I}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma}}-s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\pi^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,I}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma}}-s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]\big)\mathbbold{1}{\big\{\sigma_{\textup{min}}\big(\widehat{A}\big)>0\big\}}

•

W_{6}:=\Big(\frac{1}{m+1}\vec{1}_{m}-\pi^{\ast}\Big)\mathbbold{1}{\big\{\sigma_{\textup{min}}\big(\widehat{A}\big)=0\big\}}
,

so that
\widehat{\pi}^{(a)}-\pi^{\ast}=\sum\limits_{l=1}^{6}W_{l}
.

Let’s start with
W_{1}
. Observe that:

\displaystyle W_{1}

\displaystyle=\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})^{-1}\big(\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\pi^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{te,I}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\pi^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,I}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]\big)

\displaystyle=\bigg(\textup{\text{Var}}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{I}}}}\Big[\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})^{-1}\big(\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\pi^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{te,I}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\pi^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,I}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]\big)\Big]\bigg)^{\frac{1}{2}}\bigg(\textup{\text{Var}}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{I}}}}\Big[\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})^{-1}\big(\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\pi^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{te,I}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\pi^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,I}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]\big)\Big]\bigg)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})^{-1}\big(\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\pi^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{te,I}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\pi^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,I}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]\big)

\displaystyle\equiv\bigg(\textup{\text{Var}}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{I}}}}\Big[\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})^{-1}\big(\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\pi^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{te,I}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\pi^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,I}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]\big)\Big]\bigg)^{\frac{1}{2}}Z^{(a)}

\displaystyle=\Bigg(2\Bigg[\frac{1}{n^{\textup{\text{te}}}}+\frac{1}{n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\sum_{y=0}^{m}\frac{(\pi_{y}^{\ast})^{2}}{\pi_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\Bigg]\Big(\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})^{-1}-\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast};\textup{Cat})^{-1}\Big)+2\frac{1}{n^{\textup{\text{te}}}}\mathcal{I}(\pi^{\ast};\textup{Cat})^{-1}\Bigg)^{\frac{1}{2}}Z^{(a)}

\displaystyle=\sqrt{2}\Bigg(\Bigg[\frac{1}{n^{\textup{\text{te}}}}+\frac{1}{n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\sum_{y=0}^{m}\frac{(\pi_{y}^{\ast})^{2}}{\pi_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\Bigg]\Big(\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})^{-1}-\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast};\textup{Cat})^{-1}\Big)+\frac{1}{n^{\textup{\text{te}}}}\mathcal{I}(\pi^{\ast};\textup{Cat})^{-1}\Bigg)^{\frac{1}{2}}Z^{(a)},

where the second to last line is by Corollary (
6.7.2
), and under Assumptions (
A4
), (
A5
), and (
A6
), we have that
Z^{(a)}\xrightarrow{\hskip 4.09723ptd\hskip 4.09723pt}\mathcal{N}(0,I_{m})
by Lemma (
3.3
). We also take this opportunity to note that
W_{1}=\frac{1}{n/2}\sum_{i=1}^{n/2}\psi^{\textup{eff}}(Z_{i})
by Lemma (
6.8
), and so the claim that

\sqrt{2}\Bigg(\Bigg[\frac{1}{n^{\textup{\text{te}}}}+\frac{1}{n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\sum_{y=0}^{m}\frac{(\pi_{y}^{\ast})^{2}}{\pi_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\Bigg]\Big(\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})^{-1}-\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast};\textup{Cat})^{-1}\Big)+\frac{1}{n^{\textup{\text{te}}}}\mathcal{I}(\pi^{\ast};\textup{Cat})^{-1}\Bigg)^{\frac{1}{2}}Z^{(a)}=\frac{1}{n/2}\sum_{i=1}^{n/2}\psi^{\textup{eff}}(Z_{i})

is indeed true.

Next, let’s tackle
W_{2}
. For some finite global constant
C>0
, we have that:

\displaystyle||W_{2}||_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\sigma_{\textup{max}}\big(\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})^{-1}\big)\big|\big|\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\pi^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{te,I}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\pi^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,I}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]\big|\big|_{2}\mathbbold{1}{\big\{\sigma_{\textup{min}}\big(\widehat{A}\big)=0\big\}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\sqrt{\Lambda}}\big|\big|\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\pi^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{te,I}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\pi^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,I}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]\big|\big|_{2}\mathbbold{1}{\big\{\sigma_{\textup{min}}\big(\widehat{A}\big)=0\big\}}

\displaystyle=O_{\mathbb{P}}\bigg(\sqrt{\frac{1}{n^{\textup{\text{te}}}}+\frac{1}{n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}}\bigg)\mathbbold{1}{\big\{\sigma_{\textup{min}}\big(\widehat{A}\big)=0\big\}}

\displaystyle=O_{\mathbb{P}}\bigg(\sqrt{\frac{1}{n^{\textup{\text{te}}}}+\frac{1}{n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}}\bigg)O_{\mathbb{P}}\bigg(e^{-C(\min_{y}\lambda_{y})^{2}n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}+\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{II}}}}\Big[\big|\big|\widehat{\gamma}-\gamma^{\ast}\big|\big|_{2}\geq C\Big]\bigg),

where the third line uses Popoviciu’s variance inequality with the fact that the magnitude of the components of
s_{\gamma^{\ast}}
are bounded by a finite, global constant, and the fourth line is a consequence of the tail bounds in Lemmas (
6.9
), (
3.1
), (
3.2
), (
6.10
), (
6.11
), (
6.12
) and (
6.13
), as well as the properties of
\min_{y}\lambda_{y}
in Assumption (
A3
).

Next, let’s get control of
W_{3}
. Observe that:

\displaystyle||W_{3}||_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\sigma_{\textup{max}}\big(\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})^{-1}\big)\big|\big|\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\pi^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{te,I}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma}}-s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\pi^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,I}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma}}-s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]\big|\big|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\sqrt{\Lambda}}\big|\big|\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\pi^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{te,I}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma}}-s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\pi^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,I}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma}}-s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]\big|\big|_{2}

\displaystyle=O_{\mathbb{P}}\Bigg(\sqrt{\bigg(\frac{1}{n^{\textup{\text{te}}}}+\frac{1}{n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\bigg)\sum_{y=1}^{m}\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{II}}}}\Big[\textup{\text{Var}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},y}-s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\Big]}\Bigg)

\displaystyle=O_{\mathbb{P}}\Bigg(\sqrt{\bigg(\frac{1}{n^{\textup{\text{te}}}}+\frac{1}{n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\bigg)\sum_{y=1}^{m}\bigg\{\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{II}}}}[\widetilde{T}_{y}]+\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Big[\mathcal{U}_{y}(\lambda_{y})\Big]+\sqrt{\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{II}}}}\Big[||\widehat{\gamma}-\gamma^{\ast}||_{2}^{2}\Big]}+\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Big[\textup{\text{Var}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}-s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\Big]\bigg\}}\Bigg)

\displaystyle=O_{\mathbb{P}}\Bigg(\sqrt{\bigg(\frac{1}{n^{\textup{\text{te}}}}+\frac{1}{n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\bigg)\sum_{y=1}^{m}\bigg\{\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}[T_{y}]+\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Big[\mathcal{U}_{y}(\lambda_{y})\Big]+\sqrt{\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{II}}}}\Big[||\widehat{\gamma}-\gamma^{\ast}||_{2}^{2}\Big]}+\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Big[\textup{\text{Var}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[\widehat{s}_{\gamma^{\ast},y}-s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\Big]\bigg\}}\Bigg)

\displaystyle=O_{\mathbb{P}}\Bigg(\sqrt{\bigg(\frac{1}{n^{\textup{\text{te}}}}+\frac{1}{n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\bigg)\sum_{y=1}^{m}\bigg\{\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}[T_{y}]+\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Big[\mathcal{U}_{y}(\lambda_{y})\Big]+\sqrt{\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{II}}}}\Big[||\widehat{\gamma}-\gamma^{\ast}||_{2}^{2}\Big]}\bigg\}}\Bigg)

\displaystyle=O_{\mathbb{P}}\Bigg(\sqrt{\bigg(\frac{1}{n^{\textup{\text{te}}}}+\frac{1}{n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\bigg)\sum_{y=1}^{m}\bigg\{\frac{1}{\sqrt{n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}}+\lambda_{y}\Omega(s_{\gamma^{\ast},y})^{2}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}e^{-Cn^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}+\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Big[\mathcal{U}_{y}(\lambda_{y})\Big]+\sqrt{\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{II}}}}\Big[||\widehat{\gamma}-\gamma^{\ast}||_{2}^{2}\Big]}\bigg\}}\Bigg),

where the third line follows from Lemma (
6.14
), the fourth line follows from Lemma (
6.11
), the fifth line follows from Lemma (
6.10
), the sixth line follows from Lemma (
3.2
), and the seventh line follows from Lemma (
3.1
).

Next, let’s examine
W_{4}
. Observe that:

\displaystyle||W_{4}||_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\sigma_{\textup{max}}\big(\widehat{A}^{-1}-\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})^{-1}\big)\mathbbold{1}{\big\{\sigma_{\textup{min}}\big(\widehat{A}\big)>0\big\}}\cdot\big|\big|\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\pi^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{te,I}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\pi^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,I}}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]\big|\big|_{2}

\displaystyle=O_{\mathbb{P}}\Bigg(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}}+\sqrt{\sum_{y=1}^{m}\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{II}}}}\Big[\textup{\text{Var}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma},y}-s_{\gamma^{\ast},y}]\Big]}\Bigg)O_{\mathbb{P}}\bigg(\sqrt{\frac{1}{n^{\textup{\text{te}}}}+\frac{1}{n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}}\bigg)

\displaystyle=O_{\mathbb{P}}\Bigg(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}}+\sqrt{\sum_{y=1}^{m}\bigg\{\frac{1}{\sqrt{n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}}+\lambda_{y}\Omega(s_{\gamma^{\ast},y})^{2}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}e^{-Cn^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}+\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Big[\mathcal{U}_{y}(\lambda_{y})\Big]+\sqrt{\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{II}}}}\Big[||\widehat{\gamma}-\gamma^{\ast}||_{2}^{2}\Big]}\bigg\}}\Bigg)O_{\mathbb{P}}\bigg(\sqrt{\frac{1}{n^{\textup{\text{te}}}}+\frac{1}{n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}}\bigg)

\displaystyle=O_{\mathbb{P}}\Bigg(\sqrt{\sum_{y=1}^{m}\bigg\{\frac{1}{\sqrt{n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}}+\lambda_{y}\Omega(s_{\gamma^{\ast},y})^{2}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}e^{-Cn^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}+\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Big[\mathcal{U}_{y}(\lambda_{y})\Big]+\sqrt{\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{II}}}}\Big[||\widehat{\gamma}-\gamma^{\ast}||_{2}^{2}\Big]}\bigg\}}\Bigg)O_{\mathbb{P}}\bigg(\sqrt{\frac{1}{n^{\textup{\text{te}}}}+\frac{1}{n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}}\bigg),

where the second line is due to Lemma (
6.13
), and the third line uses the same logic employed when we bounded
||W_{3}||_{2}
.

Next, let’s examine
W_{5}
. Using the same logic used to bound
||W_{3}||_{2}
and
||W_{4}||_{2}
, we have that:

\displaystyle||W_{5}||_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\sigma_{\textup{max}}\big(\widehat{A}^{-1}-\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})^{-1}\big)\mathbbold{1}{\big\{\sigma_{\textup{min}}\big(\widehat{A}\big)>0\big\}}\cdot\big|\big|\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\pi^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{te,I}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma}}-s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\pi^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,I}}}[\widehat{s}_{\widehat{\gamma}}-s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]\big|\big|_{2}

\displaystyle=O_{\mathbb{P}}\Bigg(\sqrt{\sum_{y=1}^{m}\bigg\{\frac{1}{\sqrt{n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}}+\lambda_{y}\Omega(s_{\gamma^{\ast},y})^{2}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}e^{-Cn^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}+\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Big[\mathcal{U}_{y}(\lambda_{y})\Big]+\sqrt{\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{II}}}}\Big[||\widehat{\gamma}-\gamma^{\ast}||_{2}^{2}\Big]}\bigg\}}\Bigg)

\displaystyle\hskip 18.49988pt\times O_{\mathbb{P}}\Bigg(\sqrt{\bigg(\frac{1}{n^{\textup{\text{te}}}}+\frac{1}{n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\bigg)\sum_{y=1}^{m}\bigg\{\frac{1}{\sqrt{n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}}+\lambda_{y}\Omega(s_{\gamma^{\ast},y})^{2}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}e^{-Cn^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}+\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Big[\mathcal{U}_{y}(\lambda_{y})\Big]+\sqrt{\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{II}}}}\Big[||\widehat{\gamma}-\gamma^{\ast}||_{2}^{2}\Big]}\bigg\}}\Bigg)

\displaystyle=O_{\mathbb{P}}\Bigg(\sqrt{\bigg(\frac{1}{n^{\textup{\text{te}}}}+\frac{1}{n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\bigg)}\sum_{y=1}^{m}\bigg\{\frac{1}{\sqrt{n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}}+\lambda_{y}\Omega(s_{\gamma^{\ast},y})^{2}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}e^{-Cn^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}+\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Big[\mathcal{U}_{y}(\lambda_{y})\Big]+\sqrt{\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{II}}}}\Big[||\widehat{\gamma}-\gamma^{\ast}||_{2}^{2}\Big]}\bigg\}\Bigg).

Finally, let’s examine
W_{6}
. Observe that:

\displaystyle||W_{6}||_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\bigg|\bigg|\frac{1}{m+1}\vec{1}_{m}-\pi^{\ast}\bigg|\bigg|_{2}\mathbbold{1}{\big\{\sigma_{\textup{min}}\big(\widehat{A}\big)=0\big\}}

\displaystyle=O_{\mathbb{P}}\bigg(e^{-C(\min_{y}\lambda_{y})^{2}n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}+\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{II}}}}\Big[\big|\big|\widehat{\gamma}-\gamma^{\ast}\big|\big|_{2}\geq C\Big]\bigg),

where the second line makes use of the work done in bounding
||W_{2}||_{2}
.

Finally, having obtained bounds on
||W_{2}||_{2},\dots,||W_{6}||_{2}
, we now make two observations. First, note that the upper bound for the rate of
||W_{2}||_{2}
goes down to 0 faster than the upper bound for the rate of
||W_{6}||_{2}
. Second, note that, due to Assumption (
A3
),

\displaystyle\frac{e^{-Cn^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}}{\sqrt{\min_{y}\lambda_{y}}}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sqrt{\min_{y}\lambda_{y}}\cdot e^{Cn^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}}

\displaystyle=o\bigg(\frac{1}{(\min_{y}\lambda_{y})^{2}n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\bigg)

\displaystyle=o(1).

Thus, the upper bound for the rate of
||W_{5}||_{2}
goes down to 0 faster than the upper bound for the rate of
||W_{4}||_{2}
. Defining
\epsilon^{(a)}:=\sum_{l=2}^{6}W_{l}
, it follows that:

\displaystyle||\epsilon^{(a)}||_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{l=2}^{6}||W_{l}||_{2}

\displaystyle=O_{\mathbb{P}}\Bigg(\sqrt{\bigg(\frac{1}{n^{\textup{\text{te}}}}+\frac{1}{n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\bigg)\sum_{y=1}^{m}\bigg\{\frac{1}{\sqrt{n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}}+\lambda_{y}\Omega(s_{\gamma^{\ast},y})^{2}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}e^{-Cn^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}+\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Big[\mathcal{U}_{y}(\lambda_{y})\Big]+\sqrt{\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{II}}}}\Big[||\widehat{\gamma}-\gamma^{\ast}||_{2}^{2}\Big]}\bigg\}}\Bigg)

\displaystyle\hskip 18.49988pt+O_{\mathbb{P}}\bigg(e^{-C(\min_{y}\lambda_{y})^{2}n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}+\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{II}}}}\Big[\big|\big|\widehat{\gamma}-\gamma^{\ast}\big|\big|_{2}\geq C\Big]\bigg)

\displaystyle=O_{\mathbb{P}}\Bigg(\sqrt{\bigg(\frac{1}{n^{\textup{\text{te}}}}+\frac{1}{n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\bigg)\bigg\{\frac{1}{\sqrt{n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}}+\max_{y}\lambda_{y}\Omega(s_{\gamma^{\ast},y})^{2}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{\min_{y}\lambda_{y}}}e^{-Cn^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}+\max_{y}\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Big[\mathcal{U}_{y}(\lambda_{y})\Big]+\sqrt{\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{II}}}}\Big[||\widehat{\gamma}-\gamma^{\ast}||_{2}^{2}\Big]}\bigg\}}\Bigg)

\displaystyle\hskip 18.49988pt+O_{\mathbb{P}}\bigg(e^{-C(\min_{y}\lambda_{y})^{2}n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}+\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{II}}}}\Big[\big|\big|\widehat{\gamma}-\gamma^{\ast}\big|\big|_{2}\geq C\Big]\bigg).

So, overall, we have that:

\displaystyle\widehat{\pi}^{(a)}-\pi^{\ast}=\sqrt{2}\Bigg(\Bigg[\frac{1}{n^{\textup{\text{te}}}}+\frac{1}{n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\sum_{y=0}^{m}\frac{(\pi_{y}^{\ast})^{2}}{\pi_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\Bigg]\Big(\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})^{-1}-\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast};\textup{Cat})^{-1}\Big)+\frac{1}{n^{\textup{\text{te}}}}\mathcal{I}(\pi^{\ast};\textup{Cat})^{-1}\Bigg)^{\frac{1}{2}}Z^{(a)}+\epsilon^{(a)},

where
Z^{(a)}\xrightarrow{\hskip 4.09723ptd\hskip 4.09723pt}\mathcal{N}(0,I_{m})
, and
\epsilon^{(a)}\in\mathbb{R}^{m}
satisfies

\displaystyle||\epsilon^{(a)}||_{2}

\displaystyle=O_{\mathbb{P}}\Bigg(\sqrt{\bigg(\frac{1}{n^{\textup{\text{te}}}}+\frac{1}{n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\bigg)\bigg\{\frac{1}{\sqrt{n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}}+\max_{y}\lambda_{y}\Omega(s_{\gamma^{\ast},y})^{2}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{\min_{y}\lambda_{y}}}e^{-Cn^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}+\max_{y}\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Big[\mathcal{U}_{y}(\lambda_{y})\Big]+\sqrt{\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{II}}}}\Big[||\widehat{\gamma}-\gamma^{\ast}||_{2}^{2}\Big]}\bigg\}}\Bigg)

\displaystyle\hskip 18.49988pt+O_{\mathbb{P}}\bigg(e^{-C(\min_{y}\lambda_{y})^{2}n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}+\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{II}}}}\Big[\big|\big|\widehat{\gamma}-\gamma^{\ast}\big|\big|_{2}\geq C\Big]\bigg),

as claimed!

∎

Proof of Corollary (
6.15.1
)
.

Note that the datasets
\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{I}}}
and
\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{II}}}
have identical joint distributions, so the results of Theorem (
6.15
) also apply analogously to
\widehat{\pi}^{(b)}
; that is, there exists random vectors
Z^{(b)},\epsilon^{(b)}\in\mathbb{R}^{m}
such that

\displaystyle\widehat{\pi}^{(b)}-\pi^{\ast}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n/2}\sum_{i=n/2+1}^{n}\psi^{\textup{eff}}(Z_{i})+\epsilon^{(b)}

\displaystyle=\sqrt{2}\Bigg(\Bigg[\frac{1}{n^{\textup{\text{te}}}}+\frac{1}{n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\sum_{y=0}^{m}\frac{(\pi_{y}^{\ast})^{2}}{\pi_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\Bigg]\Big(\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})^{-1}-\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast};\textup{Cat})^{-1}\Big)+\frac{1}{n^{\textup{\text{te}}}}\mathcal{I}(\pi^{\ast};\textup{Cat})^{-1}\Bigg)^{\frac{1}{2}}Z^{(b)}+\epsilon^{(b)},

where
Z^{(b)}\xrightarrow{\hskip 4.09723ptd\hskip 4.09723pt}\mathcal{N}(0,I_{m})
, and
\epsilon^{(b)}
satisfies

\displaystyle||\epsilon^{(b)}||_{2}

\displaystyle=O_{\mathbb{P}}\Bigg(\sqrt{\bigg(\frac{1}{n^{\textup{\text{te}}}}+\frac{1}{n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\bigg)\bigg\{\frac{1}{\sqrt{n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}}+\max_{y}\lambda_{y}\Omega(s_{\gamma^{\ast},y})^{2}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{\min_{y}\lambda_{y}}}e^{-Cn^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}+\max_{y}\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Big[\mathcal{U}_{y}(\lambda_{y})\Big]+\sqrt{\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{II}}}}\Big[||\widehat{\gamma}-\gamma^{\ast}||_{2}^{2}\Big]}\bigg\}}\Bigg)

\displaystyle\hskip 18.49988pt+O_{\mathbb{P}}\bigg(e^{-C(\min_{y}\lambda_{y})^{2}n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}+\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{II}}}}\Big[\big|\big|\widehat{\gamma}-\gamma^{\ast}\big|\big|_{2}\geq C\Big]\bigg).

Thus:

\displaystyle\widehat{\pi}-\pi^{\ast}

\displaystyle=\frac{\widehat{\pi}^{(a)}+\widehat{\pi}^{(b)}}{2}-\pi^{\ast}

\displaystyle=\frac{(\widehat{\pi}^{(a)}-\pi^{\ast})}{2}+\frac{(\widehat{\pi}^{(b)}-\pi^{\ast})}{2}

\displaystyle=\Bigg(\Bigg[\frac{1}{n^{\textup{\text{te}}}}+\frac{1}{n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\sum_{y=0}^{m}\frac{(\pi_{y}^{\ast})^{2}}{\pi_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\Bigg]\Big(\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})^{-1}-\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast};\textup{Cat})^{-1}\Big)+\frac{1}{n^{\textup{\text{te}}}}\mathcal{I}(\pi^{\ast};\textup{Cat})^{-1}\Bigg)^{\frac{1}{2}}\frac{Z^{(a)}+Z^{(b)}}{\sqrt{2}}+\frac{1}{2}\big(\epsilon^{(a)}+\epsilon^{(b)}\big).

Now, recall from the proof of Theorem (
6.15
) that
Z^{(a)}
is a function of only
\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{I}}}
(and not
\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{II}}}
), and analogously,
Z^{(b)}
is a function of only
\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{II}}}
(and not
\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{I}}}
). Since
\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{I}}}
and
\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{II}}}
are independent, this means that
Z^{(a)}
and
Z^{(b)}
are also independent. Thus, it follows from the Continuity Theorem that
Z^{(a)}+Z^{(b)}\xrightarrow{\hskip 4.09723ptd\hskip 4.09723pt}\mathcal{N}(0,2I_{m})
, meaning that
Z:=\frac{Z^{(a)}+Z^{(b)}}{\sqrt{2}}\xrightarrow{\hskip 4.09723ptd\hskip 4.09723pt}\mathcal{N}(0,I_{m})
by way of Slutsky’s Theorem. Defining
\epsilon:=\frac{1}{2}(\epsilon^{(a)}+\epsilon^{(b)})
, we therefore have that:

\displaystyle\widehat{\pi}-\pi^{\ast}

\displaystyle=\Bigg(\Bigg[\frac{1}{n^{\textup{\text{te}}}}+\frac{1}{n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\sum_{y=0}^{m}\frac{(\pi_{y}^{\ast})^{2}}{\pi_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\Bigg]\Big(\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})^{-1}-\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast};\textup{Cat})^{-1}\Big)+\frac{1}{n^{\textup{\text{te}}}}\mathcal{I}(\pi^{\ast};\textup{Cat})^{-1}\Bigg)^{\frac{1}{2}}Z+\epsilon,

where
Z\xrightarrow{\hskip 4.09723ptd\hskip 4.09723pt}\mathcal{N}(0,I_{m})
, and
\epsilon
satisfies

\displaystyle||\epsilon||_{2}

\displaystyle=O_{\mathbb{P}}\Bigg(\sqrt{\bigg(\frac{1}{n^{\textup{\text{te}}}}+\frac{1}{n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\bigg)\bigg\{\frac{1}{\sqrt{n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}}+\max_{y}\lambda_{y}\Omega(s_{\gamma^{\ast},y})^{2}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{\min_{y}\lambda_{y}}}e^{-Cn^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}+\max_{y}\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Big[\mathcal{U}_{y}(\lambda_{y})\Big]+\sqrt{\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{II}}}}\Big[||\widehat{\gamma}-\gamma^{\ast}||_{2}^{2}\Big]}\bigg\}}\Bigg)

\displaystyle\hskip 18.49988pt+O_{\mathbb{P}}\bigg(e^{-C(\min_{y}\lambda_{y})^{2}n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}+\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{II}}}}\Big[\big|\big|\widehat{\gamma}-\gamma^{\ast}\big|\big|_{2}\geq C\Big]\bigg),

as claimed. Finally, observe that:

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\psi^{\textup{eff}}(Z_{i})

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\bigg(\frac{1}{n/2}\sum_{i=1}^{n/2}\psi^{\textup{eff}}(Z_{i})+\frac{1}{n/2}\sum_{i=n/2+1}^{n}\psi^{\textup{eff}}(Z_{i})\bigg)

\displaystyle=\Bigg(\Bigg[\frac{1}{n^{\textup{\text{te}}}}+\frac{1}{n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\sum_{y=0}^{m}\frac{(\pi_{y}^{\ast})^{2}}{\pi_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\Bigg]\Big(\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})^{-1}-\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast};\textup{Cat})^{-1}\Big)+\frac{1}{n^{\textup{\text{te}}}}\mathcal{I}(\pi^{\ast};\textup{Cat})^{-1}\Bigg)^{\frac{1}{2}}\frac{Z^{(a)}+Z^{(b)}}{\sqrt{2}}

\displaystyle=\Bigg(\Bigg[\frac{1}{n^{\textup{\text{te}}}}+\frac{1}{n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\sum_{y=0}^{m}\frac{(\pi_{y}^{\ast})^{2}}{\pi_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\Bigg]\Big(\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})^{-1}-\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast};\textup{Cat})^{-1}\Big)+\frac{1}{n^{\textup{\text{te}}}}\mathcal{I}(\pi^{\ast};\textup{Cat})^{-1}\Bigg)^{\frac{1}{2}}Z,

so the desiderata have all been proven.
∎

Proof of Theorem (
3.4
)
.

Define

V:=\Bigg[\frac{1}{n^{\textup{\text{te}}}}+\frac{1}{n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\sum_{y=0}^{m}\frac{(\pi_{y}^{\ast})^{2}}{\pi_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\Bigg]\Big(\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})^{-1}-\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast};\textup{Cat})^{-1}\Big)+\frac{1}{n^{\textup{\text{te}}}}\mathcal{I}(\pi^{\ast};\textup{Cat})^{-1}.

By Corollary (
6.7.2
),
V
is a symmetric positive definite matrix. So, using the results of Corollary (
6.15.1
), it follows that there exists random vectors
Z,\delta\in\mathbb{R}^{m}
such that

V^{-\frac{1}{2}}(\widehat{\pi}-\pi^{\ast})=Z+\epsilon,

where

Z=V^{-\frac{1}{2}}\bigg(\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\psi^{\textup{eff}}(Z_{i})\bigg)\xrightarrow{\hskip 4.09723ptd\hskip 4.09723pt}\mathcal{N}(0,I_{m})

and
\epsilon=V^{-\frac{1}{2}}\delta
, and
\delta\in\mathbb{R}^{m}
is a random vector which satisfies

\displaystyle||\delta||_{2}

\displaystyle=O_{\mathbb{P}}\Bigg(\sqrt{\bigg(\frac{1}{n^{\textup{\text{te}}}}+\frac{1}{n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\bigg)\bigg\{\frac{1}{\sqrt{n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}}+\max_{y}\lambda_{y}\Omega(s_{\gamma^{\ast},y})^{2}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{\min_{y}\lambda_{y}}}e^{-Cn^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}+\max_{y}\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Big[\mathcal{U}_{y}(\lambda_{y})\Big]+\sqrt{\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{II}}}}\Big[||\widehat{\gamma}-\gamma^{\ast}||_{2}^{2}\Big]}\bigg\}}\Bigg)

\displaystyle\hskip 18.49988pt+O_{\mathbb{P}}\bigg(e^{-C(\min_{y}\lambda_{y})^{2}n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}+\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{II}}}}\Big[\big|\big|\widehat{\gamma}-\gamma^{\ast}\big|\big|_{2}\geq C\Big]\bigg).

Note that

\displaystyle\sigma_{\textup{min}}\big(V\big)

\displaystyle=\lambda_{\textup{min}}\big(V\big)

\displaystyle\geq\bigg(\frac{1}{n^{\textup{\text{te}}}}+\frac{1}{n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\bigg)\xi^{2}\lambda_{\textup{min}}\big(\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})^{-1}-\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast};\textup{Cat})^{-1}\big)

\displaystyle\geq\bigg(\frac{1}{n^{\textup{\text{te}}}}+\frac{1}{n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\bigg)\xi^{2}\nu\bigg(\frac{L}{m+1}\bigg)^{2},

where the first line is because
V
is symmetric positive definite, the second line is due to Assumption (
A5
) and Lemma (
6.3
), and the third line uses the same logic employed in the proof of Lemma (
3.3
) to obtain a lower bound on
\lambda_{\textup{min}}\big(\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})^{-1}-\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast};\textup{Cat})^{-1}\big)
. Thus, it follows that:

\displaystyle||\epsilon||_{2}

\displaystyle=||V^{-\frac{1}{2}}\delta||_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\sigma_{\textup{max}}\big(V^{-\frac{1}{2}}\big)||\delta||_{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{||\delta||_{2}}{\sqrt{\sigma_{\textup{min}}\big(V\big)}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\sqrt{\frac{1}{n^{\textup{\text{te}}}}+\frac{1}{n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}}}\frac{||\delta||_{2}}{\sqrt{\xi^{2}\nu\bigg(\frac{L}{m+1}\bigg)^{2}}}.

Therefore, we have that:

\displaystyle||\epsilon||_{2}

\displaystyle=O_{\mathbb{P}}\Bigg(\sqrt{\frac{1}{\sqrt{n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}}+\max_{y}\lambda_{y}\Omega(s_{\gamma^{\ast},y})^{2}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{\min_{y}\lambda_{y}}}e^{-Cn^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}+\max_{y}\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Big[\mathcal{U}_{y}(\lambda_{y})\Big]+\sqrt{\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{II}}}}\Big[||\widehat{\gamma}-\gamma^{\ast}||_{2}^{2}\Big]}}\Bigg)

\displaystyle\hskip 18.49988pt+O_{\mathbb{P}}\Bigg(\frac{1}{\sqrt{\frac{1}{n^{\textup{\text{te}}}}+\frac{1}{n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}}}\bigg\{e^{-C(\min_{y}\lambda_{y})^{2}n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}+\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{II}}}}\Big[\big|\big|\widehat{\gamma}-\gamma^{\ast}\big|\big|_{2}\geq C\Big]\bigg\}\Bigg),

as claimed!

∎

Proof of Corollary (
6.15.2
)
.

First, we show that Assumption (
A1
) is satisfied. Since
s_{\gamma^{\ast}}\in H_{\mathcal{K}}^{m}
and
\mathcal{H}=H_{\mathcal{K}}^{m}
, we need only verify that
\mathcal{H}
is closed under scalar multiplication. This is true because if
h\in\mathcal{H}
and
c\in\mathbb{R}
, then since
\mathcal{H}=H_{\mathcal{K}}^{m}=\big[\cup_{k\in\mathcal{K}}H_{k}\big]^{m}
, it must be that
h_{y}\in\cup_{k\in\mathcal{K}}H_{k}
for each
y\in[m]
. However, since
H_{k}
is a RKHS, this means that
ch_{y}\in\cup_{k\in\mathcal{K}}H_{k}
for each
y\in[m]
as well. Thus, it follows that
ch\in\big[\cup_{k\in\mathcal{K}}H_{k}\big]^{m}=\mathcal{H}
.

Second, we show that Assumption (
•
‣
A2
) is satisfied. Let any
y\in[m]
be given. Clearly,
\Omega:\mathcal{H}_{y}\mapsto[0,\infty)
. In addition, for any
k\in\mathcal{K}
,
||\cdot||_{k}
is a norm so it is equal to
0
at the identically zero function, meaning that
\Omega
must also be equal to
0
at the identically zero function. Also, for any
c\in\mathbb{R}
and
h_{y}\in\mathcal{H}_{y}
:

\displaystyle\Omega(ch_{y})

\displaystyle=\inf\Big\{||ch_{y}||_{k}\ \Big\lvert\ k\in\mathcal{K},\ ch_{y}\in H_{k}\Big\}

\displaystyle=|c|\inf\Big\{||h_{y}||_{k}\ \Big\lvert\ k\in\mathcal{K},\ h_{y}\in H_{k}\Big\}

\displaystyle=|c|\Omega(h_{y}).

Lastly, for any
k\in\mathcal{K}
, if
h_{y}\in H_{k}
, then
||h_{y}||_{k}\geq D||h_{y}||_{\infty}
with
D=\frac{1}{\kappa}
(see the beginning of the proof of Lemma 4.23 in
[
Steinwart and Christmann, 2008
]
). This implies that:

\displaystyle\Omega(h_{y})

\displaystyle=\inf\Big\{||h_{y}||_{k}\ \Big\lvert\ k\in\mathcal{K},\ h_{y}\in H_{k}\Big\}

\displaystyle\geq\inf\Big\{D||h_{y}||_{\infty}\ \Big\lvert\ k\in\mathcal{K},\ h_{y}\in H_{k}\Big\}

\displaystyle=D||h_{y}||_{\infty}.

Thus, Assumption (
•
‣
A2
) is satisfied with
D=\frac{1}{\kappa}
.

Third, it remains for us to obtain the bound on
\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}[\mathcal{U}_{y}(\lambda_{y})]
. Towards that end, note that for each
h_{y}\in\mathcal{H}_{y}
which satisfies
\Omega(h_{y})\leq\frac{1}{L\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}
, we have that:

\displaystyle\Big|\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[h_{y}]-\textup{\text{Var}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[h_{y}]\Big|

\displaystyle\leq\Big|\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[h_{y}^{2}]-\mathbb{E}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[h_{y}^{2}]\Big|+\Big|\big(\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[h_{y}]\big)^{2}-\big(\mathbb{E}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[h_{y}]\big)^{2}\Big|

\displaystyle=\Big|\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[h_{y}^{2}]-\mathbb{E}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[h_{y}^{2}]\Big|+\Big|\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[h_{y}]+\mathbb{E}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[h_{y}]\Big|\Big|\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[h_{y}]-\mathbb{E}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[h_{y}]\Big|

\displaystyle\leq\Big|\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[h_{y}^{2}]-\mathbb{E}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[h_{y}^{2}]\Big|+2||h_{y}||_{\infty}\Big|\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[h_{y}]-\mathbb{E}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[h_{y}]\Big|

\displaystyle\leq\Big|\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[h_{y}^{2}]-\mathbb{E}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[h_{y}^{2}]\Big|+\frac{2\kappa}{L\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}\Big|\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[h_{y}]-\mathbb{E}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[h_{y}]\Big|

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{j=0}^{m}\Big|\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[h_{y}^{2}]-\mathbb{E}_{j}[h_{y}^{2}]\Big|+\frac{2\kappa}{L\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}\sum_{j=0}^{m}\Big|\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[h_{y}]-\mathbb{E}_{j}[h_{y}]\Big|,

where the fourth line uses the fact that
D||h_{y}||_{\infty}\leq\Omega(h)
where
D=\frac{1}{\kappa}
. Ergo:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}[\mathcal{U}_{y}(\lambda_{y})]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Bigg(\sup\limits_{\Omega(h_{y})\leq\frac{1}{L\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}}\Big|\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[h_{y}]-\mathbb{E}_{0}[h_{y}]\Big|\Bigg)+\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Bigg(\sup\limits_{\Omega(h_{y})\leq\frac{1}{L\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}}\Big|\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[h_{y}]-\mathbb{E}_{y}[h_{y}]\Big|\Bigg)

\displaystyle\qquad+\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Bigg(\sup\limits_{\Omega(h_{y})\leq\frac{1}{L\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}}\Big|\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[h_{y}]-\textup{\text{Var}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[h_{y}]\Big|\Bigg)

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Bigg(\sup\limits_{\Omega(h_{y})\leq\frac{1}{L\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}}\Big|\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[h_{y}]-\mathbb{E}_{0}[h_{y}]\Big|\Bigg)+\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Bigg(\sup\limits_{\Omega(h_{y})\leq\frac{1}{L\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}}\Big|\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[h_{y}]-\mathbb{E}_{y}[h_{y}]\Big|\Bigg)

\displaystyle\qquad+\sum_{j=0}^{m}\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Bigg(\sup\limits_{\Omega(h_{y})\leq\frac{1}{L\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}}\Big|\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[h_{y}^{2}]-\mathbb{E}_{j}[h_{y}^{2}]\Big|\Bigg)+\frac{2\kappa}{L\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}\sum_{j=0}^{m}\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Bigg(\sup\limits_{\Omega(h_{y})\leq\frac{1}{L\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}}\Big|\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[h_{y}]-\mathbb{E}_{j}[h_{y}]\Big|\Bigg)

\displaystyle=O\Bigg(\sum_{j=0}^{m}\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Bigg[\sup\limits_{\Omega(h_{y})\leq\frac{1}{L\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}}\Big|\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[h_{y}^{2}]-\mathbb{E}_{j}[h_{y}^{2}]\Big|\Bigg]+\frac{1}{\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}\sum_{j=0}^{m}\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Bigg[\sup\limits_{\Omega(h_{y})\leq\frac{1}{L\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}}\Big|\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[h_{y}]-\mathbb{E}_{j}[h_{y}]\Big|\Bigg]\Bigg).

Next, define the function
\Phi(t):=\min\Big\{t^{2},\frac{\kappa^{2}}{L^{2}\lambda_{y}}\Big\}
, and let
\Big\{\xi_{j,q}:0\leq j\leq m,\ 1\leq q\leq\tfrac{1}{2}\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}\Big\}
be a set of independent Rademacher random variables. For each class
j\in 0\cup[m]
, define the Rademacher Complexities

\mathcal{R}_{j}(\lambda_{y},\mathcal{H}_{y}):=\mathbb{E}_{\xi,\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Bigg[\sup\limits_{\Omega(h_{y})\leq\frac{1}{L\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}}\frac{1}{\tfrac{1}{2}\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\sum_{q=1}^{\tfrac{1}{2}\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\xi_{j,q}h_{y}(X_{j,q}^{\textup{\text{tr}}})\Bigg]

and

\displaystyle\mathcal{R}_{j}(\lambda_{y},\Phi(\mathcal{H}_{y}))

\displaystyle:=\mathbb{E}_{\xi,\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Bigg[\sup\limits_{\Omega(h_{y})\leq\frac{1}{L\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}}\frac{1}{\tfrac{1}{2}\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\sum_{q=1}^{\tfrac{1}{2}\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\xi_{j,q}\Phi(h_{y}(X_{j,q}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}))\Bigg]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{\xi,\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Bigg[\sup\limits_{\Omega(h_{y})\leq\frac{1}{L\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}}\frac{1}{\tfrac{1}{2}\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\sum_{q=1}^{\tfrac{1}{2}\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\xi_{j,q}h_{y}^{2}(X_{j,q}^{\textup{\text{tr}}})\Bigg],

where the second line above uses the fact that
\Omega(h_{y})\leq\frac{1}{L\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}\implies||h_{y}||_{\infty}\leq\frac{\kappa}{L\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}
, so
||h_{y}^{2}||_{\infty}\leq\frac{\kappa^{2}}{L^{2}\lambda_{y}}
meaning that
\Phi(h_{y}(x))=\min\Big\{h_{y}^{2}(x),\frac{\kappa^{2}}{L^{2}\lambda_{y}}\Big\}=h_{y}^{2}(x)
. Using standard symmetrization arguments (e.g., see Lemma 2.3.1 in
[
van der Vaart and Wellner, 1996
]
), it follows that:

\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Bigg[\sup\limits_{\Omega(h_{y})\leq\frac{1}{L\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}}\Big|\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[h_{y}]-\mathbb{E}_{j}[h_{y}]\Big|\Bigg]\leq 2\mathcal{R}_{j}(\lambda_{y},\mathcal{H}_{y})

and

\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Bigg[\sup\limits_{\Omega(h_{y})\leq\frac{1}{L\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}}\Big|\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[h_{y}^{2}]-\mathbb{E}_{j}[h_{y}^{2}]\Big|\Bigg]\leq 2\mathcal{R}_{j}(\lambda_{y},\Phi(\mathcal{H}_{y})).

Now, the function
\Phi(t)
is
\frac{2\kappa}{L\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}
-Lipschitz, because for any two
t_{1},t_{2}\in\mathbb{R}
:

\displaystyle|\Phi(t_{1})-\Phi(t_{2})|

\displaystyle=\bigg|\min\Big\{|t_{1}|,\frac{\kappa}{L\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}\Big\}^{2}-\min\Big\{|t_{2}|,\frac{\kappa}{L\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}\Big\}^{2}\bigg|

\displaystyle=\bigg|\min\Big\{|t_{1}|,\frac{\kappa}{L\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}\Big\}+\min\Big\{|t_{2}|,\frac{\kappa}{L\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}\Big\}\bigg|\bigg|\min\Big\{|t_{1}|,\frac{\kappa}{L^{2}\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}\Big\}-\min\Big\{|t_{2}|,\frac{\kappa}{L\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}\Big\}\bigg|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2\kappa}{L\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}\bigg|\min\Big\{|t_{1}|,\frac{\kappa}{L^{2}\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}\Big\}-\min\Big\{|t_{2}|,\frac{\kappa}{L\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}\Big\}\bigg|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2\kappa}{L\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}\big||t_{1}|-|t_{2}|\big|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2\kappa}{L\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}\big|t_{1}-t_{2}\big|,

where the fourth line is a consequence of considering 4 separate cases: (i)
|t_{1}|,|t_{2}|\leq\frac{\kappa}{L\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}
, (ii)
\frac{\kappa}{L\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}\leq|t_{1}|,|t_{2}|
, (iii)
|t_{1}|\leq\frac{\kappa}{L\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}\leq|t_{2}|
and (iv)
|t_{2}|\leq\frac{\kappa}{L\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}\leq|t_{1}|
. In case (i), the fourth line’s inequality is an equality. In case (ii), the third line equals
0
and so the fourth line is trivially true. Finally, cases (iii) and (iv) are symmetric, so we need only consider what happens in case (iii). In case (iii), the distance between
|t_{1}|
and
\frac{\kappa}{L\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}
is less than or equal to the distance between
|t_{1}|
and
|t_{2}|
, so

\bigg|\min\Big\{|t_{1}|,\frac{\kappa}{L^{2}\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}\Big\}-\min\Big\{|t_{2}|,\frac{\kappa}{L\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}\Big\}\bigg|=\Big||t_{1}|-\frac{\kappa}{L\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}\Big|\leq\Big||t_{1}|-|t_{2}|\Big|.

This establishes that the function
\Phi(t)
is indeed
\frac{2\kappa}{L\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}
-Lipschitz. So, invoking Lemma 4.12 in
Ledoux and Talagrand, 1991
, it follows that
R_{j}(\lambda_{y},\Phi(\mathcal{H}_{y}))\leq\frac{2\kappa}{L\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}R_{j}(\lambda_{y},\mathcal{H}_{y})
. Therefore, we may conclude that:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}[\mathcal{U}_{y}(\lambda_{y})]

\displaystyle=O\Bigg(\sum_{j=0}^{m}\mathcal{R}_{j}(\lambda_{y},\Phi(\mathcal{H}_{y}))+\frac{1}{\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}\sum_{j=0}^{m}\mathcal{R}_{j}(\lambda_{y},\mathcal{H}_{y})\Bigg)

\displaystyle=O\Bigg(\frac{1}{\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}\sum_{j=0}^{m}\mathcal{R}_{j}(\lambda_{y},\mathcal{H}_{y})\Bigg).

Next, we’ll develop an upper bound on
\mathcal{R}_{j}(\lambda_{y},\mathcal{H}_{y})
. Towards that end, consider any
h_{y}\in\mathcal{H}_{y}
such that
\Omega(h_{y})\leq\frac{1}{L\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}
. Trivially, this means that
\Omega(h_{y})<\frac{2}{L\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}
, which by definition of
\Omega
, implies the existence of a kernel
k\in\mathcal{K}
such that
h_{y}\in H_{k}
and
||h_{y}||_{k}<\frac{2}{L\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}
. This means that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\tfrac{1}{2}\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\sum_{q=1}^{\tfrac{1}{2}\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\xi_{j,q}h_{y}(X_{j,q}^{\textup{\text{tr}}})

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\tfrac{1}{2}\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\Bigg\langle h_{y},\sum_{q=1}^{\tfrac{1}{2}\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\xi_{j,q}k(\cdot,X_{j,q}^{\textup{\text{tr}}})\Bigg\rangle_{k}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\tfrac{1}{2}\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\big|\big|h_{y}\big|\big|_{k}\Bigg|\Bigg|\sum_{q=1}^{\tfrac{1}{2}\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\xi_{j,q}k(\cdot,X_{j,q}^{\textup{\text{tr}}})\Bigg|\Bigg|_{k}

\displaystyle<\frac{1}{\tfrac{1}{2}\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\frac{2}{L\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}\sqrt{\Bigg|\Bigg|\sum_{q=1}^{\tfrac{1}{2}\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\xi_{j,q}k(\cdot,X_{j,q}^{\textup{\text{tr}}})\Bigg|\Bigg|_{k}^{2}}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\tfrac{1}{2}\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\frac{2}{L\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}\sqrt{\sum_{q,r}^{\tfrac{1}{2}\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\xi_{j,q}\xi_{j,r}k(X_{j,q}^{\textup{\text{tr}}},X_{j,r}^{\textup{\text{tr}}})}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\tfrac{1}{2}\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\frac{2}{L\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}\sqrt{\sum_{q=1}^{\tfrac{1}{2}\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}k(X_{j,q}^{\textup{\text{tr}}},X_{j,q}^{\textup{\text{tr}}})+2\sum_{q<r}^{\tfrac{1}{2}\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\xi_{j,q}\xi_{j,r}k(X_{j,q}^{\textup{\text{tr}}},X_{j,r}^{\textup{\text{tr}}})}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\tfrac{1}{2}\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\frac{2}{L\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}\sqrt{\tfrac{1}{2}\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}\kappa^{2}+2\sum_{q<r}^{\tfrac{1}{2}\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\xi_{j,q}\xi_{j,r}k(X_{j,q}^{\textup{\text{tr}}},X_{j,r}^{\textup{\text{tr}}})},

where the first line uses the reproducing property (e.g., see Definition 4.18 in
Steinwart and Christmann, 2008
), and the fourth line uses Theorem 4.21 in
Steinwart and Christmann, 2008
. Thus, it follows that:

\displaystyle\mathcal{R}_{j}(\lambda_{y},\mathcal{H}_{y})

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{\xi,\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Bigg[\sup\limits_{\Omega(h_{y})\leq\frac{1}{L\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}}\frac{1}{\tfrac{1}{2}\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\sum_{k=1}^{\tfrac{1}{2}\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\xi_{j,q}h_{y}(X_{j,q}^{\textup{\text{tr}}})\Bigg]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\tfrac{1}{2}\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\frac{2}{L\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}\mathbb{E}_{\xi,\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Bigg[\sup\limits_{k\in\mathcal{K}}\sqrt{\tfrac{1}{2}\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}\kappa^{2}+2\sum_{q<r}^{\tfrac{1}{2}\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\xi_{j,q}\xi_{j,r}k(X_{j,q}^{\textup{\text{tr}}},X_{j,r}^{\textup{\text{tr}}})}\Bigg]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\tfrac{1}{2}\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\frac{2}{L\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}\sqrt{\mathbb{E}_{\xi,\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Bigg[\tfrac{1}{2}\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}\kappa^{2}+2\sup\limits_{k\in\mathcal{K}}\sum_{q<r}^{\tfrac{1}{2}\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\xi_{j,q}\xi_{j,r}k(X_{j,q}^{\textup{\text{tr}}},X_{j,r}^{\textup{\text{tr}}})\Bigg]}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\tfrac{1}{2}\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\frac{2}{L\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}\sqrt{\tfrac{1}{2}\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}\kappa^{2}+\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}\mathbb{E}_{\xi,\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Bigg[\frac{1}{\tfrac{1}{2}\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\sup\limits_{k\in\mathcal{K}}\sum_{q<r}^{\tfrac{1}{2}\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\xi_{j,q}\xi_{j,r}k(X_{j,q}^{\textup{\text{tr}}},X_{j,r}^{\textup{\text{tr}}})\Bigg]}.

Now, the expectation in the last line in the display above is the population-level Rademacher Chaos complexity of order two (e.g., see section 4.4 of
[
Ledoux and Talagrand, 1991
]
and Definition 2 of
[
Yiming and Colin, 2010
]
), over the set of kernels
\mathcal{K}
. By Theorem 3 of
[
Yiming and Colin, 2010
]
, this can be upper bounded by the psuedo-dimension
d_{\mathcal{K}}
of the set of kernels
\mathcal{K}
:

\mathbb{E}_{\xi,\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Bigg[\frac{1}{\tfrac{1}{2}\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\sup\limits_{k\in\mathcal{K}}\sum_{q<r}^{\tfrac{1}{2}\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\xi_{j,q}\xi_{j,r}k(X_{j,q}^{\textup{\text{tr}}},X_{j,r}^{\textup{\text{tr}}})\Bigg]\leq(96e+1)\kappa^{2}d_{\mathcal{K}}.

This implies that:

\displaystyle\implies\mathcal{R}_{j}(\lambda_{y},\mathcal{H}_{y})

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\tfrac{1}{2}\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\frac{2}{L\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}\sqrt{\tfrac{1}{2}\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}\kappa^{2}+\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}(96e+1)\kappa^{2}d_{\mathcal{K}}}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\tfrac{1}{2}\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\frac{2}{L\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}\sqrt{\big(\tfrac{\kappa^{2}}{2}+(96e+1)\kappa^{2}d_{\mathcal{K}}\big)\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}

\displaystyle=\frac{4}{L\sqrt{\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}\lambda_{y}}}\sqrt{\tfrac{\kappa^{2}}{2}+(96e+1)\kappa^{2}d_{\mathcal{K}}}

\displaystyle=O\Bigg(\sqrt{\frac{1+d_{\mathcal{K}}}{\lambda_{y}n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}}\Bigg).

Combining this result with our work earlier in the proof, it follows that:

\displaystyle\implies\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}[\mathcal{U}_{y}(\lambda_{y})]

\displaystyle=O\Bigg(\frac{1}{\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}\sum_{j=0}^{m}\mathcal{R}_{j}(\lambda_{y},\mathcal{H}_{y})\Bigg)

\displaystyle=O\Bigg(\frac{1}{\sqrt{\lambda_{y}}}\sqrt{\frac{1+d_{\mathcal{K}}}{\lambda_{y}n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}}\Bigg)

\displaystyle=O\Bigg(\frac{1}{\lambda_{y}}\sqrt{\frac{1+d_{\mathcal{K}}}{n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}}\Bigg),

as claimed!

∎

Proof of Corollary (
6.15.3
)
.

We’ll begin by demonstrating that Assumption (
A3
) holds in the setting specified by this Corollary. By Corollary (
6.15.2
), we know that, for each
y\in[m]
:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\big[\mathcal{U}_{y}(\lambda_{y})\big]

\displaystyle=O\Bigg(\frac{1}{\lambda_{y}}\sqrt{\frac{1+d_{\mathcal{K}}}{n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}}\Bigg)

\displaystyle=O\Bigg(\bigg(\frac{n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}{1+d_{\mathcal{K}}}\bigg)^{\frac{1}{4}}\sqrt{\frac{1+d_{\mathcal{K}}}{n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}}\max_{j}\Omega(s_{\gamma^{\ast},j})\Bigg)

\displaystyle=O\Bigg(\bigg(\frac{1+d_{\mathcal{K}}}{n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\bigg)^{\frac{1}{4}}\max_{j}\Omega(s_{\gamma^{\ast},j})\Bigg),

where the second line is because
\lambda_{y}\asymp\frac{1}{\max_{j}\Omega(s_{\gamma^{\ast},j})}\big(\frac{1+d_{\mathcal{K}}}{n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\big)^{\frac{1}{4}}
for each
j\in[m]
. Now, it follows from the assumption of

\max_{j}\Omega(s_{\gamma^{\ast},j})=o\bigg(\frac{(n^{\textup{\text{tr}}})^{\frac{1}{4}}}{\max\big\{\sqrt{\log(n^{\textup{\text{tr}}})},(1+d_{\mathcal{K}})^{1/4}\big\}}\bigg)
that
\max_{j}\Omega(s_{\gamma^{\ast},j})=o\bigg(\frac{(n^{\textup{\text{tr}}})^{\frac{1}{4}}}{(1+d_{\mathcal{K}})^{1/4}}\bigg)
, i.e.,
\frac{(1+d_{\mathcal{K}})^{1/4}}{(n^{\textup{\text{tr}}})^{\frac{1}{4}}}\max_{j}\Omega(s_{\gamma^{\ast},j})=o(1)
. Thus, the above display implies that
\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\big[\mathcal{U}_{y}(\lambda_{y})\big]=o(1)
.

Next, notice that for all
j\in[m]
:

\displaystyle\sqrt{\Lambda}

\displaystyle\leq\textup{\text{Var}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},j}]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2||s_{\gamma^{\ast},j}||_{\infty}}{4}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\kappa\Omega(s_{\gamma^{\ast},j})}{2},

where the first line follows from Assumption (
A6
), and the second line is by Popoviciu’s variance inequality. Thus, it follows that:

\displaystyle\lambda_{y}

\displaystyle=O\Bigg(\frac{1}{\max_{j}\Omega(s_{\gamma^{\ast},j})}\bigg(\frac{1+d_{\mathcal{K}}}{n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\bigg)^{\frac{1}{4}}\Bigg)

\displaystyle=O\Bigg(\bigg(\frac{1+d_{\mathcal{K}}}{n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\bigg)^{\frac{1}{4}}\Bigg),

which due to the Assumption of
\frac{1+d_{\mathcal{K}}}{n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}=o(1)
, implies that
\lambda_{y}=o(1)
. Next, observe that

\displaystyle\max_{y}\lambda_{y}\Omega^{2}(s_{\gamma^{\ast},y})

\displaystyle=O\Bigg(\max_{j}\Omega(s_{\gamma^{\ast},j})\bigg(\frac{1+d_{\mathcal{K}}}{n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\bigg)^{\frac{1}{4}}\Bigg),

so by the same argument used to show that
\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\big[\mathcal{U}_{y}(\lambda_{y})\big]=o(1)
, it follows that
\max_{y}\lambda_{y}\Omega^{2}(s_{\gamma^{\ast},y})=o(1)
as well. Lastly, observe that:

\displaystyle(\min_{y}\lambda_{y})^{2}n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}

\displaystyle\asymp\frac{1}{\max_{j}\Omega(s_{\gamma^{\ast},j})^{2}}\bigg(\frac{1+d_{\mathcal{K}}}{n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\bigg)^{\frac{1}{2}}n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\max_{j}\Omega(s_{\gamma^{\ast},j})^{2}}\sqrt{(1+d_{\mathcal{K}})n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}

\displaystyle=\bigg(\frac{((1+d_{\mathcal{K}})n^{\textup{\text{tr}}})^{\frac{1}{4}}}{\max_{j}\Omega(s_{\gamma^{\ast},j})}\bigg)^{2}

\displaystyle\geq\bigg(\frac{(n^{\textup{\text{tr}}})^{\frac{1}{4}}}{\max_{j}\Omega(s_{\gamma^{\ast},j})}\bigg)^{2}.

Since we already showed that
\max_{j}\Omega(s_{\gamma^{\ast},j})\big(\frac{1+d_{\mathcal{K}}}{n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\big)^{\frac{1}{4}}=o(1)
, it must also be true that
\frac{\max_{j}\Omega(s_{\gamma^{\ast},j})}{(n^{\textup{\text{tr}}})^{\frac{1}{4}}}=o(1)
, meaning that
\frac{(n^{\textup{\text{tr}}})^{\frac{1}{4}}}{\max_{j}\Omega(s_{\gamma^{\ast},j})}\to\infty
. Thus, it follows that
(\min_{y}\lambda_{y})^{2}n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}\to\infty
, and so Assumption (
A3
) holds true!

Now, this present Corollary already assumes that Assumptions (
A4
), (
A5
), (
A6
) and (
A7
) are true, and Assumptions (
A1
) and (
•
‣
A2
) are already true by virtue of Corollary (
6.15.2
). So, since we just proved that Assumption (
A3
) also holds, we can now invoke Theorem (
3.4
). Namely, we have that:

\displaystyle\Bigg(\Bigg[\frac{1}{n^{\textup{\text{te}}}}+\frac{1}{n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\sum_{y=0}^{m}\frac{(\pi_{y}^{\ast})^{2}}{\pi_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\Bigg]\Big(\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})^{-1}-\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast};\textup{Cat})^{-1}\Big)+\frac{1}{n^{\textup{\text{te}}}}\mathcal{I}(\pi^{\ast};\textup{Cat})^{-1}\Bigg)^{-\frac{1}{2}}(\widehat{\pi}-\pi^{\ast})=Z+\epsilon,

where
Z\xrightarrow{\hskip 4.09723ptd\hskip 4.09723pt}\mathcal{N}(0,I_{m})
, and
\epsilon\in\mathbb{R}^{m}
is a random vector satisfying

\displaystyle||\epsilon||_{2}

\displaystyle=O_{\mathbb{P}}\Bigg(\sqrt{\frac{1}{\sqrt{n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}}+\max_{y}\lambda_{y}\Omega(s_{\gamma^{\ast},y})^{2}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{\min_{y}\lambda_{y}}}e^{-Cn^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}+\max_{y}\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Big[\mathcal{U}_{y}(\lambda_{y})\Big]+\sqrt{\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{II}}}}\Big[||\widehat{\gamma}-\gamma^{\ast}||_{2}^{2}\Big]}}\Bigg)

\displaystyle\qquad+O_{\mathbb{P}}\Bigg(\frac{1}{\sqrt{\frac{1}{n^{\textup{\text{te}}}}+\frac{1}{n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}}}\bigg\{e^{-C(\min_{y}\lambda_{y})^{2}n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}+\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{II}}}}\Big[\big|\big|\widehat{\gamma}-\gamma^{\ast}\big|\big|_{2}\geq C\Big]\bigg\}\Bigg),

(41)

where
C>0
is a global constant. The identity for
Z
presented in the statement of the present Corollary follows from Theorem (
3.4
). Now, it remains for us to show that, in fact,

\displaystyle||\epsilon||_{2}

\displaystyle=O_{\mathbb{P}}\Bigg(\frac{1}{(n^{\textup{\text{tr}}})^{1/4}}+\bigg(\frac{1+d_{\mathcal{K}}}{n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\bigg)^{\tfrac{1}{8}}\sqrt{\max_{y}\Omega(s_{\gamma^{\ast},y})}+\Big(\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{II}}}}\Big[||\widehat{\gamma}-\gamma^{\ast}||_{2}^{2}\Big]\Big)^{\frac{1}{4}}+\frac{\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{II}}}}\big[||\widehat{\gamma}-\gamma^{\ast}||_{2}\geq C\big]}{\sqrt{\tfrac{1}{n^{\textup{\text{te}}}}+\tfrac{1}{n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}}}\Bigg),

and furthermore, that the RHS above is
O_{\mathbb{P}}(1)
. The latter task is quite simple: the second term is
o(1)
by the previous work done in the proof of this Corollary, and the third and fourth terms are
o(1)
due to Assumption (
A7
).

Thus, all that remains is for us to show that the display above is true. Towards that end, note that we have already shown that

\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\big[\mathcal{U}_{y}(\lambda_{y})\big],\text{ }\max_{y}\lambda_{y}\Omega^{2}(s_{\gamma^{\ast},y})=O\Bigg(\bigg(\frac{1+d_{\mathcal{K}}}{n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\bigg)^{\frac{1}{4}}\max_{j}\Omega(s_{\gamma^{\ast},j})\Bigg),

so it follows from line (
41
) that

\displaystyle||\epsilon||_{2}

\displaystyle=O_{\mathbb{P}}\Bigg(\sqrt{\frac{1}{\sqrt{n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{\min_{y}\lambda_{y}}}e^{-Cn^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}+\bigg(\frac{1+d_{\mathcal{K}}}{n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\bigg)^{\frac{1}{4}}\max_{j}\Omega(s_{\gamma^{\ast},j})+\sqrt{\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{II}}}}\Big[||\widehat{\gamma}-\gamma^{\ast}||_{2}^{2}\Big]}}\Bigg)

\displaystyle\qquad+O_{\mathbb{P}}\Bigg(\frac{1}{\sqrt{\frac{1}{n^{\textup{\text{te}}}}+\frac{1}{n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}}}\bigg\{e^{-C(\min_{y}\lambda_{y})^{2}n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}+\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{II}}}}\Big[\big|\big|\widehat{\gamma}-\gamma^{\ast}\big|\big|_{2}\geq C\Big]\bigg\}\Bigg).

(42)

Next, we will show that
\frac{1}{\sqrt{\min_{y}\lambda_{y}}}e^{-Cn^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}=o\Bigg(\bigg(\frac{1+d_{\mathcal{K}}}{n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\bigg)^{\frac{1}{4}}\max_{j}\Omega(s_{\gamma^{\ast},j})\Bigg)
. Towards that end, observe that:

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{\min_{y}\lambda_{y}}}e^{-Cn^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}

\displaystyle=O\Bigg(\sqrt{\max_{j}\Omega(s_{\gamma^{\ast},j})}\bigg(\frac{n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}{1+d_{\mathcal{K}}}\bigg)^{\frac{1}{8}}e^{-Cn^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\Bigg)

\displaystyle=O\Bigg(\sqrt{\max_{j}\Omega(s_{\gamma^{\ast},j})}\big(n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}\big)^{\frac{1}{8}}e^{-Cn^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\Bigg)

\displaystyle=O\Bigg(\sqrt{\frac{\max_{j}\Omega(s_{\gamma^{\ast},j})}{\big(n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}\big)^{\frac{1}{4}}}\cdot\big(n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}\big)^{\frac{1}{2}}e^{-2Cn^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}}\Bigg)

\displaystyle=O\Bigg(\sqrt{\bigg(\frac{1+d_{\mathcal{K}}}{n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\bigg)^{\frac{1}{4}}\max_{j}\Omega(s_{\gamma^{\ast},j})\cdot\big(n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}\big)^{\frac{1}{2}}e^{-2Cn^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}}\Bigg)

\displaystyle=o\Bigg(\bigg(\frac{1+d_{\mathcal{K}}}{n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\bigg)^{\frac{1}{4}}\max_{j}\Omega(s_{\gamma^{\ast},j})\Bigg),

where the last line is based on the presumption that
\sqrt{n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}e^{-2Cn^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}=o\Big(\big(\frac{1+d_{\mathcal{K}}}{n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\big)^{\frac{1}{4}}\max_{j}\Omega(s_{\gamma^{\ast},j})\Big)
. We will now verify this presumption. Since
\frac{2}{\kappa}\sqrt{\Lambda}\leq\Omega(s_{\gamma^{\ast},j})
for all
j\in[m]
, we have that:

\displaystyle\sqrt{n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}e^{-2Cn^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}=o\Bigg(\bigg(\frac{1+d_{\mathcal{K}}}{n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\bigg)^{\frac{1}{4}}\max_{j}\Omega(s_{\gamma^{\ast},j})\Bigg)

\displaystyle\Longleftarrow\sqrt{n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}e^{-2Cn^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}=o\Bigg(\bigg(\frac{1+d_{\mathcal{K}}}{n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\bigg)^{\frac{1}{4}}\Bigg)

\displaystyle\iff(n^{\textup{\text{tr}}})^{\frac{3}{4}}e^{-2Cn^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}=o\Big((1+d_{\mathcal{K}})^{\frac{1}{4}}\Big)

\displaystyle\iff(n^{\textup{\text{tr}}})^{3}e^{-8Cn^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}=o\Big(1+d_{\mathcal{K}}\Big)

\displaystyle\Longleftarrow(n^{\textup{\text{tr}}})^{3}e^{-8Cn^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}=o(1),

and the last line above is clearly true, meaning that it is indeed the case that
\sqrt{n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}e^{-2Cn^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}=o\Big(\big(\frac{1+d_{\mathcal{K}}}{n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\big)^{\frac{1}{4}}\max_{j}\Omega(s_{\gamma^{\ast},j})\Big)
. Thus, we have proved that:

\frac{1}{\sqrt{\min_{y}\lambda_{y}}}e^{-Cn^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}=o\Bigg(\bigg(\frac{1+d_{\mathcal{K}}}{n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\bigg)^{\frac{1}{4}}\max_{j}\Omega(s_{\gamma^{\ast},j})\Bigg),

so it follows from line (
42
) that:

\displaystyle||\epsilon||_{2}

\displaystyle=O_{\mathbb{P}}\Bigg(\sqrt{\frac{1}{\sqrt{n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}}+\bigg(\frac{1+d_{\mathcal{K}}}{n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\bigg)^{\frac{1}{4}}\max_{j}\Omega(s_{\gamma^{\ast},j})+\sqrt{\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{II}}}}\Big[||\widehat{\gamma}-\gamma^{\ast}||_{2}^{2}\Big]}}\Bigg)

\displaystyle\qquad+O_{\mathbb{P}}\Bigg(\frac{1}{\sqrt{\frac{1}{n^{\textup{\text{te}}}}+\frac{1}{n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}}}\bigg\{e^{-C(\min_{y}\lambda_{y})^{2}n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}+\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{II}}}}\Big[\big|\big|\widehat{\gamma}-\gamma^{\ast}\big|\big|_{2}\geq C\Big]\bigg\}\Bigg).

(43)

Finally, we will show that

\frac{e^{-C(\min_{y}\lambda_{y})^{2}n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}}{\sqrt{\frac{1}{n^{\textup{\text{te}}}}+\frac{1}{n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}}}=o\Bigg(\bigg(\frac{1+d_{\mathcal{K}}}{n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\bigg)^{\frac{1}{8}}\sqrt{\max_{j}\Omega(s_{\gamma^{\ast},j})}\Bigg).

Towards that end, note that the requirement that
\lambda_{y}\asymp\frac{1}{\max_{j}\Omega(s_{\gamma^{\ast},j})}\big(\frac{1+d_{\mathcal{K}}}{n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\big)^{\frac{1}{4}}
implies that there exists a global constant
B>0
such that
\lambda_{y}\geq B\frac{1}{\max_{j}\Omega(s_{\gamma^{\ast},j})}\big(\frac{1+d_{\mathcal{K}}}{n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\big)^{\frac{1}{4}}
. Thus, we have that:

\displaystyle\frac{e^{-C(\min_{y}\lambda_{y})^{2}n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}}{\sqrt{\frac{1}{n^{\textup{\text{te}}}}+\frac{1}{n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}}}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}e^{-C(\min_{y}\lambda_{y})^{2}n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\exp\Bigg\{-CB^{2}\frac{1}{\max_{j}\Omega(s_{\gamma^{\ast},j})^{2}}\bigg(\frac{1+d_{\mathcal{K}}}{n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\bigg)^{\frac{1}{2}}n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}\Bigg\}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\exp\Bigg\{-CB^{2}\frac{1}{\max_{j}\Omega(s_{\gamma^{\ast},j})^{2}}\sqrt{n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\Bigg\}

Now, observe that

\sqrt{n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\exp\Bigg\{-CB^{2}\frac{1}{\max_{j}\Omega(s_{\gamma^{\ast},j})^{2}}\sqrt{n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\Bigg\}=o\Bigg(\bigg(\frac{1+d_{\mathcal{K}}}{n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\bigg)^{\frac{1}{8}}\sqrt{\max_{j}\Omega(s_{\gamma^{\ast},j})}\Bigg)

\Longleftarrow\sqrt{n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\exp\Bigg\{-CB^{2}\frac{1}{\max_{j}\Omega(s_{\gamma^{\ast},j})^{2}}\sqrt{n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\Bigg\}=o\Bigg(\bigg(\frac{1}{n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\bigg)^{\frac{1}{8}}\sqrt{\max_{j}\Omega(s_{\gamma^{\ast},j})}\Bigg)

\iff(n^{\textup{\text{tr}}})^{\frac{5}{8}}\exp\Bigg\{-CB^{2}\frac{1}{\max_{j}\Omega(s_{\gamma^{\ast},j})^{2}}\sqrt{n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\Bigg\}=o\Bigg(\sqrt{\max_{j}\Omega(s_{\gamma^{\ast},j})}\Bigg).

\Longleftarrow(n^{\textup{\text{tr}}})^{\frac{5}{8}}\exp\Bigg\{-CB^{2}\frac{1}{\max_{j}\Omega(s_{\gamma^{\ast},j})^{2}}\sqrt{n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\Bigg\}=o(1)

\iff\exp\Bigg\{-CB^{2}\frac{1}{\max_{j}\Omega(s_{\gamma^{\ast},j})^{2}}\sqrt{n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}+\frac{5}{8}\log(n^{\textup{\text{tr}}})\Bigg\}=o(1)

\Longleftarrow\frac{1}{\max_{j}\Omega(s_{\gamma^{\ast},j})^{2}}\sqrt{n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}-\log(n^{\textup{\text{tr}}})\to\infty

\iff\log(n^{\textup{\text{tr}}})\Bigg[\frac{\sqrt{n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}}{\max_{j}\Omega(s_{\gamma^{\ast},j})^{2}}\frac{1}{\log(n^{\textup{\text{tr}}})}-1\Bigg]\to\infty

\Longleftarrow\frac{\max_{j}\Omega(s_{\gamma^{\ast},j})^{2}}{\sqrt{n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}}\log(n^{\textup{\text{tr}}})=o(1)

\iff\max_{j}\Omega(s_{\gamma^{\ast},j})^{2}=o\bigg(\frac{\sqrt{n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}}{\log(n^{\textup{\text{tr}}})}\bigg)

\iff\max_{j}\Omega(s_{\gamma^{\ast},j})=o\bigg(\frac{(n^{\textup{\text{tr}}})^{\frac{1}{4}}}{\sqrt{\log(n^{\textup{\text{tr}}})}}\bigg)

\iff\max_{j}\Omega(s_{\gamma^{\ast},j})=o\bigg(\frac{(n^{\textup{\text{tr}}})^{\frac{1}{4}}}{\max\{\sqrt{\log(n^{\textup{\text{tr}}})},(1+d_{\mathcal{K}})^{1/4}}\bigg)

Thus, the assumption that
\max_{j}\Omega(s_{\gamma^{\ast},j})=o\Big(\frac{(n^{\textup{\text{tr}}})^{\frac{1}{4}}}{\max\{\sqrt{\log(n^{\textup{\text{tr}}})},(1+d_{\mathcal{K}})^{1/4}\}}\Big)
implies that

\frac{e^{-C(\min_{y}\lambda_{y})^{2}n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}}{\sqrt{\frac{1}{n^{\textup{\text{te}}}}+\frac{1}{n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}}}=o\Bigg(\bigg(\frac{1+d_{\mathcal{K}}}{n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\bigg)^{\frac{1}{8}}\sqrt{\max_{j}\Omega(s_{\gamma^{\ast},j})}\Bigg).

Thus, it follows from line (
43
) that:

\displaystyle||\epsilon||_{2}

\displaystyle=O_{\mathbb{P}}\Bigg(\frac{1}{(n^{\textup{\text{tr}}})^{\frac{1}{4}}}+\bigg(\frac{1+d_{\mathcal{K}}}{n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\bigg)^{\frac{1}{8}}\sqrt{\max_{j}\Omega(s_{\gamma^{\ast},j})}+\Big(\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{II}}}}\Big[||\widehat{\gamma}-\gamma^{\ast}||_{2}^{2}\Big]\Big)^{\frac{1}{4}}+\frac{\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{II}}}}\big[||\widehat{\gamma}-\gamma^{\ast}||_{2}\geq C\big]}{\sqrt{\frac{1}{n^{\textup{\text{te}}}}+\frac{1}{n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}}}\Bigg)

\displaystyle=O_{\mathbb{P}}\Bigg(\bigg(\frac{1+d_{\mathcal{K}}}{n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\bigg)^{\frac{1}{8}}\sqrt{\max_{j}\Omega(s_{\gamma^{\ast},j})}+\Big(\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{II}}}}\Big[||\widehat{\gamma}-\gamma^{\ast}||_{2}^{2}\Big]\Big)^{\frac{1}{4}}+\frac{\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{II}}}}\big[||\widehat{\gamma}-\gamma^{\ast}||_{2}\geq C\big]}{\sqrt{\frac{1}{n^{\textup{\text{te}}}}+\frac{1}{n^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}}}\Bigg),

where the second line is because
d_{\mathcal{K}}\geq 0
and
\max_{j}\Omega(s_{\gamma^{\ast},j})
can be lower bounded by a fixed constant.

∎

6.3
Fixed
\tau
-IID Regime

6.3.1
Assumptions

These are Assumptions (
B1
), (
B2
), (
B3
), (
4
) and (
5
), which are presented in the Theoretical Results section of the paper. Note that these Assumptions are stated in terms of the more general
\tau_{n}
-IID Regime, but can be used for the Fixed
\tau
-IID Regime by substituting
\tau
for
\tau_{n}
.

6.3.2
Definition of
\tau
-RAL Estimator

Now, let
\mathbf{M}^{\text{sub},\tau}=\Big\{\mathcal{J}^{\pi,\rho,\tau}\ \Big\lvert\ (\pi,\rho)\in\Theta^{\text{sub}}\Big\}\subset\textbf{M}^{\text{semi},\tau}
denote a parametric submodel of
\textbf{M}^{\text{semi},\tau}
parameterized by
(\pi,\rho)\in\Theta^{\text{sub}}
, where
\Theta^{\text{sub}}=\Delta\times\mathcal{R}
and
\mathcal{R}
is an open set of finite dimensional vectors. Let
(\pi^{\ast},\rho^{\ast})
