be the true parameter for the submodel, so that
\mathcal{J}^{\pi^{\ast},\rho^{\ast},\tau}=\mathcal{J}^{\pi^{\ast},\mathbf{p},\tau}
.
\mathbf{M}^{\text{sub},\tau}
is called a
regular parametric submodel
if
\sqrt{\mathcal{J}^{\pi,\rho,\tau}}
satisfies the mean-square differentiability conditions in Appendix A of
Newey, 1990
for all
(\pi,\rho)\in\Theta^{\text{sub}}
.

Next, let
\{(\pi_{n},\rho_{n})\}\subset\Theta^{\text{sub}}
be any sequence such that
\sqrt{n}\big|\big|\left(\begin{smallmatrix}\pi_{n}\\
\rho_{n}\end{smallmatrix}\right)-\left(\begin{smallmatrix}\pi^{\ast}\\
\rho^{\ast}\end{smallmatrix}\right)\big|\big|_{2}
is bounded. Let the corresponding local data generating process,
\text{LDGP}(\{(\pi_{n},\rho_{n},\tau)\})
, be one where
Z_{1},\dots,Z_{n}\overset{\text{iid}}{\sim}\mathcal{J}^{\pi_{n},\rho_{n},\tau}
for each value of
n
. An estimator
\widetilde{\pi}_{n}
is called
“
regular
w.r.t
\mathbf{M}^{\text{sub},\tau}
in the fixed
\tau
IID regime”
if the limiting distribution of
\sqrt{n}(\widetilde{\pi}_{n}-\pi_{n})
is the same for all sequences
\{(\pi_{n},\rho_{n})\}
satisfying the aforementioned property. The estimator
\widetilde{\pi}_{n}
is called
“regular w.r.t
\mathbf{M}^{\text{semi},\tau}
in the fixed
\tau
-IID regime”
if it is regular w.r.t all regular parametric submodels in the fixed
\tau
-IID regime.

Finally,
\widetilde{\pi}_{n}
is
asymptotically linear
if there exists
\mathbb{R}^{m}
-valued functions
\psi
and
\delta
such that:

•

\sqrt{n}(\widetilde{\pi}_{n}-\pi^{\ast})=\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\psi(Z_{i})+\delta(Z_{1:n})

•

\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{J}^{\pi^{\ast},\mathbf{p},\tau}}[\psi(Z)]=0
and
\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{J}^{\pi^{\ast},\mathbf{p},\tau}}[\psi(Z)\psi(Z)^{\prime}]
is finite and nonsingular

•

\delta(Z_{1:n})\xrightarrow{\hskip 4.09723ptp\hskip 4.09723pt}0
.

An estimator that is both regular and asymptotically linear w.r.t
\mathbf{M}^{\text{semi},\tau}
in the fixed-
\tau
IID is called
\mathbf{\tau}
-RAL
.

6.3.3
Lemmas

It is important to note that there are several notational discrepancies between the Fixed
\tau
-IID Regime and the previous Fixed Sequence Regime:

1.

In the Fixed Sequence Regime,
\pi_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}
was the fraction of the training data that belonged to class
y
. In the Fixed
\tau
-IID Regime,
\pi_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}
is the probability of a training data point belonging to class
y
; the actual fraction of the training data that belongs to class
y
is random, and is denoted by
\widehat{\pi}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}
.

2.

In the Fixed Sequence Regime,
\gamma^{\ast}
was a non-random function that depended on the number of test/train samples, the fraction of the train samples that belong to each class, and
\pi^{\ast}
. In the Fixed
\tau
-IID Regime, all the aforementioned quantities (except for
\pi^{\ast}
) are now random, and so
\gamma^{\ast}
is a random variable (defined in line (
61
)). The non-random counterpart to
\gamma^{\ast}
is
\gamma^{\ast}(\tau)
(defined in Lemma (
6.17
)), which has all the random parts of
\gamma^{\ast}
replaced by their expected values. Additionally, in the remainder of this manuscript, whenever we work with
\gamma^{\ast}
, we do not have to worry about any issues arising from division by
0
; this is because the only time that
\gamma^{\ast}
appears in our analysis is in the context of the estimation procedure after step
2
, which we only reach when
N^{\textup{\text{te}}}
,
N^{\textup{\text{tr}}}
and
\widehat{\pi}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}
are all nonzero (see step 1 of the procedure).

3.

In the Fixed Sequence Regime, the score function for the mixture at a probability parameter
\beta
was denoted by
s_{\beta}
. In the Fixed
\tau
-IID Regime, we use
S_{\pi,\rho}
to refer to the score function for a given parametric submodel that parameterizes the class densities by a vector
\rho
. When the score function is for only one of
\pi
or
\rho
, then we write
S_{\pi}
or
S_{\rho}
, respectively.

Lemma 6.16

(
Smooth Score Function for
\pi
)
.

The density
\mathcal{J}^{\pi,\mathbf{p},\tau}
is smooth with respect to
\pi\in\Delta
in the sense of
Newey, 1990
, with score function
S_{\pi}
for the parameter of interest equal to

\displaystyle S_{\pi}(z)

\displaystyle=ds_{\pi}(x)\mathbbold{1}{\{p_{\pi}(x)>0\}},

which, in turn, is equal to
\frac{\delta}{\delta{\pi}}\Big\{\log\mathcal{J}^{\pi,\mathbf{p},\tau}(z)\Big\}
a.e.
[\mathcal{J}^{\pi,\mathbf{p},\tau}]
.

Lemma 6.17

(
A Particular Smooth Parametric Submodel
)
.

Let
\Theta^{\textup{sub}}=\Delta\times\mathcal{R}
where

\mathcal{R}:=\Big\{\rho\in\mathbb{R}^{m}\ \Big\lvert\ ||\rho||_{2}<B\Big\}

and
B
is the constant given in Assumption (
B3
). For each
j\in\mathcal{Y}
and
\rho\in\mathcal{R}
, define

q_{j}^{\rho}(x):=p_{j}(x)e^{\rho^{\prime}\frac{\pi_{j}^{\ast}}{\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau)}(x)-c_{j}(\rho)}

where

\displaystyle\gamma_{j}^{\ast}(\tau):=\frac{\frac{\pi_{j}^{\ast}}{\tau}+\frac{1}{1-\tau}\frac{(\pi_{j}^{\ast})^{2}}{\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}}{\frac{1}{\tau}+\frac{1}{1-\tau}\sum\limits_{k=0}^{m}\frac{(\pi_{k}^{\ast})^{2}}{\pi_{k}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}},

and let

\mathcal{J}^{\pi,\rho,\tau}(z)=\big(\tau q_{\pi}^{\rho}(x)\big)^{d}\Bigg((1-\tau)\prod_{j=0}^{m}\big(\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}q_{j}^{\rho}(x)\big)^{\mathbbold{1}{\{y=j\}}}\Bigg)^{1-d}\mathbbold{1}{\big\{x\in\mathcal{X},\text{ }y\in\mathcal{Y},\text{ }d\in\{0,1\}\big\}}.

Then, under Assumption (
B3
), the set

\mathbf{M}^{\textup{sub},\tau}=\Big\{\mathcal{J}^{\pi,\rho,\tau}\ \Big\lvert\ (\pi,\rho)\in\Theta^{\textup{sub}}\Big\}

is a smooth (in the sense of
Newey, 1990
) parametric submodel of
\mathbf{M}^{\textup{semi},\tau}
, with score function
S_{\pi,\rho}^{\textup{sub},\tau}
for both the parameter of interest and nuisance parameter equal to

S_{\pi,\rho}^{\textup{sub},\tau}(z)=\begin{pmatrix}ds_{\pi}^{\rho}(x)\mathbbold{1}{\big\{q_{\pi}^{\rho}(x)>0\big\}}\\
\vartheta_{\pi,\rho}^{\textup{sub},\tau,1}(z)+\vartheta_{\pi,\rho}^{\textup{sub},\tau,2}(z)\end{pmatrix},

where

\vartheta_{\pi,\rho}^{\textup{sub},\tau,1}(z):=d\sum\limits_{j=0}^{m}\pi_{j}\frac{\pi_{j}^{\ast}}{\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\frac{q_{j}^{\rho}(x)}{q_{\pi}^{\rho}(x)}(s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau)}(x)-\mathbb{E}_{j}^{\rho}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau)}])\mathbbold{1}{\big\{q_{j}^{\rho}(x)>0\big\}}

and

\vartheta_{\pi,\rho}^{\textup{sub},\tau,2}(z):=(1-d)\sum\limits_{j=0}^{m}\mathbbold{1}{\{y=j\}}\frac{\pi_{j}^{\ast}}{\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\big(s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau)}(x)-\mathbb{E}_{j}^{\rho}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau)}]\big)\mathbbold{1}{\big\{q_{j}^{\rho}(x)>0\big\}}.

Lemma 6.18

(
Semiparametric Nuisance Tangent Set is a Subset of
\mathcal{G}
)
.

The semiparametric nuisance tangent set
\mathcal{T}
defined in
Newey, 1990
is a subset of
\mathcal{G}
, where
\mathcal{G}
is given by

\displaystyle\mathcal{G}

\displaystyle:=\Bigg\{g\text{ }\bigg|\text{ }\forall j\in\mathcal{Y}\text{ }f_{j}:\mathcal{X}\mapsto\mathbb{R}^{m},\text{ }\mathbb{E}_{j}[f_{j}]=0,\text{ }\mathbb{E}_{j}[||f_{j}||_{2}^{2}]<\infty,

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad g(z)=d\sum_{j=0}^{m}\pi_{j}^{\ast}\frac{p_{j}(x)}{p_{\pi^{\ast}}(x)}f_{j}(x)+(1-d)\sum_{j=0}^{m}\mathbbold{1}{\{y=j\}}f_{j}(x)\quad\textup{ a.s. }\big[\mathcal{J}^{\pi^{\ast},\mathbf{p},\tau}\big]\Bigg\}.

Lemma 6.19

(
Projection of
S_{\pi^{\ast}}
onto
\mathcal{G}
)
.

Define:

\displaystyle\mathbf{V}^{\textup{eff}}(\tau):=\bigg[\frac{1}{\tau}+\frac{1}{1-\tau}\sum_{k=0}^{m}\frac{(\pi_{k}^{\ast})^{2}}{\pi_{k}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\bigg]\Big(\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast}(\tau))^{-1}-\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast}(\tau);\textup{Cat})^{-1}\Big)+\frac{1}{\tau}\mathcal{I}(\pi^{\ast};\textup{Cat})^{-1}

\displaystyle\psi_{\tau}^{\textup{eff}}(z)

\displaystyle:=\frac{d}{\tau}\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast}(\tau))^{-1}\big(s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau)}(x)-\mathbb{E}_{\pi^{\ast}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau)}]\big)

\displaystyle\qquad-\frac{1-d}{1-\tau}\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast}(\tau))^{-1}\sum_{j=0}^{m}\mathbbold{1}{\{y=j\}}\frac{\pi_{j}^{\ast}}{\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\big(s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau)}(x)-\mathbb{E}_{j}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau)}]\big).

Then, under Assumption (
B1
), we have that:

\frac{(\xi)^{2}\nu}{\tau(1-\tau)}\bigg(\frac{L}{m+1}\bigg)^{2}\leq\lambda_{\textup{min}}\big(\mathbf{V}^{\textup{eff}}(\tau)\big)\leq\lambda_{\textup{max}}\big(\mathbf{V}^{\textup{eff}}(\tau)\big)\leq\bigg[\frac{1}{\tau}+\frac{1}{(1-\tau)\xi}\bigg]\frac{1}{\sqrt{\Lambda}}+\frac{1}{2\tau}.

Further, under Assumptions (
B1
) and (
B2
), we have that

ds_{\pi^{\ast}}-\mathbf{V}^{\textup{eff}}(\tau)^{-1}\psi_{\tau}^{\textup{eff}}\in\mathcal{G}

and

\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{J}^{\pi^{\ast},\mathbf{p},\tau}}\Big[g^{\prime}\big(S_{\pi^{\ast}}-\big(Ds_{\pi^{\ast}}-\mathbf{V}^{\textup{eff}}(\tau)^{-1}\psi_{\tau}^{\textup{eff}}\big)\big)\Big]=0\qquad\forall\ g\in\mathcal{G}.

Lemma 6.20

(
Largest CRLB
)
.

Let
\mathbf{M}^{\textup{sub},\tau}
denote the smooth parametric submodel defined in Lemma (
6.17
), and let
\mathbf{M}^{\widetilde{\textup{sub}},\tau}
denote an arbitrary smooth parametric submodel. Then, under Assumptions (
B1
), (
B2
) and (
B3
), we have that

\textup{CRLB}\big(\mathbf{M}^{\widetilde{\textup{sub}},\tau}\big)\preceq\textup{CRLB}\big(\mathbf{M}^{\textup{sub},\tau}\big)=\mathbf{V}^{\textup{eff}}(\tau),

where
\textup{CRLB}\big(\mathbf{M}^{\widetilde{\textup{sub}},\tau}\big)
and
\textup{CRLB}\big(\mathbf{M}^{\textup{sub},\tau}\big)
denote the Cramer Rao Lower Bounds for learning only
\pi^{\ast}
in the submodels
\mathbf{M}^{\widetilde{\textup{sub}},\tau}
and
\mathbf{M}^{\textup{sub},\tau}
, respectively.

6.3.4
Proofs

Proof of Lemma (
6.16
)
.

We will show that
\mathcal{J}^{\pi,\mathbf{p},\tau}
is smooth with respect to
\pi\in\Delta
in the sense of Definition A.1 of
Newey, 1990
. This requires showing three conditions, and doing so will allow us to also prove the identities for
S_{\pi}
stated in Lemma (
6.16
).

The first condition is that
\Delta
must be an open set, which is clearly the case. The second condition is that there exists a measure
\widetilde{\mu}
dominating
\mathcal{J}^{\pi,\mathbf{p},\tau}(z)
such that
\mathcal{J}^{\pi,\mathbf{p},\tau}(z)
is continuous on
\Delta
a.s.
[\widetilde{\mu}]
. This is trivially true– while
\widetilde{\mu}
can be constructed from counting measures and
\mu
, the continuity of
\mathcal{J}^{\pi,\mathbf{p},\tau}(z)
on
\Delta
holds, in fact, for all
z
: when
d=0
,
\mathcal{J}^{\pi,\mathbf{p},\tau}(z)
does not depend on
\pi
, and when
d=1
,
\mathcal{J}^{\pi,\mathbf{p},\tau}(z)
is linear in
\pi
.

The third condition is that
\sqrt{\mathcal{J}^{\pi,\mathbf{p},\tau}(z)}
is
\widetilde{\mu}
-mean square continuously differentiable with respect to
\pi\in\Delta
with derivative
D(z,\pi)
, where

D(z,\pi):=\frac{d}{2}\sqrt{\tau p_{\pi}(x)}s_{\pi}(x).

This, in turn, requires showing three different properties. For every
\pi\in\Delta
and sequence
\pi^{(i)}\to\pi
, we must show that:

(a)

\int||D(z,\pi)||_{2}^{2}d\widetilde{\mu}(z)<\infty
.

(b)

\int||D(z,\pi^{(i)})-D(z,\pi)||_{2}^{2}d\widetilde{\mu}(z)\to 0
.

(c)

\int\big[\mathcal{J}^{\pi^{(i)},\mathbf{p},\tau}(z)^{1/2}-\mathcal{J}^{\pi,\mathbf{p},\tau}(z)^{1/2}-D(z,\pi)^{\prime}(\pi^{(i)}-\pi)\big]^{2}d\widetilde{\mu}(z)\Big/||\pi^{(i)}-\pi||_{2}^{2}\to 0
.

Let’s start with showing property (a)
. Observe that:

\displaystyle\int||D(z,\pi)||_{2}^{2}d\widetilde{\mu}(z)

\displaystyle=\frac{\tau}{4}\int dp_{\pi}(x)||s_{\pi}(x)||_{2}^{2}d\widetilde{\mu}(z)

\displaystyle=\frac{\tau}{4}\mathbb{E}_{\pi}\Big[||s_{\pi}(X)||_{2}^{2}\Big]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\tau m}{\xi^{2}}<\infty,

where the last line is because
\pi\in\Delta\implies|s_{\pi,y}(x)|\leq\frac{2}{\xi}\implies||s_{\pi}(x)||_{2}^{2}\leq\frac{4m}{\xi^{2}}
.

Next, let’s prove property (b)
. Observe that:

\displaystyle\bigg(\frac{1}{p_{\pi^{(i)}}(x)^{1/2}}-\frac{1}{p_{\pi}(x)^{1/2}}\bigg)^{2}

\displaystyle=\bigg(\frac{p_{\pi}(x)^{1/2}-p_{\pi^{(i)}}(x)^{1/2}}{p_{\pi^{(i)}}(x)^{1/2}p_{\pi}(x)^{1/2}}\bigg)^{2}

\displaystyle=\Bigg(\frac{p_{\pi}(x)-p_{\pi^{(i)}}(x)}{p_{\pi^{(i)}}(x)^{1/2}p_{\pi}(x)^{1/2}\big(p_{\pi}(x)^{1/2}+p_{\pi^{(i)}}(x)^{1/2}\big)}\Bigg)^{2}

\displaystyle=\Bigg(\frac{p_{\pi}(x)-p_{\pi^{(i)}}(x)}{p_{\pi^{(i)}}(x)^{1/2}p_{\pi}(x)+p_{\pi}(x)^{1/2}p_{\pi^{(i)}}(x)}\Bigg)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\big(p_{\pi}(x)-p_{\pi^{(i)}}(x)\big)^{2}}{p_{\pi^{(i)}}(x)p_{\pi}(x)^{2}}.

Also observe that:

\displaystyle D(z,\pi^{(i)})-D(z,\pi)

\displaystyle=\frac{d\sqrt{\tau}}{2}\Big(p_{\pi^{(i)}}(x)^{1/2}s_{\pi^{(i)}}(x)-p_{\pi}(x)^{1/2}s_{\pi}(x)\Big)

\displaystyle=\frac{d\sqrt{\tau}}{2}[p_{1}(x)-p_{0}(x),\dots,p_{m}(x)-p_{0}(x)]^{\prime}\bigg(\frac{1}{p_{\pi^{(i)}}(x)^{1/2}}-\frac{1}{p_{\pi}(x)^{1/2}}\bigg).

Thus, it follows that:

\displaystyle||D(z,\pi^{(i)})-D(z,\pi)||_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{d\tau}{4}\Big|\Big|[p_{1}(x)-p_{0}(x),\dots,p_{m}(x)-p_{0}(x)]^{\prime}\Big|\Big|_{2}^{2}\bigg(\frac{1}{p_{\pi^{(i)}}(x)^{1/2}}-\frac{1}{p_{\pi}(x)^{1/2}}\bigg)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{d\tau}{4}\Big|\Big|[p_{1}(x)-p_{0}(x),\dots,p_{m}(x)-p_{0}(x)]^{\prime}\Big|\Big|_{2}^{2}\frac{\big(p_{\pi}(x)-p_{\pi^{(i)}}(x)\big)^{2}}{p_{\pi^{(i)}}(x)p_{\pi}(x)^{2}}

\displaystyle=\frac{d\tau}{4}||s_{\pi}(x)||_{2}^{2}\frac{\big(p_{\pi}(x)-p_{\pi^{(i)}}(x)\big)^{2}}{p_{\pi^{(i)}}(x)}

\displaystyle=\frac{d\tau}{4}||s_{\pi}(x)||_{2}^{2}\frac{\big\langle(p_{1}(x)-p_{0}(x),\dots,p_{m}(x)-p_{0}(x)),\pi^{(i)}-\pi\big\rangle^{2}}{p_{\pi^{(i)}}(x)}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{d\tau}{4}||s_{\pi}(x)||_{2}^{2}\frac{||(p_{1}(x)-p_{0}(x),\dots,p_{m}(x)-p_{0}(x))||_{2}^{2}}{p_{\pi^{(i)}}(x)}||\pi^{(i)}-\pi||_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{d\tau}{4}p_{\pi^{(i)}}(x)||s_{\pi}(x)||_{2}^{2}\frac{||(p_{1}(x)-p_{0}(x),\dots,p_{m}(x)-p_{0}(x))||_{2}^{2}}{p_{\pi^{(i)}}(x)^{2}}||\pi^{(i)}-\pi||_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{d\tau}{4}p_{\pi^{(i)}}(x)||s_{\pi}(x)||_{2}^{2}||s_{\pi^{(i)}}(x)||_{2}^{2}||\pi^{(i)}-\pi||_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{d\tau}{4}\frac{16m^{2}}{\xi^{4}}p_{\pi^{(i)}}(x)||\pi^{(i)}-\pi||_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{4\tau m^{2}d}{\xi^{4}}p_{\pi^{(i)}}(x)||\pi^{(i)}-\pi||_{2}^{2}.

Thus, it follows that

\displaystyle\int||D(z,\pi^{(i)})-D(z,\pi)||_{2}^{2}d\widetilde{\mu}(z)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{4\tau m^{2}}{\xi^{4}}||\pi^{(i)}-\pi||_{2}^{2}\int dp_{\pi^{(i)}}(x)d\widetilde{\mu}(z)

\displaystyle=\frac{4\tau m^{2}}{\xi^{4}}||\pi^{(i)}-\pi||_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\to 0,

since
\pi^{(i)}\to\pi
.

Finally, let’s prove property (c)
. Note that when
d=0
,
\mathcal{J}^{\pi^{(i)},\mathbf{p},\tau}(z)-\mathcal{J}^{\pi,\mathbf{p},\tau}(z)=0
, so:

\displaystyle\mathcal{J}^{\pi^{(i)},\mathbf{p},\tau}(z)-\mathcal{J}^{\pi,\mathbf{p},\tau}(z)

\displaystyle=d(\mathcal{J}^{\pi^{(i)},\mathbf{p},\tau}(z)-\mathcal{J}^{\pi,\mathbf{p},\tau}(z))

\displaystyle=d\tau\big(p^{\pi^{(i)}}(x)-p^{\pi}(x)\big)

\displaystyle=d\tau\big\langle(p_{1}(x)-p_{0}(x),\dots,p_{m}(x)-p_{0}(x)),\pi^{(i)}-\pi\big\rangle.

So:

\displaystyle\mathcal{J}^{\pi^{(i)},\mathbf{p},\tau}(z)^{1/2}-\mathcal{J}^{\pi,\mathbf{p},\tau}(z)^{1/2}

\displaystyle=\frac{\mathcal{J}^{\pi^{(i)},\mathbf{p},\tau}(z)-\mathcal{J}^{\pi,\mathbf{p}}(z)}{\mathcal{J}^{\pi^{(i)},\mathbf{p},\tau}(z)^{1/2}+\mathcal{J}^{\pi,\mathbf{p},\tau}(z)^{1/2}}

\displaystyle=\frac{d\tau\big\langle(p_{1}(x)-p_{0}(x),\dots,p_{m}(x)-p_{0}(x)),\pi^{(i)}-\pi\big\rangle}{\mathcal{J}^{\pi^{(i)},\mathbf{p},\tau}(z)^{1/2}+\mathcal{J}^{\pi,\mathbf{p},\tau}(z)^{1/2}}

\displaystyle=\frac{d\tau\big\langle(p_{1}(x)-p_{0}(x),\dots,p_{m}(x)-p_{0}(x)),\pi^{(i)}-\pi\big\rangle}{\sqrt{\tau}p_{\pi^{(i)}}(x)^{1/2}+\sqrt{\tau}p_{\pi}(x)^{1/2}}

\displaystyle=d\sqrt{\tau}\frac{\big\langle(p_{1}(x)-p_{0}(x),\dots,p_{m}(x)-p_{0}(x)),\pi^{(i)}-\pi\big\rangle}{p_{\pi^{(i)}}(x)^{1/2}+p_{\pi}(x)^{1/2}}.

Also note that:

\displaystyle D(z,\pi)^{\prime}(\pi^{(i)}-\pi)

\displaystyle=\frac{d}{2}\sqrt{\tau p_{\pi}(x)}\langle s_{\pi}(x),\pi^{(i)}-\pi\rangle

\displaystyle=\frac{d\sqrt{\tau}}{2}\frac{1}{\sqrt{p_{\pi}(x)}}\big\langle(p_{1}(x)-p_{0}(x),\dots,p_{m}(x)-p_{0}(x)),\pi^{(i)}-\pi\big\rangle

Ergo:

\mathcal{J}^{\pi^{(i)},\mathbf{p},\tau}(z)^{1/2}-\mathcal{J}^{\pi,\mathbf{p},\tau}(z)^{1/2}-D(z,\pi)^{\prime}(\pi^{(i)}-\pi)

=d\sqrt{\tau}\bigg(\frac{1}{p_{\pi^{(i)}}(x)^{1/2}+p_{\pi}(x)^{1/2}}-\frac{1}{2p_{\pi}(x)^{1/2}}\bigg)\big\langle(p_{1}(x)-p_{0}(x),\dots,p_{m}(x)-p_{0}(x)),\pi^{(i)}-\pi\big\rangle

=d\sqrt{\tau}p_{\pi}(x)\bigg(\frac{1}{p_{\pi^{(i)}}(x)^{1/2}+p_{\pi}(x)^{1/2}}-\frac{1}{2p_{\pi}(x)^{1/2}}\bigg)\big\langle s_{\pi}(x),\pi^{(i)}-\pi\big\rangle

=d\sqrt{\tau p_{\pi}(x)}\bigg(\frac{p_{\pi}(x)^{1/2}}{p_{\pi^{(i)}}(x)^{1/2}+p_{\pi}(x)^{1/2}}-\frac{1}{2}\bigg)\big\langle s_{\pi}(x),\pi^{(i)}-\pi\big\rangle

=d\sqrt{\tau p_{\pi}(x)}\bigg(\frac{p_{\pi}(x)^{1/2}-p_{\pi^{(i)}}(x)^{1/2}}{2(p_{\pi^{(i)}}(x)^{1/2}+p_{\pi}(x)^{1/2})}\bigg)\big\langle s_{\pi}(x),\pi^{(i)}-\pi\big\rangle

=d\sqrt{\tau p_{\pi}(x)}\bigg(\frac{p_{\pi}(x)-p_{\pi^{(i)}}(x)}{2(p_{\pi^{(i)}}(x)^{1/2}+p_{\pi}(x)^{1/2})(p_{\pi}(x)^{1/2}+p_{\pi^{(i)}}(x)^{1/2})}\bigg)\big\langle s_{\pi}(x),\pi^{(i)}-\pi\big\rangle.

=\frac{d}{2}\sqrt{\tau p_{\pi}(x)}\frac{p_{\pi}(x)-p_{\pi^{(i)}}(x)}{(p_{\pi^{(i)}}(x)^{1/2}+p_{\pi}(x)^{1/2})^{2}}\big\langle s_{\pi}(x),\pi^{(i)}-\pi\big\rangle

Thus:

\displaystyle\big[\mathcal{J}^{\pi^{(i)},\mathbf{p},\tau}(z)^{1/2}-\mathcal{J}^{\pi,\mathbf{p},\tau}(z)^{1/2}-D(z,\pi)^{\prime}(\pi^{(i)}-\pi)\big]^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{d}{4}\tau p_{\pi}(x)\frac{\big(p_{\pi}(x)-p_{\pi^{(i)}}(x)\big)^{2}}{(p_{\pi^{(i)}}(x)^{1/2}+p_{\pi}(x)^{1/2})^{4}}\big\langle s_{\pi}(x),\pi^{(i)}-\pi\big\rangle^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{d}{4}\tau p_{\pi}(x)\frac{\big(p_{\pi}(x)-p_{\pi^{(i)}}(x)\big)^{2}}{(p_{\pi^{(i)}}(x)^{1/2}+p_{\pi}(x)^{1/2})^{4}}||s_{\pi}(x)||_{2}^{2}||\pi^{(i)}-\pi||_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{d}{4}\tau p_{\pi}(x)\frac{\big(p_{\pi}(x)-p_{\pi^{(i)}}(x)\big)^{2}}{\big(p_{\pi}(x)^{1/2}\big)^{4}}||s_{\pi}(x)||_{2}^{2}||\pi^{(i)}-\pi||_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{d}{4}\tau p_{\pi}(x)\bigg(\frac{p_{\pi}(x)-p_{\pi^{(i)}}(x)}{p_{\pi}(x)}\bigg)^{2}||s_{\pi}(x)||_{2}^{2}||\pi^{(i)}-\pi||_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{d}{4}\tau p_{\pi}(x)\big\langle s_{\pi}(x),\pi^{(i)}-\pi\big\rangle^{2}||s_{\pi}(x)||_{2}^{2}||\pi^{(i)}-\pi||_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{d}{4}\tau p_{\pi}(x)||s_{\pi}(x)||_{2}^{4}||\pi^{(i)}-\pi||_{2}^{4}

\displaystyle\leq 4\frac{\tau m^{2}}{\xi^{4}}dp_{\pi}(x)||\pi^{(i)}-\pi||_{2}^{4}.

Thus:

\displaystyle\int\big[\mathcal{J}^{\pi^{(i)},\mathbf{p},\tau}(z)^{1/2}-\mathcal{J}^{\pi,\mathbf{p},\tau}(z)^{1/2}-D(z,\pi)^{\prime}(\pi^{(i)}-\pi)\big]^{2}d\widetilde{\mu}(z)\Big/||\pi^{(i)}-\pi||_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 4\frac{\tau m^{2}}{\xi^{4}}||\pi^{(i)}-\pi||_{2}^{2}\int dp_{\pi}(x)d\widetilde{\mu}(z)

\displaystyle=4\frac{\tau m^{2}}{\xi^{4}}||\pi^{(i)}-\pi||_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\to 0,

since
\pi^{(i)}\to\pi
.

By
[
Newey, 1990
]
, having proved properties (a), (b) and (c), it follows that
\sqrt{\mathcal{J}^{\pi,\mathbf{p},\tau}(z)}
is
\widetilde{\mu}(z)
-mean square continuously differentiable with respect to
\pi\in\Delta
with derivative
D(z,\pi)=\frac{d}{2}\sqrt{\tau p_{\pi}(x)}s_{\pi}(x)
. By
[
Newey, 1990
]
, this means that the score for
\pi
is
S_{\pi}(z)=2\mathbbold{1}{\{\mathcal{J}^{\pi,\mathbf{p}}(z)>0\}}D(z,\pi)/\sqrt{\mathcal{J}^{\pi,\mathbf{p},\tau}(z)}
. That is,

\displaystyle S_{\pi}(z)

\displaystyle=2\mathbbold{1}{\{\mathcal{J}^{\pi,\mathbf{p},\tau}(z)>0\}}D(z,\pi)/\sqrt{\mathcal{J}^{\pi,\mathbf{p},\tau}(z)}

\displaystyle=d\mathbbold{1}{\{\mathcal{J}^{\pi,\mathbf{p},\tau}(z)>0\}}\sqrt{\tau p_{\pi}(x)}s_{\pi}(x)/\sqrt{\mathcal{J}^{\pi,\mathbf{p},\tau}(z)}

\displaystyle=d\mathbbold{1}{\{\tau p_{\pi}(x)>0\}}\sqrt{\tau p_{\pi}(x)}s_{\pi}(x)/\sqrt{\tau p_{\pi}(x)}

\displaystyle=ds_{\pi}(x)\mathbbold{1}{\{p_{\pi}(x)>0\}},

which is equal to
ds_{\pi}(x)
a.e.
[\mathcal{J}^{\pi,\mathbf{p},\tau}]
. Finally, it remains to show that
\frac{\delta}{\delta{\pi}}\Big\{\log\mathcal{J}^{\pi,\mathbf{p},\tau}(z)\Big\}
is equal to
ds_{\pi}(x)
a.e.
[\mathcal{J}^{\pi,\mathbf{p},\tau}]
. Observe that, on non-negligble sets, the indicator function in
\mathcal{J}^{\pi,\mathbf{p},\tau}(z)
is equal to 1. So, on these sets, it follows from elementary calculus that:

\displaystyle\frac{\delta}{\delta{\pi}}\Big\{\log\mathcal{J}^{\pi,\mathbf{p},\tau}(z)\Big\}

\displaystyle=d\frac{\delta}{\delta{\pi}}\Big\{\log\big(\tau p_{\pi}(x)\big)\Big\}

\displaystyle=d\frac{\delta}{\delta{\pi}}\Big\{\log p_{\pi}(x)\Big\}

\displaystyle=ds_{\pi}(x),

as desired!

∎

Proof of Lemma (
6.17
)
.

First, we need to argue that
\mathbf{M}^{\textup{sub},\tau}
is a parametric submodel of
\mathbf{M}^{\textup{semi},\tau}
. Towards that end, consider any density
\mathcal{J}^{\pi,\rho,\tau}\in\mathbf{M}^{\textup{sub},\tau}
. Since
\mathbf{p}\in\mathcal{Q}
,
\gamma^{\ast}(\tau)\in\Gamma
and
\frac{\pi_{j}^{\ast}}{\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}<\frac{1-\xi}{\xi}
for each
j\in\mathcal{Y}
, it follows from Assumption (
B3
) that

(p_{0}e^{\rho^{\prime}\frac{\pi_{0}^{\ast}}{\pi_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau)}-c_{0}(\rho)},\dots,p_{m}e^{\rho^{\prime}\frac{\pi_{m}^{\ast}}{\pi_{m}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau)}-c_{m}(\rho)})\in\mathcal{Q}

for all
\rho\in\mathbb{R}^{m}
satisfying
||\rho||_{2}<B
. These exponentially tilted densities are precisely the class densities
(q_{0}^{\rho},\dots,q_{m}^{\rho})
which appear in the definition for
\mathcal{J}^{\pi,\rho,\tau}
, so it must be that
\mathcal{J}^{\pi,\rho,\tau}\in\mathbf{M}^{\textup{semi},\tau}
. Thus,
\mathbf{M}^{\textup{sub},\tau}\subseteq\mathbf{M}^{\textup{semi},\tau}
. In addition, note that
0\in\mathcal{R}
so
\mathcal{J}^{\pi^{\ast},0,\tau}\in\mathbf{M}^{\textup{sub},\tau}
, and that when
\rho=0
,

(p_{0}e^{\rho^{\prime}\frac{\pi_{0}^{\ast}}{\pi_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau)}-c_{0}(\rho)},\dots,p_{m}e^{\rho^{\prime}\frac{\pi_{m}^{\ast}}{\pi_{m}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau)}-c_{m}(\rho)})=\mathbf{p}.

Thus,
\mathcal{J}^{\pi^{\ast},0,\tau}=\mathcal{J}^{\pi^{\ast},\mathbf{p},\tau}
, and so
\mathcal{J}^{\pi^{\ast},\mathbf{p},\tau}\in\mathbf{M}^{\textup{sub},\tau}
. Thus, indeed,
\mathbf{M}^{\textup{sub},\tau}
is a parametric submodel of
\mathbf{M}^{\textup{semi},\tau}
.

Next, we need to prove that
\mathbf{M}^{\textup{sub},\tau}
is
smooth
in the sense of Definition A.1 of
[
Newey, 1990
]
. That is, we need to prove that
\mathcal{J}^{\pi,\rho,\tau}
is smooth at each
(\pi,\rho)\in\Theta^{\text{sub}}
. This involves verifying the three conditions in Definition A.1. Since the proofs for verifying conditions (i) and (ii) are the same as what was done in the proof of Lemma (
6.16
), here we focus on only checking (iii).

Condition (iii) requires us to show that
\sqrt{\mathcal{J}^{\theta,\tau}(z)}\equiv\sqrt{\mathcal{J}^{\pi,\rho,\tau}(z)}
is
\widetilde{\mu}
-mean square continuously differentiable with respect to
\theta\equiv(\pi,\rho)\in\Theta^{\text{sub}}
, with derivative
D(z,\theta)
given by:

D(z,\theta):=\big[D(z,\pi;\rho),\ D(z,\rho;\pi)\big]^{\prime}\in\mathbb{R}^{2m},

where

D(z,\pi;\rho):=\frac{d}{2}\sqrt{\tau q_{\pi}^{\rho}(x)}s_{\pi}^{\rho}(x)

and

D(z,\rho;\pi):=d\sqrt{\tau}\sum_{j=0}^{m}\pi_{j}\frac{\pi_{j}^{\ast}}{\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\frac{q_{j}^{\rho}(x)}{2\sqrt{q_{\pi}^{\rho}(x)}}(s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau)}(x)-\mathbb{E}_{j}^{\rho}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau)}])+(1-d)\sqrt{(1-\tau)\pi_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\frac{\pi_{y}^{\ast}}{\pi_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\frac{s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau)}(x)-\mathbb{E}_{y}^{\rho}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau)}]}{2}\sqrt{q_{y}^{\rho}(x)}.\\

Showing this, in turn, requires us to verify three different properties. Namely, for every
\theta\in\Delta\times\mathcal{R}
and sequence
\theta^{(i)}\to\theta
, we must show that:

(a)

\int||D(z,\theta)||_{2}^{2}d\widetilde{\mu}(z)<\infty
.

(b)

\int||D(z,\theta^{(i)})-D(z,\theta)||_{2}^{2}d\widetilde{\mu}(z)\to 0
.

(c)

\int\big[\mathcal{J}^{\theta^{(i)},\tau}(z)^{1/2}-\mathcal{J}^{\theta,\tau}(z)^{1/2}-D(z,\theta)^{\prime}(\theta^{(i)}-\theta)\big]^{2}d\widetilde{\mu}(z)\Big/||\theta^{(i)}-\theta||_{2}^{2}\to 0
.

Let’s begin by proving property (c)
. First, we will rewrite the
\mathcal{J}^{\theta^{(i)},\tau}(z)^{1/2}-\mathcal{J}^{\theta,\tau}(z)^{1/2}
term. Observe that:

\displaystyle\mathcal{J}^{\theta^{(i)},\tau}(z)^{1/2}-\mathcal{J}^{\theta,\tau}(z)^{1/2}

\displaystyle=\frac{\mathcal{J}^{\theta^{(i)},\tau}(z)-\mathcal{J}^{\theta,\tau}(z)}{\mathcal{J}^{\theta^{(i)},\tau}(z)^{1/2}+\mathcal{J}^{\theta,\tau}(z)^{1/2}}

\displaystyle=d\frac{\mathcal{J}^{\theta^{(i)},\tau}(z)-\mathcal{J}^{\theta,\tau}(z)}{\mathcal{J}^{\theta^{(i)},\tau}(z)^{1/2}+\mathcal{J}^{\theta,\tau}(z)^{1/2}}+(1-d)\frac{\mathcal{J}^{\theta^{(i)},\tau}(z)-\mathcal{J}^{\theta,\tau}(z)}{\mathcal{J}^{\theta^{(i)},\tau}(z)^{1/2}+\mathcal{J}^{\theta,\tau}(z)^{1/2}}

\displaystyle=d\sqrt{\tau}\frac{q_{\pi^{(i)}}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)-q_{\pi}^{\rho}(x)}{\sqrt{q_{\pi^{(i)}}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)}+\sqrt{q_{\pi}^{\rho}(x)}}+(1-d)\sqrt{(1-\tau)\pi_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\frac{q_{y}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)-q_{y}^{\rho}(x)}{\sqrt{q_{y}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)}+\sqrt{q_{y}^{\rho}(x)}}.

Also observe that:

\displaystyle q_{\pi^{(i)}}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)-q_{\pi}^{\rho}(x)

\displaystyle=\sum_{j=0}^{m}\pi_{j}^{(i)}q_{j}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)-\sum_{j=0}^{m}\pi_{j}q_{j}^{\rho}(x)

\displaystyle=\sum_{j=0}^{m}\pi_{j}^{(i)}(q_{j}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)-q_{j}^{\rho}(x))+\sum_{j=0}^{m}(\pi_{j}^{(i)}-\pi_{j})q_{j}^{\rho}(x)

Note that
\pi_{0}^{(i)}-\pi_{0}=\Big(1-\sum_{j=1}^{m}\pi_{j}^{(i)}\Big)-\Big(1-\sum_{j=1}^{m}\pi_{j}\Big)=\sum_{j=1}^{m}(\pi_{j}-\pi_{j}^{(i)})
, so
(\pi_{0}^{(i)}-\pi_{0})q_{0}^{\rho}(x)=\sum_{j=1}^{m}(\pi_{j}-\pi_{j}^{(i)})q_{0}^{\rho}(x)
and therefore

\displaystyle\sum_{j=0}^{m}(\pi_{j}^{(i)}-\pi_{j})q_{j}^{\rho}(x)

\displaystyle=\sum_{j=1}^{m}(\pi_{j}^{(i)}-\pi_{j})q_{j}^{\rho}(x)+(\pi_{0}^{(i)}-\pi_{0})q_{0}^{\rho}(x)

\displaystyle=\sum_{j=1}^{m}(\pi_{j}^{(i)}-\pi_{j})(q_{j}^{\rho}(x)-q_{0}^{\rho}(x))

\displaystyle=q_{\pi}^{\rho}(x)\sum_{j=1}^{m}(\pi_{j}^{(i)}-\pi_{j})s_{\pi,j}^{\rho}(x)

\displaystyle=q_{\pi}^{\rho}(x)\langle s_{\pi}^{\rho}(x),\pi^{(i)}-\pi\rangle.

Therefore:

\implies q_{\pi^{(i)}}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)-q_{\pi}^{\rho}(x)=\sum_{j=0}^{m}\pi_{j}^{(i)}(q_{j}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)-q_{j}^{\rho}(x))+q_{\pi}^{\rho}(x)\langle s_{\pi}^{\rho}(x),\pi^{(i)}-\pi\rangle.

(44)

Ergo:

\displaystyle\mathcal{J}^{\theta^{(i)},\tau}(z)^{1/2}-\mathcal{J}^{\theta,\tau}(z)^{1/2}

\displaystyle=d\sqrt{\tau}\frac{\sum_{j=0}^{m}\pi_{j}^{(i)}(q_{j}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)-q_{j}^{\rho}(x))+q_{\pi}^{\rho}(x)\langle s_{\pi}^{\rho}(x),\pi^{(i)}-\pi\rangle}{\sqrt{q_{\pi^{(i)}}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)}+\sqrt{q_{\pi}^{\rho}(x)}}+(1-d)\sqrt{(1-\tau)\pi_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\frac{q_{y}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)-q_{y}^{\rho}(x)}{\sqrt{q_{y}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)}+\sqrt{q_{y}^{\rho}(x)}}.

Thus, we have that:

\displaystyle\mathcal{J}^{\theta^{(i)},\tau}(z)^{1/2}-\mathcal{J}^{\theta,\tau}(z)^{1/2}-D(z,\theta)^{\prime}(\theta^{(i)}-\theta)

\displaystyle=\mathcal{J}^{\theta^{(i)},\tau}(z)^{1/2}-\mathcal{J}^{\theta,\tau}(z)^{1/2}-D(z,\pi;\rho)^{\prime}(\pi^{(i)}-\pi)-D(z,\rho;\pi)(\rho^{(i)}-\rho)

\displaystyle=d\sqrt{\tau}\frac{\sum_{j=0}^{m}\pi_{j}^{(i)}(q_{j}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)-q_{j}^{\rho}(x))}{\sqrt{q_{\pi^{(i)}}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)}+\sqrt{q_{\pi}^{\rho}(x)}}+(1-d)\sqrt{(1-\tau)\pi_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\frac{q_{y}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)-q_{y}^{\rho}(x)}{\sqrt{q_{y}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)}+\sqrt{q_{y}^{\rho}(x)}}

\displaystyle\hskip 17.70859pt+d\sqrt{\tau}\frac{q_{\pi}^{\rho}(x)\langle s_{\pi}^{\rho}(x),\pi^{(i)}-\pi\rangle}{\sqrt{q_{\pi^{(i)}}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)}+\sqrt{q_{\pi}^{\rho}(x)}}-d\sqrt{\tau}\frac{\sqrt{q_{\pi}^{\rho}(x)}}{2}\langle s_{\pi}^{\rho}(x),\pi^{(i)}-\pi\rangle-D(z,\rho;\pi)(\rho^{(i)}-\rho)

\displaystyle=\Bigg[d\sqrt{\tau}\frac{\sum_{j=0}^{m}\pi_{j}^{(i)}(q_{j}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)-q_{j}^{\rho}(x))}{\sqrt{q_{\pi^{(i)}}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)}+\sqrt{q_{\pi}^{\rho}(x)}}

\displaystyle\hskip 17.70859pt\hskip 17.70859pt+(1-d)\sqrt{(1-\tau)\pi_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\frac{q_{y}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)-q_{y}^{\rho}(x)}{\sqrt{q_{y}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)}+\sqrt{q_{y}^{\rho}(x)}}-D(z,\rho;\pi)(\rho^{(i)}-\rho)\Bigg]

\displaystyle\hskip 17.70859pt+d\sqrt{\tau q_{\pi}^{\rho}(x)}\Bigg[\frac{\sqrt{q_{\pi}^{\rho}(x)}}{\sqrt{q_{\pi^{(i)}}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)}+\sqrt{q_{\pi}^{\rho}(x)}}-\frac{1}{2}\Bigg]\langle s_{\pi}^{\rho}(x),\pi^{(i)}-\pi\rangle

\displaystyle=d\sqrt{\tau}\overbrace{\Bigg[\sum_{j=0}^{m}\pi_{j}^{(i)}\frac{q_{j}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)-q_{j}^{\rho}(x)}{\sqrt{q_{\pi^{(i)}}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)}+\sqrt{q_{\pi}^{\rho}(x)}}-\sum_{j=0}^{m}\pi_{j}\frac{\pi_{j}^{\ast}}{\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\frac{q_{j}^{\rho}(x)}{2\sqrt{q_{\pi}^{\rho}(x)}}\langle s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau)}(x)-\mathbb{E}_{j}^{\rho}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau)}],\rho^{(i)}-\rho\rangle\Bigg]}^{=:\ K_{1}(z,\theta,\theta^{(i)})}

\displaystyle\hskip 17.70859pt+(1-d)\sqrt{(1-\tau)\pi_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\overbrace{\Bigg[\frac{q_{y}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)-q_{y}^{\rho}(x)}{\sqrt{q_{y}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)}+\sqrt{q_{y}^{\rho}(x)}}-\frac{\pi_{j}^{\ast}}{\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\frac{\langle s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau)}(x)-\mathbb{E}_{y}^{\rho}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau)}],\rho^{(i)}-\rho\rangle}{2}\sqrt{q_{y}^{\rho}(x)}\Bigg]}^{=:\ K_{2}(z,\theta,\theta^{(i)})}

\displaystyle\hskip 17.70859pt+d\sqrt{\tau q_{\pi}^{\rho}(x)}\overbrace{\Bigg[\frac{\sqrt{q_{\pi}^{\rho}(x)}}{\sqrt{q_{\pi^{(i)}}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)}+\sqrt{q_{\pi}^{\rho}(x)}}-\frac{1}{2}\Bigg]\langle s_{\pi}^{\rho}(x),\pi^{(i)}-\pi\rangle}^{=:\ K_{3}(z,\theta,\theta^{(i)})}.

Hence,

\displaystyle\int\frac{\big[\mathcal{J}^{\theta^{(i)},\tau}(z)^{1/2}-\mathcal{J}^{\theta,\tau}(z)^{1/2}-D(z,\theta)^{\prime}(\theta^{(i)}-\theta)\big]^{2}}{||\theta^{(i)}-\theta||_{2}^{2}}d\widetilde{\mu}(z)

\displaystyle\leq 3\int\Big[d\sqrt{\tau}K_{1}(z,\theta,\theta^{(i)})\Big]^{2}d\widetilde{\mu}(z)\Big/||\theta^{(i)}-\theta||_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\hskip 18.49988pt+3\int\Big[(1-d)\sqrt{(1-\tau)\pi_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}K_{2}(z,\theta,\theta^{(i)})\Big]^{2}d\widetilde{\mu}(z)\Big/||\theta^{(i)}-\theta||_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\hskip 18.49988pt+3\int\Big[d\sqrt{\tau q_{\pi}^{\rho}(x)}K_{3}(z,\theta,\theta^{(i)})\Big]^{2}d\widetilde{\mu}(z)\Big/||\theta^{(i)}-\theta||_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 3\int\Big[dK_{1}(z,\theta,\theta^{(i)})\Big]^{2}d\widetilde{\mu}(z)\Big/||\theta^{(i)}-\theta||_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\hskip 18.49988pt+3\int\Big[(1-d)K_{2}(z,\theta,\theta^{(i)})\Big]^{2}d\widetilde{\mu}(z)\Big/||\theta^{(i)}-\theta||_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\hskip 18.49988pt+3\int\Big[d\sqrt{q_{\pi}^{\rho}(x)}K_{3}(z,\theta,\theta^{(i)})\Big]^{2}d\widetilde{\mu}(z)\Big/||\theta^{(i)}-\theta||_{2}^{2}.

Now, for each
j\in\mathcal{Y}
, let
f_{j}(\rho;x):=e^{\rho^{\prime}\frac{\pi_{j}^{\ast}}{\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau)}(x)-c_{j}(\rho)}
. Then by the mean value theorem, for some point
\bar{\rho}_{x,j}
which depends on
x
and is a convex combination of
\rho^{(i)}
and
\rho
, we have that:

\displaystyle q_{j}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)-q_{j}^{\rho}(x)

(45)

\displaystyle=p_{j}(x)\Big[f_{j}(\rho^{(i)};x)-f_{j}(\rho;x)\Big]

\displaystyle=p_{j}(x)\Bigg[\Big\langle\frac{\delta}{\delta{\rho}}f_{j}(\rho;x),\rho^{(i)}-\rho\Big\rangle+\frac{1}{2}(\rho^{(i)}-\rho)^{\prime}\frac{\delta^{2}}{\delta{{\rho}^{2}}}f_{j}(\bar{\rho}_{x,j};x)(\rho^{(i)}-\rho)\Bigg]

\displaystyle=p_{j}(x)\Bigg[e^{\rho^{\prime}\frac{\pi_{j}^{\ast}}{\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau)}(x)-c_{j}(\rho)}\frac{\pi_{j}^{\ast}}{\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\Big\langle s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau)}(x)-\mathbb{E}_{j}^{\rho}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau)}],\rho^{(i)}-\rho\Big\rangle+\frac{1}{2}(\rho^{(i)}-\rho)^{\prime}\frac{\delta^{2}}{\delta{{\rho}^{2}}}f_{j}(\bar{\rho}_{x,j};x)(\rho^{(i)}-\rho)\Bigg]

\displaystyle=q_{j}^{\rho}(x)\frac{\pi_{j}^{\ast}}{\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\Big\langle s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau)}(x)-\mathbb{E}_{j}^{\rho}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau)}],\rho^{(i)}-\rho\Big\rangle+\frac{p_{j}(x)}{2}(\rho^{(i)}-\rho)^{\prime}\frac{\delta^{2}}{\delta{{\rho}^{2}}}f_{j}(\bar{\rho}_{x,j};x)(\rho^{(i)}-\rho).

(46)

Therefore:

\displaystyle|K_{2}(z,\theta,\theta^{(i)})|

\displaystyle=\Bigg|\frac{q_{y}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)-q_{y}^{\rho}(x)}{\sqrt{q_{y}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)}+\sqrt{q_{y}^{\rho}(x)}}-\frac{\pi_{j}^{\ast}}{\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\frac{\langle s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau)}(x)-\mathbb{E}_{y}^{\rho}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau)}],\rho^{(i)}-\rho\rangle}{2}\sqrt{q_{y}^{\rho}(x)}\Bigg|

\displaystyle=\Bigg|\frac{q_{y}^{\rho}(x)\frac{\pi_{j}^{\ast}}{\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\big\langle s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau)}(x)-\mathbb{E}_{y}^{\rho}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau)}],\rho^{(i)}-\rho\big\rangle+\frac{p_{y}(x)}{2}(\rho^{(i)}-\rho)^{\prime}\frac{\delta^{2}}{\delta{{\rho}^{2}}}f_{y}(\bar{\rho}_{x,y};x)(\rho^{(i)}-\rho)}{\sqrt{q_{y}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)}+\sqrt{q_{y}^{\rho}(x)}}

\displaystyle\hskip 18.49988pt-\frac{\pi_{j}^{\ast}}{\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\frac{\langle s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau)}(x)-\mathbb{E}_{y}^{\rho}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau)}],\rho^{(i)}-\rho\rangle}{2}\sqrt{q_{y}^{\rho}(x)}\Bigg|

\displaystyle=\Bigg|\sqrt{q_{y}^{\rho}(x)}\Bigg(\frac{\sqrt{q_{y}^{\rho}(x)}}{\sqrt{q_{y}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)}+\sqrt{q_{y}^{\rho}(x)}}-\frac{1}{2}\Bigg)\frac{\pi_{j}^{\ast}}{\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\langle s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau)}(x)-\mathbb{E}_{y}^{\rho}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau)}],\rho^{(i)}-\rho\rangle+\frac{\frac{p_{y}(x)}{2}(\rho^{(i)}-\rho)^{\prime}\frac{\delta^{2}}{\delta{{\rho}^{2}}}f_{y}(\bar{\rho}_{x,y};x)(\rho^{(i)}-\rho)}{\sqrt{q_{y}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)}+\sqrt{q_{y}^{\rho}(x)}}\Bigg|

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{q_{y}^{\rho}(x)}\Bigg|\frac{\sqrt{q_{y}^{\rho}(x)}}{\sqrt{q_{y}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)}+\sqrt{q_{y}^{\rho}(x)}}-\frac{1}{2}\Bigg|\frac{\pi_{j}^{\ast}}{\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}||s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau)}(x)-\mathbb{E}_{y}^{\rho}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau)}]||_{2}||\rho^{(i)}-\rho||_{2}+\frac{p_{y}(x)}{2}\frac{\lambda_{\textup{max}}\big(\frac{\delta^{2}}{\delta{{\rho}^{2}}}f_{y}(\bar{\rho}_{x,y};x)\big)||\rho^{(i)}-\rho||_{2}^{2}}{\sqrt{q_{y}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)}+\sqrt{q_{y}^{\rho}(x)}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{4\sqrt{m}(1-\xi)}{L\xi}\sqrt{q_{y}^{\rho}(x)}\Bigg|\frac{\sqrt{q_{y}^{\rho}(x)}}{\sqrt{q_{y}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)}+\sqrt{q_{y}^{\rho}(x)}}-\frac{1}{2}\Bigg|||\rho^{(i)}-\rho||_{2}+\frac{\sqrt{p_{y}(x)}}{2}\frac{\lambda_{\textup{max}}\big(\frac{\delta^{2}}{\delta{{\rho}^{2}}}f_{y}(\bar{\rho}_{x,y};x)\big)}{\sqrt{f_{y}(\rho^{(i)};x)}+\sqrt{f_{y}(\rho;x)}}||\rho^{(i)}-\rho||_{2}^{2}.

And so:

\displaystyle\int(1-d)\frac{K_{2}(z,\theta,\theta^{(i)})^{2}}{||\theta^{(i)}-\theta||_{2}^{2}}d\widetilde{\mu}(z)

\displaystyle\leq 2\frac{16m(1-\xi)^{2}}{L^{2}\xi^{2}}\frac{||\rho^{(i)}-\rho||_{2}^{2}}{||\theta^{(i)}-\theta||_{2}^{2}}\int(1-d)q_{y}^{\rho}(x)\Bigg|\frac{\sqrt{q_{y}^{\rho}(x)}}{\sqrt{q_{y}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)}+\sqrt{q_{y}^{\rho}(x)}}-\frac{1}{2}\Bigg|^{2}d\widetilde{\mu}(z)

\displaystyle\hskip 18.49988pt\hskip 18.49988pt+2\frac{1}{4}\frac{||\rho^{(i)}-\rho||_{2}^{4}}{||\theta^{(i)}-\theta||_{2}^{2}}\int(1-d)p_{y}(x)\Bigg(\frac{\lambda_{\textup{max}}\big(\frac{\delta^{2}}{\delta{{\rho}^{2}}}f_{y}(\bar{\rho}_{x,y};x)\big)}{\sqrt{f_{y}(\rho^{(i)};x)}+\sqrt{f_{y}(\rho;x)}}\Bigg)^{2}d\widetilde{\mu}(z)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{32m(1-\xi)^{2}}{L^{2}\xi^{2}}\mathbb{E}_{y}^{\rho}\Bigg[\bigg(\frac{\sqrt{q_{y}^{\rho}(X)}}{\sqrt{q_{y}^{\rho^{(i)}}(X)}+\sqrt{q_{y}^{\rho}(X)}}-\frac{1}{2}\bigg)^{2}\Bigg]

\displaystyle\hskip 18.49988pt\hskip 18.49988pt+\frac{1}{2}||\rho^{(i)}-\rho||_{2}^{2}\mathbb{E}_{y}\Bigg[\bigg(\frac{\lambda_{\textup{max}}\big(\frac{\delta^{2}}{\delta{{\rho}^{2}}}f_{y}(\bar{\rho}_{x,y};x)\big)}{\sqrt{f_{y}(\rho^{(i)};x)}+\sqrt{f_{y}(\rho;x)}}\bigg)^{2}\Bigg]

\displaystyle=\frac{32m(1-\xi)^{2}}{L^{2}\xi^{2}}\mathbb{E}_{y}^{\rho}\Bigg[\bigg(\frac{q_{y}^{\rho}(X)-q_{y}^{\rho^{(i)}}(X)}{2(q_{y}^{\rho^{(i)}}(X)^{1/2}+q_{y}^{\rho}(X)^{1/2})^{2}}\bigg)^{2}\Bigg]+\frac{||\rho^{(i)}-\rho||_{2}^{2}}{2}\mathbb{E}_{y}\Bigg[\bigg(\frac{\lambda_{\textup{max}}\big(\frac{\delta^{2}}{\delta{{\rho}^{2}}}f_{y}(\bar{\rho}_{x,y};x)\big)}{\sqrt{f_{y}(\rho^{(i)};x)}+\sqrt{f_{y}(\rho;x)}}\bigg)^{2}\Bigg]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{8m(1-\xi)^{2}}{L^{2}\xi^{2}}\mathbb{E}_{y}^{\rho}\Bigg[\bigg(\frac{q_{y}^{\rho}(X)-q_{y}^{\rho^{(i)}}(X)}{q_{y}^{\rho}(X)}\bigg)^{2}\Bigg]+\frac{||\rho^{(i)}-\rho||_{2}^{2}}{2}\mathbb{E}_{y}\Bigg[\bigg(\frac{\lambda_{\textup{max}}\big(\frac{\delta^{2}}{\delta{{\rho}^{2}}}f_{y}(\bar{\rho}_{x,y};x)\big)}{\sqrt{f_{y}(\rho^{(i)};x)}+\sqrt{f_{y}(\rho;x)}}\bigg)^{2}\Bigg].

Now, by line (
46
), note that:

\displaystyle\bigg(\frac{q_{y}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)-q_{y}^{\rho}(x)}{q_{y}^{\rho}(x)}\bigg)^{2}

(47)

\displaystyle=\bigg(\frac{\pi_{y}^{\ast}}{\pi_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\big\langle s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau)}(x)-\mathbb{E}_{y}^{\rho}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau)}],\rho^{(i)}-\rho\big\rangle+\frac{1}{2f_{y}(\rho;x)}(\rho^{(i)}-\rho)^{\prime}\frac{\delta^{2}}{\delta{{\rho}^{2}}}f_{y}(\bar{\rho}_{x,y};x)(\rho^{(i)}-\rho)\bigg)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 2\frac{(\pi_{y}^{\ast})^{2}}{(\pi_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr}}})^{2}}\big\langle s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau)}(x)-\mathbb{E}_{y}^{\rho}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau)}],\rho^{(i)}-\rho\big\rangle^{2}+2\bigg(\frac{1}{2f_{y}(\rho;x)}(\rho^{(i)}-\rho)^{\prime}\frac{\delta^{2}}{\delta{{\rho}^{2}}}f_{y}(\bar{\rho}_{x,y};x)(\rho^{(i)}-\rho)\bigg)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 2\frac{16m(1-\xi)^{2}}{L^{2}\xi^{2}}||\rho^{(i)}-\rho||_{2}^{2}+2\bigg(\frac{1}{2f_{y}(\rho;x)}\lambda_{\textup{max}}\Bigg\{\frac{\delta^{2}}{\delta{{\rho}^{2}}}f_{y}(\bar{\rho}_{x,y};x)\Bigg\}||\rho^{(i)}-\rho||_{2}^{2}\bigg)^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{32m(1-\xi)^{2}}{L^{2}\xi^{2}}||\rho^{(i)}-\rho||_{2}^{2}+\frac{1}{2f_{y}(\rho;x)^{2}}\lambda_{\textup{max}}\Bigg\{\frac{\delta^{2}}{\delta{{\rho}^{2}}}f_{y}(\bar{\rho}_{x,y};x)\Bigg\}^{2}||\rho^{(i)}-\rho||_{2}^{4}.

(48)

Thus,

\displaystyle\int(1-d)\frac{K_{2}(z,\theta,\theta^{(i)})^{2}}{||\theta^{(i)}-\theta||_{2}^{2}}d\widetilde{\mu}(z)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{256m^{2}(1-\xi)^{4}}{L^{4}\xi^{4}}||\rho^{(i)}-\rho||_{2}^{2}+\frac{4m}{L^{2}}\mathbb{E}_{y}^{\rho}\Bigg[\frac{1}{f_{y}(\rho;x)^{2}}\lambda_{\textup{max}}\Bigg\{\frac{\delta^{2}}{\delta{{\rho}^{2}}}f_{y}(\bar{\rho}_{x,y};x)\Bigg\}^{2}\Bigg]||\rho^{(i)}-\rho||_{2}^{4}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\frac{||\rho^{(i)}-\rho||_{2}^{2}}{2}\mathbb{E}_{y}\Bigg[\bigg(\frac{\lambda_{\textup{max}}\big(\frac{\delta^{2}}{\delta{{\rho}^{2}}}f_{y}(\bar{\rho}_{x,y};x)\big)}{\sqrt{f_{y}(\rho^{(i)};x)}+\sqrt{f_{y}(\rho;x)}}\bigg)^{2}\Bigg].

One can show that
f_{y}(\rho^{(i)};x)
is uniformly bounded away from
0
and
\infty
, and that
\lambda_{\textup{max}}\big(\frac{\delta^{2}}{\delta{{\rho}^{2}}}f_{y}(\bar{\rho}_{x,y};x)\big)
is uniformly bounded away from
\infty
. This implies that both expectations in the display above are finite. Since
\theta^{(i)}\to\theta
, the integral on the LHS therefore converges to
0
.

Next, let’s tackle the integral involving
K_{1}
. Towards that end, observe that:

\displaystyle|K_{1}(z,\theta,\theta^{(i)})|

\displaystyle=\Bigg|\sum_{j=0}^{m}\pi_{j}^{(i)}\frac{q_{j}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)-q_{j}^{\rho}(x)}{\sqrt{q_{\pi^{(i)}}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)}+\sqrt{q_{\pi}^{\rho}(x)}}-\sum_{j=0}^{m}\pi_{j}\frac{q_{j}^{\rho}(x)}{2\sqrt{q_{\pi}^{\rho}(x)}}\frac{\pi_{j}^{\ast}}{\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\langle s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau)}(x)-\mathbb{E}_{j}^{\rho}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau)}],\rho^{(i)}-\rho\rangle\Bigg|

\displaystyle=\Bigg\lvert\sum_{j=0}^{m}\pi_{j}^{(i)}\frac{q_{j}^{\rho}(x)\frac{\pi_{j}^{\ast}}{\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\big\langle s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau)}(x)-\mathbb{E}_{j}^{\rho}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau)}],\rho^{(i)}-\rho\big\rangle+\frac{p_{j}(x)}{2}(\rho^{(i)}-\rho)^{\prime}\frac{\delta^{2}}{\delta{{\rho}^{2}}}f_{j}(\bar{\rho}_{x,j};x)(\rho^{(i)}-\rho)}{\sqrt{q_{\pi^{(i)}}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)}+\sqrt{q_{\pi}^{\rho}(x)}}

\displaystyle\hskip 17.70859pt\hskip 17.70859pt\hskip 17.70859pt\hskip 17.70859pt-\sum_{j=0}^{m}\pi_{j}\frac{q_{j}^{\rho}(x)}{2\sqrt{q_{\pi}^{\rho}(x)}}\frac{\pi_{j}^{\ast}}{\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\langle s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau)}(x)-\mathbb{E}_{j}^{\rho}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau)}],\rho^{(i)}-\rho\rangle\Bigg\lvert

\displaystyle\leq\Bigg|\sum_{j=0}^{m}\pi_{j}^{(i)}\frac{q_{j}^{\rho}(x)\frac{\pi_{j}^{\ast}}{\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\big\langle s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau)}(x)-\mathbb{E}_{j}^{\rho}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau)}],\rho^{(i)}-\rho\big\rangle}{\sqrt{q_{\pi^{(i)}}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)}+\sqrt{q_{\pi}^{\rho}(x)}}-\sum_{j=0}^{m}\pi_{j}\frac{q_{j}^{\rho}(x)}{2\sqrt{q_{\pi}^{\rho}(x)}}\frac{\pi_{j}^{\ast}}{\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\langle s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau)}(x)-\mathbb{E}_{j}^{\rho}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau)}],\rho^{(i)}-\rho\rangle\Bigg|

\displaystyle\hskip 17.70859pt\hskip 17.70859pt\hskip 17.70859pt\hskip 17.70859pt+\Bigg|\sum_{j=0}^{m}\pi_{j}^{(i)}\frac{\frac{p_{j}(x)}{2}(\rho^{(i)}-\rho)^{\prime}\frac{\delta^{2}}{\delta{{\rho}^{2}}}f_{j}(\bar{\rho}_{x,j};x)(\rho^{(i)}-\rho)}{\sqrt{q_{\pi^{(i)}}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)}+\sqrt{q_{\pi}^{\rho}(x)}}\Bigg|

\displaystyle=\Bigg|\sum_{j=0}^{m}\Bigg(\pi_{j}^{(i)}\frac{q_{j}^{\rho}(x)}{\sqrt{q_{\pi^{(i)}}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)}+\sqrt{q_{\pi}^{\rho}(x)}}-\pi_{j}\frac{q_{j}^{\rho}(x)}{2\sqrt{q_{\pi}^{\rho}(x)}}\Bigg)\frac{\pi_{j}^{\ast}}{\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\langle s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau)}(x)-\mathbb{E}_{j}^{\rho}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau)}],\rho^{(i)}-\rho\rangle\Bigg|

\displaystyle\hskip 17.70859pt\hskip 17.70859pt\hskip 17.70859pt\hskip 17.70859pt+\Bigg|\sum_{j=0}^{m}\pi_{j}^{(i)}\frac{\frac{p_{j}(x)}{2}(\rho^{(i)}-\rho)^{\prime}\frac{\delta^{2}}{\delta{{\rho}^{2}}}f_{j}(\bar{\rho}_{x,j};x)(\rho^{(i)}-\rho)}{\sqrt{q_{\pi^{(i)}}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)}+\sqrt{q_{\pi}^{\rho}(x)}}\Bigg|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{4\sqrt{m}(1-\xi)}{L\xi}||\rho^{(i)}-\rho||_{2}\sum_{j=0}^{m}\Bigg|\pi_{j}^{(i)}\frac{q_{j}^{\rho}(x)}{\sqrt{q_{\pi^{(i)}}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)}+\sqrt{q_{\pi}^{\rho}(x)}}-\pi_{j}\frac{q_{j}^{\rho}(x)}{2\sqrt{q_{\pi}^{\rho}(x)}}\Bigg|+||\rho^{(i)}-\rho||_{2}^{2}\sum_{j=0}^{m}\pi_{j}^{(i)}\frac{\frac{1}{2}p_{j}(x)\lambda_{\textup{max}}\big(\frac{\delta^{2}}{\delta{{\rho}^{2}}}f_{j}(\bar{\rho}_{x,j};x)\big)}{\sqrt{q_{\pi^{(i)}}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)}+\sqrt{q_{\pi}^{\rho}(x)}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{4\sqrt{m}(1-\xi)}{L\xi}||\rho^{(i)}-\rho||_{2}\sum_{j=0}^{m}\Bigg|\pi_{j}^{(i)}\frac{q_{j}^{\rho}(x)}{\sqrt{q_{\pi^{(i)}}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)}+\sqrt{q_{\pi}^{\rho}(x)}}-\pi_{j}\frac{q_{j}^{\rho}(x)}{2\sqrt{q_{\pi}^{\rho}(x)}}\Bigg|+||\rho^{(i)}-\rho||_{2}^{2}\sum_{j=0}^{m}\frac{\pi_{j}^{(i)}}{\sqrt{\pi_{j}^{(i)}\wedge\pi_{j}}}\frac{\frac{1}{2}p_{j}(x)\lambda_{\textup{max}}\big(\frac{\delta^{2}}{\delta{{\rho}^{2}}}f_{j}(\bar{\rho}_{x,j};x)\big)}{\sqrt{q_{j}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)}+\sqrt{q_{j}^{\rho}(x)}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{4\sqrt{m}(1-\xi)}{L\xi}||\rho^{(i)}-\rho||_{2}\sum_{j=0}^{m}\Bigg|\pi_{j}^{(i)}\frac{q_{j}^{\rho}(x)}{\sqrt{q_{\pi^{(i)}}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)}+\sqrt{q_{\pi}^{\rho}(x)}}-\pi_{j}\frac{q_{j}^{\rho}(x)}{2\sqrt{q_{\pi}^{\rho}(x)}}\Bigg|+||\rho^{(i)}-\rho||_{2}^{2}\frac{1-\xi}{\sqrt{\xi}}\sum_{j=0}^{m}\frac{\frac{1}{2}\sqrt{p_{j}(x)}\lambda_{\textup{max}}\big(\frac{\delta^{2}}{\delta{{\rho}^{2}}}f_{j}(\bar{\rho}_{x,j};x)\big)}{\sqrt{f_{j}(\rho^{(i)};x)}+\sqrt{f_{j}(\rho;x)}}.

And so:

\displaystyle\int d\frac{K_{1}(z,\theta,\theta^{(i)})^{2}}{||\theta^{(i)}-\theta||_{2}^{2}}d\widetilde{\mu}(z)

\displaystyle\leq 2\frac{16m^{2}(1-\xi)^{2}}{L^{2}\xi^{2}}\frac{||\rho^{(i)}-\rho||_{2}^{2}}{||\theta^{(i)}-\theta||_{2}^{2}}\int d\sum_{j=0}^{m}\Bigg|\frac{\pi_{j}^{(i)}q_{j}^{\rho}(x)}{\sqrt{q_{\pi^{(i)}}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)}+\sqrt{q_{\pi}^{\rho}(x)}}-\frac{\pi_{j}q_{j}^{\rho}(x)}{2\sqrt{q_{\pi}^{\rho}(x)}}\Bigg|^{2}d\widetilde{\mu}(z)

\displaystyle\hskip 18.49988pt+2\frac{(1-\xi)^{2}}{\xi}\frac{||\rho^{(i)}-\rho||_{2}^{4}}{||\theta^{(i)}-\theta||_{2}^{2}}m\int d\sum_{j=0}^{m}\Bigg(\frac{\frac{1}{2}\sqrt{p_{j}(x)}\lambda_{\textup{max}}\big(\frac{\delta^{2}}{\delta{{\rho}^{2}}}f_{j}(\bar{\rho}_{x,j};x)\big)}{\sqrt{f_{j}(\rho^{(i)};x)}+\sqrt{f_{j}(\rho;x)}}\Bigg)^{2}d\widetilde{\mu}(z)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{32m^{2}(1-\xi)^{2}}{L^{2}\xi^{2}}\int d\sum_{j=0}^{m}q_{j}^{\rho}(x)\Bigg|\frac{\pi_{j}^{(i)}\sqrt{q_{j}^{\rho}(x)}}{\sqrt{q_{\pi^{(i)}}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)}+\sqrt{q_{\pi}^{\rho}(x)}}-\frac{\pi_{j}\sqrt{q_{j}^{\rho}(x)}}{2\sqrt{q_{\pi}^{\rho}(x)}}\Bigg|^{2}d\widetilde{\mu}(z)

\displaystyle\hskip 18.49988pt+\frac{1}{2}m\frac{(1-\xi)^{2}}{\xi}||\rho^{(i)}-\rho||_{2}^{2}\int d\sum_{j=0}^{m}p_{j}(x)\Bigg(\frac{\lambda_{\textup{max}}\big(\frac{\delta^{2}}{\delta{{\rho}^{2}}}f_{j}(\bar{\rho}_{x,j};x)\big)}{\sqrt{f_{j}(\rho^{(i)};x)}+\sqrt{f_{j}(\rho;x)}}\Bigg)^{2}d\widetilde{\mu}(z)

\displaystyle=\frac{32m^{2}(1-\xi)^{2}}{L^{2}\xi^{2}}\sum_{j=0}^{m}\mathbb{E}_{j}^{\rho}\Bigg[\Bigg(\frac{\pi_{j}^{(i)}\sqrt{q_{j}^{\rho}(X)}}{\sqrt{q_{\pi^{(i)}}^{\rho^{(i)}}(X)}+\sqrt{q_{\pi}^{\rho}(X)}}-\frac{\pi_{j}\sqrt{q_{j}^{\rho}(X)}}{2\sqrt{q_{\pi}^{\rho}(X)}}\Bigg)^{2}\Bigg]

\displaystyle\hskip 18.49988pt+\frac{1}{2}m\frac{(1-\xi)^{2}}{\xi}||\rho^{(i)}-\rho||_{2}^{2}\sum_{j=0}^{m}\mathbb{E}_{j}\Bigg[\Bigg(\frac{\lambda_{\textup{max}}\big(\frac{\delta^{2}}{\delta{{\rho}^{2}}}f_{j}(\bar{\rho}_{X,j};X)\big)}{\sqrt{f_{j}(\rho^{(i)};X)}+\sqrt{f_{j}(\rho;X)}}\Bigg)^{2}\Bigg]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{64m^{2}(1-\xi)^{2}}{L^{2}\xi^{2}}\sum_{j=0}^{m}\mathbb{E}_{j}^{\rho}\Bigg[\Bigg(\frac{\pi_{j}^{(i)}\sqrt{q_{j}^{\rho}(X)}}{\sqrt{q_{\pi^{(i)}}^{\rho^{(i)}}(X)}+\sqrt{q_{\pi}^{\rho}(X)}}-\frac{\pi_{j}\sqrt{q_{j}^{\rho}(X)}}{\sqrt{q_{\pi^{(i)}}^{\rho^{(i)}}(X)}+\sqrt{q_{\pi}^{\rho}(X)}}\Bigg)^{2}\Bigg]

\displaystyle\hskip 18.49988pt+\frac{64m^{2}(1-\xi)^{2}}{L^{2}\xi^{2}}\sum_{j=0}^{m}\mathbb{E}_{j}^{\rho}\Bigg[\Bigg(\frac{\pi_{j}\sqrt{q_{j}^{\rho}(X)}}{\sqrt{q_{\pi^{(i)}}^{\rho^{(i)}}(X)}+\sqrt{q_{\pi}^{\rho}(X)}}-\frac{\pi_{j}\sqrt{q_{j}^{\rho}(X)}}{2\sqrt{q_{\pi}^{\rho}(X)}}\Bigg)^{2}\Bigg]

\displaystyle\hskip 18.49988pt+\frac{1}{2}m\frac{(1-\xi)^{2}}{\xi}||\rho^{(i)}-\rho||_{2}^{2}\sum_{j=0}^{m}\mathbb{E}_{j}\Bigg[\Bigg(\frac{\lambda_{\textup{max}}\big(\frac{\delta^{2}}{\delta{{\rho}^{2}}}f_{j}(\bar{\rho}_{X,j};X)\big)}{\sqrt{f_{j}(\rho^{(i)};X)}+\sqrt{f_{j}(\rho;X)}}\Bigg)^{2}\Bigg]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{64m^{2}(1-\xi)^{2}}{L^{2}\xi^{3}}\sum_{j=0}^{m}(\pi_{j}^{(i)}-\pi_{j})^{2}+\frac{64m^{2}(1-\xi)^{2}}{L^{2}\xi^{2}}\sum_{j=0}^{m}\mathbb{E}_{j}^{\rho}\Bigg[\frac{q_{j}^{\rho}(X)}{q_{\pi}^{\rho}(X)}\Bigg(\frac{\sqrt{q_{\pi}^{\rho}(X)}}{\sqrt{q_{\pi^{(i)}}^{\rho^{(i)}}(X)}+\sqrt{q_{\pi}^{\rho}(X)}}-\frac{1}{2}\Bigg)^{2}\Bigg]

\displaystyle\hskip 18.49988pt+\frac{1}{2}m\frac{(1-\xi)^{2}}{\xi}||\rho^{(i)}-\rho||_{2}^{2}\sum_{j=0}^{m}\mathbb{E}_{j}\Bigg[\Bigg(\frac{\lambda_{\textup{max}}\big(\frac{\delta^{2}}{\delta{{\rho}^{2}}}f_{j}(\bar{\rho}_{X,j};X)\big)}{\sqrt{f_{j}(\rho^{(i)};X)}+\sqrt{f_{j}(\rho;X)}}\Bigg)^{2}\Bigg]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{64m^{2}(1-\xi)^{2}}{L^{2}\xi^{3}}||\pi^{(i)}-\pi||_{2}^{2}+\frac{64m^{2}(1-\xi)^{2}}{L^{2}\xi^{3}}\sum_{j=0}^{m}\mathbb{E}_{j}^{\rho}\Bigg[\Bigg(\frac{\sqrt{q_{\pi}^{\rho}(X)}}{\sqrt{q_{\pi^{(i)}}^{\rho^{(i)}}(X)}+\sqrt{q_{\pi}^{\rho}(X)}}-\frac{1}{2}\Bigg)^{2}\Bigg]

\displaystyle\hskip 18.49988pt+\frac{1}{2}m\frac{(1-\xi)^{2}}{\xi}||\rho^{(i)}-\rho||_{2}^{2}\sum_{j=0}^{m}\mathbb{E}_{j}\Bigg[\Bigg(\frac{\lambda_{\textup{max}}\big(\frac{\delta^{2}}{\delta{{\rho}^{2}}}f_{j}(\bar{\rho}_{X,j};X)\big)}{\sqrt{f_{j}(\rho^{(i)};X)}+\sqrt{f_{j}(\rho;X)}}\Bigg)^{2}\Bigg]

\displaystyle=\frac{64m^{2}(1-\xi)^{2}}{L^{2}\xi^{3}}||\pi^{(i)}-\pi||_{2}^{2}+\frac{64m^{2}(1-\xi)^{2}}{L^{2}\xi^{3}}\sum_{j=0}^{m}\mathbb{E}_{j}^{\rho}\Bigg[\Bigg(\frac{q_{\pi}^{\rho}(X)-q_{\pi^{(i)}}^{\rho^{(i)}}(X)}{2\big(q_{\pi^{(i)}}^{\rho^{(i)}}(X)^{1/2}+q_{\pi}^{\rho}(X)^{1/2}\big)^{2}}\Bigg)^{2}\Bigg]

\displaystyle\hskip 18.49988pt+\frac{1}{2}m\frac{(1-\xi)^{2}}{\xi}||\rho^{(i)}-\rho||_{2}^{2}\sum_{j=0}^{m}\mathbb{E}_{j}\Bigg[\Bigg(\frac{\lambda_{\textup{max}}\big(\frac{\delta^{2}}{\delta{{\rho}^{2}}}f_{j}(\bar{\rho}_{X,j};X)\big)}{\sqrt{f_{j}(\rho^{(i)};X)}+\sqrt{f_{j}(\rho;X)}}\Bigg)^{2}\Bigg].

Thus:

\displaystyle\int d\frac{K_{1}(z,\theta,\theta^{(i)})^{2}}{||\theta^{(i)}-\theta||_{2}^{2}}d\widetilde{\mu}(z)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{64m^{2}(1-\xi)^{2}}{L^{2}\xi^{3}}||\pi^{(i)}-\pi||_{2}^{2}+\frac{16m^{2}(1-\xi)^{2}}{L^{2}\xi^{3}}\sum_{j=0}^{m}\mathbb{E}_{j}^{\rho}\Bigg[\Bigg(\frac{q_{\pi}^{\rho}(X)-q_{\pi^{(i)}}^{\rho^{(i)}}(X)}{q_{\pi}^{\rho}(X)}\Bigg)^{2}\Bigg]

\displaystyle\hskip 18.49988pt+\frac{1}{2}m\frac{(1-\xi)^{2}}{\xi}||\rho^{(i)}-\rho||_{2}^{2}\sum_{j=0}^{m}\mathbb{E}_{j}\Bigg[\Bigg(\frac{\lambda_{\textup{max}}\big(\frac{\delta^{2}}{\delta{{\rho}^{2}}}f_{j}(\bar{\rho}_{X,j};X)\big)}{\sqrt{f_{j}(\rho^{(i)};X)}+\sqrt{f_{j}(\rho;X)}}\Bigg)^{2}\Bigg].

Thus, to show that the LHS above converges to
0
, it suffices to show that
\mathbb{E}_{j}^{\rho}\Big[\Big(\frac{q_{\pi}^{\rho}(X)-q_{\pi^{(i)}}^{\rho^{(i)}}(X)}{q_{\pi}^{\rho}(X)}\Big)^{2}\Big]\to 0
for each
j\in\mathcal{Y}
. Towards that end, note that by equations (
44
) and (
48
):

\displaystyle\Bigg(\frac{q_{\pi}^{\rho}(x)-q_{\pi^{(i)}}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)}{q_{\pi}^{\rho}(x)}\Bigg)^{2}

(49)

\displaystyle=\Bigg(\frac{\sum_{l=0}^{m}\pi_{l}^{(i)}(q_{l}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)-q_{l}^{\rho}(x))+q_{\pi}^{\rho}(x)\langle s_{\pi}^{\rho}(x),\pi^{(i)}-\pi\rangle}{q_{\pi}^{\rho}(x)}\Bigg)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 2\Bigg(\frac{\sum_{l=0}^{m}\pi_{l}^{(i)}(q_{l}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)-q_{l}^{\rho}(x))}{q_{\pi}^{\rho}(x)}\Bigg)^{2}+2\Big[\langle s_{\pi}^{\rho}(x),\pi^{(i)}-\pi\rangle^{2}\Big]

\displaystyle\leq 2\frac{\sum_{l=0}^{m}(q_{l}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)-q_{l}^{\rho}(x))^{2}}{q_{\pi}^{\rho}(x)^{2}}+2||\pi^{(i)}-\pi||_{2}^{2}\Big[||s_{\pi}^{\rho}(x)||_{2}^{2}\Big]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2}{\xi^{2}}\sum_{l=0}^{m}\frac{(q_{l}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)-q_{l}^{\rho}(x))^{2}}{q_{l}^{\rho}(x)^{2}}+2||\pi^{(i)}-\pi||_{2}^{2}\frac{4m}{L^{2}}

\displaystyle=\frac{2}{\xi^{2}}\sum_{l=0}^{m}\bigg(\frac{q_{l}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)-q_{l}^{\rho}(x)}{q_{l}^{\rho}(x)}\bigg)^{2}+\frac{8m}{L^{2}}||\pi^{(i)}-\pi||_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2}{\xi^{2}}\sum_{l=0}^{m}\Bigg[\frac{32m}{L^{2}}||\rho^{(i)}-\rho||_{2}^{2}+\frac{1}{2f_{l}(\rho;x)^{2}}\lambda_{\textup{max}}\Bigg\{\frac{\delta^{2}}{\delta{{\rho}^{2}}}f_{l}(\bar{\rho}_{x,l};x)\Bigg\}^{2}||\rho^{(i)}-\rho||_{2}^{4}\Bigg]+\frac{8m}{L^{2}}||\pi^{(i)}-\pi||_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{64m(m+1)}{L^{2}\xi^{2}}||\rho^{(i)}-\rho||_{2}^{2}+\frac{||\rho^{(i)}-\rho||_{2}^{4}}{\xi^{2}}\sum_{l=0}^{m}\Bigg[\frac{1}{f_{l}(\rho;x)^{2}}\lambda_{\textup{max}}\Bigg\{\frac{\delta^{2}}{\delta{{\rho}^{2}}}f_{l}(\bar{\rho}_{x,l};x)\Bigg\}^{2}\Bigg]+\frac{8m}{L^{2}}||\pi^{(i)}-\pi||_{2}^{2}.

(50)

Now,
f_{l}(\rho;x)
can be shown to be uniformly bounded away from
0
, and
\lambda_{\textup{max}}\big(\frac{\delta^{2}}{\delta{{\rho}^{2}}}f_{l}(\bar{\rho}_{x,l};x)\big)
can be shown to be uniformly bounded away from
\infty
. Thus, the expected value of the LHS above has an upper bound that converges to
0
as
\theta^{(i)}\to\theta
. That is,
\mathbb{E}_{j}^{\rho}\Big[\Big(\frac{q_{\pi}^{\rho}(X)-q_{\pi^{(i)}}^{\rho^{(i)}}(X)}{q_{\pi}^{\rho}(X)}\Big)^{2}\Big]\to 0
, and so

\int d\frac{K_{1}(z,\theta,\theta^{(i)})^{2}}{||\theta^{(i)}-\theta||_{2}^{2}}d\widetilde{\mu}(z)\to 0

as well.

Finally, we’ll show that the integral involving
K_{3}
also converges to 0.

\displaystyle\int\frac{\Big[d\sqrt{q_{\pi}^{\rho}(x)}K_{3}(z,\theta,\theta^{(i)})\Big]^{2}}{||\theta^{(i)}-\theta||_{2}^{2}}d\widetilde{\mu}(z)

\displaystyle=\int dq_{\pi}^{\rho}(x)\frac{K_{3}(z,\theta,\theta^{(i)})^{2}}{||\theta^{(i)}-\theta||_{2}^{2}}d\widetilde{\mu}(z)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{\pi}^{\rho}\Bigg[\Bigg(\frac{\sqrt{q_{\pi}^{\rho}(X)}}{\sqrt{q_{\pi^{(i)}}^{\rho^{(i)}}(X)}+\sqrt{q_{\pi}^{\rho}(X)}}-\frac{1}{2}\Bigg)^{2}\frac{\langle s_{\pi}^{\rho}(X),\pi^{(i)}-\pi\rangle^{2}}{||\theta^{(i)}-\theta||_{2}^{2}}\Bigg]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}_{\pi}^{\rho}\Bigg[\Bigg(\frac{\sqrt{q_{\pi}^{\rho}(X)}}{\sqrt{q_{\pi^{(i)}}^{\rho^{(i)}}(X)}+\sqrt{q_{\pi}^{\rho}(X)}}-\frac{1}{2}\Bigg)^{2}\frac{||s_{\pi}^{\rho}(X)||_{2}^{2}||\pi^{(i)}-\pi||_{2}^{2}}{||\theta^{(i)}-\theta||_{2}^{2}}\Bigg]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{4m}{L^{2}}\mathbb{E}_{\pi}^{\rho}\Bigg[\Bigg(\frac{\sqrt{q_{\pi}^{\rho}(X)}}{\sqrt{q_{\pi^{(i)}}^{\rho^{(i)}}(X)}+\sqrt{q_{\pi}^{\rho}(X)}}-\frac{1}{2}\Bigg)^{2}\Bigg]

\displaystyle=\frac{4m}{L^{2}}\mathbb{E}_{\pi}^{\rho}\Bigg[\Bigg(\frac{q_{\pi}^{\rho}(X)-q_{\pi^{(i)}}^{\rho^{(i)}}(X)}{2\big(q_{\pi^{(i)}}^{\rho^{(i)}}(X)^{1/2}+q_{\pi}^{\rho}(X)^{1/2}\big)^{2}}\Bigg)^{2}\Bigg]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{m}{L^{2}}\mathbb{E}_{\pi}^{\rho}\Bigg[\Bigg(\frac{q_{\pi}^{\rho}(X)-q_{\pi^{(i)}}^{\rho^{(i)}}(X)}{q_{\pi}^{\rho}(X)}\Bigg)^{2}\Bigg]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{m}{L^{2}}\sum_{j=0}^{m}\pi_{j}\mathbb{E}_{j}^{\rho}\Bigg[\Bigg(\frac{q_{\pi}^{\rho}(X)-q_{\pi^{(i)}}^{\rho^{(i)}}(X)}{q_{\pi}^{\rho}(X)}\Bigg)^{2}\Bigg].

Since we already showed that each summand in the last line displayed above converges to
0
, it follows that the LHS converges to
0
as well. Thus, we have shown that all three integrals (involving
K_{1},K_{2}
and
K_{3}
) converge to
0
, so it follows that

\int\frac{\big[\mathcal{J}^{\theta^{(i)},\tau}(z)^{1/2}-\mathcal{J}^{\theta,\tau}(z)^{1/2}-D(z,\theta)^{\prime}(\theta^{(i)}-\theta)\big]^{2}}{||\theta^{(i)}-\theta||_{2}^{2}}d\widetilde{\mu}(z)\to 0

as well, and so property (c) has been verified!

Next, let’s prove property (b)
. Observe that:

\displaystyle||D(z,\theta^{(i)})-D(z,\theta)||_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=||D(z,\pi^{(i)};\rho^{(i)})-D(z,\pi;\rho)||_{2}^{2}+||D(z,\rho^{(i)};\pi^{(i)})-D(z,\rho;\pi)||_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{d\tau}{4}\overbrace{\Big|\Big|q_{\pi^{(i)}}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)^{1/2}s_{\pi^{(i)}}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)-q_{\pi}^{\rho}(x)^{1/2}s_{\pi}^{\rho}(x)\Big|\Big|_{2}^{2}}^{=:\ W_{1}(z,\theta,\theta^{(i)})}

\displaystyle\hskip 18.49988pt+\frac{d\tau}{4}\overbrace{\Bigg|\Bigg|\sum_{j=0}^{m}\pi_{j}^{(i)}\frac{q_{j}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)}{\sqrt{q_{\pi^{(i)}}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)}}\frac{\pi_{j}^{\ast}}{\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}(s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau)}(x)-\mathbb{E}_{j}^{\rho^{(i)}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau)}])-\pi_{j}\frac{q_{j}^{\rho}(x)}{\sqrt{q_{\pi}^{\rho}(x)}}\frac{\pi_{j}^{\ast}}{\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}(s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau)}(x)-\mathbb{E}_{j}^{\rho}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau)}])\Bigg|\Bigg|_{2}^{2}}^{=:\ W_{2}(z,\theta,\theta^{(i)})}

\displaystyle\hskip 18.49988pt+\frac{(1-d)(1-\tau)\pi_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}{4}\frac{(\pi_{y}^{\ast})^{2}}{(\pi_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr}}})^{2}}\overbrace{\Big|\Big|\big(s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau)}(x)-\mathbb{E}_{y}^{\rho^{(i)}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau)}]\big)q_{y}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)^{1/2}-\big(s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau)}(x)-\mathbb{E}_{y}^{\rho}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau)}]\big)q_{y}^{\rho}(x)^{1/2}\Big|\Big|_{2}^{2}}^{=:\ W_{3}(z,\theta,\theta^{(i)})}.

Ergo:

\displaystyle\int||D(z,\theta^{(i)})-D(z,\theta)||_{2}^{2}d\widetilde{\mu}(z)

\displaystyle\leq\int dW_{1}(z,\theta,\theta^{(i)})\ d\widetilde{\mu}(z)+\int dW_{2}(z,\theta,\theta^{(i)})\ d\widetilde{\mu}(z)+\frac{(1-\xi)^{2}}{\xi}\int(1-d)W_{3}(z,\theta,\theta^{(i)})\ d\widetilde{\mu}(z).

Let’s start with tackling the
W_{1}
term. Observe that:

\displaystyle W_{1}(z,\theta,\theta^{(i)})^{1/2}

\displaystyle=\Big|\Big|q_{\pi^{(i)}}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)^{1/2}s_{\pi^{(i)}}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)-q_{\pi}^{\rho}(x)^{1/2}s_{\pi}^{\rho}(x)\Big|\Big|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\Big|\Big|q_{\pi^{(i)}}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)^{1/2}s_{\pi^{(i)}}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)-q_{\pi}^{\rho}(x)^{1/2}s_{\pi^{(i)}}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)\Big|\Big|_{2}+\Big|\Big|q_{\pi}^{\rho}(x)^{1/2}s_{\pi^{(i)}}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)-q_{\pi}^{\rho}(x)^{1/2}s_{\pi}^{\rho}(x)\Big|\Big|_{2}

\displaystyle=\Big|\Big|q_{\pi^{(i)}}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)^{1/2}s_{\pi^{(i)}}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)-q_{\pi}^{\rho}(x)^{1/2}s_{\pi^{(i)}}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)\Big|\Big|_{2}+q_{\pi}^{\rho}(x)^{1/2}\Big|\Big|s_{\pi^{(i)}}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)-s_{\pi}^{\rho}(x)\Big|\Big|_{2}.

Note that:

\displaystyle\Big|\Big|q_{\pi^{(i)}}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)^{1/2}s_{\pi^{(i)}}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)-q_{\pi}^{\rho}(x)^{1/2}s_{\pi^{(i)}}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)\Big|\Big|_{2}

\displaystyle=\sqrt{\sum_{j=1}^{m}\Big(q_{\pi^{(i)}}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)^{1/2}s_{\pi^{(i)},j}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)-q_{\pi}^{\rho}(x)^{1/2}s_{\pi^{(i)},j}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)\Big)^{2}}

\displaystyle=\sqrt{\sum_{j=1}^{m}s_{\pi^{(i)},j}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)^{2}\Big(q_{\pi^{(i)}}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)^{1/2}-q_{\pi}^{\rho}(x)^{1/2}\Big)^{2}}

\displaystyle=\Big|q_{\pi^{(i)}}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)^{1/2}-q_{\pi}^{\rho}(x)^{1/2}\Big|\sqrt{\sum_{j=1}^{m}s_{\pi^{(i)},j}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)^{2}}

\displaystyle=\Big|q_{\pi^{(i)}}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)^{1/2}-q_{\pi}^{\rho}(x)^{1/2}\Big|||s_{\pi^{(i)}}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)||_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2\sqrt{m}}{\xi}\Big|q_{\pi^{(i)}}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)^{1/2}-q_{\pi}^{\rho}(x)^{1/2}\Big|

\displaystyle=\frac{2\sqrt{m}}{\xi}\frac{\big|q_{\pi^{(i)}}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)-q_{\pi}^{\rho}(x)\big|}{q_{\pi^{(i)}}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)^{1/2}+q_{\pi}^{\rho}(x)^{1/2}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2\sqrt{m}}{\xi}\frac{\big|q_{\pi^{(i)}}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)-q_{\pi}^{\rho}(x)\big|}{q_{\pi}^{\rho}(x)^{1/2}}.

Thus, by equations (
44
) and (
46
), we have that:

\displaystyle\Big|\Big|q_{\pi^{(i)}}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)^{1/2}s_{\pi^{(i)}}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)-q_{\pi}^{\rho}(x)^{1/2}s_{\pi^{(i)}}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)\Big|\Big|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2\sqrt{m}}{\xi}\frac{\big|\sum_{j=0}^{m}\pi_{j}^{(i)}(q_{j}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)-q_{j}^{\rho}(x))+q_{\pi}^{\rho}(x)\langle s_{\pi}^{\rho}(x),\pi^{(i)}-\pi\rangle\big|}{q_{\pi}^{\rho}(x)^{1/2}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2\sqrt{m}}{\xi}\Bigg\{\frac{\sum_{j=0}^{m}\pi_{j}^{(i)}\big|q_{j}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)-q_{j}^{\rho}(x)\big|+q_{\pi}^{\rho}(x)|\langle s_{\pi}^{\rho}(x),\pi^{(i)}-\pi\rangle|}{q_{\pi}^{\rho}(x)^{1/2}}\Bigg\}

\displaystyle=\frac{2\sqrt{m}}{\xi}\Bigg\{\sum_{j=0}^{m}\pi_{j}^{(i)}\frac{\big|q_{j}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)-q_{j}^{\rho}(x)\big|}{q_{\pi}^{\rho}(x)^{1/2}}+q_{\pi}^{\rho}(x)^{1/2}|\langle s_{\pi}^{\rho}(x),\pi^{(i)}-\pi\rangle|\Bigg\}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2\sqrt{m}}{\xi^{3/2}}\Bigg\{\sum_{j=0}^{m}\frac{\big|q_{j}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)-q_{j}^{\rho}(x)\big|}{q_{j}^{\rho}(x)^{1/2}}+\frac{2\sqrt{m}}{\xi}||\pi^{(i)}-\pi||_{2}q_{\pi}^{\rho}(x)^{1/2}\Bigg\}

\displaystyle=\frac{2\sqrt{m}}{\xi^{3/2}}\Bigg\{\sum_{j=0}^{m}q_{j}^{\rho}(x)^{1/2}\frac{\big|q_{j}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)-q_{j}^{\rho}(x)\big|}{q_{j}^{\rho}(x)}+\frac{2\sqrt{m}}{\xi}||\pi^{(i)}-\pi||_{2}q_{\pi}^{\rho}(x)^{1/2}\Bigg\}

\displaystyle=\frac{2\sqrt{m}}{\xi^{3/2}}\Bigg\{\sum_{j=0}^{m}q_{j}^{\rho}(x)^{1/2}\frac{\pi_{j}^{\ast}}{\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\Bigg|\big\langle s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau)}(x)-\mathbb{E}_{j}^{\rho}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau)}],\rho^{(i)}-\rho\big\rangle+\frac{||\rho^{(i)}-\rho||_{2}^{2}}{2f_{j}(\rho;x)}\lambda_{\textup{max}}\Bigg\{\frac{\delta^{2}}{\delta{{\rho}^{2}}}f_{j}(\bar{\rho}_{x,j};x)\Bigg\}\Bigg|

\displaystyle\hskip 17.70859pt\hskip 17.70859pt\hskip 17.70859pt+\frac{2\sqrt{m}}{\xi}||\pi^{(i)}-\pi||_{2}q_{\pi}^{\rho}(x)^{1/2}\Bigg\}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2\sqrt{m}}{\xi^{3/2}}\Bigg\{\sum_{j=0}^{m}q_{j}^{\rho}(x)^{1/2}\Bigg|\frac{4\sqrt{m}(1-\xi)}{L\xi}||\rho^{(i)}-\rho||_{2}+\frac{||\rho^{(i)}-\rho||_{2}^{2}}{2f_{j}(\rho;x)}\lambda_{\textup{max}}\Bigg\{\frac{\delta^{2}}{\delta{{\rho}^{2}}}f_{j}(\bar{\rho}_{x,j};x)\Bigg\}\Bigg|

\displaystyle\hskip 17.70859pt\hskip 17.70859pt\hskip 17.70859pt+\frac{2\sqrt{m}}{\xi}||\pi^{(i)}-\pi||_{2}q_{\pi}^{\rho}(x)^{1/2}\Bigg\}

\displaystyle=\frac{2\sqrt{m}}{\xi^{3/2}}\sum_{j=0}^{m}q_{j}^{\rho}(x)^{1/2}\Bigg|\frac{4\sqrt{m}(1-\xi)}{L\xi}||\rho^{(i)}-\rho||_{2}+\frac{||\rho^{(i)}-\rho||_{2}^{2}}{2f_{j}(\rho;x)}\lambda_{\textup{max}}\Bigg\{\frac{\delta^{2}}{\delta{{\rho}^{2}}}f_{j}(\bar{\rho}_{x,j};x)\Bigg\}\Bigg|

\displaystyle\hskip 17.70859pt\hskip 17.70859pt\hskip 17.70859pt+\frac{4m}{\xi^{5/2}}||\pi^{(i)}-\pi||_{2}q_{\pi}^{\rho}(x)^{1/2}.

Next, observe that:

\displaystyle\Big|\Big|s_{\pi^{(i)}}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)-s_{\pi}^{\rho}(x)\Big|\Big|_{2}

\displaystyle=\sqrt{\sum_{j=1}^{m}\Big(s_{\pi^{(i)},j}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)-s_{\pi,j}^{\rho}(x)\Big)^{2}}

\displaystyle=\sqrt{\sum_{j=1}^{m}\Bigg(\frac{q_{j}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)-q_{0}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)}{q_{\pi^{(i)}}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)}-\frac{q_{j}^{\rho}(x)-q_{0}^{\rho}(x)}{q_{\pi}^{\rho}(x)}\Bigg)^{2}}

which, after some simple algebra, can be shown to be upper bounded by

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{2\sum_{j=1}^{m}\Bigg[\Bigg(\frac{[q_{j}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)-q_{0}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)]-[q_{j}^{\rho}(x)-q_{0}^{\rho}(x)]}{q_{\pi^{(i)}}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)}\Bigg)^{2}+\Bigg(\frac{[q_{j}^{\rho}(x)-q_{0}^{\rho}(x)][q_{\pi^{(i)}}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)-q_{\pi}^{\rho}(x)]}{q_{\pi^{(i)}}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)q_{\pi}^{\rho}(x)}\Bigg)^{2}\Bigg]}

\displaystyle=\sqrt{2\sum_{j=1}^{m}\Bigg(\frac{[q_{j}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)-q_{j}^{\rho}(x)]}{q_{\pi^{(i)}}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)}+\frac{[q_{0}^{\rho}(x)-q_{0}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)]}{q_{\pi^{(i)}}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)}\Bigg)^{2}+2\Bigg(\frac{[q_{\pi^{(i)}}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)-q_{\pi}^{\rho}(x)]}{q_{\pi}^{\rho}(x)}\Bigg)^{2}\sum_{j=1}^{m}\Bigg(\frac{[q_{j}^{\rho}(x)-q_{0}^{\rho}(x)]}{q_{\pi^{(i)}}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)}\Bigg)^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{4m\sum_{j=1}^{m}\Bigg(\frac{q_{j}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)-q_{j}^{\rho}(x)}{q_{\pi^{(i)}}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)}\Bigg)^{2}+4m\Bigg(\frac{q_{0}^{\rho}(x)-q_{0}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)}{q_{\pi^{(i)}}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)}\Bigg)^{2}+2\Bigg(\frac{q_{\pi^{(i)}}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)-q_{\pi}^{\rho}(x)}{q_{\pi}^{\rho}(x)}\Bigg)^{2}\sum_{j=1}^{m}\Bigg(\frac{q_{j}^{\rho}(x)}{q_{\pi^{(i)}}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)}+\frac{q_{0}^{\rho}(x)}{q_{\pi^{(i)}}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)}\Bigg)^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{4m\sum_{j=0}^{m}\Bigg(\frac{q_{j}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)-q_{j}^{\rho}(x)}{\pi_{j}^{(i)}q_{j}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)}\Bigg)^{2}+2\Bigg(\frac{q_{\pi^{(i)}}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)-q_{\pi}^{\rho}(x)}{q_{\pi}^{\rho}(x)}\Bigg)^{2}\sum_{j=1}^{m}\Bigg(\frac{q_{j}^{\rho}(x)}{\pi_{j}^{(i)}q_{j}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)}+\frac{q_{0}^{\rho}(x)}{\pi_{0}^{(i)}q_{0}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)}\Bigg)^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{\frac{4m}{\xi^{2}}\sum_{j=0}^{m}\Bigg(\frac{q_{j}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)-q_{j}^{\rho}(x)}{q_{j}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)}\Bigg)^{2}+\frac{2}{\xi^{2}}\Bigg(\frac{q_{\pi^{(i)}}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)-q_{\pi}^{\rho}(x)}{q_{\pi}^{\rho}(x)}\Bigg)^{2}\sum_{j=1}^{m}\Bigg(\frac{q_{j}^{\rho}(x)}{q_{j}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)}+\frac{q_{0}^{\rho}(x)}{q_{0}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)}\Bigg)^{2}}

\displaystyle=\sqrt{\frac{4m}{\xi^{2}}\sum_{j=0}^{m}\Bigg(\frac{q_{j}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)-q_{j}^{\rho}(x)}{q_{j}^{\rho}(x)}\Bigg)^{2}\Bigg(\frac{f_{j}(\rho;x)}{f_{j}(\rho^{(i)};x)}\Bigg)^{2}+\frac{2}{\xi^{2}}\Bigg(\frac{q_{\pi^{(i)}}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)-q_{\pi}^{\rho}(x)}{q_{\pi}^{\rho}(x)}\Bigg)^{2}\sum_{j=1}^{m}\Bigg(\frac{f_{j}(\rho;x)}{f_{j}(\rho^{(i)};x)}+\frac{f_{0}(\rho;x)}{f_{0}(\rho^{(i)};x)}\Bigg)^{2}}.

Thus, overall, we have that:

\displaystyle\int dW_{1}(z,\theta,\theta^{(i)})\ d\widetilde{\mu}(z)

\displaystyle\leq\int d\bigg(\Big|\Big|q_{\pi^{(i)}}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)^{1/2}s_{\pi^{(i)}}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)-q_{\pi}^{\rho}(x)^{1/2}s_{\pi^{(i)}}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)\Big|\Big|_{2}+q_{\pi}^{\rho}(x)^{1/2}\Big|\Big|s_{\pi^{(i)}}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)-s_{\pi}^{\rho}(x)\Big|\Big|_{2}\bigg)^{2}\ d\widetilde{\mu}(z)

\displaystyle\leq 2\int d\Big|\Big|q_{\pi^{(i)}}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)^{1/2}s_{\pi^{(i)}}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)-q_{\pi}^{\rho}(x)^{1/2}s_{\pi^{(i)}}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)\Big|\Big|_{2}^{2}\ d\widetilde{\mu}(z)+2\int dq_{\pi}^{\rho}(x)\Big|\Big|s_{\pi^{(i)}}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)-s_{\pi}^{\rho}(x)\Big|\Big|_{2}^{2}\ d\widetilde{\mu}(z)

\displaystyle=2\int d\Big|\Big|q_{\pi^{(i)}}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)^{1/2}s_{\pi^{(i)}}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)-q_{\pi}^{\rho}(x)^{1/2}s_{\pi^{(i)}}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)\Big|\Big|_{2}^{2}\ d\widetilde{\mu}(z)+2\mathbb{E}_{\pi}^{\rho}\Big[\Big|\Big|s_{\pi^{(i)}}^{\rho^{(i)}}(X)-s_{\pi}^{\rho}(X)\Big|\Big|_{2}^{2}\Big],

where

\displaystyle\int d\Big|\Big|q_{\pi^{(i)}}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)^{1/2}s_{\pi^{(i)}}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)-q_{\pi}^{\rho}(x)^{1/2}s_{\pi^{(i)}}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)\Big|\Big|_{2}^{2}\ d\widetilde{\mu}(z)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{8m^{2}}{\xi^{3}}\sum_{j=0}^{m}\int dq_{j}^{\rho}(x)\Bigg(\frac{4\sqrt{m}(1-\xi)}{L\xi}||\rho^{(i)}-\rho||_{2}+\frac{||\rho^{(i)}-\rho||_{2}^{2}}{2f_{j}(\rho;x)}\lambda_{\textup{max}}\Bigg\{\frac{\delta^{2}}{\delta{{\rho}^{2}}}f_{j}(\bar{\rho}_{x,j};x)\Bigg\}\Bigg)^{2}\ d\widetilde{\mu}(z)

\displaystyle\hskip 17.70859pt\hskip 17.70859pt+\frac{32m^{2}}{\xi^{5}}||\pi^{(i)}-\pi||_{2}^{2}\int dq_{\pi}^{\rho}(x)\ d\widetilde{\mu}(z)

\displaystyle=\frac{8m^{2}}{\xi^{3}}\sum_{j=0}^{m}\mathbb{E}_{j}^{\rho}\Bigg[\Bigg(\frac{4\sqrt{m}(1-\xi)}{L\xi}||\rho^{(i)}-\rho||_{2}+\frac{||\rho^{(i)}-\rho||_{2}^{2}}{2f_{j}(\rho;X)}\lambda_{\textup{max}}\Bigg\{\frac{\delta^{2}}{\delta{{\rho}^{2}}}f_{j}(\bar{\rho}_{X,j};X)\Bigg\}\Bigg)^{2}\Bigg]

\displaystyle\hskip 17.70859pt\hskip 17.70859pt+\frac{32m^{2}}{\xi^{5}}||\pi^{(i)}-\pi||_{2}^{2}.

Since
||s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau)}||_{2}
and
||\rho||_{2}
are bounded, one may show that
\frac{1}{f_{j}(\rho;x)}\lambda_{\textup{max}}\Big\{\frac{\delta^{2}}{\delta{{\rho}^{2}}}f_{j}(\bar{\rho}_{x,j};x)\Big\}
is uniformly bounded for all
x\in\mathcal{X}
, meaning that the last line above tends to
0
as
\theta^{(i)}\to\theta
. Also note that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\pi}^{\rho}\Big[\Big|\Big|s_{\pi^{(i)}}^{\rho^{(i)}}(X)-s_{\pi}^{\rho}(X)\Big|\Big|_{2}^{2}\Big]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}_{\pi}^{\rho}\Bigg[\frac{4m}{\xi^{2}}\sum_{j=0}^{m}\Bigg(\frac{q_{j}^{\rho^{(i)}}(X)-q_{j}^{\rho}(X)}{q_{j}^{\rho}(X)}\Bigg)^{2}\Bigg(\frac{f_{j}(\rho;x)}{f_{j}(\rho^{(i)};X)}\Bigg)^{2}+\frac{2}{\xi^{2}}\Bigg(\frac{q_{\pi^{(i)}}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)-q_{\pi}^{\rho}(X)}{q_{\pi}^{\rho}(X)}\Bigg)^{2}\sum_{j=1}^{m}\Bigg(\frac{f_{j}(\rho;X)}{f_{j}(\rho^{(i)};X)}+\frac{f_{0}(\rho;X)}{f_{0}(\rho^{(i)};X)}\Bigg)^{2}\Bigg],

which by applying equations (
48
) and (
50
), can be showed to converge to zero via an analogous argument. Thus, we may conclude that
\int dW_{1}(z,\theta,\theta^{(i)})\ d\widetilde{\mu}(z)\to 0
as
\theta^{(i)}\to\theta
.

Next, we show that
\int dW_{2}(z,\theta,\theta^{(i)})\ d\widetilde{\mu}(z)
converges to 0. Towards that end, define
\epsilon_{j}(x,\rho,\rho^{(i)}):=\frac{\delta}{\delta{\rho}}\{f_{j}(\rho^{(i)};x)\}-\frac{\delta}{\delta{\rho}}\{f_{j}(\rho;x)\}
, and observe that:

\displaystyle W_{2}(z,\theta,\theta^{(i)})^{1/2}

\displaystyle=\Bigg|\Bigg|\sum_{j=0}^{m}\pi_{j}^{(i)}\frac{\pi_{j}^{\ast}}{\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\frac{q_{j}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)}{\sqrt{q_{\pi^{(i)}}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)}}(s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau)}(x)-\mathbb{E}_{j}^{\rho^{(i)}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau)}])-\pi_{j}\frac{\pi_{j}^{\ast}}{\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\frac{q_{j}^{\rho}(x)}{\sqrt{q_{\pi}^{\rho}(x)}}(s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau)}(x)-\mathbb{E}_{j}^{\rho}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau)}])\Bigg|\Bigg|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{j=0}^{m}\frac{\pi_{j}^{\ast}}{\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\Bigg|\Bigg|\pi_{j}^{(i)}\frac{q_{j}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)}{\sqrt{q_{\pi^{(i)}}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)}}(s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau)}(x)-\mathbb{E}_{j}^{\rho^{(i)}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau)}])-\pi_{j}\frac{q_{j}^{\rho}(x)}{\sqrt{q_{\pi}^{\rho}(x)}}(s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau)}(x)-\mathbb{E}_{j}^{\rho}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau)}])\Bigg|\Bigg|_{2}

\displaystyle=\sum_{j=0}^{m}\frac{\pi_{j}^{\ast}}{\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\Bigg|\Bigg|\pi_{j}^{(i)}\frac{p_{j}(x)\frac{\delta}{\delta{\rho}}\{f_{j}(\rho^{(i)};x)\}}{\sqrt{q_{\pi^{(i)}}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)}}-\pi_{j}\frac{p_{j}(x)\frac{\delta}{\delta{\rho}}\{f_{j}(\rho;x)\}}{\sqrt{q_{\pi}^{\rho}(x)}}\Bigg|\Bigg|_{2}

\displaystyle=\sum_{j=0}^{m}\frac{\pi_{j}^{\ast}}{\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}p_{j}(x)\Bigg|\Bigg|\pi_{j}^{(i)}\frac{\frac{\delta}{\delta{\rho}}\{f_{j}(\rho;x)\}+\epsilon_{j}(x,\rho,\rho^{(i)})}{\sqrt{q_{\pi^{(i)}}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)}}-\pi_{j}\frac{\frac{\delta}{\delta{\rho}}\{f_{j}(\rho;x)\}}{\sqrt{q_{\pi}^{\rho}(x)}}\Bigg|\Bigg|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1-\xi}{\xi}\sum_{j=0}^{m}p_{j}(x)\Bigg|\Bigg|\pi_{j}^{(i)}\frac{\frac{\delta}{\delta{\rho}}\{f_{j}(\rho;x)\}}{\sqrt{q_{\pi^{(i)}}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)}}-\pi_{j}\frac{\frac{\delta}{\delta{\rho}}\{f_{j}(\rho;x)\}}{\sqrt{q_{\pi}^{\rho}(x)}}\Bigg|\Bigg|_{2}+\frac{1-\xi}{\xi}\sum_{j=0}^{m}p_{j}(x)\Bigg|\Bigg|\frac{\epsilon_{j}(x,\rho,\rho^{(i)})}{\sqrt{q_{\pi^{(i)}}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)}}\Bigg|\Bigg|_{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{1-\xi}{\xi}\sum_{j=0}^{m}p_{j}(x)\bigg|\bigg|\frac{\delta}{\delta{\rho}}\{f_{j}(\rho;x)\}\bigg|\bigg|_{2}\Bigg|\frac{\pi_{j}^{(i)}}{q_{\pi^{(i)}}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)^{1/2}}-\frac{\pi_{j}}{q_{\pi}^{\rho}(x)^{1/2}}\Bigg|+\frac{1-\xi}{\xi}\sum_{j=0}^{m}p_{j}(x)\Bigg|\Bigg|\frac{\epsilon_{j}(x,\rho,\rho^{(i)})}{\sqrt{q_{\pi^{(i)}}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)}}\Bigg|\Bigg|_{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{1-\xi}{\xi}\sum_{j=0}^{m}q_{j}^{\rho}(x)\big|\big|s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau)}(x)-\mathbb{E}_{j}^{\rho}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau)}]\big|\big|_{2}\Bigg|\frac{\pi_{j}^{(i)}}{q_{\pi^{(i)}}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)^{1/2}}-\frac{\pi_{j}}{q_{\pi}^{\rho}(x)^{1/2}}\Bigg|+\frac{1-\xi}{\xi}\sum_{j=0}^{m}p_{j}(x)\Bigg|\Bigg|\frac{\epsilon_{j}(x,\rho,\rho^{(i)})}{\sqrt{q_{\pi^{(i)}}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)}}\Bigg|\Bigg|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{4\sqrt{m}(1-\xi)}{L\xi}\sum_{j=0}^{m}q_{j}^{\rho}(x)\Bigg|\frac{\pi_{j}^{(i)}}{q_{\pi^{(i)}}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)^{1/2}}-\frac{\pi_{j}}{q_{\pi}^{\rho}(x)^{1/2}}\Bigg|+\frac{1-\xi}{\xi^{3/2}}\sum_{j=0}^{m}p_{j}(x)\Bigg|\Bigg|\frac{\epsilon_{j}(x,\rho,\rho^{(i)})}{\sqrt{q_{j}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)}}\Bigg|\Bigg|_{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{4\sqrt{m}(1-\xi)}{L\xi}\sum_{j=0}^{m}q_{j}^{\rho}(x)\Bigg|\frac{\pi_{j}^{(i)}}{q_{\pi^{(i)}}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)^{1/2}}-\frac{\pi_{j}}{q_{\pi}^{\rho}(x)^{1/2}}\Bigg|+\frac{1-\xi}{\xi^{3/2}}\sum_{j=0}^{m}\frac{\sqrt{p_{j}(x)}}{\sqrt{f_{j}(\rho^{(i)},x)}}\big|\big|\epsilon_{j}(x,\rho,\rho^{(i)})\big|\big|_{2}.

Note that:

\displaystyle\Bigg|\frac{\pi_{j}^{(i)}}{q_{\pi^{(i)}}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)^{1/2}}-\frac{\pi_{j}}{q_{\pi}^{\rho}(x)^{1/2}}\Bigg|

\displaystyle=\Bigg|\frac{\pi_{j}^{(i)}q_{\pi}^{\rho}(x)^{1/2}-\pi_{j}q_{\pi^{(i)}}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)^{1/2}}{q_{\pi^{(i)}}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)^{1/2}q_{\pi}^{\rho}(x)^{1/2}}\Bigg|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\Big|\pi_{j}^{(i)}q_{\pi}^{\rho}(x)^{1/2}-\pi_{j}^{(i)}q_{\pi^{(i)}}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)^{1/2}\Big|+\Big|\pi_{j}^{(i)}q_{\pi^{(i)}}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)^{1/2}-\pi_{j}q_{\pi^{(i)}}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)^{1/2}\Big|}{q_{\pi^{(i)}}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)^{1/2}q_{\pi}^{\rho}(x)^{1/2}}

\displaystyle=\frac{\pi_{j}^{(i)}}{q_{\pi}^{\rho}(x)^{1/2}}\frac{\Big|q_{\pi}^{\rho}(x)^{1/2}-q_{\pi^{(i)}}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)^{1/2}\Big|}{q_{\pi^{(i)}}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)^{1/2}}+\frac{\big|\pi_{j}^{(i)}-\pi_{j}\big|}{q_{\pi}^{\rho}(x)^{1/2}}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{q_{\pi}^{\rho}(x)^{1/2}}\Bigg(\pi_{j}^{(i)}\frac{\big|q_{\pi}^{\rho}(x)^{1/2}-q_{\pi^{(i)}}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)^{1/2}\big|}{q_{\pi^{(i)}}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)^{1/2}}+\big|\pi_{j}^{(i)}-\pi_{j}\big|\Bigg)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\sqrt{\xi}q_{j}^{\rho}(x)^{1/2}}\Bigg(\frac{\big|q_{\pi}^{\rho}(x)^{1/2}-q_{\pi^{(i)}}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)^{1/2}\big|}{q_{\pi^{(i)}}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)^{1/2}}+\big|\pi_{j}^{(i)}-\pi_{j}\big|\Bigg)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sqrt{\xi}q_{j}^{\rho}(x)^{1/2}}\Bigg(\frac{\big|q_{\pi}^{\rho}(x)^{1/2}-q_{\pi^{(i)}}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)^{1/2}\big|}{q_{\pi}^{\rho}(x)^{1/2}}\frac{q_{\pi}^{\rho}(x)^{1/2}}{q_{\pi^{(i)}}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)^{1/2}}+\big|\pi_{j}^{(i)}-\pi_{j}\big|\Bigg)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sqrt{\xi}q_{j}^{\rho}(x)^{1/2}}\Bigg(\frac{\big|q_{\pi}^{\rho}(x)-q_{\pi^{(i)}}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)\big|}{q_{\pi}^{\rho}(x)^{1/2}\big(q_{\pi}^{\rho}(x)^{1/2}+q_{\pi^{(i)}}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)^{1/2}\big)}\frac{q_{\pi}^{\rho}(x)^{1/2}}{q_{\pi^{(i)}}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)^{1/2}}+\big|\pi_{j}^{(i)}-\pi_{j}\big|\Bigg)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\sqrt{\xi}q_{j}^{\rho}(x)^{1/2}}\Bigg(\frac{\big|q_{\pi}^{\rho}(x)-q_{\pi^{(i)}}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)\big|}{q_{\pi}^{\rho}(x)}\frac{q_{\pi}^{\rho}(x)^{1/2}}{q_{\pi^{(i)}}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)^{1/2}}+\big|\pi_{j}^{(i)}-\pi_{j}\big|\Bigg).

Therefore:

\displaystyle W_{2}(z,\theta,\theta^{(i)})^{1/2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{4\sqrt{m}}{L\xi^{3/2}}\sum_{j=0}^{m}q_{j}^{\rho}(x)^{1/2}\Bigg(\frac{\big|q_{\pi}^{\rho}(x)-q_{\pi^{(i)}}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)\big|}{q_{\pi}^{\rho}(x)}\frac{q_{\pi}^{\rho}(x)^{1/2}}{q_{\pi^{(i)}}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)^{1/2}}+\big|\pi_{j}^{(i)}-\pi_{j}\big|\Bigg)+\frac{1}{\xi^{3/2}}\sum_{j=0}^{m}\frac{\sqrt{p_{j}(x)}}{\sqrt{f_{j}(\rho^{(i)},x)}}\big|\big|\epsilon_{j}(x,\rho,\rho^{(i)})\big|\big|_{2}.

And so:

\displaystyle W_{2}(z,\theta,\theta^{(i)})

\displaystyle\leq\frac{32m^{2}}{L^{2}\xi^{3}}\sum_{j=0}^{m}q_{j}^{\rho}(x)\Bigg(\frac{\big|q_{\pi}^{\rho}(x)-q_{\pi^{(i)}}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)\big|}{q_{\pi}^{\rho}(x)}\frac{q_{\pi}^{\rho}(x)^{1/2}}{q_{\pi^{(i)}}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)^{1/2}}+\big|\pi_{j}^{(i)}-\pi_{j}\big|\Bigg)^{2}+\frac{2m}{\xi^{3}}\sum_{j=0}^{m}\frac{p_{j}(x)}{f_{j}(\rho^{(i)},x)}\big|\big|\epsilon_{j}(x,\rho,\rho^{(i)})\big|\big|_{2}^{2}

Meaning:

\displaystyle\int dW_{2}(z,\theta,\theta^{(i)})\ d\widetilde{\mu}(z)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{32m^{2}}{L^{2}\xi^{3}}\sum_{j=0}^{m}\mathbb{E}_{j}^{\rho}\Bigg[\Bigg(\frac{\big|q_{\pi}^{\rho}(X)-q_{\pi^{(i)}}^{\rho^{(i)}}(X)\big|}{q_{\pi}^{\rho}(X)}\frac{q_{\pi}^{\rho}(X)^{1/2}}{q_{\pi^{(i)}}^{\rho^{(i)}}(X)^{1/2}}+\big|\pi_{j}^{(i)}-\pi_{j}\big|\Bigg)^{2}\Bigg]+\frac{2m}{\xi^{3}}\sum_{j=0}^{m}\mathbb{E}_{j}\Bigg[\frac{\big|\big|\epsilon_{j}(X,\rho,\rho^{(i)})\big|\big|_{2}^{2}}{f_{j}(\rho^{(i)},X)}\Bigg]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{64m^{2}}{L^{2}\xi^{3}}\sum_{j=0}^{m}\mathbb{E}_{j}^{\rho}\Bigg[\Bigg(\frac{\big|q_{\pi}^{\rho}(X)-q_{\pi^{(i)}}^{\rho^{(i)}}(X)\big|}{q_{\pi}^{\rho}(X)}\frac{q_{\pi}^{\rho}(X)^{1/2}}{q_{\pi^{(i)}}^{\rho^{(i)}}(X)^{1/2}}\Bigg)^{2}\Bigg]+\frac{64m^{2}}{L^{2}\xi^{3}}\sum_{j=0}^{m}\big|\pi_{j}^{(i)}-\pi_{j}\big|^{2}+\frac{2m}{\xi^{3}}\sum_{j=0}^{m}\mathbb{E}_{j}\Bigg[\frac{\big|\big|\epsilon_{j}(X,\rho,\rho^{(i)})\big|\big|_{2}^{2}}{f_{j}(\rho^{(i)},X)}\Bigg]

\displaystyle=\frac{64m^{2}}{L^{2}\xi^{3}}\sum_{j=0}^{m}\mathbb{E}_{j}^{\rho}\Bigg[\Bigg(\frac{q_{\pi}^{\rho}(X)-q_{\pi^{(i)}}^{\rho^{(i)}}(X)}{q_{\pi}^{\rho}(X)}\Bigg)^{2}\frac{q_{\pi}^{\rho}(X)}{q_{\pi^{(i)}}^{\rho^{(i)}}(X)}\Bigg]+\frac{64m^{2}}{L^{2}\xi^{3}}||\pi^{(i)}-\pi||_{2}^{2}+\frac{2m}{\xi^{3}}\sum_{j=0}^{m}\mathbb{E}_{j}\Bigg[\frac{\big|\big|\epsilon_{j}(X,\rho,\rho^{(i)})\big|\big|_{2}^{2}}{f_{j}(\rho^{(i)},X)}\Bigg]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{64m^{2}}{L^{2}\xi^{4}}\sum_{j=0}^{m}\mathbb{E}_{j}^{\rho}\Bigg[\Bigg(\frac{q_{\pi}^{\rho}(X)-q_{\pi^{(i)}}^{\rho^{(i)}}(X)}{q_{\pi}^{\rho}(X)}\Bigg)^{2}\frac{q_{\pi}^{\rho}(X)}{q_{j}^{\rho^{(i)}}(X)}\Bigg]+\frac{64m^{2}}{L^{2}\xi^{3}}||\pi^{(i)}-\pi||_{2}^{2}+\frac{2m}{\xi^{3}}\sum_{j=0}^{m}\mathbb{E}_{j}\Bigg[\frac{\big|\big|\epsilon_{j}(X,\rho,\rho^{(i)})\big|\big|_{2}^{2}}{f_{j}(\rho^{(i)},X)}\Bigg]

\displaystyle=\frac{64m^{2}}{L^{2}\xi^{4}}\sum_{j=0}^{m}\mathbb{E}_{\pi}^{\rho}\Bigg[\Bigg(\frac{q_{\pi}^{\rho}(X)-q_{\pi^{(i)}}^{\rho^{(i)}}(X)}{q_{\pi}^{\rho}(X)}\Bigg)^{2}\frac{q_{j}^{\rho}(X)}{q_{j}^{\rho^{(i)}}(X)}\Bigg]+\frac{64m^{2}}{L^{2}\xi^{3}}||\pi^{(i)}-\pi||_{2}^{2}+\frac{2m}{\xi^{3}}\sum_{j=0}^{m}\mathbb{E}_{j}\Bigg[\frac{\big|\big|\epsilon_{j}(X,\rho,\rho^{(i)})\big|\big|_{2}^{2}}{f_{j}(\rho^{(i)},X)}\Bigg]

\displaystyle=\frac{64m^{2}}{L^{2}\xi^{4}}\mathbb{E}_{\pi}^{\rho}\Bigg[\Bigg(\frac{q_{\pi}^{\rho}(X)-q_{\pi^{(i)}}^{\rho^{(i)}}(X)}{q_{\pi}^{\rho}(X)}\Bigg)^{2}\sum_{j=0}^{m}\frac{f_{j}(\rho;X)}{f_{j}(\rho^{(i)};X)}\Bigg]+\frac{64m^{2}}{L^{2}\xi^{3}}||\pi^{(i)}-\pi||_{2}^{2}+\frac{2m}{\xi^{3}}\sum_{j=0}^{m}\mathbb{E}_{j}\Bigg[\frac{\big|\big|\epsilon_{j}(X,\rho,\rho^{(i)})\big|\big|_{2}^{2}}{f_{j}(\rho^{(i)},X)}\Bigg].

As stated earlier, the
f_{j}(\rho;x)
are uniformly bounded away from
0
and
\infty
, for all
x\in\mathcal{X}
and
\rho\in\mathcal{R}
. Combined with the inequality in display (
50
), this implies that the first expectation in the last line displayed above converges to
0
as
\theta^{(i)}\to\theta
. Consequentially, to show that
\int dW_{2}(z,\theta,\theta^{(i)})\ d\widetilde{\mu}(z)\to 0
as
\theta^{(i)}\to\theta
, it suffices to show that
\mathbb{E}_{j}\Big[\big|\big|\epsilon_{j}(X,\rho,\rho^{(i)})\big|\big|_{2}^{2}\Big]\to 0
as
\theta^{(i)}\to\theta
.

With that goal in mind, note that
\frac{\delta}{\delta{\rho}}\{f_{j}(\rho;x)\}:\mathcal{R}\mapsto\mathbb{R}^{m}
is a differentiable, vector-valued function and that
\mathcal{R}\subset\mathbb{R}^{m}
is an open convex set. Thus, by the mean value theorem for vector-valued functions proved in
Hall and Newell, 1979
, we have that:

\displaystyle\big|\big|\epsilon_{j}(X,\rho,\rho^{(i)})\big|\big|_{2}

\displaystyle=\bigg|\bigg|\frac{\delta}{\delta{\rho}}\{f_{j}(\rho^{(i)};x)\}-\frac{\delta}{\delta{\rho}}\{f_{j}(\rho;x)\}\bigg|\bigg|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq||\rho^{(i)}-\rho||_{2}\sup_{0<t<1}\sigma_{\textup{max}}\Bigg\{\bigg\{\frac{\delta^{2}}{\delta a\delta b}f_{j}\big((1-t)\rho+t\rho^{(i)};x\big)\bigg\}_{1\leq a,b\leq m}\Bigg\}.

Using the fact that
||(1-t)\rho+t\rho^{(i)}||_{2}<B<\infty
and
||s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau)}(x)||_{2}\leq\frac{2\sqrt{m}}{\xi}<\infty
for all
x\in\mathcal{X}
, one can show that the singular values of the Hessian matrix of
f_{j}\big((1-t)\rho+t\rho^{(i)};x\big)
are bounded, uniformly over all choices of
x\in\mathcal{X}
and
\rho,\rho^{(i)}\in\mathcal{R}
. Hence,
\mathbb{E}_{j}\Big[\big|\big|\epsilon_{j}(X,\rho,\rho^{(i)})\big|\big|_{2}^{2}\Big]
is upper bounded by a constant times
||\rho^{(i)}-\rho||_{2}
, meaning that
\mathbb{E}_{j}\Big[\big|\big|\epsilon_{j}(X,\rho,\rho^{(i)})\big|\big|_{2}^{2}\Big]\to 0
as
\theta^{(i)}\to\theta
. Thus, we have shown that
\int dW_{2}(z,\theta,\theta^{(i)})\ d\widetilde{\mu}(z)\to 0
as
\theta^{(i)}\to\theta
.

Finally, we show that
\int(1-d)W_{3}(z,\theta,\theta^{(i)})\ d\widetilde{\mu}(z)
converges to 0. Towards that end, observe that:

\displaystyle W_{3}(z,\theta,\theta^{(i)})

\displaystyle=\Big|\Big|\big(s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau)}(x)-\mathbb{E}_{y}^{\rho^{(i)}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau)}]\big)q_{y}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)^{1/2}-\big(s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau)}(x)-\mathbb{E}_{y}^{\rho}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau)}]\big)q_{y}^{\rho}(x)^{1/2}\Big|\Big|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 2\Big|\Big|\big(s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau)}(x)-\mathbb{E}_{y}^{\rho^{(i)}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau)}]\big)q_{y}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)^{1/2}-\big(s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau)}(x)-\mathbb{E}_{y}^{\rho^{(i)}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau)}]\big)q_{y}^{\rho}(x)^{1/2}\Big|\Big|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad+2\Big|\Big|\big(s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau)}(x)-\mathbb{E}_{y}^{\rho^{(i)}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau)}]\big)q_{y}^{\rho}(x)^{1/2}-\big(s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau)}(x)-\mathbb{E}_{y}^{\rho}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau)}]\big)q_{y}^{\rho}(x)^{1/2}\Big|\Big|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=2\Big|\Big|s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau)}(x)-\mathbb{E}_{y}^{\rho^{(i)}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau)}]\Big|\Big|_{2}^{2}\Big(q_{y}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)^{1/2}-q_{y}^{\rho}(x)^{1/2}\Big)^{2}+2q_{y}^{\rho}(x)\Big|\Big|\mathbb{E}_{y}^{\rho^{(i)}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau)}]-\mathbb{E}_{y}^{\rho}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau)}]\Big|\Big|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{32m}{L^{2}}\Big(q_{y}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)^{1/2}-q_{y}^{\rho}(x)^{1/2}\Big)^{2}+2q_{y}^{\rho}(x)\Big|\Big|\mathbb{E}_{y}^{\rho^{(i)}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau)}]-\mathbb{E}_{y}^{\rho}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau)}]\Big|\Big|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{32m}{L^{2}}q_{y}^{\rho}(x)\Bigg(\frac{q_{y}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)^{1/2}-q_{y}^{\rho}(x)^{1/2}}{q_{y}^{\rho}(x)^{1/2}}\Bigg)^{2}+2q_{y}^{\rho}(x)\Big|\Big|\mathbb{E}_{y}^{\rho^{(i)}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau)}]-\mathbb{E}_{y}^{\rho}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau)}]\Big|\Big|_{2}^{2}.

Ergo:

\displaystyle\int(1-d)W_{3}(z,\theta,\theta^{(i)})\ d\widetilde{\mu}(z)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{32m}{L^{2}}\sum_{y=0}^{m}\mathbb{E}_{y}^{\rho}\Bigg[\Bigg(\frac{q_{y}^{\rho^{(i)}}(x)^{1/2}-q_{y}^{\rho}(x)^{1/2}}{q_{y}^{\rho}(x)^{1/2}}\Bigg)^{2}\Bigg]+2\sum_{y=0}^{m}\Big|\Big|\mathbb{E}_{y}^{\rho^{(i)}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau)}]-\mathbb{E}_{y}^{\rho}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau)}]\Big|\Big|_{2}^{2}.

Note that:

\displaystyle\Big|\Big|\mathbb{E}_{y}^{\rho^{(i)}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau)}]-\mathbb{E}_{y}^{\rho}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau)}]\Big|\Big|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=\bigg|\bigg|\mathbb{E}_{y}\Big[f_{y}(\rho^{(i)};X)s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau)}(X)\Big]-\mathbb{E}_{y}\Big[f_{y}(\rho;X)s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau)}(X)\Big]\bigg|\bigg|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=\bigg|\bigg|\mathbb{E}_{y}\Big[\big(f_{y}(\rho^{(i)};X)-f_{y}(\rho;X)\big)s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau)}(X)\Big]\bigg|\bigg|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}_{y}\Big[\big|\big|\big(f_{y}(\rho^{(i)};X)-f_{y}(\rho;X)\big)s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau)}(X)\big|\big|_{2}^{2}\Big]
