\displaystyle\textup{\text{Var}}\bigg[\frac{\widehat{\varphi}_{n}-\varphi_{n}}{\varphi_{n}}\bigg]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\varphi_{n}^{2}}\textup{\text{Var}}\big[\widehat{\varphi}_{n}\big]

\displaystyle=\frac{\varphi_{n}(1-\varphi_{n})}{\varphi_{n}^{2}n}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\varphi_{n}n},

which since
\mathbb{E}\Big[\frac{\widehat{\varphi}_{n}-\varphi_{n}}{\varphi_{n}}\Big]=0
, implies that

\implies\frac{\widehat{\varphi}_{n}-\varphi_{n}}{\varphi_{n}}=O_{\mathbb{P}}\bigg(\frac{1}{\sqrt{\varphi_{n}n}}\bigg),

as desired. Second, we will prove that
\frac{\widehat{\varphi}_{n}-\varphi_{n}}{\widehat{\varphi}_{n}}=O_{\mathbb{P}}\Big(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n\varphi_{n}}}\Big)
. Towards that end, it will be helpful to first demonstrate that
\frac{\sqrt{n}(\widehat{\varphi}_{n}-\varphi_{n})}{\sqrt{\widehat{\varphi}_{n}}}=O_{\mathbb{P}}(1)
. Note that for any
a>0
, we have that:

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\Bigg[\sqrt{n}\bigg|\frac{\widehat{\varphi}_{n}-\varphi_{n}}{\sqrt{\widehat{\varphi}_{n}}}\bigg|\geq a\Bigg]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{P}\bigg[\sqrt{n}\big|\widehat{\varphi}_{n}-\varphi_{n}\big|\geq a\sqrt{\widehat{\varphi}_{n}}\bigg]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{P}\bigg[n(\widehat{\varphi}_{n}-\varphi_{n})^{2}\geq a^{2}\widehat{\varphi}_{n}\bigg]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{P}\bigg[n(\widehat{\varphi}_{n}-\varphi_{n})^{2}\geq a^{2}\varphi_{n}+a^{2}(\widehat{\varphi}_{n}-\varphi_{n})\bigg]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{P}\bigg[a^{2}|\widehat{\varphi}_{n}-\varphi_{n}|+n(\widehat{\varphi}_{n}-\varphi_{n})^{2}\geq a^{2}\varphi_{n}\bigg]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{P}\bigg[a^{2}|\widehat{\varphi}_{n}-\varphi_{n}|\geq a^{2}\varphi_{n}/2\bigg]+\mathbb{P}\bigg[n(\widehat{\varphi}_{n}-\varphi_{n})^{2}\geq a^{2}\varphi_{n}/2\bigg]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{P}\bigg[|\widehat{\varphi}_{n}-\varphi_{n}|\geq\varphi_{n}/2\bigg]+\mathbb{P}\bigg[|\widehat{\varphi}_{n}-\varphi_{n}|\geq a\sqrt{\frac{\varphi_{n}}{2n}}\bigg]

\displaystyle\leq 4\frac{\textup{\text{Var}}[\widehat{\varphi}_{n}]}{\varphi_{n}^{2}}+\frac{2n}{\varphi_{n}}\frac{\textup{\text{Var}}[\widehat{\varphi}_{n}]}{a^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq 4\frac{\frac{\varphi_{n}}{n}}{\varphi_{n}^{2}}+\frac{2n}{\varphi_{n}}\frac{\frac{\varphi_{n}}{n}}{a^{2}}

\displaystyle=\frac{4}{n\varphi_{n}}+\frac{2}{a^{2}},

where the second to last inequality is due to Chebychev, and the last inequality is because
0<1-\varphi_{n}<1
. Now, since
n\varphi_{n}\to\infty
, the work above shows that the LHS can be made arbitrarily small by choosing
n
and
a
to be sufficiently large, so indeed,
\frac{\sqrt{n}(\widehat{\varphi}_{n}-\varphi_{n})}{\sqrt{\widehat{\varphi}_{n}}}=O_{\mathbb{P}}(1)
. Thus:

\displaystyle\bigg|\frac{\widehat{\varphi}_{n}-\varphi_{n}}{\widehat{\varphi}_{n}}\bigg|

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sqrt{\widehat{\varphi}_{n}}}\bigg|\frac{\widehat{\varphi}_{n}-\varphi_{n}}{\sqrt{\widehat{\varphi}_{n}}}\bigg|

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sqrt{n\widehat{\varphi}_{n}}}\sqrt{n}\bigg|\frac{\widehat{\varphi}_{n}-\varphi_{n}}{\sqrt{\widehat{\varphi}_{n}}}\bigg|

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sqrt{n\widehat{\varphi}_{n}}}\cdot O_{\mathbb{P}}(1).

Now, we can also show that
\frac{1}{\sqrt{\widehat{\varphi}_{n}n}}=O_{\mathbb{P}}\Big(\frac{1}{\sqrt{\varphi_{n}n}}\Big)
. Towards that end, observe that:

\displaystyle\Bigg|\frac{1}{\sqrt{\widehat{\varphi}_{n}}}-\frac{1}{\sqrt{\varphi_{n}}}\Bigg|\sqrt{n}\varphi_{n}

\displaystyle=\Bigg|\frac{\sqrt{\varphi_{n}}-\sqrt{\widehat{\varphi}_{n}}}{\sqrt{\widehat{\varphi}_{n}}\sqrt{\varphi_{n}}}\Bigg|\sqrt{n}\varphi_{n}

\displaystyle=\Bigg|\frac{\varphi_{n}-\widehat{\varphi}_{n}}{\sqrt{\widehat{\varphi}_{n}}\sqrt{\varphi_{n}}(\sqrt{\varphi_{n}}+\sqrt{\widehat{\varphi}_{n}})}\Bigg|\sqrt{n}\varphi_{n}

\displaystyle=\sqrt{n}\Bigg|\frac{\varphi_{n}-\widehat{\varphi}_{n}}{\sqrt{\widehat{\varphi}_{n}}}\Bigg|\cdot\frac{\varphi_{n}}{\sqrt{\varphi_{n}}(\sqrt{\varphi_{n}}+\sqrt{\widehat{\varphi}_{n}})}

\displaystyle=\sqrt{n}\Bigg|\frac{\varphi_{n}-\widehat{\varphi}_{n}}{\sqrt{\widehat{\varphi}_{n}}}\Bigg|\cdot\frac{\sqrt{\varphi_{n}}}{\sqrt{\varphi_{n}}+\sqrt{\widehat{\varphi}_{n}}}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{n}\Bigg|\frac{\varphi_{n}-\widehat{\varphi}_{n}}{\sqrt{\widehat{\varphi}_{n}}}\Bigg|.

Since we already showed that
\frac{\sqrt{n}(\widehat{\varphi}_{n}-\varphi_{n})}{\sqrt{\widehat{\varphi}_{n}}}=O_{\mathbb{P}}(1)
, the work above implies that:

\implies\Big|\frac{1}{\sqrt{\widehat{\varphi}_{n}}}-\frac{1}{\sqrt{\varphi_{n}}}\Big|\sqrt{n}\varphi_{n}=O_{\mathbb{P}}(1)

\implies\frac{1}{\sqrt{\widehat{\varphi}_{n}}}-\frac{1}{\sqrt{\varphi_{n}}}=O_{\mathbb{P}}\bigg(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}\varphi_{n}}\bigg)

\implies\frac{1}{\sqrt{n\widehat{\varphi}_{n}}}=\frac{1}{\sqrt{n\varphi_{n}}}+O_{\mathbb{P}}\bigg(\frac{1}{n\varphi_{n}}\bigg)

(62)

\implies\frac{1}{\sqrt{n\widehat{\varphi}_{n}}}=O_{\mathbb{P}}\bigg(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n\varphi_{n}}}\bigg).

Thus, we have that:

\implies\bigg|\frac{\widehat{\varphi}_{n}-\varphi_{n}}{\widehat{\varphi}_{n}}\bigg|=O_{\mathbb{P}}\bigg(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n\varphi_{n}}}\bigg).

Finally, note that, by line (
62
), we have that:

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n\widehat{\varphi}_{n}}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n\varphi_{n}}+O_{\mathbb{P}}\bigg(\frac{1}{(n\varphi_{n})^{3/2}}+\frac{1}{(n\varphi_{n})^{2}}\bigg)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n\varphi_{n}}+O_{\mathbb{P}}\bigg(\frac{1}{(n\varphi_{n})^{3/2}}\bigg),

where the last line is because
n\varphi_{n}\to\infty
.

∎

Proof of Lemma (
6.22
)
.

Define the following notation for each
k\in\mathcal{Y}
:

•

\widehat{\psi}_{k}:=\frac{\pi_{k}^{\ast}}{N^{\textup{\text{te}}}}+\frac{1}{N^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\frac{(\pi_{k}^{\ast})^{2}}{\widehat{\pi}_{k}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}

•

\widehat{\Psi}:=\frac{1}{N^{\textup{\text{te}}}}+\frac{1}{N^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\sum_{l=0}^{m}\frac{(\pi_{l}^{\ast})^{2}}{\widehat{\pi}_{l}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}

•

\psi_{k}:=\frac{\pi_{k}^{\ast}}{\tau_{n}n}+\frac{1}{(1-\tau_{n})n}\frac{(\pi_{k}^{\ast})^{2}}{\pi_{k}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}

•

\Psi:=\frac{1}{\tau_{n}n}+\frac{1}{(1-\tau_{n})n}\sum_{l=0}^{m}\frac{(\pi_{l}^{\ast})^{2}}{\pi_{l}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}
.

Clearly,
\gamma_{k}^{\ast}=\widehat{\psi}_{k}/\widehat{\Psi}
and
\gamma_{k}^{\ast}(\tau_{n})=\psi_{k}/\Psi
. Now, let any
j\in\mathcal{Y}
and
\epsilon>0
be given, and define
\delta:=\frac{\epsilon}{\sqrt{n\tau_{n}(1-\tau_{n})}}
. It is easy to show that:

\gamma_{j}^{\ast}-\gamma_{j}^{\ast}(\tau_{n})=\frac{\widehat{\psi}_{j}-\psi_{j}}{\widehat{\Psi}}+\frac{\psi_{j}}{\Psi}\frac{\Psi-\widehat{\Psi}}{\widehat{\Psi}},

from which it follows that

\displaystyle\implies|\gamma_{j}^{\ast}-\gamma_{j}^{\ast}(\tau_{n})|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{|\widehat{\psi}_{j}-\psi_{j}|}{\widehat{\Psi}}+\frac{|\widehat{\Psi}-\Psi|}{\widehat{\Psi}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{|\widehat{\psi}_{j}-\psi_{j}|}{\widehat{\Psi}}+\frac{\sum_{k=0}^{m}|\widehat{\psi}_{k}-\psi_{k}|}{\widehat{\Psi}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{|\widehat{\psi}_{j}-\psi_{j}|}{\widehat{\psi}_{j}}+\sum_{k=0}^{m}\frac{|\widehat{\psi}_{k}-\psi_{k}|}{\widehat{\psi}_{k}}

\displaystyle\leq 2\sum_{k=0}^{m}\frac{|\widehat{\psi}_{k}-\psi_{k}|}{\widehat{\psi}_{k}}.

Consequentially:

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}_{D_{1:n},Y_{1:n}}\Big[|\gamma_{j}^{\ast}-\gamma_{j}^{\ast}(\tau_{n})|\geq\delta\Big]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{P}_{D_{1:n},Y_{1:n}}\Bigg[\sum_{k=0}^{m}\frac{|\widehat{\psi}_{k}-\psi_{k}|}{\widehat{\psi}_{k}}\geq\frac{\delta}{2}\Bigg]

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{k=0}^{m}\mathbb{P}_{D_{1:n},Y_{1:n}}\Bigg[\frac{|\widehat{\psi}_{k}-\psi_{k}|}{\widehat{\psi}_{k}}\geq\frac{\delta}{2m}\Bigg]

\displaystyle=\sum_{k=0}^{m}\mathbb{P}_{D_{1:n},Y_{1:n}}\Big[|\widehat{\psi}_{k}-\psi_{k}|\geq\frac{\delta}{2m}\widehat{\psi}_{k}\Big]

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{k=0}^{m}\mathbb{P}_{D_{1:n},Y_{1:n}}\Bigg[\bigg|\frac{\pi_{k}^{\ast}}{N^{\textup{\text{te}}}}-\frac{\pi_{k}^{\ast}}{\tau_{n}n}\bigg|+\bigg|\frac{1}{N^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\frac{(\pi_{k}^{\ast})^{2}}{\widehat{\pi}_{k}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}-\frac{1}{(1-\tau_{n})n}\frac{(\pi_{k}^{\ast})^{2}}{\pi_{k}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\bigg|\geq\frac{\delta}{2m}\bigg(\frac{\pi_{k}^{\ast}}{N^{\textup{\text{te}}}}+\frac{1}{N^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\frac{(\pi_{k}^{\ast})^{2}}{\widehat{\pi}_{k}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\bigg)\Bigg]

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{k=0}^{m}\mathbb{P}_{D_{1:n},Y_{1:n}}\Bigg[\bigg|\frac{\pi_{k}^{\ast}}{N^{\textup{\text{te}}}}-\frac{\pi_{k}^{\ast}}{\tau_{n}n}\bigg|\geq\frac{\delta}{2m}\frac{\pi_{k}^{\ast}}{N^{\textup{\text{te}}}}\Bigg]

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\sum_{k=0}^{m}\mathbb{P}_{D_{1:n},Y_{1:n}}\Bigg[\bigg|\frac{1}{N^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\frac{(\pi_{k}^{\ast})^{2}}{\widehat{\pi}_{k}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}-\frac{1}{(1-\tau_{n})n}\frac{(\pi_{k}^{\ast})^{2}}{\pi_{k}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\bigg|\geq\frac{\delta}{2m}\frac{1}{N^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\frac{(\pi_{k}^{\ast})^{2}}{\widehat{\pi}_{k}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\Bigg]

\displaystyle=(m+1)\mathbb{P}_{D_{1:n},Y_{1:n}}\Bigg[\bigg|\frac{1}{N^{\textup{\text{te}}}}-\frac{1}{\tau_{n}n}\bigg|\geq\frac{\delta}{2m}\frac{1}{N^{\textup{\text{te}}}}\Bigg]

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\sum_{k=0}^{m}\mathbb{P}_{D_{1:n},Y_{1:n}}\Bigg[\bigg|\frac{1}{\widehat{\pi}_{k}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}N^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}-\frac{1}{\pi_{k}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}(1-\tau_{n})n}\bigg|\geq\frac{\delta}{2m}\frac{1}{\widehat{\pi}_{k}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}N^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\Bigg]

\displaystyle=(m+1)\mathbb{P}_{D_{1:n},Y_{1:n}}\Bigg[\frac{\big|N^{\textup{\text{te}}}-\tau_{n}n\big|}{\tau_{n}n}\geq\frac{\delta}{2m}\Bigg]+\sum_{k=0}^{m}\mathbb{P}_{D_{1:n},Y_{1:n}}\Bigg[\frac{\big|\widehat{\pi}_{k}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}N^{\textup{\text{tr}}}-\pi_{k}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}(1-\tau_{n})n\big|}{\pi_{k}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}(1-\tau_{n})n}\geq\frac{\delta}{2m}\Bigg]

\displaystyle=(m+1)\mathbb{P}_{D_{1:n},Y_{1:n}}\Bigg[\frac{\big|N^{\textup{\text{te}}}/n-\tau_{n}\big|}{\tau_{n}}\geq\frac{\delta}{2m}\Bigg]+\sum_{k=0}^{m}\mathbb{P}_{D_{1:n},Y_{1:n}}\Bigg[\frac{\big|\widehat{\pi}_{k}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}N^{\textup{\text{tr}}}/n-\pi_{k}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}(1-\tau_{n})\big|}{\pi_{k}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}(1-\tau_{n})}\geq\frac{\delta}{2m}\Bigg].

Note that:

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}_{D_{1:n},Y_{1:n}}\Bigg[\frac{\big|N^{\textup{\text{te}}}/n-\tau_{n}\big|}{\tau_{n}}\geq\frac{\delta}{2m}\Bigg]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{P}_{D_{1:n},Y_{1:n}}\Bigg[\frac{\big|N^{\textup{\text{te}}}/n-\tau_{n}\big|}{\tau_{n}}\geq\frac{1}{2m}\frac{\epsilon}{\sqrt{n\tau_{n}(1-\tau_{n})}}\Bigg]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{P}_{D_{1:n},Y_{1:n}}\Bigg[\frac{\big|N^{\textup{\text{te}}}/n-\tau_{n}\big|}{\tau_{n}}\geq\frac{\epsilon}{2m}\frac{1}{\sqrt{n\tau_{n}}}\Bigg]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{P}_{D_{1:n},Y_{1:n}}\Bigg[\sqrt{n\tau_{n}}\frac{\big|N^{\textup{\text{te}}}/n-\tau_{n}\big|}{\tau_{n}}\geq\frac{\epsilon}{2m}\Bigg],

and likewise:

\mathbb{P}_{D_{1:n},Y_{1:n}}\Bigg[\frac{\big|\widehat{\pi}_{k}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}N^{\textup{\text{tr}}}/n-\pi_{k}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}(1-\tau_{n})\big|}{\pi_{k}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}(1-\tau_{n})}\geq\frac{\delta}{2m}\Bigg]\leq\mathbb{P}_{D_{1:n},Y_{1:n}}\Bigg[\sqrt{n(1-\tau_{n})}\frac{\big|\widehat{\pi}_{k}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}N^{\textup{\text{tr}}}/n-\pi_{k}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}(1-\tau_{n})\big|}{\pi_{k}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}(1-\tau_{n})}\geq\frac{\epsilon}{2m}\Bigg].

Ergo, we have that:

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}_{D_{1:n},Y_{1:n}}\Big[\sqrt{n\tau_{n}(1-\tau_{n})}|\gamma_{j}^{\ast}-\gamma_{j}^{\ast}(\tau_{n})|\geq\epsilon\Big]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{P}_{D_{1:n},Y_{1:n}}\Big[|\gamma_{j}^{\ast}-\gamma_{j}^{\ast}(\tau_{n})|\geq\delta\Big]

\displaystyle\leq(m+1)\mathbb{P}_{D_{1:n},Y_{1:n}}\Bigg[\sqrt{n\tau_{n}}\frac{\big|N^{\textup{\text{te}}}/n-\tau_{n}\big|}{\tau_{n}}\geq\frac{\epsilon}{2m}\Bigg]

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\sum_{k=0}^{m}\mathbb{P}_{D_{1:n},Y_{1:n}}\Bigg[\sqrt{n(1-\tau_{n})}\frac{\big|\widehat{\pi}_{k}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}N^{\textup{\text{tr}}}/n-\pi_{k}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}(1-\tau_{n})\big|}{\pi_{k}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}(1-\tau_{n})}\geq\frac{\epsilon}{2m}\Bigg].

Due to Assumptions (
8
) and (
9
), Lemma (
6.21
) implies that
\sqrt{n\tau_{n}}\frac{|N^{\textup{\text{te}}}/n-\tau_{n}|}{\tau_{n}}=O_{\mathbb{P}}(1)
, and since
\pi_{k}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}
is fixed,
\sqrt{n(1-\tau_{n})}\frac{|\widehat{\pi}_{k}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}N^{\textup{\text{tr}}}/n-\pi_{k}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}(1-\tau_{n})|}{\pi_{k}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}(1-\tau_{n})}=O_{\mathbb{P}}(1)
. Thus, by the display above, it follows that

\mathbb{P}_{D_{1:n},Y_{1:n}}\Big[\sqrt{n\tau_{n}(1-\tau_{n})}|\gamma_{j}^{\ast}-\gamma_{j}^{\ast}(\tau_{n})|\geq\epsilon\Big]

can be made arbitrarily small by choosing
n
and
\epsilon
to be sufficiently large, so
\sqrt{n\tau_{n}(1-\tau_{n})}|\gamma_{j}^{\ast}-\gamma_{j}^{\ast}(\tau_{n})|=O_{\mathbb{P}}(1)
, i.e.,

|\gamma_{j}^{\ast}-\gamma_{j}^{\ast}(\tau_{n})|=O_{\mathbb{P}}\bigg(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n\tau_{n}(1-\tau_{n})}}\bigg),

as desired!

∎

Proof of Lemma (
6.23
)
.

The proof for the second asymptotic statement is equivalent to the proof for the first statement, so for conciseness, we only focus on proving the first statement. Towards that end, define

\displaystyle\phi_{i}^{\textup{\text{te}}}

\displaystyle:=\frac{D_{i}}{\sqrt{\tau_{n}}}\Big[\big(s_{\gamma^{\ast}}(X_{i})-\mathbb{E}_{\pi^{\ast}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]\big)-\big(s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau_{n})}(X_{i})-\mathbb{E}_{\pi^{\ast}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau_{n})}]\big)\Big]\Bigg/\sum_{k=0}^{m}|\gamma_{k}^{\ast}-\gamma_{k}^{\ast}(\tau)|\in\mathbb{R}^{m}.

We want to prove that:

\displaystyle\Bigg|\Bigg|\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\phi_{i}^{\textup{\text{te}}}\Bigg|\Bigg|_{2}=O_{\mathbb{P}}(1).

So, let any
\delta>0
be given. Observe that:

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}_{Z_{1:n}}\Bigg[\bigg|\bigg|\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\phi_{i}^{\textup{\text{te}}}\bigg|\bigg|_{2}\geq\delta\Bigg]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{N^{\textup{\text{te}}},\widehat{\pi}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\Bigg\{\mathbb{P}_{Z_{1:n}\mid N^{\textup{\text{te}}},\widehat{\pi}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\Bigg[\bigg|\bigg|\sum_{i=1}^{n}\phi_{i}^{\textup{\text{te}}}\bigg|\bigg|_{2}\geq\sqrt{n}\delta\Bigg]\Bigg\}

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}_{N^{\textup{\text{te}}},\widehat{\pi}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\Bigg\{\mathbb{P}_{Z_{1:n}\mid N^{\textup{\text{te}}},\widehat{\pi}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\Bigg[\bigg|\bigg|\sum_{i=1}^{n}\phi_{i}^{\textup{\text{te}}}\bigg|\bigg|_{1}\geq\sqrt{n}\delta\Bigg]\Bigg\}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{j=1}^{m}\mathbb{E}_{N^{\textup{\text{te}}},\widehat{\pi}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\Bigg\{\mathbb{P}_{Z_{1:n}\mid N^{\textup{\text{te}}},\widehat{\pi}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\Bigg[\bigg|\sum_{i=1}^{n}\phi_{i,j}^{\textup{\text{te}}}\bigg|\geq\frac{\sqrt{n}\delta}{m}\Bigg]\Bigg\}

\displaystyle=\sum_{j=1}^{m}\mathbb{E}_{N^{\textup{\text{te}}},\widehat{\pi}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\Bigg\{\mathbb{P}_{Z_{1:n}\mid N^{\textup{\text{te}}},\widehat{\pi}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\Bigg[\bigg|\sum_{i=1}^{n}\phi_{i,j}^{\textup{\text{te}}}\bigg|\geq\frac{\sqrt{n}\delta}{m}\Bigg]\mathbbold{1}{\{\widehat{\pi}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}\in(\xi,1-\xi)\ \forall y\in\mathcal{Y}\}}\Bigg\}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\sum_{j=1}^{m}\mathbb{E}_{N^{\textup{\text{te}}},\widehat{\pi}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\Bigg\{\mathbb{P}_{Z_{1:n}\mid N^{\textup{\text{te}}},\widehat{\pi}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\Bigg[\bigg|\sum_{i=1}^{n}\phi_{i,j}^{\textup{\text{te}}}\bigg|\geq\frac{\sqrt{n}\delta}{m}\Bigg]\mathbbold{1}{\{\exists\ y\in\mathcal{Y}\text{ s.t. }\widehat{\pi}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}\notin(\xi,1-\xi)\}}\Bigg\}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{j=1}^{m}\mathbb{E}_{N^{\textup{\text{te}}},\widehat{\pi}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\Bigg\{\mathbb{P}_{Z_{1:n}\mid N^{\textup{\text{te}}},\widehat{\pi}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\Bigg[\bigg|\sum_{i=1}^{n}\phi_{i,j}^{\textup{\text{te}}}\bigg|\geq\frac{\sqrt{n}\delta}{m}\Bigg]\mathbbold{1}{\{\widehat{\pi}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}\in(\xi,1-\xi)\ \forall y\in\mathcal{Y}\}}\Bigg\}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+m\mathbb{P}_{N^{\textup{\text{te}}},\widehat{\pi}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\Big[\exists\ y\in\mathcal{Y}\text{ s.t. }\widehat{\pi}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}\notin(\xi,1-\xi)\Big]

\displaystyle=\sum_{j=1}^{m}\mathbb{E}_{N^{\textup{\text{te}}},\widehat{\pi}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\Bigg\{\mathbb{P}_{Z_{1:n}\mid N^{\textup{\text{te}}},\widehat{\pi}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\Bigg[\bigg|\sum_{i=1}^{n}\phi_{i,j}^{\textup{\text{te}}}\bigg|\geq\frac{\sqrt{n}\delta}{m}\Bigg]\mathbbold{1}{\{\widehat{\pi}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}\in(\xi,1-\xi)\ \forall y\in\mathcal{Y}\}}\mathbbold{1}{\{N^{\textup{\text{te}}}\geq 1\}}\Bigg\}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+m\mathbb{P}_{N^{\textup{\text{te}}},\widehat{\pi}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\Big[\exists\ y\in\mathcal{Y}\text{ s.t. }\widehat{\pi}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}\notin(\xi,1-\xi)\Big],

where the last line is because if
N^{\textup{\text{te}}}=0
, then
\sum_{i=1}^{n}\phi_{i,j}^{\textup{\text{te}}}=0
with probability
1
, in which case we must have that
\mathbb{P}_{Z_{1:n}\mid N^{\textup{\text{te}}},\widehat{\pi}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\Big[\big|\sum_{i=1}^{n}\phi_{i,j}^{\textup{\text{te}}}\big|\geq\frac{\sqrt{n}\delta}{m}\Big]=0
since
\delta>0
. Next, let’s focus on the
\mathbb{P}_{N^{\textup{\text{te}}},\widehat{\pi}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\Big[\exists\ y\in\mathcal{Y}\text{ s.t. }\widehat{\pi}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}\notin(\xi,1-\xi)\Big]
term. Note that, for each
y\in\mathcal{Y}
, we have that
\pi_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}\in(\xi,1-\xi)
, so if
\widehat{\pi}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}\notin(\xi,1-\xi)
, then
|\widehat{\pi}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}-\pi_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}|\geq(\pi_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}-\xi)\wedge(1-\xi-\pi_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr}}})
. Thus:

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}_{N^{\textup{\text{te}}},\widehat{\pi}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\Big[\exists\ y\in\mathcal{Y}\text{ s.t. }\widehat{\pi}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}\notin(\xi,1-\xi)\Big]

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{y=0}^{m}\mathbb{P}_{N^{\textup{\text{te}}},\widehat{\pi}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\Big[\widehat{\pi}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}\notin(\xi,1-\xi)\Big]

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{y=0}^{m}\mathbb{P}_{N^{\textup{\text{te}}},\widehat{\pi}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\Big[|\widehat{\pi}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}-\pi_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}|\geq(\pi_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}-\xi)\wedge(1-\xi-\pi_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr}}})\Big]

\displaystyle=\sum_{y=0}^{m}\mathbb{E}_{N^{\textup{\text{te}}}}\Bigg\{\mathbb{P}_{\widehat{\pi}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}\mid N^{\textup{\text{te}}}}\Big[(n-N^{\textup{\text{te}}})|\widehat{\pi}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}-\pi_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}|\geq(n-N^{\textup{\text{te}}})\cdot(\pi_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}-\xi)\wedge(1-\xi-\pi_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr}}})\Big]\Bigg\}.

Note that, conditional on
N^{\textup{\text{te}}}
, the random variable
(n-N^{\textup{\text{te}}})\widehat{\pi}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}
is the sum of
n-N^{\textup{\text{te}}}
independent
\text{Bernoulli}(\pi_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr}}})
random variables. Thus, by Hoeffding’s inequality:

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}_{\widehat{\pi}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}\mid N^{\textup{\text{te}}}}\Big[(n-N^{\textup{\text{te}}})|\widehat{\pi}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}-\pi_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}|\geq(n-N^{\textup{\text{te}}})\cdot(\pi_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}-\xi)\wedge(1-\xi-\pi_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr}}})\Big]

\displaystyle\leq 2\exp\bigg(-2(n-N^{\textup{\text{te}}})\Big((\pi_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}-\xi)\wedge(1-\xi-\pi_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr}}})\Big)^{2}\bigg)

\displaystyle\leq 2\exp\big(-c(n-N^{\textup{\text{te}}})\big),

where
c:=2\min\limits_{0\leq y\leq m}\Big((\pi_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}-\xi)\wedge(1-\xi-\pi_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr}}})\Big)^{2}
. Thus, we have that:

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}_{N^{\textup{\text{te}}},\widehat{\pi}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\Big[\exists\ y\in\mathcal{Y}\text{ s.t. }\widehat{\pi}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}\notin(\xi,1-\xi)\Big]

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{y=0}^{m}\mathbb{E}_{N^{\textup{\text{te}}}}\Big\{2\exp\big(-c(n-N^{\textup{\text{te}}})\big)\Big\}

\displaystyle=2(m+1)\mathbb{E}_{N^{\textup{\text{te}}}}\Big\{\exp\big(-c(n-N^{\textup{\text{te}}})\big)\Big\}

\displaystyle=2(m+1)\Big(\big(1-[1-\tau_{n}]\big)+[1-\tau_{n}]e^{-c}\Big)^{n}

\displaystyle=2(m+1)\Big(1-[1-\tau_{n}](1-e^{-c})\Big)^{n}

\displaystyle\leq 2(m+1)e^{-n[1-\tau_{n}](1-e^{-c})},

where the third line uses the formula for the MGF of a Binomial random variable and the fact that
n-N^{\textup{\text{te}}}\sim\text{Binomial}(n,1-\tau_{n})
, and the last line uses the fact that
[1-\tau_{n}](1-e^{-c})\in(0,1)
under Assumption (
8
) and
(1-u)^{n}\leq e^{-nu}
for all
u\in(0,1)
.

Having obtained a bound on
\mathbb{P}_{N^{\textup{\text{te}}},\widehat{\pi}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\Big[\exists\ y\in\mathcal{Y}\text{ s.t. }\widehat{\pi}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}\notin(\xi,1-\xi)\Big]
, we now turn to bounding

\sum_{j=1}^{m}\mathbb{E}_{N^{\textup{\text{te}}},\widehat{\pi}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\Bigg\{\mathbb{P}_{Z_{1:n}\mid N^{\textup{\text{te}}},\widehat{\pi}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\Bigg[\bigg|\sum_{i=1}^{n}\phi_{i,j}^{\textup{\text{te}}}\bigg|\geq\frac{\sqrt{n}\delta}{m}\Bigg]\mathbbold{1}{\{\widehat{\pi}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}\in(\xi,1-\xi)\ \forall y\in\mathcal{Y}\}}\mathbbold{1}{\{N^{\textup{\text{te}}}\geq 1\}}\Bigg\}.

Towards that end, we make the following observations about the display above. By conditioning on
N^{\textup{\text{te}}},\widehat{\pi}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}
, we have that
\gamma^{\ast}
is no longer random, and
\sum_{i=1}^{n}\phi_{i,j}^{\textup{\text{te}}}
has the same distribution as a sum of
N^{\textup{\text{te}}}
independent copies of

\frac{1}{\sqrt{\tau_{n}}}\Big[\big(s_{\gamma^{\ast},j}(X)-\mathbb{E}_{\pi^{\ast}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},j}]\big)-\big(s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau),j}(X)-\mathbb{E}_{\pi^{\ast}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau),j}]\big)\Big]\Bigg/\sum_{k=0}^{m}|\gamma_{k}^{\ast}-\gamma_{k}^{\ast}(\tau)|

where
X\sim p_{\pi^{\ast}}
. Additionally, note that if
\widehat{\pi}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}\in(\xi,1-\xi)\ \forall y\in\mathcal{Y}
, then by Lemma (
6.1
), we have that
\gamma^{\ast}\in\Gamma
, and so by Lemma (
6.6
), it follows that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{\tau_{n}}}\Big|\big(s_{\gamma^{\ast},j}(X)-\mathbb{E}_{\pi^{\ast}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},j}]\big)-\big(s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau),j}(X)-\mathbb{E}_{\pi^{\ast}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau),j}]\big)\Big|\Bigg/\sum_{k=0}^{m}|\gamma_{k}^{\ast}-\gamma_{k}^{\ast}(\tau)|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{4}{\sqrt{\tau_{n}}L^{2}},

i.e.,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{\tau_{n}}}\Big[\big(s_{\gamma^{\ast},j}(X)-\mathbb{E}_{\pi^{\ast}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast},j}]\big)-\big(s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau),j}(X)-\mathbb{E}_{\pi^{\ast}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau),j}]\big)\Big]\Bigg/\sum_{k=0}^{m}|\gamma_{k}^{\ast}-\gamma_{k}^{\ast}(\tau)|

\displaystyle\in\Bigg[-\frac{4}{\sqrt{\tau_{n}}L^{2}},\ \frac{4}{\sqrt{\tau_{n}}L^{2}}\Bigg].

Bringing these observations together, it follows from Hoeffding’s inequality that:

\mathbb{P}_{Z_{1:n}\mid N^{\textup{\text{te}}},\widehat{\pi}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\Bigg[\bigg|\sum_{i=1}^{n}\phi_{i,j}^{\textup{\text{te}}}\bigg|\geq\frac{\sqrt{n}\delta}{m}\Bigg]\mathbbold{1}{\{\widehat{\pi}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}\in(\xi,1-\xi)\ \forall y\in\mathcal{Y}\}}\mathbbold{1}{\{N^{\textup{\text{te}}}\geq 1\}}

\leq 2\exp\bigg(-\frac{1}{32}\delta^{2}m^{-2}L^{4}\cdot\frac{n\tau_{n}}{N^{\textup{\text{te}}}}\bigg)\mathbbold{1}{\{\widehat{\pi}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}\in(\xi,1-\xi)\ \forall y\in\mathcal{Y}\}}\mathbbold{1}{\{N^{\textup{\text{te}}}\geq 1\}}

\leq 2\exp\bigg(-\frac{1}{32}\delta^{2}m^{-2}L^{4}\cdot\frac{n\tau_{n}}{1+N^{\textup{\text{te}}}}\bigg)

=2\exp\bigg(-\frac{hn\tau_{n}\delta^{2}}{1+N^{\textup{\text{te}}}}\bigg).

where

h:=\frac{1}{32}m^{-2}L^{4}

Ergo:

\displaystyle\sum_{j=1}^{m}\mathbb{E}_{N^{\textup{\text{te}}},\widehat{\pi}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\Bigg\{\mathbb{P}_{Z_{1:n}\mid N^{\textup{\text{te}}},\widehat{\pi}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\Bigg[\bigg|\sum_{i=1}^{n}\phi_{i,j}^{\textup{\text{te}}}\bigg|\geq\frac{\sqrt{n}\delta}{m}\Bigg]\mathbbold{1}{\{\widehat{\pi}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}\in(\xi,1-\xi)\ \forall y\in\mathcal{Y}\}}\mathbbold{1}{\{N^{\textup{\text{te}}}\geq 1\}}\Bigg\}

\displaystyle\leq 2\sum_{j=1}^{m}\mathbb{E}_{N^{\textup{\text{te}}}}\Bigg\{\exp\bigg(-\frac{hn\tau_{n}\delta^{2}}{1+N^{\textup{\text{te}}}}\bigg)\Bigg\}.

Now, observe that:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{N^{\textup{\text{te}}}}\Bigg\{\exp\bigg(-\frac{h_{n}\delta^{2}}{1+N^{\textup{\text{te}}}}\bigg)\Bigg\}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{N^{\textup{\text{te}}}\mid N^{\textup{\text{te}}}\geq hn\tau_{n}\delta}\Bigg\{\exp\bigg(-\frac{h_{n}\delta^{2}}{1+N^{\textup{\text{te}}}}\bigg)\Bigg\}\mathbb{P}[N^{\textup{\text{te}}}\geq hn\tau_{n}\delta]

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\mathbb{E}_{N^{\textup{\text{te}}}\mid N^{\textup{\text{te}}}<hn\tau_{n}\delta}\Bigg\{\exp\bigg(-\frac{hn\tau_{n}\delta^{2}}{1+N^{\textup{\text{te}}}}\bigg)\Bigg\}\mathbb{P}[N^{\textup{\text{te}}}<hn\tau_{n}\delta]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{P}[N^{\textup{\text{te}}}\geq hn\tau_{n}\delta]+\mathbb{E}_{N^{\textup{\text{te}}}\mid N^{\textup{\text{te}}}<hn\tau_{n}\delta}\Bigg\{\exp\bigg(-\frac{hn\tau_{n}\delta}{1+N^{\textup{\text{te}}}}\delta\bigg)\Bigg\}

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{P}[N^{\textup{\text{te}}}\geq hn\tau_{n}\delta]+\exp\bigg(-\frac{hn\tau_{n}\delta}{1+hn\tau_{n}\delta}\delta\bigg)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\mathbb{E}[N^{\textup{\text{te}}}]}{hn\tau_{n}\delta}+\exp\bigg(-\frac{hn\tau_{n}\delta}{1+hn\tau_{n}\delta}\delta\bigg)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{h\delta}+\exp\bigg(-\frac{hn\tau_{n}\delta}{1+hn\tau_{n}\delta}\delta\bigg).

Thus:

\implies\sum_{j=1}^{m}\mathbb{E}_{N^{\textup{\text{te}}},\widehat{\pi}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\Bigg\{\mathbb{P}_{Z_{1:n}\mid N^{\textup{\text{te}}},\widehat{\pi}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\Bigg[\bigg|\sum_{i=1}^{n}\phi_{i,j}^{\textup{\text{te}}}\bigg|\geq\frac{\sqrt{n}\delta}{m}\Bigg]\mathbbold{1}{\{\widehat{\pi}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}\in(\xi,1-\xi)\ \forall y\in\mathcal{Y}\}}\mathbbold{1}{\{N^{\textup{\text{te}}}\geq 1\}}\Bigg\}

\leq\frac{2m}{h\delta}+2m\exp\bigg(-\frac{hn\tau_{n}\delta}{1+hn\tau_{n}\delta}\delta\bigg)

Thus, overall, we have that:

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}_{Z_{1:n}}\Bigg[\bigg|\bigg|\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\phi_{i}^{\textup{\text{te}}}\bigg|\bigg|_{2}\geq\delta\Bigg]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2m}{h\delta}+2m\exp\bigg(-\frac{hn\tau_{n}\delta}{1+hn\tau_{n}\delta}\delta\bigg)+2m(m+1)e^{-n[1-\tau_{n}](1-e^{-c})},

which can be made arbitrarily small by making
n
and
\delta
sufficiently large due to Assumption (
9
). This implies that
\big|\big|\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\phi_{i}^{\textup{\text{te}}}\big|\big|_{2}=O_{\mathbb{P}}(1)
, as claimed.

∎

Proof of Lemma (
6.24
)
.

By inspection, both
\gamma^{\ast}
and
\gamma^{\ast}(\tau_{n})
are members of the
m+1
dimensional probability simplex. As such, by Lemma (
2.2
), we have that

\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast}(\tau_{n}))=\textup{\text{Cov}}_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau_{n})}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau_{n})},s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau_{n})}]=A_{s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau_{n})}}.

However, Lemma (
2.2
) also implies that:

A_{s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau_{n})}}=\textup{\text{Cov}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau_{n})},s_{\gamma^{\ast}}],

meaning that
\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast}(\tau_{n}))=\textup{\text{Cov}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau_{n})},s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]
. Now, by Lemma (
6.1
), we know that
\gamma^{\ast}(\tau_{n})\in\Gamma
, so by Assumption (
B1
), we have that
\sqrt{\Lambda}<\sigma_{\textup{min}}\big(\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast}(\tau_{n}))\big)=\lambda_{\textup{min}}\big(\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast}(\tau_{n}))\big)
. Bringing these observations together, we have that:

\displaystyle\sqrt{\Lambda}

\displaystyle<\lambda_{\textup{min}}\big(\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast}(\tau_{n}))\big)

\displaystyle=\lambda_{\textup{min}}\big(\textup{\text{Cov}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau_{n})},s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]\big)

\displaystyle=\min_{||c||_{2}=1}\textup{\text{Cov}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[c^{\prime}s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau_{n})},c^{\prime}s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{\min_{||c||_{2}=1}\textup{\text{Var}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[c^{\prime}s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau_{n})}]\textup{\text{Var}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[c^{\prime}s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{\max_{||c||_{2}=1}\textup{\text{Var}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[c^{\prime}s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau_{n})}]}\sqrt{\min_{||c||_{2}=1}\textup{\text{Var}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[c^{\prime}s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]}

\displaystyle=\sqrt{\lambda_{\textup{max}}\big(\textup{\text{Var}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau_{n})}]\big)}\sqrt{\lambda_{\textup{min}}\big(\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})\big)}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{\sum_{i,j=1}^{m}\Big|\textup{\text{Cov}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau_{n}),i},s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau_{n}),j}]\Big|}\times\sqrt{\lambda_{\textup{min}}\big(\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})\big)}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{\sum_{i,j=1}^{m}\sqrt{\textup{\text{Var}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau_{n}),i}]\textup{\text{Var}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau_{n}),j}]}}\times\sqrt{\lambda_{\textup{min}}\big(\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})\big)}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{\frac{m^{2}}{L^{2}}}\times\sqrt{\lambda_{\textup{min}}\big(\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})\big)}

\displaystyle=\frac{m}{L}\sqrt{\lambda_{\textup{min}}\big(\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})\big)},

where the second to last line is by Popoviciu’s variance inequality and the fact that
\gamma^{\ast}(\tau_{n})\in\Gamma
so
-\frac{1}{L}\leq s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau_{n}),j}\leq\frac{1}{L}
for each
j\in\mathcal{Y}
. Thus, we have that:

\Lambda\frac{L^{2}}{m^{2}}<\lambda_{\textup{min}}\big(\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})\big),

as claimed.

∎

Proof of Lemma (
3.8
)
.

Under Assumption (
B1
), we have by Lemma (
6.24
) that the smallest eigenvalue of
\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})
is always greater than
0
, and so
\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})
is invertible with probability
1
. Thus,
\psi^{\textup{eff}}
is well-defined in Lemma (
6.8
), and we can write:

\displaystyle\sqrt{\tau_{n}(1-\tau_{n})n}(\widehat{\pi}_{n}-\pi^{\ast})

\displaystyle=\sqrt{\frac{\tau_{n}(1-\tau_{n})}{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\psi^{\textup{eff}}(Z_{i})+\delta_{2,\tau_{n}}(Z_{1:n})

where

\delta_{2,\tau_{n}}(Z_{1:n}):=\sqrt{\tau_{n}(1-\tau_{n})n}(\widehat{\pi}_{n}-\pi^{\ast})-\sqrt{\frac{\tau_{n}(1-\tau_{n})}{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\psi^{\textup{eff}}(Z_{i}).

Rewrite
\sqrt{\frac{\tau_{n}(1-\tau_{n})}{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\psi^{\textup{eff}}(Z_{i})
in the following manner:

\displaystyle\sqrt{\frac{\tau_{n}(1-\tau_{n})}{n}}\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})\sum_{i=1}^{n}\psi^{\textup{eff}}(Z_{i})

\displaystyle=\frac{\sqrt{\tau_{n}(1-\tau_{n})}}{\widehat{\tau}_{n}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}D_{i}\big(s_{\gamma^{\ast}}(X_{i})-\mathbb{E}_{\pi^{\ast}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]\big)-\sum_{j=0}^{m}\frac{\sqrt{\tau_{n}(1-\tau_{n})}}{1-\widehat{\tau}_{n}}\frac{\pi_{j}^{\ast}}{\widehat{\pi}_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}(1-D_{i})\mathbbold{1}{\{Y_{i}=j\}}\big(s_{\gamma^{\ast}}(X_{i})-\mathbb{E}_{j}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]\big)

\displaystyle=\frac{\sqrt{\tau_{n}(1-\tau_{n})}}{\tau_{n}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}D_{i}\big(s_{\gamma^{\ast}}(X_{i})-\mathbb{E}_{\pi^{\ast}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]\big)-\sum_{j=0}^{m}\frac{\sqrt{\tau_{n}(1-\tau_{n})}}{1-\tau_{n}}\frac{\pi_{j}^{\ast}}{\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}(1-D_{i})\mathbbold{1}{\{Y_{i}=j\}}\big(s_{\gamma^{\ast}}(X_{i})-\mathbb{E}_{j}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]\big)

\displaystyle\hskip 17.70859pt\hskip 17.70859pt+\bigg(\frac{1}{\widehat{\tau}_{n}}-\frac{1}{\tau_{n}}\bigg)\frac{\sqrt{\tau_{n}(1-\tau_{n})}}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}D_{i}\big(s_{\gamma^{\ast}}(X_{i})-\mathbb{E}_{\pi^{\ast}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]\big)

\displaystyle\hskip 17.70859pt\hskip 17.70859pt+\sum_{j=0}^{m}\bigg(\frac{1}{1-\tau_{n}}\frac{\pi_{j}^{\ast}}{\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}-\frac{1}{1-\widehat{\tau}_{n}}\frac{\pi_{j}^{\ast}}{\widehat{\pi}_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\bigg)\frac{\sqrt{\tau_{n}(1-\tau_{n})}}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}(1-D_{i})\mathbbold{1}{\{Y_{i}=j\}}\big(s_{\gamma^{\ast}}(X_{i})-\mathbb{E}_{j}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]\big)

\displaystyle=\frac{\sqrt{\tau_{n}(1-\tau_{n})}}{\tau_{n}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}D_{i}\big(s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau_{n})}(X_{i})-\mathbb{E}_{\pi^{\ast}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau_{n})}]\big)-\sum_{j=0}^{m}\frac{\sqrt{\tau_{n}(1-\tau_{n})}}{1-\tau_{n}}\frac{\pi_{j}^{\ast}}{\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}(1-D_{i})\mathbbold{1}{\{Y_{i}=j\}}\big(s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau_{n})}(X_{i})-\mathbb{E}_{j}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau_{n})}]\big)

\displaystyle\hskip 17.70859pt\hskip 17.70859pt+\frac{\sqrt{\tau_{n}(1-\tau_{n})}}{\tau_{n}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}D_{i}\Big[\big(s_{\gamma^{\ast}}(X_{i})-\mathbb{E}_{\pi^{\ast}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]\big)-\big(s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau_{n})}(X_{i})-\mathbb{E}_{\pi^{\ast}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau_{n})}]\big)\Big]

\displaystyle\hskip 17.70859pt\hskip 17.70859pt+\sum_{j=0}^{m}\frac{\sqrt{\tau_{n}(1-\tau_{n})}}{1-\tau_{n}}\frac{\pi_{j}^{\ast}}{\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}(1-D_{i})\mathbbold{1}{\{Y_{i}=j\}}\Big[\big(s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau_{n})}(X_{i})-\mathbb{E}_{j}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau_{n})}]\big)-\big(s_{\gamma^{\ast}}(X_{i})-\mathbb{E}_{j}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]\big)\Big]

\displaystyle\hskip 17.70859pt\hskip 17.70859pt+\bigg(\frac{1}{\widehat{\tau}_{n}}-\frac{1}{\tau_{n}}\bigg)\frac{\sqrt{\tau_{n}(1-\tau_{n})}}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}D_{i}\big(s_{\gamma^{\ast}}(X_{i})-\mathbb{E}_{\pi^{\ast}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]\big)

\displaystyle\hskip 17.70859pt\hskip 17.70859pt+\sum_{j=0}^{m}\bigg(\frac{1}{1-\tau_{n}}\frac{\pi_{j}^{\ast}}{\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}-\frac{1}{1-\widehat{\tau}_{n}}\frac{\pi_{j}^{\ast}}{\widehat{\pi}_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\bigg)\frac{\sqrt{\tau_{n}(1-\tau_{n})}}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}(1-D_{i})\mathbbold{1}{\{Y_{i}=j\}}\big(s_{\gamma^{\ast}}(X_{i})-\mathbb{E}_{j}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]\big)

\displaystyle=\frac{\sqrt{\tau_{n}(1-\tau_{n})}}{\tau_{n}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}D_{i}\big(s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau_{n})}(X_{i})-\mathbb{E}_{\pi^{\ast}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau_{n})}]\big)-\sum_{j=0}^{m}\frac{\sqrt{\tau_{n}(1-\tau_{n})}}{1-\tau_{n}}\frac{\pi_{j}^{\ast}}{\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}(1-D_{i})\mathbbold{1}{\{Y_{i}=j\}}\big(s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau_{n})}(X_{i})-\mathbb{E}_{j}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau_{n})}]\big)

\displaystyle\hskip 17.70859pt\hskip 17.70859pt+\underbrace{\frac{\sqrt{\tau_{n}(1-\tau_{n})}}{\tau_{n}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}D_{i}\Big[\big(s_{\gamma^{\ast}}(X_{i})-\mathbb{E}_{\pi^{\ast}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]\big)-\big(s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau_{n})}(X_{i})-\mathbb{E}_{\pi^{\ast}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau_{n})}]\big)\Big]}_{=:V_{1,n}}

\displaystyle\hskip 17.70859pt\hskip 17.70859pt+\underbrace{\sum_{j=0}^{m}\frac{\sqrt{\tau_{n}(1-\tau_{n})}}{1-\tau_{n}}\frac{\pi_{j}^{\ast}}{\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}(1-D_{i})\mathbbold{1}{\{Y_{i}=j\}}\Big[\big(s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau_{n})}(X_{i})-\mathbb{E}_{j}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau_{n})}]\big)-\big(s_{\gamma^{\ast}}(X_{i})-\mathbb{E}_{j}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]\big)\Big]}_{=:V_{2,n}}

\displaystyle\hskip 17.70859pt\hskip 17.70859pt+\underbrace{\bigg(\frac{1}{\widehat{\tau}_{n}}-\frac{1}{\tau_{n}}\bigg)\frac{\sqrt{\tau_{n}(1-\tau_{n})}}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}D_{i}\big(s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau_{n})}(X_{i})-\mathbb{E}_{\pi^{\ast}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau_{n})}]\big)}_{=:V_{3,n}}

\displaystyle\hskip 17.70859pt\hskip 17.70859pt+\underbrace{\bigg(\frac{1}{\widehat{\tau}_{n}}-\frac{1}{\tau_{n}}\bigg)\frac{\sqrt{\tau_{n}(1-\tau_{n})}}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}D_{i}\Big[\big(s_{\gamma^{\ast}}(X_{i})-\mathbb{E}_{\pi^{\ast}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]\big)-\big(s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau_{n})}(X_{i})-\mathbb{E}_{\pi^{\ast}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau_{n})}]\big)\Big]}_{=:V_{4,n}}

\displaystyle\hskip 17.70859pt\hskip 17.70859pt+\underbrace{\sum_{j=0}^{m}\bigg(\frac{1}{1-\tau_{n}}\frac{\pi_{j}^{\ast}}{\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}-\frac{1}{1-\widehat{\tau}_{n}}\frac{\pi_{j}^{\ast}}{\widehat{\pi}_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\bigg)\frac{\sqrt{\tau_{n}(1-\tau_{n})}}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}(1-D_{i})\mathbbold{1}{\{Y_{i}=j\}}\big(s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau_{n})}(X_{i})-\mathbb{E}_{j}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau_{n})}]\big)}_{=:V_{5,n}}

\displaystyle\hskip 17.70859pt\hskip 17.70859pt+\underbrace{\sum_{j=0}^{m}\bigg(\frac{1}{1-\tau_{n}}\frac{\pi_{j}^{\ast}}{\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}-\frac{1}{1-\widehat{\tau}_{n}}\frac{\pi_{j}^{\ast}}{\widehat{\pi}_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\bigg)\frac{\sqrt{\tau_{n}(1-\tau_{n})}}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}(1-D_{i})\mathbbold{1}{\{Y_{i}=j\}}\Big[\big(s_{\gamma^{\ast}}(X_{i})-\mathbb{E}_{j}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]\big)-\big(s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau_{n})}(X_{i})-\mathbb{E}_{j}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau_{n})}]\big)\Big]}_{=:V_{6,n}}.

Thus, making use of the definition of
\psi_{\tau_{n}}^{\textup{eff}}
in Lemma (
6.19
), we have that:

\implies\sqrt{\frac{\tau_{n}(1-\tau_{n})}{n}}\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})\sum_{i=1}^{n}\psi^{\textup{eff}}(Z_{i})=\sqrt{\frac{\tau_{n}(1-\tau_{n})}{n}}\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast}(\tau_{n}))\sum_{i=1}^{n}\psi_{\tau_{n}}^{\textup{eff}}(Z_{i})+\sum_{l=1}^{6}V_{l,n}

\implies\sqrt{\frac{\tau_{n}(1-\tau_{n})}{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\psi^{\textup{eff}}(Z_{i})=\sqrt{\frac{\tau_{n}(1-\tau_{n})}{n}}\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})^{-1}\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast}(\tau_{n}))\sum_{i=1}^{n}\psi_{\tau_{n}}^{\textup{eff}}(Z_{i})+\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})^{-1}\sum_{l=1}^{6}V_{l,n}

\implies\sqrt{\frac{\tau_{n}(1-\tau_{n})}{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\psi^{\textup{eff}}(Z_{i})=\sqrt{\frac{\tau_{n}(1-\tau_{n})}{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\psi_{\tau_{n}}^{\textup{eff}}(Z_{i})+\delta_{1,\tau_{n}}(Z_{1:n}),

where

\delta_{1,\tau_{n}}(Z_{1:n}):=\big(\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})^{-1}\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast}(\tau_{n}))-I_{m\times m}\big)\sqrt{\frac{\tau_{n}(1-\tau_{n})}{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\psi_{\tau_{n}}^{\textup{eff}}(Z_{i})+\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})^{-1}\sum_{l=1}^{6}V_{l,n}.

Then we have that:

\displaystyle\sqrt{\tau_{n}(1-\tau)n}(\widehat{\pi}_{n}-\pi^{\ast})

\displaystyle=\sqrt{\frac{\tau_{n}(1-\tau_{n})}{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\psi^{\textup{eff}}(Z_{i})+\delta_{2,\tau_{n}}(Z_{1:n})

\displaystyle=\sqrt{\frac{\tau_{n}(1-\tau_{n})}{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\psi_{\tau_{n}}^{\textup{eff}}(Z_{i})+\underbrace{\delta_{1,\tau_{n}}(Z_{1:n})+\delta_{2,\tau_{n}}(Z_{1:n})}_{=:\delta_{\tau_{n}}(Z_{1:n})}

\displaystyle=\sqrt{\frac{\tau_{n}(1-\tau_{n})}{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\psi_{\tau_{n}}^{\textup{eff}}(Z_{i})+\delta_{\tau_{n}}(Z_{1:n}).

(63)

First, we analyze
V_{1,n}
. Observe that:

\displaystyle||V_{1,n}||_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\bigg|\bigg|\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{D_{i}}{\sqrt{\tau_{n}}}\Big[\big(s_{\gamma^{\ast}}(X_{i})-\mathbb{E}_{\pi^{\ast}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]\big)-\big(s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau_{n})}(X_{i})-\mathbb{E}_{\pi^{\ast}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau_{n})}]\big)\Big]\bigg|\bigg|_{2}

\displaystyle=\sum_{k=0}^{m}\big|\gamma_{k}^{\ast}-\gamma_{k}^{\ast}(\tau_{n})\big|\times\bigg|\bigg|\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{D_{i}}{\sqrt{\tau_{n}}}\Big[\big(s_{\gamma^{\ast}}(X_{i})-\mathbb{E}_{\pi^{\ast}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]\big)-\big(s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau_{n})}(X_{i})-\mathbb{E}_{\pi^{\ast}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau_{n})}]\big)\Big]\bigg/\sum_{k=0}^{m}\big|\gamma_{k}^{\ast}-\gamma_{k}^{\ast}(\tau_{n})\big|\bigg|\bigg|_{2}

\displaystyle=O_{\mathbb{P}}\Bigg(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n\tau_{n}(1-\tau_{n})}}\Bigg)\times O_{\mathbb{P}}(1)

\displaystyle=O_{\mathbb{P}}\Bigg(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n\tau_{n}(1-\tau_{n})}}\Bigg),

where the second to last line is due to Assumptions (
8
) and (
9
), and Lemmas (
6.22
) and (
6.23
) .

Second, we analyze
V_{2,n}
. Observe that:

\displaystyle||V_{2,n}||_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\sqrt{\xi}}\sum_{j=0}^{m}\Bigg|\Bigg|\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{(1-D_{i})\mathbbold{1}{\{Y_{i}=j\}}}{\sqrt{(1-\tau_{n})\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}}\Big[\big(s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau_{n})}(X_{i})-\mathbb{E}_{j}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau_{n})}]\big)-\big(s_{\gamma^{\ast}}(X_{i})-\mathbb{E}_{j}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]\big)\Big]\Bigg|\Bigg|_{2}

\displaystyle=\sum_{k=0}^{m}\big|\gamma_{k}^{\ast}-\gamma_{k}^{\ast}(\tau_{n})\big|\times\frac{1}{\sqrt{\xi}}\sum_{j=0}^{m}\Bigg|\Bigg|\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{\frac{(1-D_{i})\mathbbold{1}{\{Y_{i}=j\}}}{\sqrt{(1-\tau_{n})\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}}\Big[\big(s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau_{n})}(X_{i})-\mathbb{E}_{j}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau_{n})}]\big)-\big(s_{\gamma^{\ast}}(X_{i})-\mathbb{E}_{j}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]\big)\Big]}{\sum_{k=0}^{m}\big|\gamma_{k}^{\ast}-\gamma_{k}^{\ast}(\tau_{n})\big|}\Bigg|\Bigg|_{2}

\displaystyle=O_{\mathbb{P}}\Bigg(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n\tau_{n}(1-\tau_{n})}}\Bigg)\times O_{\mathbb{P}}(1)

\displaystyle=O_{\mathbb{P}}\Bigg(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n\tau_{n}(1-\tau_{n})}}\Bigg),

where the second to last line is due to Assumptions (
8
) and (
9
), and Lemmas (
6.22
) and (
6.23
) .

Third, we analyze
V_{3,n}
. Observe that

\displaystyle||V_{3,n}||_{2}

\displaystyle=\Bigg|\Bigg|\bigg(\frac{1}{\widehat{\tau}_{n}}-\frac{1}{\tau_{n}}\bigg)\frac{\sqrt{\tau_{n}(1-\tau_{n})}}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}D_{i}\big(s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau_{n})}(X_{i})-\mathbb{E}_{\pi^{\ast}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau_{n})}]\big)\Bigg|\Bigg|_{2}

\displaystyle=\Bigg|\Bigg|\frac{\tau_{n}-\widehat{\tau}_{n}}{\widehat{\tau}_{n}\tau_{n}}\frac{\sqrt{\tau_{n}(1-\tau_{n})}}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}D_{i}\big(s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau_{n})}(X_{i})-\mathbb{E}_{\pi^{\ast}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau_{n})}]\big)\Bigg|\Bigg|_{2}

\displaystyle=\Bigg|\Bigg|\frac{\tau_{n}-\widehat{\tau}_{n}}{\widehat{\tau}_{n}\sqrt{\tau_{n}}}\frac{\sqrt{1-\tau_{n}}}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}D_{i}\big(s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau_{n})}(X_{i})-\mathbb{E}_{\pi^{\ast}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau_{n})}]\big)\Bigg|\Bigg|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\Bigg|\Bigg|\frac{\tau_{n}-\widehat{\tau}_{n}}{\widehat{\tau}_{n}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{D_{i}}{\sqrt{\tau_{n}}}\big(s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau_{n})}(X_{i})-\mathbb{E}_{\pi^{\ast}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau_{n})}]\big)\Bigg|\Bigg|_{2}

\displaystyle=\bigg|\frac{\widehat{\tau}_{n}-\tau_{n}}{\widehat{\tau}_{n}}\bigg|\Bigg|\Bigg|\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{D_{i}}{\sqrt{\tau_{n}}}\big(s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau_{n})}(X_{i})-\mathbb{E}_{\pi^{\ast}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau_{n})}]\big)\Bigg|\Bigg|_{2}

\displaystyle=O_{\mathbb{P}}\bigg(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n\tau_{n}}}\bigg)\Bigg|\Bigg|\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{D_{i}}{\sqrt{\tau_{n}}}\big(s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau_{n})}(X_{i})-\mathbb{E}_{\pi^{\ast}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau_{n})}]\big)\Bigg|\Bigg|_{2},

where the last line is because, under Assumptions (
8
) and (
9
), we have by Lemma (
6.21
) that
\frac{\widehat{\tau}_{n}-\tau_{n}}{\widehat{\tau}_{n}}=O_{\mathbb{P}}\big(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n\tau_{n}}}\big)
. We now show that
\Big|\Big|\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{D_{i}}{\sqrt{\tau_{n}}}\big(s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau_{n})}(X_{i})-\mathbb{E}_{\pi^{\ast}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau_{n})}]\big)\Big|\Big|_{2}=O_{\mathbb{P}}(1)
. Towards that end, observe that for each
j\in[m]
:

\displaystyle\textup{\text{Var}}_{\mathcal{J}^{\pi^{\ast},\mathbf{p},\tau_{n}}}\bigg[\frac{D}{\sqrt{\tau_{n}}}\big(s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau_{n}),j}-\mathbb{E}_{\pi^{\ast}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau_{n}),j}]\big)\bigg]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\tau_{n}}\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{J}^{\pi^{\ast},\mathbf{p},\tau_{n}}}\Big[D\big(s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau_{n}),j}-\mathbb{E}_{\pi^{\ast}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau_{n}),j}]\big)^{2}\Big]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{\pi^{\ast}}\Big[\big(s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau_{n}),j}-\mathbb{E}_{\pi^{\ast}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau_{n}),j}]\big)^{2}\Big]

\displaystyle=\textup{\text{Var}}_{\pi^{\ast}}\Big[s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau_{n}),j}\Big]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{L^{2}},

where the last line is by Lemma (
6.1
) and Popoviciu’s variance inequality. So, since

\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{J}^{\pi^{\ast},\mathbf{p},\tau_{n}}}\bigg[\frac{D}{\sqrt{\tau_{n}}}\big(s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau_{n})}-\mathbb{E}_{\pi^{\ast}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau_{n})}]\big)\bigg]=0,

it follows that

\displaystyle\Big|\Big|\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{D_{i}}{\sqrt{\tau_{n}}}\big(s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau_{n})}(X_{i})-\mathbb{E}_{\pi^{\ast}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau_{n})}]\big)\Big|\Big|_{2}

\displaystyle=O_{\mathbb{P}}\Bigg(\sqrt{\sum_{j=1}^{m}\textup{\text{Var}}_{\mathcal{J}^{\pi^{\ast},\mathbf{p},\tau_{n}}}\bigg[\frac{D}{\sqrt{\tau_{n}}}\big(s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau_{n}),j}-\mathbb{E}_{\pi^{\ast}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau_{n}),j}]\big)\bigg]}\Bigg)

\displaystyle=O_{\mathbb{P}}\Bigg(\sqrt{\frac{m}{L^{2}}}\Bigg)

\displaystyle=O_{\mathbb{P}}(1).

Thus, we have shown that:

\displaystyle||V_{3,n}||_{2}

\displaystyle=O_{\mathbb{P}}\bigg(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n\tau_{n}}}\bigg).

Fourth, we analyze
V_{4,n}
. Observe that:

\displaystyle||V_{4,n}||_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\Bigg|\Bigg|\bigg(\frac{1}{\widehat{\tau}_{n}}-\frac{1}{\tau_{n}}\bigg)\frac{\tau_{n}}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{D_{i}}{\sqrt{\tau_{n}}}\Big[\big(s_{\gamma^{\ast}}(X_{i})-\mathbb{E}_{\pi^{\ast}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]\big)-\big(s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau_{n})}(X_{i})-\mathbb{E}_{\pi^{\ast}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau_{n})}]\big)\Big]\Bigg|\Bigg|_{2}

\displaystyle=\Bigg|\Bigg|\frac{\tau_{n}-\widehat{\tau}_{n}}{\widehat{\tau}_{n}\tau_{n}}\frac{\tau_{n}}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{D_{i}}{\sqrt{\tau_{n}}}\Big[\big(s_{\gamma^{\ast}}(X_{i})-\mathbb{E}_{\pi^{\ast}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]\big)-\big(s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau_{n})}(X_{i})-\mathbb{E}_{\pi^{\ast}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau_{n})}]\big)\Big]\Bigg|\Bigg|_{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{|\tau_{n}-\widehat{\tau}_{n}|}{\widehat{\tau}_{n}}\times\Bigg|\Bigg|\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{D_{i}}{\sqrt{\tau_{n}}}\Big[\big(s_{\gamma^{\ast}}(X_{i})-\mathbb{E}_{\pi^{\ast}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]\big)-\big(s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau_{n})}(X_{i})-\mathbb{E}_{\pi^{\ast}}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau_{n})}]\big)\Big]\Bigg|\Bigg|_{2}

\displaystyle=O_{\mathbb{P}}\bigg(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n\tau_{n}}}\bigg)\times O_{\mathbb{P}}\Bigg(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n\tau_{n}(1-\tau_{n})}}\Bigg)

\displaystyle=O_{\mathbb{P}}\Bigg(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n\tau_{n}}\sqrt{n\tau_{n}(1-\tau_{n})}}\Bigg),

where the second to last line follows from the work used to analyze
||V_{1,n}||_{2}
, as well as from Assumptions (
8
) and (
9
) and Lemma (
6.21
).

Fifth, we analyze
V_{5,n}
. Observe that:

\displaystyle V_{5,n}

\displaystyle=\sum_{j=0}^{m}\bigg(\frac{1}{1-\tau_{n}}\frac{\pi_{j}^{\ast}}{\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}-\frac{1}{1-\widehat{\tau}_{n}}\frac{\pi_{j}^{\ast}}{\widehat{\pi}_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\bigg)\frac{\sqrt{\tau_{n}(1-\tau_{n})}}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}(1-D_{i})\mathbbold{1}{\{Y_{i}=j\}}\big(s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau_{n})}(X_{i})-\mathbb{E}_{j}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau_{n})}]\big)

\displaystyle=\sum_{j=0}^{m}\bigg(\frac{1}{1-\tau_{n}}\frac{\pi_{j}^{\ast}}{\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}-\frac{1}{1-\widehat{\tau}_{n}}\frac{\pi_{j}^{\ast}}{\widehat{\pi}_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\bigg)\frac{\sqrt{\tau_{n}}(1-\tau_{n})\sqrt{\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{(1-D_{i})\mathbbold{1}{\{Y_{i}=j\}}}{\sqrt{(1-\tau_{n})\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}}\big(s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau_{n})}(X_{i})-\mathbb{E}_{j}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau_{n})}]\big),

and that, for each
j\in[m]
:

\displaystyle\bigg|\frac{1}{1-\tau_{n}}\frac{\pi_{j}^{\ast}}{\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}-\frac{1}{1-\widehat{\tau}_{n}}\frac{\pi_{j}^{\ast}}{\widehat{\pi}_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\bigg|\sqrt{\tau_{n}}(1-\tau_{n})\sqrt{\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}

\displaystyle=\bigg|\frac{1}{1-\tau_{n}}\frac{1}{\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}-\frac{1}{1-\widehat{\tau}_{n}}\frac{1}{\widehat{\pi}_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\bigg|\pi_{j}^{\ast}\sqrt{\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}\tau_{n}}(1-\tau_{n})

\displaystyle=\bigg|\frac{1}{\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}-\frac{1-\tau_{n}}{1-\widehat{\tau}_{n}}\frac{1}{\widehat{\pi}_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\bigg|\pi_{j}^{\ast}\sqrt{\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}\tau_{n}}

\displaystyle=\bigg|\frac{1}{\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}-\frac{1}{\widehat{\pi}_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}+\frac{1}{\widehat{\pi}_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}-\frac{1-\tau_{n}}{1-\widehat{\tau}_{n}}\frac{1}{\widehat{\pi}_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\bigg|\pi_{j}^{\ast}\sqrt{\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}\tau_{n}}

\displaystyle\leq\Bigg(\bigg|\frac{1}{\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}-\frac{1}{\widehat{\pi}_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\bigg|+\bigg|1-\frac{1-\tau_{n}}{1-\widehat{\tau}_{n}}\bigg|\frac{1}{\widehat{\pi}_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\Bigg)\pi_{j}^{\ast}\sqrt{\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}\tau_{n}}

\displaystyle\leq\Bigg(\bigg|\frac{1}{\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}-\frac{1}{\widehat{\pi}_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\bigg|+\bigg|1-\frac{1-\tau_{n}}{1-\widehat{\tau}_{n}}\bigg|\bigg(\frac{1}{\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}+\bigg|\frac{1}{\widehat{\pi}_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}-\frac{1}{\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\bigg|\bigg)\Bigg)\pi_{j}^{\ast}\sqrt{\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}\tau_{n}}

\displaystyle=\Bigg(\bigg|\frac{\widehat{\pi}_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}-\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}{\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}\widehat{\pi}_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\bigg|+\bigg|\frac{(1-\widehat{\tau}_{n})-(1-\tau_{n})}{1-\widehat{\tau}_{n}}\bigg|\bigg(\frac{1}{\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}+\bigg|\frac{\widehat{\pi}_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}-\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}{\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}\widehat{\pi}_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\bigg|\bigg)\Bigg)\pi_{j}^{\ast}\sqrt{\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}\tau_{n}}

\displaystyle=\Bigg(\bigg|\frac{\widehat{\pi}_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}-\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}{\widehat{\pi}_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\bigg|+\bigg|\frac{(1-\widehat{\tau}_{n})-(1-\tau_{n})}{1-\widehat{\tau}_{n}}\bigg|\bigg(1+\bigg|\frac{\widehat{\pi}_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}-\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}{\widehat{\pi}_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\bigg|\bigg)\Bigg)\frac{\pi_{j}^{\ast}}{\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\sqrt{\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}\tau_{n}}

\displaystyle\leq\Bigg(\bigg|\frac{\widehat{\pi}_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}-\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}{\widehat{\pi}_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\bigg|+\bigg|\frac{(1-\widehat{\tau}_{n})-(1-\tau_{n})}{1-\widehat{\tau}_{n}}\bigg|\bigg(1+\bigg|\frac{\widehat{\pi}_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}-\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}{\widehat{\pi}_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\bigg|\bigg)\Bigg)\frac{1}{\xi}

\displaystyle=\Bigg(\bigg|\frac{\widehat{\pi}_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}-\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}{\widehat{\pi}_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\bigg|+O_{\mathbb{P}}\bigg(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n(1-\tau_{n})}}\bigg)\bigg(1+\bigg|\frac{\widehat{\pi}_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}-\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}{\widehat{\pi}_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\bigg|\bigg)\Bigg)\frac{1}{\xi},

where the last line is because, under Assumptions (
8
) and (
9
), we have by Lemma (
6.21
) that
\frac{(1-\widehat{\tau}_{n})-(1-\tau_{n})}{1-\widehat{\tau}_{n}}=O_{\mathbb{P}}\big(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n(1-\tau_{n})}}\big)
. Also note that:

\displaystyle\bigg|\frac{\widehat{\pi}_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}-\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}{\widehat{\pi}_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\bigg|

\displaystyle=\bigg|\frac{N_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}-\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}N^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}{N_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\bigg|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\big|N_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}-\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}(1-\tau_{n})n\big|}{N_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}+\frac{\big|\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}(1-\tau_{n})n-\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}N^{\textup{\text{tr}}}\big|}{N_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}

\displaystyle=\frac{\big|N_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}/n-\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}(1-\tau_{n})\big|}{N_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}/n}+\frac{\big|(1-\tau_{n})n-N^{\textup{\text{tr}}}\big|}{N_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}

\displaystyle=\frac{\big|N_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}/n-\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}(1-\tau_{n})\big|}{N_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}/n}+\frac{\big|(1-\tau_{n})-N^{\textup{\text{tr}}}/n\big|}{N^{\textup{\text{tr}}}/n}\frac{\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}N^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}{N_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}.

Observe that:

\displaystyle\frac{\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}N^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}{N_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}

\displaystyle=\frac{\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}(1-\tau_{n})n}{N_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\frac{N^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}{(1-\tau_{n})n}

\displaystyle=\bigg|\frac{\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}(1-\tau_{n})}{N_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}/n}-1+1\bigg|\bigg|\frac{N^{\textup{\text{tr}}}/n}{1-\tau_{n}}-1+1\bigg|

\displaystyle=\bigg|\frac{\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}(1-\tau_{n})-N_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}/n}{N_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}/n}+1\bigg|\bigg|\frac{N^{\textup{\text{tr}}}/n-(1-\tau_{n})}{1-\tau_{n}}+1\bigg|.

Hence:

\displaystyle\bigg|\frac{\widehat{\pi}_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}-\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}{\widehat{\pi}_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\bigg|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\big|N_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}/n-\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}(1-\tau_{n})\big|}{N_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}/n}+\frac{\big|(1-\tau_{n})-(1-\widehat{\tau}_{n})\big|}{1-\widehat{\tau}_{n}}\Bigg(\bigg|\frac{\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}(1-\tau_{n})-N_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}/n}{N_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}/n}+1\bigg|\bigg|\frac{(1-\widehat{\tau}_{n})-(1-\tau_{n})}{1-\tau_{n}}+1\bigg|\Bigg).

Under Assumptions (
8
) and (
9
), we have by Lemma (
6.21
) that
\frac{|N_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}/n-\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}(1-\tau_{n})|}{N_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}/n}
,
\frac{|(1-\tau_{n})-(1-\widehat{\tau}_{n})|}{1-\widehat{\tau}_{n}}
and
\frac{(1-\widehat{\tau}_{n})-(1-\tau_{n})}{1-\tau_{n}}
are all
O_{\mathbb{P}}\Big(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n(1-\tau_{n})}}\Big)
. Thus, it follows that
\Big|\frac{\widehat{\pi}_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}-\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}{\widehat{\pi}_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\Big|=O_{\mathbb{P}}\Big(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n(1-\tau_{n})}}\Big)
, and so we may conclude that:

\displaystyle\bigg|\frac{1}{1-\tau_{n}}\frac{\pi_{j}^{\ast}}{\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}-\frac{1}{1-\widehat{\tau}_{n}}\frac{\pi_{j}^{\ast}}{\widehat{\pi}_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\bigg|\sqrt{\tau_{n}}(1-\tau_{n})\sqrt{\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}

(64)

\displaystyle\leq\Bigg(\bigg|\frac{\widehat{\pi}_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}-\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}{\widehat{\pi}_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\bigg|+O_{\mathbb{P}}\bigg(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n(1-\tau_{n})}}\bigg)\bigg(1+\bigg|\frac{\widehat{\pi}_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}-\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}{\widehat{\pi}_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\bigg|\bigg)\Bigg)\frac{1}{\xi}

\displaystyle=O_{\mathbb{P}}\bigg(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n(1-\tau_{n})}}\bigg)+O_{\mathbb{P}}\bigg(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n(1-\tau_{n})}}\bigg)\Bigg(1+O_{\mathbb{P}}\bigg(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n(1-\tau_{n})}}\bigg)\Bigg)

\displaystyle=O_{\mathbb{P}}\bigg(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n(1-\tau_{n})}}\bigg).

(65)

This implies that
||V_{5,n}||_{2}
is upper bounded by the summation of
m+1
terms, where the
{j}^{\text{th}}
term has the form:

O_{\mathbb{P}}\bigg(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n(1-\tau_{n})}}\bigg)\times\bigg|\bigg|\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{(1-D_{i})\mathbbold{1}{\{Y_{i}=j\}}}{\sqrt{(1-\tau_{n})\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}}\big(s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau_{n})}(X_{i})-\mathbb{E}_{j}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau_{n})}]\big)\bigg|\bigg|_{2}.

Consequentially, to show that
||V_{5,n}||_{2}=O_{\mathbb{P}}\Big(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n(1-\tau_{n})}}\Big)
, it suffices to show that

\Bigg|\Bigg|\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{(1-D_{i})\mathbbold{1}{\{Y_{i}=j\}}}{\sqrt{(1-\tau_{n})\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}}\big(s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau_{n})}(X_{i})-\mathbb{E}_{j}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau_{n})}]\big)\Bigg|\Bigg|_{2}=O_{\mathbb{P}}(1)

for each
j\in\mathcal{Y}
. So, towards verifying the display above, observe that, for each
k\in[m]
:

\displaystyle\textup{\text{Var}}_{\mathcal{J}^{\pi^{\ast},\mathbf{p},\tau_{n}}}\Bigg[\frac{(1-D)\mathbbold{1}{\{Y=j\}}}{\sqrt{(1-\tau_{n})\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}}\big(s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau_{n}),k}-\mathbb{E}_{j}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau_{n}),k}]\big)\Bigg]

\displaystyle=\frac{\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{J}^{\pi^{\ast},\mathbf{p},\tau_{n}}}\Big[(1-D)\mathbbold{1}{\{Y=j\}}\big(s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau_{n}),k}-\mathbb{E}_{j}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau_{n}),k}]\big)^{2}\Big]}{(1-\tau_{n})\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}

\displaystyle=\frac{(1-\tau_{n})\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}\mathbb{E}_{j}\Big[\big(s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau_{n}),k}-\mathbb{E}_{j}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau_{n}),k}]\big)^{2}\Big]}{(1-\tau_{n})\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}

\displaystyle=\textup{\text{Var}}_{j}\big[s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau_{n}),k}\big]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{L^{2}},

where the last line is by Lemma (
6.1
) and Popoviciu’s variance inequality. So, since

\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{J}^{\pi^{\ast},\mathbf{p},\tau_{n}}}\Bigg[\frac{(1-D)\mathbbold{1}{\{Y=j\}}}{\sqrt{(1-\tau_{n})\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}}\big(s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau_{n}),k}-\mathbb{E}_{j}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau_{n}),k}]\big)\Bigg]=0,

it follows that

\displaystyle\Bigg|\Bigg|\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{(1-D_{i})\mathbbold{1}{\{Y_{i}=j\}}}{\sqrt{(1-\tau_{n})\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}}\big(s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau_{n})}(X_{i})-\mathbb{E}_{j}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau_{n})}]\big)\Bigg|\Bigg|_{2}

\displaystyle=O_{\mathbb{P}}\Bigg(\sqrt{\sum_{k=1}^{m}\textup{\text{Var}}_{\mathcal{J}^{\pi^{\ast},\mathbf{p},\tau_{n}}}\Bigg[\frac{(1-D)\mathbbold{1}{\{Y=j\}}}{\sqrt{(1-\tau_{n})\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}}\big(s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau_{n}),k}-\mathbb{E}_{j}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau_{n}),k}]\big)\Bigg]}\Bigg)

\displaystyle=O_{\mathbb{P}}\Bigg(\sqrt{\frac{m}{L^{2}}}\Bigg)

\displaystyle=O_{\mathbb{P}}(1).

Thus, as argued earlier, we may conclude that
||V_{5,n}||_{2}=O_{\mathbb{P}}\Big(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n(1-\tau_{n})}}\Big)
.

Sixth, we analyze
V_{6,n}
. Observe that:

\displaystyle||V_{6,n}||_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{j=0}^{m}\Bigg\{\bigg|\frac{1}{1-\tau_{n}}\frac{\pi_{j}^{\ast}}{\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}-\frac{1}{1-\widehat{\tau}_{n}}\frac{\pi_{j}^{\ast}}{\widehat{\pi}_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\bigg|\sqrt{\tau_{n}}(1-\tau_{n})\sqrt{\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}

\displaystyle\hskip 17.70859pt\hskip 17.70859pt\hskip 17.70859pt\hskip 17.70859pt\times\Bigg|\Bigg|\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{(1-D_{i})\mathbbold{1}{\{Y_{i}=j\}}}{\sqrt{(1-\tau_{n})\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}}\Big[\big(s_{\gamma^{\ast}}(X_{i})-\mathbb{E}_{j}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]\big)-\big(s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau_{n})}(X_{i})-\mathbb{E}_{j}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau_{n})}]\big)\Big]\Bigg|\Bigg|_{2}\Bigg\},

so to show that
||V_{6,n}||_{2}=O_{\mathbb{P}}\Big(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n(1-\tau_{n})}\sqrt{n\tau_{n}(1-\tau_{n})}}\Big)
, it suffices to show that the
{j}^{\text{th}}
summand above is
O_{\mathbb{P}}\Big(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n(1-\tau_{n})}\sqrt{n\tau_{n}(1-\tau_{n})}}\Big)
, for each
j\in\mathcal{Y}
. To see this, recall that

\bigg|\frac{1}{1-\tau_{n}}\frac{\pi_{j}^{\ast}}{\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}-\frac{1}{1-\widehat{\tau}_{n}}\frac{\pi_{j}^{\ast}}{\widehat{\pi}_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\bigg|\sqrt{\tau_{n}}(1-\tau_{n})\sqrt{\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}=O_{\mathbb{P}}\bigg(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n(1-\tau_{n})}}\bigg)

by line (
65
) and

\Bigg|\Bigg|\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{(1-D_{i})\mathbbold{1}{\{Y_{i}=j\}}}{\sqrt{(1-\tau_{n})\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}}\Big[\big(s_{\gamma^{\ast}}(X_{i})-\mathbb{E}_{j}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}}]\big)-\big(s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau_{n})}(X_{i})-\mathbb{E}_{j}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau_{n})}]\big)\Big]\Bigg|\Bigg|_{2}=O_{\mathbb{P}}\Bigg(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n\tau_{n}(1-\tau_{n})}}\Bigg)

by the same work used to analyze
||V_{2,n}||_{2}
. This shows that the aforementioned
{j}^{\text{th}}
summand goes down like
O_{\mathbb{P}}\Big(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n(1-\tau_{n})}\sqrt{n\tau_{n}(1-\tau_{n})}}\Big)
. Thus,
||V_{6,n}||_{2}=O_{\mathbb{P}}\Big(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n(1-\tau_{n})}\sqrt{n\tau_{n}(1-\tau_{n})}}\Big)
.

Bringing together our analysis of
V_{1,n},\dots,V_{6,n}
, it follows that:

\displaystyle\Bigg|\Bigg|\sum_{l=1}^{6}V_{l,n}\Bigg|\Bigg|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{l=1}^{6}\big|\big|V_{l,n}\big|\big|_{2}

\displaystyle=O_{\mathbb{P}}\Bigg(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n\tau_{n}(1-\tau_{n})}}\Bigg)+O_{\mathbb{P}}\Bigg(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n\tau_{n}(1-\tau_{n})}}\Bigg)+O_{\mathbb{P}}\bigg(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n\tau_{n}}}\bigg)

\displaystyle\hskip 18.49988pt\hskip 18.49988pt+O_{\mathbb{P}}\Bigg(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n\tau_{n}}\sqrt{n\tau_{n}(1-\tau_{n})}}\Bigg)+O_{\mathbb{P}}\Bigg(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n(1-\tau_{n})}}\Bigg)+O_{\mathbb{P}}\Bigg(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n(1-\tau_{n})}\sqrt{n\tau_{n}(1-\tau_{n})}}\Bigg)

\displaystyle=O_{\mathbb{P}}\Bigg(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n\tau_{n}(1-\tau_{n})}}\Bigg),

where the last line follows from Assumptions (
8
) and (
9
). Now, under Assumption (
B1
), we have by Lemma (
6.24
) that all the eigenvalues of
\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})
are positive, which since
\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})
is symmetric, means that
\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})
is positive definite. Thus, the eigenvalues and singular values are equal, and so again by Lemma (
6.24
), we have that
\sigma_{\textup{min}}\big(\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})\big)>\Lambda\frac{L^{2}}{m^{2}}
. Hence:

\displaystyle\bigg|\bigg|\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})^{-1}\sum_{l=1}^{6}V_{l,n}\bigg|\bigg|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\sigma_{\textup{max}}\big(\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})^{-1}\big)\Bigg|\Bigg|\sum_{l=1}^{6}V_{l,n}\Bigg|\Bigg|_{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sigma_{\textup{min}}\big(\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})\big)}\Bigg|\Bigg|\sum_{l=1}^{6}V_{l,n}\Bigg|\Bigg|_{2}

\displaystyle<\frac{m^{2}}{\Lambda L^{2}}\Bigg|\Bigg|\sum_{l=1}^{6}V_{l,n}\Bigg|\Bigg|_{2}

\displaystyle=O_{\mathbb{P}}\Bigg(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n\tau_{n}(1-\tau_{n})}}\Bigg).

Next, we focus on bounding
\big(\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})^{-1}\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast}(\tau_{n}))-I_{m\times m}\big)\sqrt{\frac{\tau_{n}(1-\tau_{n})}{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\psi_{\tau_{n}}^{\textup{eff}}(Z_{i})
. Towards that end, observe that:

\displaystyle\sigma_{\textup{max}}\big(\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})^{-1}\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast}(\tau_{n}))-I_{m\times m}\big)

\displaystyle=\sigma_{\textup{max}}\Big\{\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})^{-1}\big(\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast}(\tau_{n}))-\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})\big)\Big\}

\displaystyle\leq\sigma_{\textup{max}}\big(\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})^{-1}\big)\sigma_{\textup{max}}\big(\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast}(\tau_{n}))-\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})\big)

\displaystyle<\frac{m^{2}}{\Lambda L^{2}}\sigma_{\textup{max}}\big(\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast}(\tau_{n}))-\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})\big).

Also observe that, by Lemma (
2.2
)

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast}(\tau_{n}))-\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})=A_{s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau_{n})}}-A_{s_{\gamma^{\ast}}}=A_{s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau_{n})}-s_{\gamma^{\ast}}},

and so:

\displaystyle\sigma_{\textup{max}}\big(\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})^{-1}\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast}(\tau_{n}))-I_{m\times m}\big)

\displaystyle<\frac{m^{2}}{\Lambda L^{2}}\sigma_{\textup{max}}\big(A_{s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau_{n})}-s_{\gamma^{\ast}}}\big)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{m^{2}}{\Lambda L^{2}}\sum_{i,j=1}^{m}\Big|\big[A_{s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau_{n})}-s_{\gamma^{\ast}}}\big]_{i,j}\Big|

\displaystyle=\frac{m^{2}}{\Lambda L^{2}}\sum_{i,j=1}^{m}\Big|\mathbb{E}_{j}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau_{n}),i}-s_{\gamma^{\ast},i}]-\mathbb{E}_{0}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau_{n}),i}-s_{\gamma^{\ast},i}]\Big|.

Now, by an argument similar to the one employed in the proof of Lemma (
6.23
), it is possible to show, for each
j\in\mathcal{Y}
and
i\in[m]
, that

\mathbb{E}_{j}[s_{\gamma^{\ast}(\tau_{n}),i}-s_{\gamma^{\ast},i}]\Big/\sum_{k=0}^{m}\big|\gamma_{k}^{\ast}-\gamma_{k}^{\ast}(\tau_{n})\big|=O_{\mathbb{P}}(1).

As
m
is finite, this implies that:

\displaystyle\sigma_{\textup{max}}\big(\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})^{-1}\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast}(\tau_{n}))-I_{m\times m}\big)

\displaystyle=O_{\mathbb{P}}(1)\times\sum_{k=0}^{m}\big|\gamma_{k}^{\ast}-\gamma_{k}^{\ast}(\tau_{n})\big|

\displaystyle=O_{\mathbb{P}}\bigg(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n\tau_{n}(1-\tau_{n})}}\bigg).

where the last line is due to Lemma (
6.22
) and Assumptions (
8
) and (
9
). Next, observe that:

\displaystyle\textup{\text{Var}}_{Z_{1:n}}\Bigg[\sqrt{\frac{\tau_{n}(1-\tau_{n})}{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\psi_{\tau_{n}}^{\textup{eff}}(Z_{i})\Bigg]

\displaystyle=\tau_{n}(1-\tau_{n})\textup{\text{Var}}_{\mathcal{J}^{\pi^{\ast},\mathbf{p},\tau_{n}}}\Big[\psi_{\tau_{n}}^{\textup{eff}}\Big]

\displaystyle=\tau_{n}(1-\tau_{n})\mathbf{V}^{\textup{eff}}(\tau_{n})

\displaystyle=\Bigg[(1-\tau_{n})+\tau_{n}\sum_{k=0}^{m}\frac{(\pi_{k}^{\ast})^{2}}{\pi_{k}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\Bigg]\Big(\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast}(\tau_{n}))^{-1}-\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast}(\tau_{n});\textup{Cat})^{-1}\Big)+(1-\tau_{n})\mathcal{I}(\pi^{\ast};\textup{Cat})^{-1},

where the second equality is due to line (
57
). Now, we know by Lemma (
6.1
) that
\gamma^{\ast}(\tau_{n})
is bounded, and so by Lemma (
3.5
), we have that
\text{Tr}\big\{\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast}(\tau_{n}))^{-1}\big\}-\text{Tr}\big\{\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast}(\tau_{n});\textup{Cat})^{-1}\big\}\geq 0
. We also know that
\mathcal{I}(\pi^{\ast};\textup{Cat})
is positive definite by Lemma (
6.3
), meaning that
\mathcal{I}(\pi^{\ast};\textup{Cat})^{-1}
is positive definite and hence
\text{Tr}\big\{\mathcal{I}(\pi^{\ast};\textup{Cat})^{-1}\big\}\geq 0
. One can also use the same logic to conclude that
\text{Tr}\big\{\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast}(\tau_{n});\textup{Cat})^{-1}\big\}\geq 0
. Thus:

\displaystyle\text{Tr}\Bigg\{\textup{\text{Var}}_{Z_{1:n}}\Bigg[\sqrt{\frac{\tau_{n}(1-\tau_{n})}{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\psi_{\tau_{n}}^{\textup{eff}}(Z_{i})\Bigg]\Bigg\}

\displaystyle\leq\Bigg[1+\sum_{k=0}^{m}\frac{(\pi_{k}^{\ast})^{2}}{\pi_{k}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\Bigg]\Big(\text{Tr}\big\{\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast}(\tau_{n}))^{-1}\big\}-\text{Tr}\big\{\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast}(\tau_{n});\textup{Cat})^{-1}\big\}\Big)+\text{Tr}\big\{\mathcal{I}(\pi^{\ast};\textup{Cat})^{-1}\big\}

\displaystyle\leq\Bigg[1+\sum_{k=0}^{m}\frac{(\pi_{k}^{\ast})^{2}}{\pi_{k}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\Bigg]\text{Tr}\big\{\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast}(\tau_{n}))^{-1}\big\}+\text{Tr}\big\{\mathcal{I}(\pi^{\ast};\textup{Cat})^{-1}\big\}

\displaystyle\leq\Bigg[1+\sum_{k=0}^{m}\frac{(\pi_{k}^{\ast})^{2}}{\pi_{k}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\Bigg]m\lambda_{\textup{max}}\Big\{\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast}(\tau_{n}))^{-1}\Big\}+m\lambda_{\textup{max}}\Big\{\mathcal{I}(\pi^{\ast};\textup{Cat})^{-1}\Big\}

\displaystyle=\Bigg[1+\sum_{k=0}^{m}\frac{(\pi_{k}^{\ast})^{2}}{\pi_{k}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\Bigg]\frac{m}{\sigma_{\textup{min}}\Big\{\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast}(\tau_{n}))\Big\}}+\frac{m}{\lambda_{\textup{min}}\Big\{\mathcal{I}(\pi^{\ast};\textup{Cat})\Big\}}

\displaystyle\leq\Bigg[1+\sum_{k=0}^{m}\frac{(\pi_{k}^{\ast})^{2}}{\pi_{k}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\Bigg]\frac{m}{\sqrt{\Lambda}}+\frac{m}{2}

\displaystyle=O(1),

where the last inequality is by Assumption (
B1
) and Lemma (
6.3
). Since
\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{J}^{\pi^{\ast},\mathbf{p},\tau_{n}}}\big[\psi_{\tau_{n}}^{\textup{eff}}\big]=0
, it follows that

\displaystyle\Bigg|\Bigg|\sqrt{\frac{\tau_{n}(1-\tau_{n})}{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\psi_{\tau_{n}}^{\textup{eff}}(Z_{i})\Bigg|\Bigg|_{2}

\displaystyle=O_{\mathbb{P}}\Bigg(\sqrt{\text{Tr}\Bigg\{\textup{\text{Var}}_{Z_{1:n}}\Bigg[\sqrt{\frac{\tau_{n}(1-\tau_{n})}{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\psi_{\tau_{n}}^{\textup{eff}}(Z_{i})\Bigg]\Bigg\}}\Bigg)

\displaystyle=O_{\mathbb{P}}(1),

meaning that:

\displaystyle\Bigg|\Bigg|\big(\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})^{-1}\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast}(\tau_{n}))-I_{m\times m}\big)\sqrt{\frac{\tau_{n}(1-\tau_{n})}{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\psi_{\tau_{n}}^{\textup{eff}}(Z_{i})\Bigg|\Bigg|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\sigma_{\textup{max}}\big(\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})^{-1}\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast}(\tau_{n}))-I_{m\times m}\big)\Bigg|\Bigg|\sqrt{\frac{\tau_{n}(1-\tau_{n})}{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\psi_{\tau_{n}}^{\textup{eff}}(Z_{i})\Bigg|\Bigg|_{2}

\displaystyle=O_{\mathbb{P}}\bigg(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n\tau_{n}(1-\tau_{n})}}\bigg).

Thus, overall, we have that:

\displaystyle\big|\big|\delta_{1,\tau_{n}}(Z_{1:n})\big|\big|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\bigg|\bigg|\big(\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})^{-1}\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast}(\tau_{n}))-I_{m\times m}\big)\sqrt{\frac{\tau_{n}(1-\tau_{n})}{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\psi_{\tau_{n}}^{\textup{eff}}(Z_{i})\bigg|\bigg|_{2}+\bigg|\bigg|\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})^{-1}\sum_{l=1}^{6}V_{l,n}\bigg|\bigg|_{2}

\displaystyle=O_{\mathbb{P}}\Bigg(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n\tau_{n}(1-\tau_{n})}}\Bigg).

Next, we bound
||\delta_{2,\tau_{n}}(Z_{1:n})||_{2}
. Let
\epsilon
be as defined in Corollary (
6.15.1
), i.e.,
\epsilon=(\widehat{\pi}_{n}-\pi^{\ast})-\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\psi^{\textup{eff}}(Z_{i})
. Then:

\displaystyle||\delta_{2,\tau_{n}}(Z_{1:n})||_{2}

\displaystyle=\bigg|\bigg|\sqrt{\tau_{n}(1-\tau_{n})n}(\widehat{\pi}_{n}-\pi^{\ast})-\sqrt{\frac{\tau_{n}(1-\tau_{n})}{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\psi^{\textup{eff}}(Z_{i})\bigg|\bigg|_{2}

\displaystyle=\sqrt{\tau_{n}(1-\tau_{n})n}\bigg|\bigg|(\widehat{\pi}_{n}-\pi^{\ast})-\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\psi^{\textup{eff}}(Z_{i})\bigg|\bigg|_{2}

\displaystyle=\sqrt{\tau_{n}(1-\tau_{n})n}||\epsilon||_{2}.

Thus, for any
b,b_{n}>0
and
c^{\textup{\text{te}}},c_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr}}},\dots,c_{m}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}>0
(where
b,c^{\textup{\text{te}}},c_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr}}},\dots,c_{m}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}
do not depend on
n
):

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}_{Z_{1:n}}\Big[||\delta_{2,\tau_{n}}(Z_{1:n})||_{2}/b_{n}\geq b\Big]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{P}_{Z_{1:n}}\Big[\sqrt{\tau_{n}(1-\tau_{n})n}||\epsilon||_{2}/b_{n}\geq b\Big]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{P}_{Z_{1:n}}\Big[\sqrt{\tau_{n}(1-\tau_{n})n}||\epsilon||_{2}/b_{n}\geq b\ \Big\lvert\ |N^{\textup{\text{te}}}-\tau_{n}n|\leq c^{\textup{\text{te}}}\sqrt{\tau_{n}(1-\tau_{n})n},

\displaystyle\hskip 18.49988pt\hskip 18.49988pt\hskip 18.49988pt\hskip 18.49988pt\hskip 18.49988pt\hskip 18.49988pt\hskip 18.49988pt\ |N_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}-(1-\tau_{n})\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}n|\leq c_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}\sqrt{(1-\tau_{n})\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}[1-(1-\tau_{n})\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}]n}\ \forall j\in\mathcal{Y}\Big]

\displaystyle\hskip 18.49988pt+\mathbb{P}_{Z_{1:n}}\Big[|N^{\textup{\text{te}}}-\tau_{n}n|>c^{\textup{\text{te}}}\sqrt{\tau_{n}(1-\tau_{n})n}\Big]

\displaystyle\hskip 18.49988pt+\sum_{j=0}^{m}\mathbb{P}_{Z_{1:n}}\Big[|N_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}-(1-\tau_{n})\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}n|>c_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}\sqrt{(1-\tau_{n})\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}[1-(1-\tau_{n})\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}]n}\Big].

For each
n
, let
\bar{n}^{\textup{\text{te}}},\bar{n}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr}}},\dots,\bar{n}_{m}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}
be the sample sizes for which

\mathbb{P}_{Z_{1:n}}\Big[\sqrt{\tau_{n}(1-\tau_{n})n}||\epsilon||_{2}/b_{n}\geq b\ \Big\lvert\ N^{\textup{\text{te}}}=\bar{n}^{\textup{\text{te}}},\ N_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}=\bar{n}_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}\ \forall j\in\mathcal{Y}\Big]

is maximized, subject to the following constraints:

•

\bar{n}^{\textup{\text{te}}}+\bar{n}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}+\dots+\bar{n}_{m}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}=n

•

|\bar{n}^{\textup{\text{te}}}-\tau_{n}n|\leq c^{\textup{\text{te}}}\sqrt{\tau_{n}(1-\tau_{n})n}

•

|\bar{n}_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}-(1-\tau_{n})\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}n|\leq c_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}\sqrt{(1-\tau_{n})\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}[1-(1-\tau_{n})\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}]n}\ \forall j\in\mathcal{Y}
.

Such sample sizes must exist, because the feasible set is nonempty (e.g.,
\bar{n}^{\textup{\text{te}}}=\tau_{n}n
and
\bar{n}_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}=(1-\tau_{n})\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}n
for each
j\in\mathcal{Y}
satisfies the constraints) and the feasible set is countably finite. It follows that:

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}_{Z_{1:n}}\Big[||\delta_{2,\tau_{n}}(Z_{1:n})||_{2}/b_{n}\geq b\Big]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{P}_{Z_{1:n}}\Big[\sqrt{\tau_{n}(1-\tau_{n})n}||\epsilon||_{2}/b_{n}\geq b\ \Big\lvert\ N^{\textup{\text{te}}}=\bar{n}^{\textup{\text{te}}},\ N_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}=\bar{n}_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}\ \forall j\in\mathcal{Y}\Big]

\displaystyle\qquad+\mathbb{P}_{Z_{1:n}}\Big[|N^{\textup{\text{te}}}-\tau_{n}n|>c^{\textup{\text{te}}}\sqrt{\tau_{n}(1-\tau_{n})n}\Big]

\displaystyle\qquad+\sum_{j=0}^{m}\mathbb{P}_{Z_{1:n}}\Big[|N_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}-(1-\tau_{n})\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}n|>c_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}\sqrt{(1-\tau_{n})\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}[1-(1-\tau_{n})\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}]n}\Big].

(66)

Now, due to the aforementioned constraints on
\bar{n}^{\textup{\text{te}}},\bar{n}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr}}},\dots,\bar{n}_{m}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}
, it is easy to show that
\bar{n}^{\textup{\text{te}}},\bar{n}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}\to\infty
, as a result of Assumption (
9
), where
\bar{n}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}=n-\bar{n}^{\textup{\text{te}}}
. In addition, since
c^{\textup{\text{te}}},c_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr}}},\dots,c_{m}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}
and
\pi^{\textup{\text{tr}}}
do not depend on
n
, it is not hard to show that
\frac{\bar{n}_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}{\bar{n}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\to\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}
for each
j\in\mathcal{Y}
as
n\to\infty
. This latter observation implies that, for all sufficiently large
n
,
\frac{\bar{n}_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}{\bar{n}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\in(\xi,1-\xi)
, since
\pi_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}\in(\xi,1-\xi)
. Altogether, this means that for all sufficiently large
n
, when conditioning on the event that
N^{\textup{\text{te}}}=\bar{n}^{\textup{\text{te}}}
and
\ N_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}=\bar{n}_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}\ \forall j\in\mathcal{Y}
, the data generation mechanism in the
\tau_{n}
-IID Regime is
identical
to the data generation mechanism in a Fixed Sequence Regime with the following sequence of sample sizes:

•

at each
n
,
\bar{n}^{\textup{\text{te}}}
samples are drawn IID from
p_{\pi^{\ast}}

•

at each
n
and for each
j\in\mathcal{Y}
,
\bar{n}_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}=\frac{\bar{n}_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}{\bar{n}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\bar{n}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}
samples are drawn IID from
p_{j}
.

The asymptotic behavior of the true parameters in the
\tau_{n}
-IID Regime is also a special case of the Fixed Sequence Regime: for each
n
, the parameters
\pi^{\ast}
and
\mathbf{p}
are unchanged. One can also show that the assumptions of the
\tau_{n}
-IID Regime (when we condition on the event that
N^{\textup{\text{te}}}=\bar{n}^{\textup{\text{te}}}
and
\ N_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}=\bar{n}_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}\ \forall j\in\mathcal{Y}
) are a special case of the assumptions of the Fixed Sequence Regime, whenever
n
is sufficiently large. In particular:

•

Assumption (
A1
) in the Fixed Sequence Regime is satisfied by Assumption (
4
) and the fact that
s_{\gamma^{\ast}}\in\mathcal{H}
in the
\tau_{n}
-IID Regime, whenever
n
is sufficiently large and we condition on the event that
N^{\textup{\text{te}}}=\bar{n}^{\textup{\text{te}}}
and
N_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}=\bar{n}_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}\ \forall j\in\mathcal{Y}
. To see that the latter is true, note that
\mathbf{p}\in\mathcal{Q}
, so to show that
s_{\gamma^{\ast}}\in\mathcal{H}^{\text{pre}}
, we only need to show that
\gamma^{\ast}\in\Gamma
. This would prove that
s_{\gamma^{\ast}}\in\mathcal{H}
because
\mathcal{H}^{\text{pre}}\subseteq\mathcal{H}
. Now, to see that
\gamma^{\ast}\in\Gamma
, note that because we are conditioning on the event that
N^{\textup{\text{te}}}=\bar{n}^{\textup{\text{te}}}
and
N_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}=\bar{n}_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}\ \forall j\in\mathcal{Y}
, it follows that
\gamma^{\ast}
is non-random, with the
{y}^{\text{th}}
component of
\gamma^{\ast}
equal to:

\gamma_{y}^{\ast}=\frac{\frac{\pi_{y}^{\ast}}{\bar{n}^{\textup{\text{te}}}}+\frac{1}{\bar{n}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\frac{(\pi_{y}^{\ast})^{2}}{\bar{n}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}/\bar{n}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}}{\frac{1}{\bar{n}^{\textup{\text{te}}}}+\frac{1}{\bar{n}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\sum_{k=0}^{m}\frac{(\pi_{k}^{\ast})^{2}}{\bar{n}_{k}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}/\bar{n}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}}.

(67)

Since
\pi^{\ast}\in\Delta
,
\sum_{y=0}^{m}\frac{\bar{n}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}{\bar{n}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}=1
, and
\frac{\bar{n}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}{\bar{n}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}}\in(\xi,1-\xi)
for all sufficiently large
n
and
y\in\mathcal{Y}
, it follows from Lemma (
6.1
) that
L\leq\gamma_{y}^{\ast}\leq U
for all sufficiently large
n
and
y\in\mathcal{Y}
. That is,
\gamma^{\ast}\in\Gamma
for all sufficiently large
n
.

•

Assumption (
•
‣
A2
) in the Fixed Sequence Regime is satisfied by Assumption (
5
) in the
\tau_{n}
-IID Regime.

•

Assumption (
A3
) in the Fixed Sequence Regime is satisfied by Assumptions (
5
) and (
6
) in the
\tau_{n}
-IID Regime, when we condition on the event that
N^{\textup{\text{te}}}=\bar{n}^{\textup{\text{te}}}
and
N_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}=\bar{n}_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}\ \forall j\in\mathcal{Y}
. To demonstrate this, we begin by verifying that each of the conditions needed to invoke Assumption (
6
) hold. Towards that end, note that
\pi^{\ast}\in\Delta
and
\mathbf{p}\in\mathcal{Q}
, and that by virtue of how
(\bar{n}^{\textup{\text{te}}},\bar{n}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr}}},\dots,\bar{n}_{m}^{\textup{\text{tr}}})
was constructed, each of the foregoing sample sizes grow towards infinity. Also, the definition of
\gamma^{\ast}
in (
67
) equals the definition of
\bar{\gamma}
in Assumptions section for the
\tau_{n}
-IID Regime. Thus, all of the conditions have been met, so by Assumption (
6
), we have for each
j\in[m]
that

–

\lambda_{j}\sup_{\gamma\in\Gamma}\Omega^{2}\Big(\frac{q_{j}-q_{0}}{q_{\gamma}}\Big)\to 0

–

\lambda_{j}^{2}\bar{n}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}\to\infty

–

\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}\Bigg\{\sup\limits_{\Omega(h_{j})\leq\frac{1}{L\sqrt{\lambda_{j}}}}\Big|\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{0}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[h_{j}]-\mathbb{E}_{0}[h_{j}]\Big|+\sup\limits_{\Omega(h_{j})\leq\frac{1}{L\sqrt{\lambda_{j}}}}\Big|\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[h_{j}]-\mathbb{E}_{j}[h_{j}]\Big|+\sup\limits_{\Omega(h_{j})\leq\frac{1}{L\sqrt{\lambda_{j}}}}\Big|\widehat{\textup{\text{Var}}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}[h_{j}]-\textup{\text{Var}}_{\gamma^{\ast}}[h_{j}]\Big|\Bigg\}\to 0
,

where, for each
n
,
\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}
is the analogue of
\bar{\mathcal{D}}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}
described in the Assumptions section for the
\tau_{n}
-IID Regime, i.e.,
\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}
contains
\frac{1}{2}\bar{n}_{y}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}
IID samples from
p_{y}
for each
y\in\mathcal{Y}
. Using the display above, we will now verify that all the conditions listed in Assumption (
A3
) are met. Since
\lambda_{j}\sup_{\gamma\in\Gamma}\Omega^{2}(s_{\gamma,j})=o(1)
for each
j\in[m]
, it follows that
\lambda_{j}\Omega^{2}(s_{\gamma^{\ast},j})=o(1)
for each
j\in[m]
, because as was argued earlier,
\gamma^{\ast}\in\Gamma
for all sufficiently large
n
. In addition, consider any fixed
\gamma\in\Gamma
. Clearly,
\lambda_{j}\Omega(s_{\gamma,j})=o(1)
, so because
s_{\gamma,j}
is independent of
n
and
\Omega(s_{\gamma,j})
is finite due to Assumption (
5
), we have that
\lambda_{j}=o(1)
. We also have that
\lambda_{j}^{2}\bar{n}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}\to\infty
. Finally, the uniform convergence in the display above is equivalent to the requirement in Assumption (
A3
) that
\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}^{\textup{\text{tr,II}}}}[\mathcal{U}_{j}(\lambda_{j})]\to 0
. Thus, Assumption (
A3
) does indeed hold.

•

Assumption (
A4
) in the Fixed Sequence Regime is satisfied by Assumption (
B2
) in the
\tau_{n}
-IID Regime, whenever
n
is sufficiently large and we condition on the event that
N^{\textup{\text{te}}}=\bar{n}^{\textup{\text{te}}}
and
N_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}=\bar{n}_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}\ \forall j\in\mathcal{Y}
. To see why, note that as argued earlier, conditioning on the aforementioned event ensures that
\gamma^{\ast}\in\Gamma
for all sufficiently large
n
. Since
\mathbf{p}\in\mathcal{Q}
, it therefore follows from Assumption (
B2
) that
\sigma_{\textup{min}}\big(\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast};\text{Cat})-\mathcal{I}(\gamma^{\ast})\big)>\nu
for all sufficiently large
n
.

•

Assumption (
A5
) in the Fixed Sequence Regime is always true in the
\tau_{n}
-IID Regime, because
\pi^{\ast},\pi^{\textup{\text{tr}}}\in\Delta
.

•

Assumption (
A6
) in the Fixed Sequence Regime is satisfied by Assumption (
B1
) in the
\tau_{n}
-IID Regime, whenever
n
is sufficiently large and we condition on the event that
N^{\textup{\text{te}}}=\bar{n}^{\textup{\text{te}}}
and
N_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}=\bar{n}_{j}^{\textup{\text{tr}}}\ \forall j\in\mathcal{Y}
. To see why, note that as argued earlier, conditioning on the aforementioned event ensures that
\gamma^{\ast}\in\Gamma
for all sufficiently large
n
. Since
